# Isaiah

ישעיהו

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah)

ביקורת על שחיתות דתית וקריאות לשינוי, תיאורים של עתיד אוטופי.

Criticism of religious corruption, calls for change, and descriptions of a utopian future.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Tanakh/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Miqra according to the Masorah (Segments: 1291, License: CC-BY-SA)

Version (En): The Koren Jerusalem Bible (Segments: 1291, License: CC-BY-NC)


## Chapter 1

###### Isaiah 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:1)


חֲזוֹן֙ יְשַֽׁעְיָ֣הוּ בֶן־אָמ֔וֹץ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָזָ֔ה עַל־יְהוּדָ֖ה וִירֽוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם בִּימֵ֨י עֻזִּיָּ֧הוּ יוֹתָ֛ם אָחָ֥ז יְחִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ מַלְכֵ֥י יְהוּדָֽה׃


The vision of Yesha῾yahu the son of Amoż, which he saw concerning Yehuda and Yerushalayim in the days of ῾Uzziyyahu, Yotam, Aĥaz, Yeĥizqiyyahu, kings of Yehuda.


###### Isaiah 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:2)


שִׁמְע֤וּ שָׁמַ֙יִם֙ וְהַאֲזִ֣ינִי אֶ֔רֶץ כִּ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבֵּ֑ר בָּנִים֙ גִּדַּ֣לְתִּי וְרוֹמַ֔מְתִּי וְהֵ֖ם פָּ֥שְׁעוּ בִֽי׃


Hear, O heavens, and give ear, O earth, for the Lord has spoken: I have reared and brought up children, and they have rebelled against me.


###### Isaiah 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:3)


יָדַ֥ע שׁוֹר֙ קֹנֵ֔הוּ וַחֲמ֖וֹר אֵב֣וּס בְּעָלָ֑יו יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ לֹ֣א יָדַ֔ע עַמִּ֖י לֹ֥א הִתְבּוֹנָֽן׃


The ox knows his owner, and the ass his master’s crib: but Yisra᾽el does not know, my people does not consider.


###### Isaiah 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:4)


ה֣וֹי**׀** גּ֣וֹי חֹטֵ֗א עַ֚ם כֶּ֣בֶד עָוֺ֔ן זֶ֣רַע מְרֵעִ֔ים בָּנִ֖ים מַשְׁחִיתִ֑ים עָזְב֣וּ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֗ה נִֽאֲצ֛וּ אֶת־קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל נָזֹ֥רוּ אָחֽוֹר׃


Ah sinful nation, a people laden with iniquity, a seed of evildoers, children that deal corruptly: they have forsaken the Lord, they have provoked the Holy One of Yisra᾽el to anger, they are gone away backward.


###### Isaiah 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:5)


עַ֣ל מֶ֥ה תֻכּ֛וּ ע֖וֹד תּוֹסִ֣יפוּ סָרָ֑ה כׇּל־רֹ֣אשׁ לׇֽחֳלִ֔י וְכׇל־לֵבָ֖ב דַּוָּֽי׃


Where could you still be smitten, that you revolt again: every head is sick, and every heart faint.


###### Isaiah 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:6)


מִכַּף־רֶ֤גֶל וְעַד־רֹאשׁ֙ אֵֽין־בּ֣וֹ מְתֹ֔ם פֶּ֥צַע וְחַבּוּרָ֖ה וּמַכָּ֣ה טְרִיָּ֑ה לֹא־זֹ֙רוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א חֻבָּ֔שׁוּ וְלֹ֥א רֻכְּכָ֖ה בַּשָּֽׁמֶן׃


From the sole of the foot even to the head there is no soundness in it; but wounds, and bruises, and putrefying sores: they have not been pressed, neither bound up, nor mollified with oil.


###### Isaiah 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:7)


אַרְצְכֶ֣ם שְׁמָמָ֔ה עָרֵיכֶ֖ם שְׂרֻפ֣וֹת אֵ֑שׁ אַדְמַתְכֶ֗ם לְנֶגְדְּכֶם֙ זָרִים֙ אֹכְלִ֣ים אֹתָ֔הּ וּשְׁמָמָ֖ה כְּמַהְפֵּכַ֥ת זָרִֽים׃


Your country is desolate, your cities are burned with fire: as for your land, strangers devour it in your presence, and it is desolate, as though overthrown by strangers.


###### Isaiah 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:8)


וְנוֹתְרָ֥ה בַת־צִיּ֖וֹן כְּסֻכָּ֣ה בְכָ֑רֶם כִּמְלוּנָ֥ה בְמִקְשָׁ֖ה כְּעִ֥יר נְצוּרָֽה׃


And the daughter of Żiyyon is left like a shelter in a vineyard, like a lodge in a garden of cucumbers, like a besieged city.


###### Isaiah 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:9)


לוּלֵי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת הוֹתִ֥יר לָ֛נוּ שָׂרִ֖יד כִּמְעָ֑ט כִּסְדֹ֣ם הָיִ֔ינוּ לַעֲמֹרָ֖ה דָּמִֽינוּ׃{פ}


If the Lord of hosts had not left us a very small remnant, we should have been like Sedom, and we should have been like ῾Amora.


###### Isaiah 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:10)


שִׁמְע֥וּ דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה קְצִינֵ֣י סְדֹ֑ם הַאֲזִ֛ינוּ תּוֹרַ֥ת אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ עַ֥ם עֲמֹרָֽה׃


Hear the word of the Lord, rulers of Sedom; give ear to the Tora of our God, you people of ῾Amora.


###### Isaiah 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:11)


לָמָּה־לִּ֤י רֹב־זִבְחֵיכֶם֙ יֹאמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה שָׂבַ֛עְתִּי עֹל֥וֹת אֵילִ֖ים וְחֵ֣לֶב מְרִיאִ֑ים וְדַ֨ם פָּרִ֧ים וּכְבָשִׂ֛ים וְעַתּוּדִ֖ים לֹ֥א חָפָֽצְתִּי׃


To what purpose is the multitude of your sacrifices to me? says the Lord: I am sated with the burnt offerings of rams, and the fat of fed beasts; and I delight not in the blood of bullocks, or of lambs, or of he goats.


###### Isaiah 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:12)


כִּ֣י תָבֹ֔אוּ לֵֽרָא֖וֹת פָּנָ֑י מִֽי־בִקֵּ֥שׁ זֹ֛את מִיֶּדְכֶ֖ם רְמֹ֥ס חֲצֵרָֽי׃


When you come to appear before me, who has required this at your hand, to trample my courts?


###### Isaiah 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:13)


לֹ֣א תוֹסִ֗יפוּ הָבִיא֙ מִנְחַת־שָׁ֔וְא קְטֹ֧רֶת תּוֹעֵבָ֛ה הִ֖יא לִ֑י חֹ֤דֶשׁ וְשַׁבָּת֙ קְרֹ֣א מִקְרָ֔א לֹא־אוּכַ֥ל אָ֖וֶן וַעֲצָרָֽה׃


Bring no more vain offerings; incense of abomination they are to me; as for new moons and sabbaths and the calling of assemblies, I cannot bear iniquity along with solemn meeting.


###### Isaiah 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:14)


חׇדְשֵׁיכֶ֤ם וּמֽוֹעֲדֵיכֶם֙ שָֽׂנְאָ֣ה נַפְשִׁ֔י הָי֥וּ עָלַ֖י לָטֹ֑רַח נִלְאֵ֖יתִי נְשֹֽׂא׃


Your new moons and your appointed feasts my soul hates: they are a trouble to me; I am weary of enduring them.


###### Isaiah 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:15)


וּבְפָרִשְׂכֶ֣ם כַּפֵּיכֶ֗ם אַעְלִ֤ים עֵינַי֙ מִכֶּ֔ם גַּ֛ם כִּֽי־תַרְבּ֥וּ תְפִלָּ֖ה אֵינֶ֣נִּי שֹׁמֵ֑עַ יְדֵיכֶ֖ם דָּמִ֥ים מָלֵֽאוּ׃


And when you spread out your hands, I will hide my eyes from you: even when you make many prayers, I will not hear: your hands are full of blood.


###### Isaiah 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:16)


רַֽחֲצוּ֙ הִזַּכּ֔וּ הָסִ֛ירוּ רֹ֥עַ מַעַלְלֵיכֶ֖ם מִנֶּ֣גֶד עֵינָ֑י חִדְל֖וּ הָרֵֽעַ׃


Wash you, make you clean; put away the evil of your doings from before my eyes; cease to do evil;


###### Isaiah 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:17)


לִמְד֥וּ הֵיטֵ֛ב דִּרְשׁ֥וּ מִשְׁפָּ֖ט אַשְּׁר֣וּ חָמ֑וֹץ שִׁפְט֣וּ יָת֔וֹם רִ֖יבוּ אַלְמָנָֽה׃{ס}


learn to do well; seek judgment, relieve the oppressed, judge the fatherless, plead for the widow.


###### Isaiah 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:18)


לְכוּ־נָ֛א וְנִוָּכְחָ֖ה יֹאמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה אִם־יִהְי֨וּ חֲטָאֵיכֶ֤ם כַּשָּׁנִים֙ כַּשֶּׁ֣לֶג יַלְבִּ֔ינוּ אִם־יַאְדִּ֥ימוּ כַתּוֹלָ֖ע כַּצֶּ֥מֶר יִֽהְיֽוּ׃


Come now, and let us reason together, says the Lord: though your sins be like scarlet, they shall be as white as snow; though they be red like crimson, they shall be white as wool.


###### Isaiah 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:19)


אִם־תֹּאב֖וּ וּשְׁמַעְתֶּ֑ם ט֥וּב הָאָ֖רֶץ תֹּאכֵֽלוּ׃


If you are willing and obedient, you shall eat the good of the land:


###### Isaiah 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:20)


וְאִם־תְּמָאֲנ֖וּ וּמְרִיתֶ֑ם חֶ֣רֶב תְּאֻכְּל֔וּ כִּ֛י פִּ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבֵּֽר׃{פ}


but if you refuse and rebel, you shall be devoured with the sword: for the mouth of the Lord has spoken it.


###### Isaiah 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:21)


אֵיכָה֙ הָיְתָ֣ה לְזוֹנָ֔ה קִרְיָ֖ה נֶאֱמָנָ֑ה מְלֵאֲתִ֣י מִשְׁפָּ֗ט צֶ֛דֶק יָלִ֥ין בָּ֖הּ וְעַתָּ֥ה מְרַצְּחִֽים׃


How is the faithful city become a harlot! it was full of judgment; righteousness lodged in it; but now murderers.


###### Isaiah 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:22)


כַּסְפֵּ֖ךְ הָיָ֣ה לְסִיגִ֑ים סׇבְאֵ֖ךְ מָה֥וּל בַּמָּֽיִם׃


Thy silver is become dross, thy wine is mixed with water:


###### Isaiah 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:23)


שָׂרַ֣יִךְ סוֹרְרִ֗ים וְחַבְרֵי֙ גַּנָּבִ֔ים כֻּלּוֹ֙ אֹהֵ֣ב שֹׁ֔חַד וְרֹדֵ֖ף שַׁלְמֹנִ֑ים יָתוֹם֙ לֹ֣א יִשְׁפֹּ֔טוּ וְרִ֥יב אַלְמָנָ֖ה לֹא־יָב֥וֹא אֲלֵיהֶֽם׃{ס}


thy princes are rebellious, and companions of thieves: every one loves bribes, and follows after rewards: they judge not the fatherless neither does the cause of the widow reach them.


###### Isaiah 1:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:24)


לָכֵ֗ן נְאֻ֤ם הָאָדוֹן֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת אֲבִ֖יר יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל ה֚וֹי אֶנָּחֵ֣ם מִצָּרַ֔י וְאִנָּקְמָ֖ה מֵאוֹיְבָֽי׃


Therefore says the Master, the Lord of hosts, the mighty One of Yisra᾽el, Ah, I will ease me of my adversaries, and avenge me of my enemies:


###### Isaiah 1:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:25)


וְאָשִׁ֤יבָה יָדִי֙ עָלַ֔יִךְ וְאֶצְרֹ֥ף כַּבֹּ֖ר סִיגָ֑יִךְ וְאָסִ֖ירָה כׇּל־בְּדִילָֽיִךְ׃


and I will turn my hand against thee, and purge away thy dross as with lye, and take away all thy base alloy.


###### Isaiah 1:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:26)


וְאָשִׁ֤יבָה שֹׁפְטַ֙יִךְ֙ כְּבָרִ֣אשֹׁנָ֔ה וְיֹעֲצַ֖יִךְ כְּבַתְּחִלָּ֑ה אַֽחֲרֵי־כֵ֗ן יִקָּ֤רֵא לָךְ֙ עִ֣יר הַצֶּ֔דֶק קִרְיָ֖ה נֶאֱמָנָֽה׃


And I will restore thy judges as at the first, and thy counsellors as at the beginning: afterwards thou shalt be called, The city of righteousness, a faithful city.


###### Isaiah 1:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:27)


צִיּ֖וֹן בְּמִשְׁפָּ֣ט תִּפָּדֶ֑ה וְשָׁבֶ֖יהָ בִּצְדָקָֽה׃


Żiyyon shall be redeemed with judgment, and those that return to her with righteousness.


###### Isaiah 1:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:28)


וְשֶׁ֧בֶר פֹּשְׁעִ֛ים וְחַטָּאִ֖ים יַחְדָּ֑ו וְעֹזְבֵ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה יִכְלֽוּ׃


And the destruction of the transgressors and of the sinners shall be together, and they that forsake the Lord shall be consumed.


###### Isaiah 1:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:29)


כִּ֣י יֵבֹ֔שׁוּ מֵאֵילִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר חֲמַדְתֶּ֑ם וְתַ֨חְפְּר֔וּ מֵהַגַּנּ֖וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר בְּחַרְתֶּֽם׃


For they shall be ashamed of the sacred oaks on which you set your desire, and you shall be confounded on account of the gardens of idolatry that you did choose.


###### Isaiah 1:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:30)


כִּ֣י תִֽהְי֔וּ כְּאֵלָ֖ה נֹבֶ֣לֶת עָלֶ֑הָ וּֽכְגַנָּ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־מַ֖יִם אֵ֥ין לָֽהּ׃


For you shall be like an oak whose leave are fading, and like a garden that has no water.


###### Isaiah 1:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/1:31)


וְהָיָ֤ה הֶחָסֹן֙ לִנְעֹ֔רֶת וּפֹעֲל֖וֹ לְנִיצ֑וֹץ וּבָעֲר֧וּ שְׁנֵיהֶ֛ם יַחְדָּ֖ו וְאֵ֥ין מְכַבֶּֽה׃{פ}


And the strong shall be as tow, and the maker of it as a spark, and they shall both burn together, and none shall quench them.



## Chapter 2

###### Isaiah 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:1)


הַדָּבָר֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָזָ֔ה יְשַֽׁעְיָ֖הוּ בֶּן־אָמ֑וֹץ עַל־יְהוּדָ֖ה וִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


The word that Yesha῾yahu the son of Amoż saw concerning Yehuda and Yerushalayim.


###### Isaiah 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:2)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** בְּאַחֲרִ֣ית הַיָּמִ֗ים נָכ֨וֹן יִֽהְיֶ֜ה הַ֤ר בֵּית־יְהֹוָה֙ בְּרֹ֣אשׁ הֶהָרִ֔ים וְנִשָּׂ֖א מִגְּבָע֑וֹת וְנָהֲר֥וּ אֵלָ֖יו כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִֽם׃


And it shall come to pass in the last days, that the mountain of the Lord’s house shall be established on the top of the mountains, and shall be exalted above the hills; and all the nations shall flow unto it.


###### Isaiah 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:3)


וְֽהָלְכ֞וּ עַמִּ֣ים רַבִּ֗ים וְאָֽמְרוּ֙ לְכ֣וּ**׀** וְנַעֲלֶ֣ה אֶל־הַר־יְהֹוָ֗ה אֶל־בֵּית֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יַעֲקֹ֔ב וְיֹרֵ֙נוּ֙ מִדְּרָכָ֔יו וְנֵלְכָ֖ה בְּאֹרְחֹתָ֑יו כִּ֤י מִצִּיּוֹן֙ תֵּצֵ֣א תוֹרָ֔ה וּדְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה מִירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


And many people shall go and say, Come, and let us go up to the mountain of the Lord, to the house of the God of Ya῾aqov; and he will teach us of his ways, and we will walk in his paths: for out of Żiyyon shall go forth Tora, and the word of the Lord from Yerushalayim.


###### Isaiah 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:4)


וְשָׁפַט֙ בֵּ֣ין הַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְהוֹכִ֖יחַ לְעַמִּ֣ים רַבִּ֑ים וְכִתְּת֨וּ חַרְבוֹתָ֜ם לְאִתִּ֗ים וַחֲנִיתֽוֹתֵיהֶם֙ לְמַזְמֵר֔וֹת לֹא־יִשָּׂ֨א ג֤וֹי אֶל־גּוֹי֙ חֶ֔רֶב וְלֹֽא־יִלְמְד֥וּ ע֖וֹד מִלְחָמָֽה׃{פ}


And he shall judge among the nations, and shall decide among many people: and they shall beat their swords into plowshares, and their spears into pruning hooks: nation shall not lift up sword against nation, neither shall they learn war any more.


###### Isaiah 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:5)


בֵּ֖ית יַעֲקֹ֑ב לְכ֥וּ וְנֵלְכָ֖ה בְּא֥וֹר יְהֹוָֽה׃


O house of Ya῾aqov, come, and let us walk in the light of the Lord.


###### Isaiah 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:6)


כִּ֣י נָטַ֗שְׁתָּה עַמְּךָ֙ בֵּ֣ית יַעֲקֹ֔ב כִּ֤י מָֽלְאוּ֙ מִקֶּ֔דֶם וְעֹנְנִ֖ים כַּפְּלִשְׁתִּ֑ים וּבְיַלְדֵ֥י נׇכְרִ֖ים יַשְׂפִּֽיקוּ׃


For thou hast abandoned thy people, the house of Ya῾aqov; for they are replenished from the east, and are soothsayers like the Pelishtim, and they please themselves in the children of strangers.


###### Isaiah 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:7)


וַתִּמָּלֵ֤א אַרְצוֹ֙ כֶּ֣סֶף וְזָהָ֔ב וְאֵ֥ין קֵ֖צֶה לְאֹצְרֹתָ֑יו וַתִּמָּלֵ֤א אַרְצוֹ֙ סוּסִ֔ים וְאֵ֥ין קֵ֖צֶה לְמַרְכְּבֹתָֽיו׃


Their land also is full of silver and gold, neither is there any end of their treasures; their land is also full of horses, neither is there any end of their chariots:


###### Isaiah 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:8)


וַתִּמָּלֵ֥א אַרְצ֖וֹ אֱלִילִ֑ים לְמַעֲשֵׂ֤ה יָדָיו֙ יִֽשְׁתַּחֲו֔וּ לַאֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשׂ֖וּ אֶצְבְּעֹתָֽיו׃


their land also is full of idols; they worship the work of their own hands, that which their own fingers have made:


###### Isaiah 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:9)


וַיִּשַּׁ֥ח אָדָ֖ם וַיִּשְׁפַּל־אִ֑ישׁ וְאַל־תִּשָּׂ֖א לָהֶֽם׃


and the mean man is bowed down, and the great man is brought low; forgive them not.


###### Isaiah 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:10)


בּ֣וֹא בַצּ֔וּר וְהִטָּמֵ֖ן בֶּעָפָ֑ר מִפְּנֵי֙ פַּ֣חַד יְהֹוָ֔ה וּמֵהֲדַ֖ר גְּאֹנֽוֹ׃


Enter into the rock, and hide in the dust, for fear of the Lord, and for the glory of his majesty.


###### Isaiah 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:11)


עֵינֵ֞י גַּבְה֤וּת אָדָם֙ שָׁפֵ֔ל וְשַׁ֖ח ר֣וּם אֲנָשִׁ֑ים וְנִשְׂגַּ֧ב יְהֹוָ֛ה לְבַדּ֖וֹ בַּיּ֥וֹם הַהֽוּא׃{פ}


The lofty looks of man shall be humbled, and the haughtiness of men shall be bowed down, and the Lord alone shall be exalted on that day.


###### Isaiah 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:12)


כִּ֣י י֞וֹם לַיהֹוָ֧ה צְבָא֛וֹת עַ֥ל כׇּל־גֵּאֶ֖ה וָרָ֑ם וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־נִשָּׂ֥א וְשָׁפֵֽל׃


For the day of the Lord of hosts shall be upon every one that is proud and lofty, and upon every one that is lifted up that he shall be brought low:


###### Isaiah 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:13)


וְעַל֙ כׇּל־אַרְזֵ֣י הַלְּבָנ֔וֹן הָרָמִ֖ים וְהַנִּשָּׂאִ֑ים וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־אַלּוֹנֵ֥י הַבָּשָֽׁן׃


and upon all the cedars of the Levanon, that are high and lifted up, and upon all the oaks of the Bashan,


###### Isaiah 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:14)


וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־הֶהָרִ֣ים הָרָמִ֑ים וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־הַגְּבָע֥וֹת הַנִּשָּׂאֽוֹת׃


and upon all the high mountains, and upon all the lofty hills,


###### Isaiah 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:15)


וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־מִגְדָּ֣ל גָּבֹ֑הַּ וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־חוֹמָ֥ה בְצוּרָֽה׃


and upon every high tower, and upon every fortified wall,


###### Isaiah 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:16)


וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־אֳנִיּ֣וֹת תַּרְשִׁ֑ישׁ וְעַ֖ל כׇּל־שְׂכִיּ֥וֹת הַחֶמְדָּֽה׃


and upon all the ships of Tarshish, and upon all delightful craftsmanship.


###### Isaiah 2:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:17)


וְשַׁח֙ גַּבְה֣וּת הָאָדָ֔ם וְשָׁפֵ֖ל ר֣וּם אֲנָשִׁ֑ים וְנִשְׂגַּ֧ב יְהֹוָ֛ה לְבַדּ֖וֹ בַּיּ֥וֹם הַהֽוּא׃


And the loftiness of man shall be bowed down, and the haughtiness of men shall be made low: and the Lord alone shall be exalted on that day.


###### Isaiah 2:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:18)


וְהָאֱלִילִ֖ים כָּלִ֥יל יַחֲלֹֽף׃


And the idols shall utterly be abolished.


###### Isaiah 2:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:19)


וּבָ֙אוּ֙ בִּמְעָר֣וֹת צֻרִ֔ים וּבִמְחִלּ֖וֹת עָפָ֑ר מִפְּנֵ֞י פַּ֤חַד יְהֹוָה֙ וּמֵהֲדַ֣ר גְּאוֹנ֔וֹ בְּקוּמ֖וֹ לַעֲרֹ֥ץ הָאָֽרֶץ׃


And they shall go into the holes of the rocks, and into the caves of the earth, for fear of the Lord, and for the glory of his majesty, when he arises to shake the earth terribly.


###### Isaiah 2:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:20)


בַּיּ֤וֹם הַהוּא֙ יַשְׁלִ֣יךְ הָאָדָ֔ם אֵ֚ת אֱלִילֵ֣י כַסְפּ֔וֹ וְאֵ֖ת אֱלִילֵ֣י זְהָב֑וֹ אֲשֶׁ֤ר עָֽשׂוּ־לוֹ֙ לְהִֽשְׁתַּחֲוֺ֔ת לַחְפֹּ֥ר פֵּר֖וֹת וְלָעֲטַלֵּפִֽים׃


In that day a man shall cast his idols of silver, and his idols of gold, which had been made for him to worship, to the moles and to the bats;


###### Isaiah 2:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:21)


לָבוֹא֙ בְּנִקְר֣וֹת הַצֻּרִ֔ים וּבִסְעִפֵ֖י הַסְּלָעִ֑ים מִפְּנֵ֞י פַּ֤חַד יְהֹוָה֙ וּמֵהֲדַ֣ר גְּאוֹנ֔וֹ בְּקוּמ֖וֹ לַעֲרֹ֥ץ הָאָֽרֶץ׃


to go into the clefts of the rocks, and into the crevices of the ragged rocks, for fear of the Lord, and for the glory of his majesty, when he arises to shake the earth terribly.


###### Isaiah 2:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/2:22)


חִדְל֤וּ לָכֶם֙ מִן־הָ֣אָדָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֥ר נְשָׁמָ֖ה בְּאַפּ֑וֹ כִּֽי־בַמֶּ֥ה נֶחְשָׁ֖ב הֽוּא׃{פ}


Cease from man, though his breath be in his nostrils: for in what is he to be accounted of?



## Chapter 3

###### Isaiah 3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:1)


כִּי֩ הִנֵּ֨ה הָאָד֜וֹן יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֗וֹת מֵסִ֤יר מִירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ וּמִ֣יהוּדָ֔ה מַשְׁעֵ֖ן וּמַשְׁעֵנָ֑ה כֹּ֚ל מִשְׁעַן־לֶ֔חֶם וְכֹ֖ל מִשְׁעַן־מָֽיִם׃


For, behold, the Master, the Lord of hosts, takes away from Yerushalayim and from Yehuda the stay and the staff, the whole stay of bread, and the whole stay of water,


###### Isaiah 3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:2)


גִּבּ֖וֹר וְאִ֣ישׁ מִלְחָמָ֑ה שׁוֹפֵ֥ט וְנָבִ֖יא וְקֹסֵ֥ם וְזָקֵֽן׃


the mighty man, and the man of war, the judge, and the prophet, and the diviner, and the elder,


###### Isaiah 3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:3)


שַׂר־חֲמִשִּׁ֖ים וּנְשׂ֣וּא פָנִ֑ים וְיוֹעֵ֛ץ וַחֲכַ֥ם חֲרָשִׁ֖ים וּנְב֥וֹן לָֽחַשׁ׃


the captain of fifty, and the honourable man, and the counsellor, and the cunning artificer, and the eloquent orator.


###### Isaiah 3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:4)


וְנָתַתִּ֥י נְעָרִ֖ים שָׂרֵיהֶ֑ם וְתַעֲלוּלִ֖ים יִמְשְׁלוּ־בָֽם׃


And I will make youngsters their princes, and babes shall rule over them.


###### Isaiah 3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:5)


וְנִגַּ֣שׂ הָעָ֔ם אִ֥ישׁ בְּאִ֖ישׁ וְאִ֣ישׁ בְּרֵעֵ֑הוּ יִרְהֲב֗וּ הַנַּ֙עַר֙ בַּזָּקֵ֔ן וְהַנִּקְלֶ֖ה בַּנִּכְבָּֽד׃


And the people shall be oppressed, every one by another, and every one by his neighbour: the child shall behave himself proudly against the elder, and the base against the honourable.


###### Isaiah 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:6)


כִּי־יִתְפֹּ֨שׂ אִ֤ישׁ בְּאָחִיו֙ בֵּ֣ית אָבִ֔יו שִׂמְלָ֣ה לְכָ֔ה קָצִ֖ין תִּֽהְיֶה־לָּ֑נוּ וְהַמַּכְשֵׁלָ֥ה הַזֹּ֖את תַּ֥חַת יָדֶֽךָ׃


When a man shall take hold of his brother of the house of his father, saying, Thou hast clothing, be thou our ruler, and let this ruin be under thy hand:


###### Isaiah 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:7)


יִשָּׂא֩ בַיּ֨וֹם הַה֤וּא׀לֵאמֹר֙ לֹא־אֶהְיֶ֣ה חֹבֵ֔שׁ וּבְבֵיתִ֕י אֵ֥ין לֶ֖חֶם וְאֵ֣ין שִׂמְלָ֑ה לֹ֥א תְשִׂימֻ֖נִי קְצִ֥ין עָֽם׃


in that day shall he swear, saying, I will not be a healer; for in my house is neither bread nor clothing: make me not a ruler of the people.


###### Isaiah 3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:8)


כִּ֤י כָֽשְׁלָה֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וִיהוּדָ֖ה נָפָ֑ל כִּֽי־לְשׁוֹנָ֤ם וּמַֽעַלְלֵיהֶם֙ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֔ה לַמְר֖וֹת עֵנֵ֥י כְבוֹדֽוֹ׃


For Yerushalayim is ruined, and Yehuda is fallen: because their tongue and their doings are against the Lord, to provoke the eyes of his glory.


###### Isaiah 3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:9)


הַכָּרַ֤ת פְּנֵיהֶם֙ עָ֣נְתָה בָּ֔ם וְחַטָּאתָ֛ם כִּסְדֹ֥ם הִגִּ֖ידוּ לֹ֣א כִחֵ֑דוּ א֣וֹי לְנַפְשָׁ֔ם כִּי־גָמְל֥וּ לָהֶ֖ם רָעָֽה׃


The show of their countenance witnesses against them; and they declare their sin as Sedom, they cannot hide it. Woe to their soul! for they have requited evil to themselves.


###### Isaiah 3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:10)


אִמְר֥וּ צַדִּ֖יק כִּי־ט֑וֹב כִּֽי־פְרִ֥י מַעַלְלֵיהֶ֖ם יֹאכֵֽלוּ׃


Say of the righteous, that it shall be well with him: for they shall eat the fruit of their doings.


###### Isaiah 3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:11)


א֖וֹי לְרָשָׁ֣ע רָ֑ע כִּֽי־גְמ֥וּל יָדָ֖יו יֵעָ֥שֶׂה לּֽוֹ׃


Alas! it shall be ill with the wicked: for according to the deserving of his hands shall be done to him.


###### Isaiah 3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:12)


עַמִּי֙ נֹגְשָׂ֣יו מְעוֹלֵ֔ל וְנָשִׁ֖ים מָ֣שְׁלוּ ב֑וֹ עַמִּי֙ מְאַשְּׁרֶ֣יךָ מַתְעִ֔ים וְדֶ֥רֶךְ אֹרְחֹתֶ֖יךָ בִּלֵּֽעוּ׃{פ}


As for my people, children are their oppressors, and women rule over them. O my people, they that lead thee cause thee to err, and destroy the way of thy paths.


###### Isaiah 3:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:13)


נִצָּ֥ב לָרִ֖יב יְהֹוָ֑ה וְעֹמֵ֖ד לָדִ֥ין עַמִּֽים׃


The Lord stands up to plead, and stands to judge the people.


###### Isaiah 3:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:14)


יְהֹוָה֙ בְּמִשְׁפָּ֣ט יָב֔וֹא עִם־זִקְנֵ֥י עַמּ֖וֹ וְשָׂרָ֑יו וְאַתֶּם֙ בִּעַרְתֶּ֣ם הַכֶּ֔רֶם גְּזֵלַ֥ת הֶעָנִ֖י בְּבָתֵּיכֶֽם׃


The Lord will enter into judgment with the elders of his people, and its princes, saying, It is you who have eaten up the vineyard; the robbery of the poor is in your houses.


###### Isaiah 3:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:15)


(מלכם) [מַה־לָּכֶם֙] תְּדַכְּא֣וּ עַמִּ֔י וּפְנֵ֥י עֲנִיִּ֖ים תִּטְחָ֑נוּ נְאֻם־אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִ֖ה צְבָאֽוֹת׃{ס}


What mean you by crushing my people to pieces, and grinding the faces of the poor? says the Lord God of hosts.


###### Isaiah 3:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:16)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְהֹוָ֗ה יַ֚עַן כִּ֤י גָֽבְהוּ֙ בְּנ֣וֹת צִיּ֔וֹן וַתֵּלַ֙כְנָה֙ (נטוות) [נְטוּי֣וֹת] גָּר֔וֹן וּֽמְשַׂקְּר֖וֹת עֵינָ֑יִם הָל֤וֹךְ וְטָפֹף֙ תֵּלַ֔כְנָה וּבְרַגְלֵיהֶ֖ם תְּעַכַּֽסְנָה׃


Moreover the Lord says, Because the daughters of Żiyyon are haughty, and walk with outstretched necks and ogling eyes, walking and mincing as they go, and making a tinkling with their feet:


###### Isaiah 3:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:17)


וְשִׂפַּ֣ח אֲדֹנָ֔י קׇדְקֹ֖ד בְּנ֣וֹת צִיּ֑וֹן וַיהֹוָ֖ה פׇּתְהֵ֥ן יְעָרֶֽה׃{ס}


therefore the Lord will smite with a scab the crown of the head of the daughters of Żiyyon, and the Lord will lay bare their secret parts.


###### Isaiah 3:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:18)


בַּיּ֨וֹם הַה֜וּא יָסִ֣יר אֲדֹנָ֗י אֵ֣ת תִּפְאֶ֧רֶת הָעֲכָסִ֛ים וְהַשְּׁבִיסִ֖ים וְהַשַּׂהֲרֹנִֽים׃


In that day the Lord will take away the bravery of the anklets, and the tiaras, and the necklaces,


###### Isaiah 3:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:19)


הַנְּטִפ֥וֹת וְהַשֵּׁיר֖וֹת וְהָרְעָלֽוֹת׃


the eardrops, and the bracelets, and the scarves,


###### Isaiah 3:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:20)


הַפְּאֵרִ֤ים וְהַצְּעָדוֹת֙ וְהַקִּשֻּׁרִ֔ים וּבָתֵּ֥י הַנֶּ֖פֶשׁ וְהַלְּחָשִֽׁים׃


the bonnets, and the armbands, and the belts, and the perfume boxes, and the amulets,


###### Isaiah 3:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:21)


הַטַּבָּע֖וֹת וְנִזְמֵ֥י הָאָֽף׃


the rings, and nose ornaments,


###### Isaiah 3:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:22)


הַמַּֽחֲלָצוֹת֙ וְהַמַּ֣עֲטָפ֔וֹת וְהַמִּטְפָּח֖וֹת וְהָחֲרִיטִֽים׃


the cloaks, and the mantles, and the gowns, and the handbags,


###### Isaiah 3:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:23)


וְהַגִּלְיֹנִים֙ וְהַסְּדִינִ֔ים וְהַצְּנִיפ֖וֹת וְהָרְדִידִֽים׃


the gauze, and the fine linen, and the hoods, and the veils.


###### Isaiah 3:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:24)


וְהָיָה֩ תַ֨חַת בֹּ֜שֶׂם מַ֣ק יִֽהְיֶ֗ה וְתַ֨חַת חֲגוֹרָ֤ה נִקְפָּה֙ וְתַ֨חַת מַעֲשֶׂ֤ה מִקְשֶׁה֙ קׇרְחָ֔ה וְתַ֥חַת פְּתִיגִ֖יל מַחֲגֹ֣רֶת שָׂ֑ק כִּי־תַ֖חַת יֹֽפִי׃


And it shall come to pass, that instead of a sweet smell there shall be a stench; and instead of a girdle a rope; and instead of well set hair, baldness; and instead of a fine dress a girding of sackcloth; instead of beauty, a brand.


###### Isaiah 3:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:25)


מְתַ֖יִךְ בַּחֶ֣רֶב יִפֹּ֑לוּ וּגְבוּרָתֵ֖ךְ בַּמִּלְחָמָֽה׃


Thy men shall fall by the sword, and thy mighty in the war,


###### Isaiah 3:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/3:26)


וְאָנ֥וּ וְאָבְל֖וּ פְּתָחֶ֑יהָ וְנִקָּ֖תָה לָאָ֥רֶץ תֵּשֵֽׁב׃


and her gates shall both lament and mourn; and she being desolate shall sit upon the ground.



## Chapter 4

###### Isaiah 4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:1)


וְהֶחֱזִ֩יקוּ֩ שֶׁ֨בַע נָשִׁ֜ים בְּאִ֣ישׁ אֶחָ֗ד בַּיּ֤וֹם הַהוּא֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר לַחְמֵ֣נוּ נֹאכֵ֔ל וְשִׂמְלָתֵ֖נוּ נִלְבָּ֑שׁ רַ֗ק יִקָּרֵ֤א שִׁמְךָ֙ עָלֵ֔ינוּ אֱסֹ֖ף חֶרְפָּתֵֽנוּ׃{ס}


And in that day seven women shall take hold of one man, saying, We will eat our own bread, and wear our own apparel: only let us be called by thy name; take away our reproach.


###### Isaiah 4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:2)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא יִֽהְיֶה֙ צֶ֣מַח יְהֹוָ֔ה לִצְבִ֖י וּלְכָב֑וֹד וּפְרִ֤י הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ לְגָא֣וֹן וּלְתִפְאֶ֔רֶת לִפְלֵיטַ֖ת יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


In that day shall the plant of the Lord be beautiful and glorious, and the fruit of the land shall be excellent and comely for them that are escaped of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:3)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** הַנִּשְׁאָ֣ר בְּצִיּ֗וֹן וְהַנּוֹתָר֙ בִּיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם קָד֖וֹשׁ יֵאָ֣מֶר ל֑וֹ כׇּל־הַכָּת֥וּב לַחַיִּ֖ים בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


And it shall come to pass, that he that is left in Żiyyon, and he that remains in Yerushalayim, shall be called holy, every one in Yerushalayim that is written to life:


###### Isaiah 4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:4)


אִ֣ם**׀** רָחַ֣ץ אֲדֹנָ֗י אֵ֚ת צֹאַ֣ת בְּנוֹת־צִיּ֔וֹן וְאֶת־דְּמֵ֥י יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם יָדִ֣יחַ מִקִּרְבָּ֑הּ בְּר֥וּחַ מִשְׁפָּ֖ט וּבְר֥וּחַ בָּעֵֽר׃


when the Lord shall have washed away the filth of the daughters of Żiyyon, and shall have purged the blood of Yerushalayim from its midst by the spirit of judgment, and by the spirit of burning.


###### Isaiah 4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:5)


וּבָרָ֣א יְהֹוָ֡ה עַל֩ כׇּל־מְכ֨וֹן הַר־צִיּ֜וֹן וְעַל־מִקְרָאֶ֗הָ עָנָ֤ן׀יוֹמָם֙ וְעָשָׁ֔ן וְנֹ֛גַהּ אֵ֥שׁ לֶהָבָ֖ה לָ֑יְלָה כִּ֥י עַל־כׇּל־כָּב֖וֹד חֻפָּֽה׃


And the Lord will create upon every dwelling place of mount Żiyyon, and upon her assemblies, a cloud and smoke by day, and the shining of a flaming fire by night: for upon all the glory shall there be a canopy.


###### Isaiah 4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/4:6)


וְסֻכָּ֛ה תִּהְיֶ֥ה לְצֵל־יוֹמָ֖ם מֵחֹ֑רֶב וּלְמַחְסֶה֙ וּלְמִסְתּ֔וֹר מִזֶּ֖רֶם וּמִמָּטָֽר׃{פ}


And there shall be a tabernacle for a shadow in the daytime from the heat, and for a place of refuge, and for a covert from storm and from rain.



## Chapter 5

###### Isaiah 5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:1)


אָשִׁ֤ירָה נָּא֙ לִֽידִידִ֔י שִׁירַ֥ת דּוֹדִ֖י לְכַרְמ֑וֹ כֶּ֛רֶם הָיָ֥ה לִידִידִ֖י בְּקֶ֥רֶן בֶּן־שָֽׁמֶן׃


Now will I sing to my wellbeloved a song of my beloved concerning his vineyard. My wellbeloved had a vineyard in a very fruitful hill:


###### Isaiah 5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:2)


וַֽיְעַזְּקֵ֣הוּ וַֽיְסַקְּלֵ֗הוּ וַיִּטָּעֵ֙הוּ֙ שֹׂרֵ֔ק וַיִּ֤בֶן מִגְדָּל֙ בְּתוֹכ֔וֹ וְגַם־יֶ֖קֶב חָצֵ֣ב בּ֑וֹ וַיְקַ֛ו לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת עֲנָבִ֖ים וַיַּ֥עַשׂ בְּאֻשִֽׁים׃


and he dug it, and cleared away its stones, and planted it with the choicest vine, and built a tower in the midst of it, and also hewed out a wine vat in it: and he looked that it should bring forth good grapes, but it brought forth bad grapes.


###### Isaiah 5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:3)


וְעַתָּ֛ה יוֹשֵׁ֥ב יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וְאִ֣ישׁ יְהוּדָ֑ה שִׁפְטוּ־נָ֕א בֵּינִ֖י וּבֵ֥ין כַּרְמִֽי׃


And now, O inhabitant of Yerushalayim, and man of Yehuda, judge, I pray you, between me and my vineyard.


###### Isaiah 5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:4)


מַה־לַּעֲשׂ֥וֹת עוֹד֙ לְכַרְמִ֔י וְלֹ֥א עָשִׂ֖יתִי בּ֑וֹ מַדּ֧וּעַ קִוֵּ֛יתִי לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת עֲנָבִ֖ים וַיַּ֥עַשׂ בְּאֻשִֽׁים׃


What could have been done more to my vineyard, that I have not done it? why was it, when I looked that it should bring forth good grapes, that it brought forth bad grapes?


###### Isaiah 5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:5)


וְעַתָּה֙ אוֹדִיעָה־נָּ֣א אֶתְכֶ֔ם אֵ֛ת אֲשֶׁר־אֲנִ֥י עֹשֶׂ֖ה לְכַרְמִ֑י הָסֵ֤ר מְשׂוּכָּתוֹ֙ וְהָיָ֣ה לְבָעֵ֔ר פָּרֹ֥ץ גְּדֵר֖וֹ וְהָיָ֥ה לְמִרְמָֽס׃


And now; I will tell you what I will do to my vineyard: I will take away its hedge, and it shall be eaten up; and break down its wall, and it shall be trodden down:


###### Isaiah 5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:6)


וַאֲשִׁיתֵ֣הוּ בָתָ֗ה לֹ֤א יִזָּמֵר֙ וְלֹ֣א יֵעָדֵ֔ר וְעָלָ֥ה שָׁמִ֖יר וָשָׁ֑יִת וְעַ֤ל הֶֽעָבִים֙ אֲצַוֶּ֔ה מֵהַמְטִ֥יר עָלָ֖יו מָטָֽר׃


and I will lay it waste: it shall be neither pruned, nor hoed; but there shall come up briers and thorns: I will also command the clouds that they drop no rain upon it.


###### Isaiah 5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:7)


כִּ֣י כֶ֜רֶם יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְאִ֣ישׁ יְהוּדָ֔ה נְטַ֖ע שַׁעֲשׁוּעָ֑יו וַיְקַ֤ו לְמִשְׁפָּט֙ וְהִנֵּ֣ה מִשְׂפָּ֔ח לִצְדָקָ֖ה וְהִנֵּ֥ה צְעָקָֽה׃{פ}


For the vineyard of the Lord of hosts is the house of Yisra᾽el, and the men of Yehuda his pleasant plant: and he looked for judgment, but behold oppression; for righteousness, but behold a cry.


###### Isaiah 5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:8)


ה֗וֹי מַגִּיעֵ֥י בַ֙יִת֙ בְּבַ֔יִת שָׂדֶ֥ה בְשָׂדֶ֖ה יַקְרִ֑יבוּ עַ֚ד אֶ֣פֶס מָק֔וֹם וְהוּשַׁבְתֶּ֥ם לְבַדְּכֶ֖ם בְּקֶ֥רֶב הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Woe to them that join house to house, that lay field to field till there is no room, that you may dwell alone in the midst of the earth!


###### Isaiah 5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:9)


בְּאׇזְנָ֖י יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֑וֹת אִם־לֹ֞א בָּתִּ֤ים רַבִּים֙ לְשַׁמָּ֣ה יִֽהְי֔וּ גְּדֹלִ֥ים וְטוֹבִ֖ים מֵאֵ֥ין יוֹשֵֽׁב׃


In my ears said the Lord of hosts, Of a truth many houses shall be desolate, great and fair, without inhabitant.


###### Isaiah 5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:10)


כִּ֗י עֲשֶׂ֙רֶת֙ צִמְדֵּי־כֶ֔רֶם יַעֲשׂ֖וּ בַּ֣ת אֶחָ֑ת וְזֶ֥רַע חֹ֖מֶר יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה אֵיפָֽה׃{ס}


For ten acres of vineyard shall yield the measure of a bat, and the seed of a ĥomer shall yield one efa.


###### Isaiah 5:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:11)


ה֛וֹי מַשְׁכִּימֵ֥י בַבֹּ֖קֶר שֵׁכָ֣ר יִרְדֹּ֑פוּ מְאַחֲרֵ֣י בַנֶּ֔שֶׁף יַ֖יִן יַדְלִיקֵֽם׃


Woe to them that rise up early in the morning, that they may follow strong drink; that continue until night, till wine inflame them!


###### Isaiah 5:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:12)


וְהָיָ֨ה כִנּ֜וֹר וָנֶ֗בֶל תֹּ֧ף וְחָלִ֛יל וָיַ֖יִן מִשְׁתֵּיהֶ֑ם וְאֵ֨ת פֹּ֤עַל יְהֹוָה֙ לֹ֣א יַבִּ֔יטוּ וּמַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יָדָ֖יו לֹ֥א רָאֽוּ׃


And the lyre, and the lute, the tambourine, and pipe, and wine, are in their feasts: but they regard not the work of the Lord, neither consider the operation of his hands.


###### Isaiah 5:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:13)


לָכֵ֛ן גָּלָ֥ה עַמִּ֖י מִבְּלִי־דָ֑עַת וּכְבוֹדוֹ֙ מְתֵ֣י רָעָ֔ב וַהֲמוֹנ֖וֹ צִחֵ֥ה צָמָֽא׃


Therefore my people are gone into captivity, because they have no knowledge: and their honourable men are famished, and their multitude dried up with thirst.


###### Isaiah 5:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:14)


לָכֵ֗ן הִרְחִ֤יבָה שְּׁאוֹל֙ נַפְשָׁ֔הּ וּפָעֲרָ֥ה פִ֖יהָ לִבְלִי־חֹ֑ק וְיָרַ֨ד הֲדָרָ֧הּ וַהֲמוֹנָ֛הּ וּשְׁאוֹנָ֖הּ וְעָלֵ֥ז בָּֽהּ׃


Therefore She᾽ol has enlarged herself, and opened her mouth without measure: and their glory, and their multitude, and their pomp, and he that is joyful, shall go down into it.


###### Isaiah 5:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:15)


וַיִּשַּׁ֥ח אָדָ֖ם וַיִּשְׁפַּל־אִ֑ישׁ וְעֵינֵ֥י גְבֹהִ֖ים תִּשְׁפַּֽלְנָה׃


And the mean man shall be brought down, and the mighty man shall be humbled, and the eyes of the lofty shall be brought low.


###### Isaiah 5:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:16)


וַיִּגְבַּ֛הּ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת בַּמִּשְׁפָּ֑ט וְהָאֵל֙ הַקָּד֔וֹשׁ נִקְדָּ֖שׁ בִּצְדָקָֽה׃


But the Lord of hosts shall be exalted in the judgment, and God that is holy shall be sanctified in righteousness.


###### Isaiah 5:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:17)


וְרָע֥וּ כְבָשִׂ֖ים כְּדׇבְרָ֑ם וְחׇרְב֥וֹת מֵחִ֖ים גָּרִ֥ים יֹאכֵֽלוּ׃{ס}


Then shall the lambs feed as in their pasture, and fat ones and strangers shall feed in the ruins.


###### Isaiah 5:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:18)


ה֛וֹי מֹשְׁכֵ֥י הֶעָוֺ֖ן בְּחַבְלֵ֣י הַשָּׁ֑וְא וְכַעֲב֥וֹת הָעֲגָלָ֖ה חַטָּאָֽה׃


Woe to them that draw iniquity with cords of vanity, and sin as it were with a cart rope:


###### Isaiah 5:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:19)


הָאֹמְרִ֗ים יְמַהֵ֧ר׀יָחִ֛ישָׁה מַעֲשֵׂ֖הוּ לְמַ֣עַן נִרְאֶ֑ה וְתִקְרַ֣ב וְתָב֗וֹאָה עֲצַ֛ת קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וְנֵדָֽעָה׃{פ}


that say, Let him make speed, and hasten his work, that we may see it: and let the counsel of the Holy One of Yisra᾽el draw near and come, that we may know it!


###### Isaiah 5:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:20)


ה֣וֹי הָאֹמְרִ֥ים לָרַ֛ע ט֖וֹב וְלַטּ֣וֹב רָ֑ע שָׂמִ֨ים חֹ֤שֶׁךְ לְאוֹר֙ וְא֣וֹר לְחֹ֔שֶׁךְ שָׂמִ֥ים מַ֛ר לְמָת֖וֹק וּמָת֥וֹק לְמָֽר׃{ס}


Woe to them that call evil good, and good evil; that put darkness for light, and light for darkness; that put bitter for sweet, and sweet for bitter!


###### Isaiah 5:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:21)


ה֖וֹי חֲכָמִ֣ים בְּעֵינֵיהֶ֑ם וְנֶ֥גֶד פְּנֵיהֶ֖ם נְבֹנִֽים׃{ס}


Woe to them that are wise in their own eyes, and prudent in their own sight!


###### Isaiah 5:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:22)


ה֕וֹי גִּבּוֹרִ֖ים לִשְׁתּ֣וֹת יָ֑יִן וְאַנְשֵׁי־חַ֖יִל לִמְסֹ֥ךְ שֵׁכָֽר׃


Woe to them that are mighty to drink wine, and warriors to mingle strong drink:


###### Isaiah 5:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:23)


מַצְדִּיקֵ֥י רָשָׁ֖ע עֵ֣קֶב שֹׁ֑חַד וְצִדְקַ֥ת צַדִּיקִ֖ים יָסִ֥ירוּ מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃{פ}


who justify the wicked for a bribe, and take away the righteousness of the righteous from him!


###### Isaiah 5:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:24)


לָכֵן֩ כֶּאֱכֹ֨ל קַ֜שׁ לְשׁ֣וֹן אֵ֗שׁ וַחֲשַׁ֤שׁ לֶהָבָה֙ יִרְפֶּ֔ה שׇׁרְשָׁם֙ כַּמָּ֣ק יִֽהְיֶ֔ה וּפִרְחָ֖ם כָּאָבָ֣ק יַעֲלֶ֑ה כִּ֣י מָאֲס֗וּ אֵ֚ת תּוֹרַת֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת וְאֵ֛ת אִמְרַ֥ת קְדֽוֹשׁ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל נִאֵֽצוּ׃


Therefore as the fire devours the stubble, and as the chaff is consumed on the flame, so their root shall be as rottenness, and their blossom shall go up as dust: because they have cast away the Tora of the Lord of hosts, and despised the word of the Holy One of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 5:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:25)


עַל־כֵּ֡ן חָרָה֩ אַף־יְהֹוָ֨ה בְּעַמּ֜וֹ וַיֵּ֣ט יָד֧וֹ עָלָ֣יו וַיַּכֵּ֗הוּ וַֽיִּרְגְּזוּ֙ הֶהָרִ֔ים וַתְּהִ֧י נִבְלָתָ֛ם כַּסּוּחָ֖ה בְּקֶ֣רֶב חוּצ֑וֹת בְּכׇל־זֹאת֙ לֹא־שָׁ֣ב אַפּ֔וֹ וְע֖וֹד יָד֥וֹ נְטוּיָֽה׃


Therefore the anger of the Lord burns against his people, and he has stretched out his hand against them, and has smitten them; and the hills did tremble, and their dead bodies, were as refuse in the midst of the streets. For all this his anger is not turned away, but his hand is stretched out still.


###### Isaiah 5:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:26)


וְנָֽשָׂא־נֵ֤ס לַגּוֹיִם֙ מֵרָח֔וֹק וְשָׁ֥רַק ל֖וֹ מִקְצֵ֣ה הָאָ֑רֶץ וְהִנֵּ֥ה מְהֵרָ֖ה קַ֥ל יָבֽוֹא׃


And he will lift up a banner to the nations from far, and will whistle to them from the ends of the earth: and, behold, they shall come with speed swiftly:


###### Isaiah 5:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:27)


אֵין־עָיֵ֤ף וְאֵין־כּוֹשֵׁל֙ בּ֔וֹ לֹ֥א יָנ֖וּם וְלֹ֣א יִישָׁ֑ן וְלֹ֤א נִפְתַּח֙ אֵז֣וֹר חֲלָצָ֔יו וְלֹ֥א נִתַּ֖ק שְׂר֥וֹךְ נְעָלָֽיו׃


none shall be weary or stumble among them; none shall slumber or sleep; neither shall the girdle of their loins be loosed, nor the latchet of their shoes be broken:


###### Isaiah 5:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:28)


אֲשֶׁ֤ר חִצָּיו֙ שְׁנוּנִ֔ים וְכׇל־קַשְּׁתֹתָ֖יו דְּרֻכ֑וֹת פַּרְס֤וֹת סוּסָיו֙ כַּצַּ֣ר נֶחְשָׁ֔בוּ וְגַלְגִּלָּ֖יו כַּסּוּפָֽה׃


whose arrows are sharp, and all their bows bent, their horses’ hoofs shall be counted like flint, and their wheels like the whirlwind:


###### Isaiah 5:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:29)


שְׁאָגָ֥ה ל֖וֹ כַּלָּבִ֑יא (ושאג) [יִשְׁאַ֨ג] כַּכְּפִירִ֤ים וְיִנְהֹם֙ וְיֹאחֵ֣ז טֶ֔רֶף וְיַפְלִ֖יט וְאֵ֥ין מַצִּֽיל׃


their roaring shall be like a lion, they shall shout and roar like young lions: and shall lay hold of the prey, and carry it away safe, and none shall deliver it.


###### Isaiah 5:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/5:30)


וְיִנְהֹ֥ם עָלָ֛יו בַּיּ֥וֹם הַה֖וּא כְּנַהֲמַת־יָ֑ם וְנִבַּ֤ט לָאָ֙רֶץ֙ וְהִנֵּה־חֹ֔שֶׁךְ צַ֣ר וָא֔וֹר חָשַׁ֖ךְ בַּעֲרִיפֶֽיהָ׃{פ}


And in that day they shall roar against them like the roaring of the sea: and if one look to the land, behold narrow darkness: the light also is darkened in its heavens.



## Chapter 6

###### Isaiah 6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:1)


בִּשְׁנַת־מוֹת֙ הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ עֻזִּיָּ֔הוּ וָאֶרְאֶ֧ה אֶת־אֲדֹנָ֛י יֹשֵׁ֥ב עַל־כִּסֵּ֖א רָ֣ם וְנִשָּׂ֑א וְשׁוּלָ֖יו מְלֵאִ֥ים אֶת־הַהֵיכָֽל׃


In the year that king ῾Uzziyyahu died I saw the Lord sitting upon a throne, high and lifted up, and his train filled the temple.


###### Isaiah 6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:2)


שְׂרָפִ֨ים עֹמְדִ֤ים׀מִמַּ֙עַל֙ ל֔וֹ שֵׁ֧שׁ כְּנָפַ֛יִם שֵׁ֥שׁ כְּנָפַ֖יִם לְאֶחָ֑ד בִּשְׁתַּ֣יִם**׀** יְכַסֶּ֣ה פָנָ֗יו וּבִשְׁתַּ֛יִם יְכַסֶּ֥ה רַגְלָ֖יו וּבִשְׁתַּ֥יִם יְעוֹפֵֽף׃


Serafim stood above him: each one had six wings; with two he covered his face, and with two he covered his feet, and with two he did fly.


###### Isaiah 6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:3)


וְקָרָ֨א זֶ֤ה אֶל־זֶה֙ וְאָמַ֔ר קָד֧וֹשׁ׀קָד֛וֹשׁ קָד֖וֹשׁ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֑וֹת מְלֹ֥א כׇל־הָאָ֖רֶץ כְּבוֹדֽוֹ׃


And one cried to another, and said, Holy, holy, holy, is the Lord of hosts: the whole earth is full of his glory.


###### Isaiah 6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:4)


וַיָּנֻ֙עוּ֙ אַמּ֣וֹת הַסִּפִּ֔ים מִקּ֖וֹל הַקּוֹרֵ֑א וְהַבַּ֖יִת יִמָּלֵ֥א עָשָֽׁן׃


And the posts of the door moved at the voice of him that cried, and the house was filled with smoke.


###### Isaiah 6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:5)


וָאֹמַ֞ר אֽוֹי־לִ֣י כִֽי־נִדְמֵ֗יתִי כִּ֣י אִ֤ישׁ טְמֵֽא־שְׂפָתַ֙יִם֙ אָנֹ֔כִי וּבְתוֹךְ֙ עַם־טְמֵ֣א שְׂפָתַ֔יִם אָנֹכִ֖י יוֹשֵׁ֑ב כִּ֗י אֶת־הַמֶּ֛לֶךְ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת רָא֥וּ עֵינָֽי׃


Then said I, Woe is me! for I am ruined; because I am a man of unclean lips, and I dwell in the midst of a people of unclean lips: for my eyes have seen the King, the Lord of hosts.


###### Isaiah 6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:6)


וַיָּ֣עׇף אֵלַ֗י אֶחָד֙ מִן־הַשְּׂרָפִ֔ים וּבְיָד֖וֹ רִצְפָּ֑ה בְּמֶ֨לְקַחַ֔יִם לָקַ֖ח מֵעַ֥ל הַמִּזְבֵּֽחַ׃


Then one of the serafim flew to me, having a live coal in his hand, which he had taken with tongs from off the altar:


###### Isaiah 6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:7)


וַיַּגַּ֣ע עַל־פִּ֔י וַיֹּ֕אמֶר הִנֵּ֛ה נָגַ֥ע זֶ֖ה עַל־שְׂפָתֶ֑יךָ וְסָ֣ר עֲוֺנֶ֔ךָ וְחַטָּאתְךָ֖ תְּכֻפָּֽר׃


and he laid it upon my mouth, and said, Lo, this has touched thy lips; and thy iniquity is taken away, and thy sin is purged.


###### Isaiah 6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:8)


וָאֶשְׁמַ֞ע אֶת־ק֤וֹל אֲדֹנָי֙ אֹמֵ֔ר אֶת־מִ֥י אֶשְׁלַ֖ח וּמִ֣י יֵֽלֶךְ־לָ֑נוּ וָאֹמַ֖ר הִנְנִ֥י שְׁלָחֵֽנִי׃


Also I heard the voice of the Lord, saying, Whom shall I send, and who will go for us? Then said I, Here am I; send me.


###### Isaiah 6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:9)


וַיֹּ֕אמֶר לֵ֥ךְ וְאָמַרְתָּ֖ לָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֑ה שִׁמְע֤וּ שָׁמ֙וֹעַ֙ וְאַל־תָּבִ֔ינוּ וּרְא֥וּ רָא֖וֹ וְאַל־תֵּדָֽעוּ׃


And he said, Go, and tell this people, Hear indeed, but understand not; and see indeed, but perceive not.


###### Isaiah 6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:10)


הַשְׁמֵן֙ לֵב־הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֔ה וְאׇזְנָ֥יו הַכְבֵּ֖ד וְעֵינָ֣יו הָשַׁ֑ע פֶּן־יִרְאֶ֨ה בְעֵינָ֜יו וּבְאׇזְנָ֣יו יִשְׁמָ֗ע וּלְבָב֥וֹ יָבִ֛ין וָשָׁ֖ב וְרָ֥פָא לֽוֹ׃


Make the heart of this people fat, and make their ears heavy, and smear over their eyes; lest they see with their eyes, and hear with their ears, and understand with their heart, and return, and be healed.


###### Isaiah 6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:11)


וָאֹמַ֕ר עַד־מָתַ֖י אֲדֹנָ֑י וַיֹּ֡אמֶר עַ֣ד אֲשֶׁר֩ אִם־שָׁא֨וּ עָרִ֜ים מֵאֵ֣ין יוֹשֵׁ֗ב וּבָתִּים֙ מֵאֵ֣ין אָדָ֔ם וְהָאֲדָמָ֖ה תִּשָּׁאֶ֥ה שְׁמָמָֽה׃


Then said I, Lord, how long? And he answered, Until the cities be wasted without inhabitant, and the houses without man, and the land be utterly desolate,


###### Isaiah 6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:12)


וְרִחַ֥ק יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־הָאָדָ֑ם וְרַבָּ֥ה הָעֲזוּבָ֖ה בְּקֶ֥רֶב הָאָֽרֶץ׃


and the Lord has removed men far away, and there be a great forsaking in the midst of the land.


###### Isaiah 6:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/6:13)


וְע֥וֹד בָּהּ֙ עֲשִׂ֣רִיָּ֔ה וְשָׁ֖בָה וְהָיְתָ֣ה לְבָעֵ֑ר כָּאֵלָ֣ה וְכָאַלּ֗וֹן אֲשֶׁ֤ר בְּשַׁלֶּ֙כֶת֙ מַצֶּ֣בֶת בָּ֔ם זֶ֥רַע קֹ֖דֶשׁ מַצַּבְתָּֽהּ׃{פ}


And if one tenth remain in it, then that shall again be consumed: but like a terebinth and like an oak, whose stump remains when they cast their leaves, so the holy seed is its immovable stump.



## Chapter 7

###### Isaiah 7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:1)


וַיְהִ֡י בִּימֵ֣י אָ֠חָ֠ז בֶּן־יוֹתָ֨ם בֶּן־עֻזִּיָּ֜הוּ מֶ֣לֶךְ יְהוּדָ֗ה עָלָ֣ה רְצִ֣ין מֶֽלֶךְ־אֲ֠רָ֠ם וּפֶ֨קַח בֶּן־רְמַלְיָ֤הוּ מֶֽלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם לַמִּלְחָמָ֖ה עָלֶ֑יהָ וְלֹ֥א יָכֹ֖ל לְהִלָּחֵ֥ם עָלֶֽיהָ׃


And it came to pass in the days of Aĥaz the son of Yotam, the son of ῾Uzziyyahu, king of Yehuda, that Reżin the king of Aram, and Peqaĥ the son of Remalyahu, king of Yisra᾽el, went up to Yerushalayim to war against it, but could not prevail against it.


###### Isaiah 7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:2)


וַיֻּגַּ֗ד לְבֵ֤ית דָּוִד֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר נָ֥חָה אֲרָ֖ם עַל־אֶפְרָ֑יִם וַיָּ֤נַע לְבָבוֹ֙ וּלְבַ֣ב עַמּ֔וֹ כְּנ֥וֹעַ עֲצֵי־יַ֖עַר מִפְּנֵי־רֽוּחַ׃{ס}


And it was told the house of David, saying, Aram is confederate with Efrayim. And his heart was moved, and the heart of his people, as the trees of the forest are moved with the wind.


###### Isaiah 7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:3)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְהֹוָה֮ אֶֽל־יְשַֽׁעְיָ֒הוּ֒ צֵא־נָא֙ לִקְרַ֣את אָחָ֔ז אַתָּ֕ה וּשְׁאָ֖ר יָשׁ֣וּב בְּנֶ֑ךָ אֶל־קְצֵ֗ה תְּעָלַת֙ הַבְּרֵכָ֣ה הָעֶלְיוֹנָ֔ה אֶל־מְסִלַּ֖ת שְׂדֵ֥ה כוֹבֵֽס׃


Then said the Lord to Yesha῾yahu, Go out now to meet Aĥaz, thou, and She᾽ar-yashuv thy son, at the end of the aqueduct of the upper pool in the highway of the washers’ field;


###### Isaiah 7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:4)


וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֵ֠לָ֠יו הִשָּׁמֵ֨ר וְהַשְׁקֵ֜ט אַל־תִּירָ֗א וּלְבָֽבְךָ֙ אַל־יֵרַ֔ךְ מִשְּׁנֵ֨י זַנְב֧וֹת הָאוּדִ֛ים הָעֲשֵׁנִ֖ים הָאֵ֑לֶּה בׇּחֳרִי־אַ֛ף רְצִ֥ין וַאֲרָ֖ם וּבֶן־רְמַלְיָֽהוּ׃


and say to him, Take heed, and be quiet; fear not, neither be fainthearted on account of the two tails of these smoking firebrands, for the fierce anger of Reżin and Aram, and of the son of Remalyahu.


###### Isaiah 7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:5)


יַ֗עַן כִּֽי־יָעַ֥ץ עָלֶ֛יךָ אֲרָ֖ם רָעָ֑ה אֶפְרַ֥יִם וּבֶן־רְמַלְיָ֖הוּ לֵאמֹֽר׃


Because Aram, Efrayim, and the son of Remalyahu, have taken evil counsel against thee, saying,


###### Isaiah 7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:6)


נַעֲלֶ֤ה בִֽיהוּדָה֙ וּנְקִיצֶ֔נָּה וְנַבְקִעֶ֖נָּה אֵלֵ֑ינוּ וְנַמְלִ֥יךְ מֶ֙לֶךְ֙ בְּתוֹכָ֔הּ אֵ֖ת בֶּן־טָֽבְאַֽל׃{פ}


Let us go up against Yehuda, and harass it, and let us make a breach in it for us, and set a king in the midst of it, namely the son of Tave᾽all:


###### Isaiah 7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:7)


כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֖ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה לֹ֥א תָק֖וּם וְלֹ֥א תִֽהְיֶֽה׃


thus says the Lord God, It shall not stand; neither shall it come to pass.


###### Isaiah 7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:8)


כִּ֣י רֹ֤אשׁ אֲרָם֙ דַּמֶּ֔שֶׂק וְרֹ֥אשׁ דַּמֶּ֖שֶׂק רְצִ֑ין וּבְע֗וֹד שִׁשִּׁ֤ים וְחָמֵשׁ֙ שָׁנָ֔ה יֵחַ֥ת אֶפְרַ֖יִם מֵעָֽם׃


For the head of Aram is Dammeseq, and the head of Dammeseq is Reżin; and within sixty five years Efrayim shall be broken in pieces, and no more a people.


###### Isaiah 7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:9)


וְרֹ֤אשׁ אֶפְרַ֙יִם֙ שֹׁמְר֔וֹן וְרֹ֥אשׁ שֹׁמְר֖וֹן בֶּן־רְמַלְיָ֑הוּ אִ֚ם לֹ֣א תַאֲמִ֔ינוּ כִּ֖י לֹ֥א תֵאָמֵֽנוּ׃{פ}


And the head of Efrayim is Shomeron, and the head of Shomeron is the son of Remalyahu. If you have no faith you shall not be established.


###### Isaiah 7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:10)


וַיּ֣וֹסֶף יְהֹוָ֔ה דַּבֵּ֥ר אֶל־אָחָ֖ז לֵאמֹֽר׃


Moreover the Lord spoke again to Aĥaz, saying,


###### Isaiah 7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:11)


שְׁאַל־לְךָ֣ א֔וֹת מֵעִ֖ם יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ הַעְמֵ֣ק שְׁאָ֔לָה א֖וֹ הַגְבֵּ֥הַּ לְמָֽעְלָה׃


Ask a sign of the Lord thy God; ask it either in the depth, or in the height above.


###### Isaiah 7:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:12)


וַיֹּ֖אמֶר אָחָ֑ז לֹא־אֶשְׁאַ֥ל וְלֹֽא־אֲנַסֶּ֖ה אֶת־יְהֹוָֽה׃


But Aĥaz said, I will not ask, neither will I try the Lord.


###### Isaiah 7:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:13)


וַיֹּ֕אמֶר שִׁמְעוּ־נָ֖א בֵּ֣ית דָּוִ֑ד הַמְעַ֤ט מִכֶּם֙ הַלְא֣וֹת אֲנָשִׁ֔ים כִּ֥י תַלְא֖וּ גַּ֥ם אֶת־אֱלֹהָֽי׃


And he said, Hear now, O house of David; Is it a small thing for you to weary men, but will you weary my God also?


###### Isaiah 7:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:14)


לָ֠כֵ֠ן יִתֵּ֨ן אֲדֹנָ֥י ה֛וּא לָכֶ֖ם א֑וֹת הִנֵּ֣ה הָעַלְמָ֗ה הָרָה֙ וְיֹלֶ֣דֶת בֵּ֔ן וְקָרָ֥את שְׁמ֖וֹ עִמָּ֥נוּ אֵֽל׃


Therefore the Lord himself shall give you a sign; Behold, the young woman is with child, and she will bear a son, and shall call his name ῾Immanu᾽el.


###### Isaiah 7:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:15)


חֶמְאָ֥ה וּדְבַ֖שׁ יֹאכֵ֑ל לְדַעְתּ֛וֹ מָא֥וֹס בָּרָ֖ע וּבָח֥וֹר בַּטּֽוֹב׃


Butter and honey shall he eat, when he shall know how to refuse the evil, and choose the good.


###### Isaiah 7:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:16)


כִּ֠י בְּטֶ֨רֶם יֵדַ֥ע הַנַּ֛עַר מָאֹ֥ס בָּרָ֖ע וּבָחֹ֣ר בַּטּ֑וֹב תֵּעָזֵ֤ב הָאֲדָמָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר אַתָּ֣ה קָ֔ץ מִפְּנֵ֖י שְׁנֵ֥י מְלָכֶֽיהָ׃


For before the child shall know how to refuse the evil, and choose the good, the land whose two kings thou dreadest shall be deserted.


###### Isaiah 7:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:17)


יָבִ֨יא יְהֹוָ֜ה עָלֶ֗יךָ וְעַֽל־עַמְּךָ֮ וְעַל־בֵּ֣ית אָבִ֒יךָ֒ יָמִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־בָ֔אוּ לְמִיּ֥וֹם סוּר־אֶפְרַ֖יִם מֵעַ֣ל יְהוּדָ֑ה אֵ֖ת מֶ֥לֶךְ אַשּֽׁוּר׃{פ}


The Lord shall bring upon thee, and upon thy people, and upon thy father’s house, days that have not come since the day that Efrayim departed from Yehuda; namely the king of Ashshur.


###### Isaiah 7:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:18)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא יִשְׁרֹ֤ק יְהֹוָה֙ לַזְּב֔וּב אֲשֶׁ֥ר בִּקְצֵ֖ה יְאֹרֵ֣י מִצְרָ֑יִם וְלַ֨דְּבוֹרָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר בְּאֶ֥רֶץ אַשּֽׁוּר׃


And it shall come to pass on that day, that the Lord shall whistle to the fly that is in the uttermost end of the canals of Miżrayim, and to the bee that is in the land of Ashshur.


###### Isaiah 7:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:19)


וּבָ֨אוּ וְנָח֤וּ כֻלָּם֙ בְּנַחֲלֵ֣י הַבַּתּ֔וֹת וּבִנְקִיקֵ֖י הַסְּלָעִ֑ים וּבְכֹל֙ הַנַּ֣עֲצוּצִ֔ים וּבְכֹ֖ל הַנַּהֲלֹלִֽים׃


And they shall come, and shall rest all of them in the desolate wadis, and in the holes of the rocks, and upon all thorns, and upon all branches.


###### Isaiah 7:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:20)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֡וּא יְגַלַּ֣ח אֲדֹנָי֩ בְּתַ֨עַר הַשְּׂכִירָ֜ה בְּעֶבְרֵ֤י נָהָר֙ בְּמֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֔וּר אֶת־הָרֹ֖אשׁ וְשַׂ֣עַר הָרַגְלָ֑יִם וְגַ֥ם אֶת־הַזָּקָ֖ן תִּסְפֶּֽה׃{פ}


On the same day shall the Lord shave with a hired razor that is hired (namely with them beyond the river with the king of Ashshur) the head, and the hair of the legs: and it shall also sweep away the beard.


###### Isaiah 7:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:21)


וְהָיָ֖ה בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֑וּא יְחַיֶּה־אִ֛ישׁ עֶגְלַ֥ת בָּקָ֖ר וּשְׁתֵּי־צֹֽאן׃


And it shall come to pass in that day, that a man shall nourish a young cow, and two sheep;


###### Isaiah 7:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:22)


וְהָיָ֗ה מֵרֹ֛ב עֲשׂ֥וֹת חָלָ֖ב יֹאכַ֣ל חֶמְאָ֑ה כִּֽי־חֶמְאָ֤ה וּדְבַשׁ֙ יֹאכֵ֔ל כׇּל־הַנּוֹתָ֖ר בְּקֶ֥רֶב הָאָֽרֶץ׃{ס}


and it shall come to pass, from the abundance of milk that they shall give he shall eat butter: for butter and honey shall every one eat that is left in the land.


###### Isaiah 7:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:23)


וְהָיָה֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא יִהְיֶ֣ה כׇל־מָק֗וֹם אֲשֶׁ֧ר יִֽהְיֶה־שָּׁ֛ם אֶ֥לֶף גֶּ֖פֶן בְּאֶ֣לֶף כָּ֑סֶף לַשָּׁמִ֥יר וְלַשַּׁ֖יִת יִהְיֶֽה׃


And it shall come to pass in that day, that every place shall be, where there were a thousand vines worth a thousand silver shekels, it shall be for briers and thorns.


###### Isaiah 7:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:24)


בַּחִצִּ֥ים וּבַקֶּ֖שֶׁת יָ֣בוֹא שָׁ֑מָּה כִּֽי־שָׁמִ֥יר וָשַׁ֖יִת תִּֽהְיֶ֥ה כׇל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


With the arrows and with the bow shall men come there; because all the land shall become briers and thorns.


###### Isaiah 7:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/7:25)


וְכֹ֣ל הֶהָרִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֤ר בַּמַּעְדֵּר֙ יֵעָ֣דֵר֔וּן לֹא־תָב֣וֹא שָׁ֔מָּה יִרְאַ֖ת שָׁמִ֣יר וָשָׁ֑יִת וְהָיָה֙ לְמִשְׁלַ֣ח שׁ֔וֹר וּלְמִרְמַ֖ס שֶֽׂה׃{פ}


And on all hills that are hoed with the hoe, thou shalt not come there for fear of briers and thorns: but it shall be for the sending forth of oxen, and for the treading of sheep.



## Chapter 8

###### Isaiah 8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/8:1)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י קַח־לְךָ֖ גִּלָּי֣וֹן גָּד֑וֹל וּכְתֹ֤ב עָלָיו֙ בְּחֶ֣רֶט אֱנ֔וֹשׁ לְמַהֵ֥ר שָׁלָ֖ל חָ֥שׁ בַּֽז׃


Moreover the Lord said to me, Take a great roll, and write on it with a common pen, The spoil speeds; the prey hastens.


###### Isaiah 8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/8:2)


וְאָעִ֣ידָה לִּ֔י עֵדִ֖ים נֶאֱמָנִ֑ים אֵ֚ת אוּרִיָּ֣ה הַכֹּהֵ֔ן וְאֶת־זְכַרְיָ֖הוּ בֶּ֥ן יְבֶרֶכְיָֽהוּ׃


And I took to myself faithful witnesses namely Uriyya the priest, and Zekharyahu the son of Yeverekhyahu.


###### Isaiah 8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/8:3)


וָֽאֶקְרַב֙ אֶל־הַנְּבִיאָ֔ה וַתַּ֖הַר וַתֵּ֣לֶד בֵּ֑ן{ס}וַיֹּ֤אמֶר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י קְרָ֣א שְׁמ֔וֹ מַהֵ֥ר שָׁלָ֖ל חָ֥שׁ בַּֽז׃


And I came near to the prophetess; and she conceived and bore a son. And the Lord said to me, call his name Maher-shalal-ĥash-baz (the spoil speeds; the prey hastens).


###### Isaiah 8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/8:4)


כִּ֗י בְּטֶ֙רֶם֙ יֵדַ֣ע הַנַּ֔עַר קְרֹ֖א אָבִ֣י וְאִמִּ֑י יִשָּׂ֣א**׀** אֶת־חֵ֣יל דַּמֶּ֗שֶׂק וְאֵת֙ שְׁלַ֣ל שֹׁמְר֔וֹן לִפְנֵ֖י מֶ֥לֶךְ אַשּֽׁוּר׃{ס}


For before the child shall know how to cry, My father, and my mother, the riches of Dammeseq and the spoil of Shomeron shall be taken away before the king of Ashshur.


###### Isaiah 8:5
