[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:13)


וַיַּכְרֵ֨ת יְהֹוָ֜ה מִיִּשְׂרָאֵ֗ל רֹ֧אשׁ וְזָנָ֛ב כִּפָּ֥ה וְאַגְמ֖וֹן י֥וֹם אֶחָֽד׃


Therefore the Lord cut off from Yisra᾽el, head and tail, branch and rush, in one day.


###### Isaiah 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:14)


זָקֵ֥ן וּנְשֽׂוּא־פָנִ֖ים ה֣וּא הָרֹ֑אשׁ וְנָבִ֥יא מֽוֹרֶה־שֶּׁ֖קֶר ה֥וּא הַזָּנָֽב׃


The elder and the honourable man, he is the head; and the prophet that teaches lies, he is the tail.


###### Isaiah 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:15)


וַיִּֽהְי֛וּ מְאַשְּׁרֵ֥י הָֽעָם־הַזֶּ֖ה מַתְעִ֑ים וּמְאֻשָּׁרָ֖יו מְבֻלָּעִֽים׃


For the leaders of this people cause them to err; and they that are led by them are destroyed.


###### Isaiah 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:16)


עַל־כֵּ֨ן עַל־בַּחוּרָ֜יו לֹא־יִשְׂמַ֣ח**׀** אֲדֹנָ֗י וְאֶת־יְתֹמָ֤יו וְאֶת־אַלְמְנוֹתָיו֙ לֹ֣א יְרַחֵ֔ם כִּ֤י כֻלּוֹ֙ חָנֵ֣ף וּמֵרַ֔ע וְכׇל־פֶּ֖ה דֹּבֵ֣ר נְבָלָ֑ה בְּכׇל־זֹאת֙ לֹא־שָׁ֣ב אַפּ֔וֹ וְע֖וֹד יָד֥וֹ נְטוּיָֽה׃


Therefore the Lord shall have no joy in their young men, neither shall have mercy on their fatherless and widows: for everyone is a flatterer and an evildoer, and every mouth speaks folly. For all this his anger is not turned away, but his hand is stretched out still.


###### Isaiah 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:17)


כִּֽי־בָעֲרָ֤ה כָאֵשׁ֙ רִשְׁעָ֔ה שָׁמִ֥יר וָשַׁ֖יִת תֹּאכֵ֑ל וַתִּצַּת֙ בְּסִֽבְכֵ֣י הַיַּ֔עַר וַיִּֽתְאַבְּכ֖וּ גֵּא֥וּת עָשָֽׁן׃


For wickedness burns like the fire: it devours the briers and thorns, and kindles in the thickets of the forest, and they mount up in a billow of smoke.


###### Isaiah 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:18)


בְּעֶבְרַ֛ת יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת נֶעְתַּ֣ם אָ֑רֶץ וַיְהִ֤י הָעָם֙ כְּמַאֲכֹ֣לֶת אֵ֔שׁ אִ֥ישׁ אֶל־אָחִ֖יו לֹ֥א יַחְמֹֽלוּ׃


Through the wrath of the Lord of hosts is the land darkened, and the people are as the fuel of the fire: no man spares his brother.


###### Isaiah 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:19)


וַיִּגְזֹ֤ר עַל־יָמִין֙ וְרָעֵ֔ב וַיֹּ֥אכַל עַל־שְׂמֹ֖אול וְלֹ֣א שָׂבֵ֑עוּ אִ֥ישׁ בְּשַׂר־זְרֹע֖וֹ יֹאכֵֽלוּ׃


And he snatches on the right hand, and is hungry; and he eats on the left hand, and they are not satisfied: they eat every man the flesh of his own arm:


###### Isaiah 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/9:20)


מְנַשֶּׁ֣ה אֶת־אֶפְרַ֗יִם וְאֶפְרַ֙יִם֙ אֶת־מְנַשֶּׁ֔ה יַחְדָּ֥ו הֵ֖מָּה עַל־יְהוּדָ֑ה בְּכׇל־זֹאת֙ לֹא־שָׁ֣ב אַפּ֔וֹ וְע֖וֹד יָד֥וֹ נְטוּיָֽה׃{ס}


Menashshe against Efrayim; and Efrayim against Menashshe: and they together are against Yehuda. For all this his anger is not turned away, but his hand is stretched out still.



## Chapter 10

###### Isaiah 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:1)


ה֥וֹי הַחֹֽקְקִ֖ים חִקְקֵי־אָ֑וֶן וּֽמְכַתְּבִ֥ים עָמָ֖ל כִּתֵּֽבוּ׃


Woe them that decree unrighteous decrees, and to the writers that prescribe oppression.


###### Isaiah 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:2)


לְהַטּ֤וֹת מִדִּין֙ דַּלִּ֔ים וְלִגְזֹ֕ל מִשְׁפַּ֖ט עֲנִיֵּ֣י עַמִּ֑י לִֽהְי֤וֹת אַלְמָנוֹת֙ שְׁלָלָ֔ם וְאֶת־יְתוֹמִ֖ים יָבֹֽזּוּ׃


To turn aside the needy from judgment, and to take away the right from the poor of my people, that widows may be their prey, and that they may rob the fatherless!


###### Isaiah 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:3)


וּמַֽה־תַּעֲשׂוּ֙ לְי֣וֹם פְּקֻדָּ֔ה וּלְשׁוֹאָ֖ה מִמֶּרְחָ֣ק תָּב֑וֹא עַל־מִי֙ תָּנ֣וּסוּ לְעֶזְרָ֔ה וְאָ֥נָה תַעַזְב֖וּ כְּבוֹדְכֶֽם׃


And what will you do in the day of visitation, and in the desolation which shall come from far? to whom will you flee for help? and where will you leave your glory?


###### Isaiah 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:4)


בִּלְתִּ֤י כָרַע֙ תַּ֣חַת אַסִּ֔יר וְתַ֥חַת הֲרוּגִ֖ים יִפֹּ֑לוּ בְּכׇל־זֹאת֙ לֹא־שָׁ֣ב אַפּ֔וֹ וְע֖וֹד יָד֥וֹ נְטוּיָֽה׃{פ}


There is nothing to be done except to crouch among the prisoners, and fall among the slain. For all this his anger is not turned away, but his hand is stretched out still.


###### Isaiah 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:5)


ה֥וֹי אַשּׁ֖וּר שֵׁ֣בֶט אַפִּ֑י וּמַטֶּה־ה֥וּא בְיָדָ֖ם זַעְמִֽי׃


Woe to Ashshur the rod of my anger; for the staff in their hand is my indignation.


###### Isaiah 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:6)


בְּג֤וֹי חָנֵף֙ אֲשַׁלְּחֶ֔נּוּ וְעַל־עַ֥ם עֶבְרָתִ֖י אֲצַוֶּ֑נּוּ לִשְׁלֹ֤ל שָׁלָל֙ וְלָבֹ֣ז בַּ֔ז (ולשימו) [וּלְשׂוּמ֥וֹ] מִרְמָ֖ס כְּחֹ֥מֶר חוּצֽוֹת׃


I will send him against a hypocritical nation, and against the people of my wrath will I give him a charge, to take the spoil, and to take the prey, and to tread them down like the mire of the streets.


###### Isaiah 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:7)


וְהוּא֙ לֹא־כֵ֣ן יְדַמֶּ֔ה וּלְבָב֖וֹ לֹא־כֵ֣ן יַחְשֹׁ֑ב כִּ֚י לְהַשְׁמִ֣יד בִּלְבָב֔וֹ וּלְהַכְרִ֥ית גּוֹיִ֖ם לֹ֥א מְעָֽט׃


Howbeit he means not so, neither does his heart think so; but it is in his heart to destroy and cut off nations not a few.


###### Isaiah 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:8)


כִּ֖י יֹאמַ֑ר הֲלֹ֥א שָׂרַ֛י יַחְדָּ֖ו מְלָכִֽים׃


For he says, Are not my princes all of them kings?


###### Isaiah 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:9)


הֲלֹ֥א כְּכַרְכְּמִ֖ישׁ כַּלְנ֑וֹ אִם־לֹ֤א כְאַרְפַּד֙ חֲמָ֔ת אִם־לֹ֥א כְדַמֶּ֖שֶׂק שֹׁמְרֽוֹן׃


Is not Kalno as Karkemish? is not Ḥamat as Arpad? is not Shomeron as Dammeseq?


###### Isaiah 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:10)


כַּֽאֲשֶׁר֙ מָצְאָ֣ה יָדִ֔י לְמַמְלְכֹ֖ת הָאֱלִ֑יל וּפְסִ֣ילֵיהֶ֔ם מִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וּמִשֹּׁמְרֽוֹן׃


As my hand has reached the kingdoms of the idols, whose carved idols did exceed those of Yerushalayim and of Shomeron,


###### Isaiah 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:11)


הֲלֹ֗א כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר עָשִׂ֛יתִי לְשֹׁמְר֖וֹן וְלֶאֱלִילֶ֑יהָ כֵּ֛ן אֶעֱשֶׂ֥ה לִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וְלַעֲצַבֶּֽיהָ׃{פ}


shall I not, as I have done to Shomeron and her idols, so do to Yerushalayim and her idols?


###### Isaiah 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:12)


וְהָיָ֗ה כִּֽי־יְבַצַּ֤ע אֲדֹנָי֙ אֶת־כׇּל־מַֽעֲשֵׂ֔הוּ בְּהַ֥ר צִיּ֖וֹן וּבִירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם אֶפְקֹ֗ד עַל־פְּרִֽי־גֹ֙דֶל֙ לְבַ֣ב מֶלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֔וּר וְעַל־תִּפְאֶ֖רֶת ר֥וּם עֵינָֽיו׃


Wherefore it shall come to pass, that when the Lord has performed his whole work upon mount Żiyyon and on Yerushalayim, I will punish the fruit of the proud heart of the king of Ashshur, and the glory of his high looks.


###### Isaiah 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:13)


כִּ֣י אָמַ֗ר בְּכֹ֤חַ יָדִי֙ עָשִׂ֔יתִי וּבְחׇכְמָתִ֖י כִּ֣י נְבֻנ֑וֹתִי וְאָסִ֣יר**׀** גְּבוּלֹ֣ת עַמִּ֗ים (ועתידתיהם) [וַעֲתֽוּדֹתֵיהֶם֙] שׁוֹשֵׂ֔תִי וְאוֹרִ֥יד כַּאבִּ֖יר יוֹשְׁבִֽים׃


For he says, By the strength of my hand I have done it, and by my wisdom; for I am prudent: and I have removed the bounds of the people, and have robbed their treasures, and I have put down the mighty number of their inhabitants.


###### Isaiah 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:14)


וַתִּמְצָ֨א כַקֵּ֤ן׀יָדִי֙ לְחֵ֣יל הָעַמִּ֔ים וְכֶֽאֱסֹף֙ בֵּיצִ֣ים עֲזֻב֔וֹת כׇּל־הָאָ֖רֶץ אֲנִ֣י אָסָ֑פְתִּי וְלֹ֤א הָיָה֙ נֹדֵ֣ד כָּנָ֔ף וּפֹצֶ֥ה פֶ֖ה וּמְצַפְצֵֽף׃


And my hand has found as a nest the riches of the people: and as one gathers eggs that are left, have I gathered all the earth; and there was none that moved the wing, or opened the mouth, or chirped.


###### Isaiah 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:15)


הֲיִתְפָּאֵר֙ הַגַּרְזֶ֔ן עַ֖ל הַחֹצֵ֣ב בּ֑וֹ אִם־יִתְגַּדֵּ֤ל הַמַּשּׂוֹר֙ עַל־מְנִיפ֔וֹ כְּהָנִ֥יף שֵׁ֙בֶט֙ וְאֶת־מְרִימָ֔יו כְּהָרִ֥ים מַטֶּ֖ה לֹא־עֵֽץ׃{פ}


Shall the ax boast itself against him that hews with it? or shall the saw magnify itself against him that wields it? as if the rod should shake them that lift it up, or as if the staff should lift up them that are no wood!


###### Isaiah 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:16)


לָ֠כֵן יְשַׁלַּ֨ח הָאָד֜וֹן יְהֹוָ֧ה צְבָא֛וֹת בְּמִשְׁמַנָּ֖יו רָז֑וֹן וְתַ֧חַת כְּבֹד֛וֹ יֵקַ֥ד יְקֹ֖ד כִּיק֥וֹד אֵֽשׁ׃


Therefore shall the Master, the Lord of hosts, send among his fat ones leanness; and under his glory shall be kindled a burning like the burning of a fire.


###### Isaiah 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:17)


וְהָיָ֤ה אֽוֹר־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ לְאֵ֔שׁ וּקְדוֹשׁ֖וֹ לְלֶהָבָ֑ה וּבָעֲרָ֗ה וְאָכְלָ֛ה שִׁית֥וֹ וּשְׁמִיר֖וֹ בְּי֥וֹם אֶחָֽד׃


And the light of Yisra᾽el shall be for a fire, and his Holy One for a flame: and it shall burn and devour his thorns and his briers in one day;


###### Isaiah 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:18)


וּכְב֤וֹד יַעְרוֹ֙ וְכַרְמִלּ֔וֹ מִנֶּ֥פֶשׁ וְעַד־בָּשָׂ֖ר יְכַלֶּ֑ה וְהָיָ֖ה כִּמְסֹ֥ס נֹסֵֽס׃


and shall consume the glory of his forest, and of his fruitful field, both soul and body: and they shall be as when a sick person wastes away.


###### Isaiah 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:19)


וּשְׁאָ֥ר עֵ֛ץ יַעְר֖וֹ מִסְפָּ֣ר יִהְי֑וּ וְנַ֖עַר יִכְתְּבֵֽם׃{ס}


And the rest of the trees of his forest shall be few, that a child may write them down.


###### Isaiah 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:20)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא לֹא־יוֹסִ֨יף ע֜וֹד שְׁאָ֤ר יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ וּפְלֵיטַ֣ת בֵּֽית־יַעֲקֹ֔ב לְהִשָּׁעֵ֖ן עַל־מַכֵּ֑הוּ וְנִשְׁעַ֗ן עַל־יְהֹוָ֛ה קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בֶּאֱמֶֽת׃


And it shall come to pass on that day, that the remnant of Yisra᾽el, and such as are escaped of the house of Ya῾aqov, shall no more again rely upon him that smote them; but shall rely upon the Lord, the Holy One of Yisra᾽el in truth.


###### Isaiah 10:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:21)


שְׁאָ֥ר יָשׁ֖וּב שְׁאָ֣ר יַעֲקֹ֑ב אֶל־אֵ֖ל גִּבּֽוֹר׃


A remnant shall return, even the remnant of Ya῾aqov, to the mighty God.


###### Isaiah 10:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:22)


כִּ֣י אִם־יִהְיֶ֞ה עַמְּךָ֤ יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ כְּח֣וֹל הַיָּ֔ם שְׁאָ֖ר יָשׁ֣וּב בּ֑וֹ כִּלָּי֥וֹן חָר֖וּץ שׁוֹטֵ֥ף צְדָקָֽה׃


For though Thy people Yisra᾽el be as the sand of the sea, yet a remnant of them shall return: total destruction is decreed but overflowing with righteousness.


###### Isaiah 10:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:23)


כִּ֥י כָלָ֖ה וְנֶחֱרָצָ֑ה אֲדֹנָ֤י יֱהֹוִה֙ צְבָא֔וֹת עֹשֶׂ֖ה בְּקֶ֥רֶב כׇּל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


For it is determined and decreed; the Lord God of hosts shall do it.


###### Isaiah 10:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:24)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּֽה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנָ֤י יֱהֹוִה֙ צְבָא֔וֹת אַל־תִּירָ֥א עַמִּ֛י יֹשֵׁ֥ב צִיּ֖וֹן מֵאַשּׁ֑וּר בַּשֵּׁ֣בֶט יַכֶּ֔כָּה וּמַטֵּ֥הוּ יִשָּֽׂא־עָלֶ֖יךָ בְּדֶ֥רֶךְ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God of hosts, O my people that dwellest in Żiyyon, be not afraid of Ashshur: he shall smite thee with a rod, and shall lift up his staff against thee, after the manner of Miżrayim.


###### Isaiah 10:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:25)


כִּי־ע֖וֹד מְעַ֣ט מִזְעָ֑ר וְכָ֣לָה זַ֔עַם וְאַפִּ֖י עַל־תַּבְלִיתָֽם׃


But yet a very little while, and the indignation shall cease, and my anger shall be turned to their destruction.


###### Isaiah 10:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:26)


וְעוֹרֵ֨ר עָלָ֜יו יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ שׁ֔וֹט כְּמַכַּ֥ת מִדְיָ֖ן בְּצ֣וּר עוֹרֵ֑ב וּמַטֵּ֙הוּ֙ עַל־הַיָּ֔ם וּנְשָׂא֖וֹ בְּדֶ֥רֶךְ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


And the Lord of hosts shall stir up a whip for him according to the slaughter of Midyan at the rock of ῾Orev: and as his rod was upon the sea, so shall he lift it up after the manner of Miżrayim.


###### Isaiah 10:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:27)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא יָס֤וּר סֻבֳּלוֹ֙ מֵעַ֣ל שִׁכְמֶ֔ךָ וְעֻלּ֖וֹ מֵעַ֣ל צַוָּארֶ֑ךָ וְחֻבַּ֥ל עֹ֖ל מִפְּנֵי־שָֽׁמֶן׃


And it shall come to pass on that day, that his burden shall be taken away from off thy shoulder, and his yoke from off thy neck, and the yoke shall be destroyed because of the fatness.


###### Isaiah 10:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:28)


בָּ֥א עַל־עַיַּ֖ת עָבַ֣ר בְּמִגְר֑וֹן לְמִכְמָ֖שׂ יַפְקִ֥יד כֵּלָֽיו׃


He is come to ῾Ayyat, he is passed to Migron; at Mikhmash he has left his baggage:


###### Isaiah 10:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:29)


עָֽבְרוּ֙ מַעְבָּרָ֔ה גֶּ֖בַע מָל֣וֹן לָ֑נוּ חָֽרְדָה֙ הָרָמָ֔ה גִּבְעַ֥ת שָׁא֖וּל נָֽסָה׃


they are gone over the pass: they have taken up their lodging at Geva; Rama is afraid; Giv῾at-Sha᾽ul is fled.


###### Isaiah 10:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:30)


צַהֲלִ֥י קוֹלֵ֖ךְ בַּת־גַּלִּ֑ים הַקְשִׁ֥יבִי לַ֖יְשָׁה עֲנִיָּ֥ה עֲנָתֽוֹת׃


Lift up thy voice, O Bat-gallim: hearken Laysha, O poor ῾Anatot.


###### Isaiah 10:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:31)


נָֽדְדָ֖ה מַדְמֵנָ֑ה יֹשְׁבֵ֥י הַגֵּבִ֖ים הֵעִֽיזוּ׃


Madmena is removed; the inhabitants of Gevim flee for safety.


###### Isaiah 10:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:32)


ע֥וֹד הַיּ֖וֹם בְּנֹ֣ב לַעֲמֹ֑ד יְנֹפֵ֤ף יָדוֹ֙ הַ֣ר (בית) [בַּת־]צִיּ֔וֹן גִּבְעַ֖ת יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{פ}


This very day he will halt at Nov: he will shake his hands against the mountain of the daughter of Żiyyon, the hill of Yerushalayim.


###### Isaiah 10:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:33)


הִנֵּ֤ה הָאָדוֹן֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת מְסָעֵ֥ף פֻּארָ֖ה בְּמַעֲרָצָ֑ה וְרָמֵ֤י הַקּוֹמָה֙ גְּדוּעִ֔ים וְהַגְּבֹהִ֖ים יִשְׁפָּֽלוּ׃


Behold, the Master, the Lord of hosts, shall lop the bough with terror: and the high ones of stature shall be hewn down, and the haughty shall be humbled.


###### Isaiah 10:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/10:34)


וְנִקַּ֛ף סִֽבְכֵ֥י הַיַּ֖עַר בַּבַּרְזֶ֑ל וְהַלְּבָנ֖וֹן בְּאַדִּ֥יר יִפּֽוֹל׃{ס}


And he shall cut down the thickets of the forest with iron, and Levanon shall fall by a mighty one.



## Chapter 11

###### Isaiah 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:1)


וְיָצָ֥א חֹ֖טֶר מִגֵּ֣זַע יִשָׁ֑י וְנֵ֖צֶר מִשׇּׁרָשָׁ֥יו יִפְרֶֽה׃


And there shall come forth a rod out of the stem of Yishay, and a branch shall grow out of his roots:


###### Isaiah 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:2)


וְנָחָ֥ה עָלָ֖יו ר֣וּחַ יְהֹוָ֑ה ר֧וּחַ חׇכְמָ֣ה וּבִינָ֗ה ר֤וּחַ עֵצָה֙ וּגְבוּרָ֔ה ר֥וּחַ דַּ֖עַת וְיִרְאַ֥ת יְהֹוָֽה׃


and the spirit of the Lord shall rest upon him, the spirit of wisdom and understanding, the spirit of counsel and might, the spirit of knowledge and of the fear of the Lord;


###### Isaiah 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:3)


וַהֲרִיח֖וֹ בְּיִרְאַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֑ה וְלֹֽא־לְמַרְאֵ֤ה עֵינָיו֙ יִשְׁפּ֔וֹט וְלֹא־לְמִשְׁמַ֥ע אׇזְנָ֖יו יוֹכִֽיחַ׃


and his delight shall be in the fear of the Lord: and he shall not judge after the sight of his eyes, neither decide after the hearing of his ears:


###### Isaiah 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:4)


וְשָׁפַ֤ט בְּצֶ֙דֶק֙ דַּלִּ֔ים וְהוֹכִ֥יחַ בְּמִישׁ֖וֹר לְעַנְוֵי־אָ֑רֶץ וְהִֽכָּה־אֶ֙רֶץ֙ בְּשֵׁ֣בֶט פִּ֔יו וּבְר֥וּחַ שְׂפָתָ֖יו יָמִ֥ית רָשָֽׁע׃


but with righteousness shall he judge the poor, and decide with equity for the meek of the earth: and he shall smite the earth with the rod of his mouth, and with the breath of his lips shall he slay the wicked.


###### Isaiah 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:5)


וְהָ֥יָה צֶ֖דֶק אֵז֣וֹר מׇתְנָ֑יו וְהָאֱמוּנָ֖ה אֵז֥וֹר חֲלָצָֽיו׃


And righteousness shall be the girdle of his loins, and faithfulness the girdle of his reins.


###### Isaiah 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:6)


וְגָ֤ר זְאֵב֙ עִם־כֶּ֔בֶשׂ וְנָמֵ֖ר עִם־גְּדִ֣י יִרְבָּ֑ץ וְעֵ֨גֶל וּכְפִ֤יר וּמְרִיא֙ יַחְדָּ֔ו וְנַ֥עַר קָטֹ֖ן נֹהֵ֥ג בָּֽם׃


The wolf also shall dwell with the lamb, and the leopard shall lie down with the kid; and the calf and the young lion and the fatling together; and a little child shall lead them.


###### Isaiah 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:7)


וּפָרָ֤ה וָדֹב֙ תִּרְעֶ֔ינָה יַחְדָּ֖ו יִרְבְּצ֣וּ יַלְדֵיהֶ֑ן וְאַרְיֵ֖ה כַּבָּקָ֥ר יֹאכַל־תֶּֽבֶן׃


And the cow and the bear shall feed; their young ones shall lie down together: and the lion shall eat straw like the ox.


###### Isaiah 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:8)


וְשִֽׁעֲשַׁ֥ע יוֹנֵ֖ק עַל־חֻ֣ר פָּ֑תֶן וְעַל֙ מְאוּרַ֣ת צִפְעוֹנִ֔י גָּמ֖וּל יָד֥וֹ הָדָֽה׃


And the sucking child play on the hole of the cobra, and the weaned child shall put his hand on the viper’s nest.


###### Isaiah 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:9)


לֹא־יָרֵ֥עוּ וְלֹֽא־יַשְׁחִ֖יתוּ בְּכׇל־הַ֣ר קׇדְשִׁ֑י כִּֽי־מָלְאָ֣ה הָאָ֗רֶץ דֵּעָה֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה כַּמַּ֖יִם לַיָּ֥ם מְכַסִּֽים׃{ס}


They shall not hurt nor destroy in all my holy mountain: for the earth shall be full of the knowledge of the Lord, as the waters cover the sea.


###### Isaiah 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:10)


וְהָיָה֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא שֹׁ֣רֶשׁ יִשַׁ֗י אֲשֶׁ֤ר עֹמֵד֙ לְנֵ֣ס עַמִּ֔ים אֵלָ֖יו גּוֹיִ֣ם יִדְרֹ֑שׁוּ וְהָיְתָ֥ה מְנֻחָת֖וֹ כָּבֽוֹד׃{פ}


And in that day it shall be, that the root of Yishay, that stands for a banner of the peoples, to it shall the nations seek: and his resting place shall be glorious.


###### Isaiah 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:11)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא יוֹסִ֨יף אֲדֹנָ֤י׀שֵׁנִית֙ יָד֔וֹ לִקְנ֖וֹת אֶת־שְׁאָ֣ר עַמּ֑וֹ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יִשָּׁאֵר֩ מֵאַשּׁ֨וּר וּמִמִּצְרַ֜יִם וּמִפַּתְר֣וֹס וּמִכּ֗וּשׁ וּמֵעֵילָ֤ם וּמִשִּׁנְעָר֙ וּמֵ֣חֲמָ֔ת וּמֵאִיֵּ֖י הַיָּֽם׃


And it shall come to pass in that day, that the Lord shall set his hand again the second time to recover the remnant of his people, that shall be left, from Ashshur, and from Miżrayim, from Patros, and from Kush, and from ῾Elam, and from Shin῾ar, and from Ḥamat, and from the islands of the sea.


###### Isaiah 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:12)


וְנָשָׂ֥א נֵס֙ לַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְאָסַ֖ף נִדְחֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וּנְפֻצ֤וֹת יְהוּדָה֙ יְקַבֵּ֔ץ מֵאַרְבַּ֖ע כַּנְפ֥וֹת הָאָֽרֶץ׃


And he shall set up a banner for the nations, and shall assemble the outcasts of Yisra᾽el, and gather together the dispersed of Yehuda from the four corners of the earth.


###### Isaiah 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:13)


וְסָ֙רָה֙ קִנְאַ֣ת אֶפְרַ֔יִם וְצֹרְרֵ֥י יְהוּדָ֖ה יִכָּרֵ֑תוּ אֶפְרַ֙יִם֙ לֹא־יְקַנֵּ֣א אֶת־יְהוּדָ֔ה וִיהוּדָ֖ה לֹא־יָצֹ֥ר אֶת־אֶפְרָֽיִם׃


The envy also of Efrayim shall depart, and the adversaries of Yehuda shall be cut off: Efrayim shall not envy Yehuda, and Yehuda shall not vex Efrayim.


###### Isaiah 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:14)


וְעָפ֨וּ בְכָתֵ֤ף פְּלִשְׁתִּים֙ יָ֔מָּה יַחְדָּ֖ו יָבֹ֣זּוּ אֶת־בְּנֵי־קֶ֑דֶם אֱד֤וֹם וּמוֹאָב֙ מִשְׁל֣וֹחַ יָדָ֔ם וּבְנֵ֥י עַמּ֖וֹן מִשְׁמַעְתָּֽם׃


But they shall fly upon the shoulders of the Pelishtim toward the sea; they shall spoil the children of the east together: they shall lay their hand upon Edom and Mo᾽av; and the children of ῾Ammon shall obey them.


###### Isaiah 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:15)


וְהֶחֱרִ֣ים יְהֹוָ֗ה אֵ֚ת לְשׁ֣וֹן יָם־מִצְרַ֔יִם וְהֵנִ֥יף יָד֛וֹ עַל־הַנָּהָ֖ר בַּעְיָ֣ם רוּח֑וֹ וְהִכָּ֙הוּ֙ לְשִׁבְעָ֣ה נְחָלִ֔ים וְהִדְרִ֖יךְ בַּנְּעָלִֽים׃


And the Lord shall utterly destroy the tongue of the sea of Miżrayim; and with his scorching wind he shall shake his hand over the river, and shall smite it in seven streams, and make men go over dryshod.


###### Isaiah 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/11:16)


וְהָיְתָ֣ה מְסִלָּ֔ה לִשְׁאָ֣ר עַמּ֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִשָּׁאֵ֖ר מֵאַשּׁ֑וּר כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר הָֽיְתָה֙ לְיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל בְּי֥וֹם עֲלֹת֖וֹ מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מִצְרָֽיִם׃


And there shall be a highway for the remnant of his people, which shall be left, from Ashshur; as there was for Yisra᾽el in the day that he came up out of the land of Miżrayim.



## Chapter 12

###### Isaiah 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:1)


וְאָֽמַרְתָּ֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא אוֹדְךָ֣ יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֥י אָנַ֖פְתָּ בִּ֑י יָשֹׁ֥ב אַפְּךָ֖ וּֽתְנַחֲמֵֽנִי׃


And in that day thou shalt say, O Lord, I will praise Thee: though wast angry with me, Thy anger is turned away, and Thou dost comfort me.


###### Isaiah 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:2)


הִנֵּ֨ה אֵ֧ל יְשׁוּעָתִ֛י אֶבְטַ֖ח וְלֹ֣א אֶפְחָ֑ד כִּֽי־עׇזִּ֤י וְזִמְרָת֙ יָ֣הּ יְהֹוָ֔ה וַֽיְהִי־לִ֖י לִישׁוּעָֽה׃


Behold, God is my salvation; I will trust, and not be afraid: for the Lord God is my strength and my song; he also is become my salvation.


###### Isaiah 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:3)


וּשְׁאַבְתֶּם־מַ֖יִם בְּשָׂשׂ֑וֹן מִמַּעַיְנֵ֖י הַיְשׁוּעָֽה׃


Therefore with joy shall you draw water out of the wells of salvation.


###### Isaiah 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:4)


וַאֲמַרְתֶּ֞ם בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא הוֹד֤וּ לַֽיהֹוָה֙ קִרְא֣וּ בִשְׁמ֔וֹ הוֹדִ֥יעוּ בָעַמִּ֖ים עֲלִילֹתָ֑יו הַזְכִּ֕ירוּ כִּ֥י נִשְׂגָּ֖ב שְׁמֽוֹ׃


And in that day shall you say, Praise the Lord, call upon his name, declare his doings among the people, make mention that his name is exalted.


###### Isaiah 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:5)


זַמְּר֣וּ יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֥י גֵא֖וּת עָשָׂ֑ה (מידעת) [מוּדַ֥עַת] זֹ֖את בְּכׇל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Sing to the Lord; for he has done excellent things: this is known in all the earth.


###### Isaiah 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/12:6)


צַהֲלִ֥י וָרֹ֖נִּי יוֹשֶׁ֣בֶת צִיּ֑וֹן כִּי־גָד֥וֹל בְּקִרְבֵּ֖ךְ קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


Cry out and shout, thou inhabitant of Żiyyon: for great is the Holy One of Yisra᾽el in the midst of thee.

Lift up a banner upon the high mountain, exalt the voice unto them, wave the hand, that they may go into the gates of the nobles.

I have commanded my sanctified ones, I have also called my warriors for my anger, my proudly exalting ones.


###### Isaiah 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:4)


ק֥וֹל הָמ֛וֹן בֶּהָרִ֖ים דְּמ֣וּת עַם־רָ֑ב ק֠וֹל שְׁא֞וֹן מַמְלְכ֤וֹת גּוֹיִם֙ נֶאֱסָפִ֔ים יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת מְפַקֵּ֖ד צְבָ֥א מִלְחָמָֽה׃


The noise of a multitude in the mountains, like as of a great people; a tumultuous noise of the kingdoms of nations gathered together: the Lord of hosts musters the host of the battle.


###### Isaiah 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:5)


בָּאִ֛ים מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מֶרְחָ֖ק מִקְצֵ֣ה הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם יְהֹוָה֙ וּכְלֵ֣י זַעְמ֔וֹ לְחַבֵּ֖ל כׇּל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃{ס}


They come from a far country, from the end of heaven, the Lord, and the weapons of his indignation, to destroy the whole land.


###### Isaiah 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:6)


הֵילִ֕ילוּ כִּ֥י קָר֖וֹב י֣וֹם יְהֹוָ֑ה כְּשֹׁ֖ד מִשַּׁדַּ֥י יָבֽוֹא׃


Howl; for the day of the Lord is at hand; it shall come as a destruction from the Almighty.


###### Isaiah 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:7)


עַל־כֵּ֖ן כׇּל־יָדַ֣יִם תִּרְפֶּ֑ינָה וְכׇל־לְבַ֥ב אֱנ֖וֹשׁ יִמָּֽס׃


Therefore shall all hands be slack, and every man’s heart shall melt:


###### Isaiah 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:8)


וְֽנִבְהָ֓לוּ**׀** צִירִ֤ים וַחֲבָלִים֙ יֹֽאחֵז֔וּן כַּיּוֹלֵדָ֖ה יְחִיל֑וּן אִ֤ישׁ אֶל־רֵעֵ֙הוּ֙ יִתְמָ֔הוּ פְּנֵ֥י לְהָבִ֖ים פְּנֵיהֶֽם׃


and they shall be afraid: pangs and sorrows shall take hold of them; they shall be in pain as a woman in travail: they shall be amazed at one another; their faces shall be as flames.


###### Isaiah 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:9)


הִנֵּ֤ה יוֹם־יְהֹוָה֙ בָּ֔א אַכְזָרִ֥י וְעֶבְרָ֖ה וַחֲר֣וֹן אָ֑ף לָשׂ֤וּם הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ לְשַׁמָּ֔ה וְחַטָּאֶ֖יהָ יַשְׁמִ֥יד מִמֶּֽנָּה׃


Behold, the day of the Lord comes, a day of cruelty, wrath and fierce anger, to lay the land desolate: and he shall destroy its sinners from out of it.


###### Isaiah 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:10)


כִּֽי־כוֹכְבֵ֤י הַשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ וּכְסִ֣ילֵיהֶ֔ם לֹ֥א יָהֵ֖לּוּ אוֹרָ֑ם חָשַׁ֤ךְ הַשֶּׁ֙מֶשׁ֙ בְּצֵאת֔וֹ וְיָרֵ֖חַ לֹא־יַגִּ֥יהַּ אוֹרֽוֹ׃


For the stars of heaven and its constellations shall not give their light: the sun shall be darkened in his going forth, and the moon shall not shed her light.


###### Isaiah 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:11)


וּפָקַדְתִּ֤י עַל־תֵּבֵל֙ רָעָ֔ה וְעַל־רְשָׁעִ֖ים עֲוֺנָ֑ם וְהִשְׁבַּתִּי֙ גְּא֣וֹן זֵדִ֔ים וְגַאֲוַ֥ת עָרִיצִ֖ים אַשְׁפִּֽיל׃

###### Isaiah 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:12)

I will make men more rare than fine gold; and mankind, than the pure gold of Ofir.


###### Isaiah 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:13)


עַל־כֵּן֙ שָׁמַ֣יִם אַרְגִּ֔יז וְתִרְעַ֥שׁ הָאָ֖רֶץ מִמְּקוֹמָ֑הּ בְּעֶבְרַת֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת וּבְי֖וֹם חֲר֥וֹן אַפּֽוֹ׃


Therefore I will shake the heavens, and the earth shall remove out of her place, by the wrath of the Lord of host, and on the day of his fierce anger.


###### Isaiah 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:14)


וְהָיָה֙ כִּצְבִ֣י מֻדָּ֔ח וּכְצֹ֖אן וְאֵ֣ין מְקַבֵּ֑ץ אִ֤ישׁ אֶל־עַמּוֹ֙ יִפְנ֔וּ וְאִ֥ישׁ אֶל־אַרְצ֖וֹ יָנֽוּסוּ׃


And it shall be like the outcast gazelle, and the sheep that no man takes up; they shall every man turn to his own people, and flee every one into his own land.


###### Isaiah 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:15)

Every one that is found shall be thrust through; and every one that is caught shall fall by the sword.


###### Isaiah 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:16)


וְעֹלְלֵיהֶ֥ם יְרֻטְּשׁ֖וּ לְעֵינֵיהֶ֑ם יִשַּׁ֙סּוּ֙ בָּתֵּיהֶ֔ם וּנְשֵׁיהֶ֖ם (תשגלנה) [תִּשָּׁכַֽבְנָה]׃


Their children also shall be dashed to pieces before their eyes; their houses shall be spoiled, and their wives ravished.


###### Isaiah 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:17)


הִנְנִ֛י מֵעִ֥יר עֲלֵיהֶ֖ם אֶת־מָדָ֑י אֲשֶׁר־כֶּ֙סֶף֙ לֹ֣א יַחְשֹׁ֔בוּ וְזָהָ֖ב לֹ֥א יַחְפְּצוּ־בֽוֹ׃


Behold, I will stir up the Maday against them, who shall not regard silver; and as for gold, they shall not delight in it.


###### Isaiah 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:18)


וּקְשָׁת֖וֹת נְעָרִ֣ים תְּרַטַּ֑שְׁנָה וּפְרִי־בֶ֙טֶן֙ לֹ֣א יְרַחֵ֔מוּ עַל־בָּנִ֖ים לֹא־תָח֥וּס עֵינָֽם׃


Their bows also shall dash the young men to pieces; and they shall have no pity on the fruit of the womb; their eye shall not spare children.


###### Isaiah 13:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:19)


וְהָיְתָ֤ה בָבֶל֙ צְבִ֣י מַמְלָכ֔וֹת תִּפְאֶ֖רֶת גְּא֣וֹן כַּשְׂדִּ֑ים כְּמַהְפֵּכַ֣ת אֱלֹהִ֔ים אֶת־סְדֹ֖ם וְאֶת־עֲמֹרָֽה׃


And Bavel, the glory of kingdoms, the beauty of the pride of the Kasdim, shall be as when God overthrew Sedom and ῾Amora.


###### Isaiah 13:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:20)


לֹא־תֵשֵׁ֣ב לָנֶ֔צַח וְלֹ֥א תִשְׁכֹּ֖ן עַד־דּ֣וֹר וָד֑וֹר וְלֹא־יַהֵ֥ל שָׁם֙ עֲרָבִ֔י וְרֹעִ֖ים לֹא־יַרְבִּ֥צוּ שָֽׁם׃


It shall never be inhabited, neither shall it be dwelt in from generation to generation: neither shall the ῾Aravi pitch tent there; neither shall the shepherds make their flock lie down there.


###### Isaiah 13:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:21)


וְרָבְצוּ־שָׁ֣ם צִיִּ֔ים וּמָלְא֥וּ בָתֵּיהֶ֖ם אֹחִ֑ים וְשָׁ֤כְנוּ שָׁם֙ בְּנ֣וֹת יַעֲנָ֔ה וּשְׂעִירִ֖ים יְרַקְּדוּ־שָֽׁם׃


But wild beasts of the desert shall lie there; and their houses shall be full of owls; and ostriches shall dwell there, and the scops owl shall hop about there.


###### Isaiah 13:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/13:22)


וְעָנָ֤ה אִיִּים֙ בְּאַלְמְנוֹתָ֔יו וְתַנִּ֖ים בְּהֵ֣יכְלֵי עֹ֑נֶג וְקָר֤וֹב לָבוֹא֙ עִתָּ֔הּ וְיָמֶ֖יהָ לֹ֥א יִמָּשֵֽׁכוּ׃


And jackals shall cry in their castles, and wild dogs in their pleasant palaces: and her time is near at hand, and her days shall not be prolonged.



## Chapter 14

###### Isaiah 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:1)


כִּי֩ יְרַחֵ֨ם יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶֽת־יַעֲקֹ֗ב וּבָחַ֥ר עוֹד֙ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְהִנִּיחָ֖ם עַל־אַדְמָתָ֑ם וְנִלְוָ֤ה הַגֵּר֙ עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם וְנִסְפְּח֖וּ עַל־בֵּ֥ית יַעֲקֹֽב׃


For the Lord will have mercy on Ya῾aqov, and will yet choose Yisra᾽el, and set them in their own land: and the stranger shall be joined with them, and they shall cleave to the house of Ya῾aqov.


###### Isaiah 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:2)


וּלְקָח֣וּם עַמִּים֮ וֶהֱבִיא֣וּם אֶל־מְקוֹמָם֒ וְהִֽתְנַחֲל֣וּם בֵּֽית־יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל עַ֚ל אַדְמַ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה לַעֲבָדִ֖ים וְלִשְׁפָח֑וֹת וְהָיוּ֙ שֹׁבִ֣ים לְשֹׁבֵיהֶ֔ם וְרָד֖וּ בְּנֹגְשֵׂיהֶֽם׃{ס}


And the people shall take them, and bring them to their place: and the house of Yisra᾽el shall possess them in the land of the Lord for servants and handmaids: and they shall take them captives, whose captives they were; and they shall rule over their oppressors.


###### Isaiah 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:3)


וְהָיָ֗ה בְּי֨וֹם הָנִ֤יחַ יְהֹוָה֙ לְךָ֔ מֵֽעׇצְבְּךָ֖ וּמִֽרׇגְזֶ֑ךָ וּמִן־הָעֲבֹדָ֥ה הַקָּשָׁ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר עֻבַּד־בָּֽךְ׃


And it shall come to pass on the day that the Lord shall give thee rest from thy sorrow, and from thy fear, and from the hard bondage in which thou wast made to serve,


###### Isaiah 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:4)


וְנָשָׂ֜אתָ הַמָּשָׁ֥ל הַזֶּ֛ה עַל־מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶ֖ל וְאָמָ֑רְתָּ אֵ֚יךְ שָׁבַ֣ת נֹגֵ֔שׂ שָׁבְתָ֖ה מַדְהֵבָֽה׃


that thou shalt take up this proverb against the king of Bavel, and say, How has the oppressor ceased! the golden city ceased!


###### Isaiah 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:5)


שָׁבַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה מַטֵּ֣ה רְשָׁעִ֑ים שֵׁ֖בֶט מֹשְׁלִֽים׃


The Lord has broken the staff of the wicked, the sceptre of the rulers,


###### Isaiah 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:6)


מַכֶּ֤ה עַמִּים֙ בְּעֶבְרָ֔ה מַכַּ֖ת בִּלְתִּ֣י סָרָ֑ה רֹדֶ֤ה בָאַף֙ גּוֹיִ֔ם מֻרְדָּ֖ף בְּלִ֥י חָשָֽׂךְ׃


that smote the people in wrath with a continual stroke, that ruled the nations in anger with unrelenting persecution.


###### Isaiah 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:7)


נָ֥חָה שָׁקְטָ֖ה כׇּל־הָאָ֑רֶץ פָּצְח֖וּ רִנָּֽה׃


The whole earth is at rest, and is quiet: they break forth into singing.


###### Isaiah 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:8)


גַּם־בְּרוֹשִׁ֛ים שָׂמְח֥וּ לְךָ֖ אַרְזֵ֣י לְבָנ֑וֹן מֵאָ֣ז שָׁכַ֔בְתָּ לֹא־יַעֲלֶ֥ה הַכֹּרֵ֖ת עָלֵֽינוּ׃


Yea, the cypresses rejoice at thee, and the cedars of Levanon, saying, Since thou art laid down, no feller is come up against us.


###### Isaiah 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:9)


שְׁא֗וֹל מִתַּ֛חַת רָגְזָ֥ה לְךָ֖ לִקְרַ֣את בּוֹאֶ֑ךָ עוֹרֵ֨ר לְךָ֤ רְפָאִים֙ כׇּל־עַתּ֣וּדֵי אָ֔רֶץ הֵקִים֙ מִכִּסְאוֹתָ֔ם כֹּ֖ל מַלְכֵ֥י גוֹיִֽם׃


She᾽ol from beneath is moved for thee to meet thee at thy coming: it stirs up the shades for thee, all the chief ones of the earth; it has raised up from their thrones all the kings of the nations.


###### Isaiah 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:10)


כֻּלָּ֣ם יַעֲנ֔וּ וְיֹאמְר֖וּ אֵלֶ֑יךָ גַּם־אַתָּ֛ה חֻלֵּ֥יתָ כָמ֖וֹנוּ אֵלֵ֥ינוּ נִמְשָֽׁלְתָּ׃


They all shall speak and say to thee, Art thou also become weak as we? art thou become like us?


###### Isaiah 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:11)


הוּרַ֥ד שְׁא֛וֹל גְּאוֹנֶ֖ךָ הֶמְיַ֣ת נְבָלֶ֑יךָ תַּחְתֶּ֙יךָ֙ יֻצַּ֣ע רִמָּ֔ה וּמְכַסֶּ֖יךָ תּוֹלֵעָֽה׃


Thy pomp is brought down to She᾽ol, and the noise of thy lutes: worms are spread under thee, and worms cover thee.


###### Isaiah 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:12)


אֵ֛יךְ נָפַ֥לְתָּ מִשָּׁמַ֖יִם הֵילֵ֣ל בֶּן־שָׁ֑חַר נִגְדַּ֣עְתָּ לָאָ֔רֶץ חוֹלֵ֖שׁ עַל־גּוֹיִֽם׃


How art thou fallen from heaven, O bright star, son of the morning! how art thou cut down to the ground, that didst rule over the nations!


###### Isaiah 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:13)


וְאַתָּ֞ה אָמַ֤רְתָּ בִֽלְבָבְךָ֙ הַשָּׁמַ֣יִם אֶעֱלֶ֔ה מִמַּ֥עַל לְכוֹכְבֵי־אֵ֖ל אָרִ֣ים כִּסְאִ֑י וְאֵשֵׁ֥ב בְּהַר־מוֹעֵ֖ד בְּיַרְכְּתֵ֥י צָפֽוֹן׃


For thou hast said in thy heart, I will ascend into heaven, I will exalt my throne above the stars of God: I will sit also upon the mount of the congregation, in the farthest north:


###### Isaiah 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:14)


אֶעֱלֶ֖ה עַל־בָּ֣מֳתֵי עָ֑ב אֶדַּמֶּ֖ה לְעֶלְיֽוֹן׃


I will ascend above the heights of the clouds; I will be like the most High.


###### Isaiah 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:15)


אַ֧ךְ אֶל־שְׁא֛וֹל תּוּרָ֖ד אֶל־יַרְכְּתֵי־בֽוֹר׃


Yet thou shalt be brought down to She᾽ol, to the depths of the pit.


###### Isaiah 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:16)


רֹאֶ֙יךָ֙ אֵלֶ֣יךָ יַשְׁגִּ֔יחוּ אֵלֶ֖יךָ יִתְבּוֹנָ֑נוּ הֲזֶ֤ה הָאִישׁ֙ מַרְגִּ֣יז הָאָ֔רֶץ מַרְעִ֖ישׁ מַמְלָכֽוֹת׃


They that see thee shall narrowly look upon thee, and consider thee, saying, Is this the man that made the earth to tremble, that did shake kingdoms;


###### Isaiah 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:17)


שָׂ֥ם תֵּבֵ֛ל כַּמִּדְבָּ֖ר וְעָרָ֣יו הָרָ֑ס אֲסִירָ֖יו לֹא־פָ֥תַח בָּֽיְתָה׃


that made the world as a wilderness, and destroyed its cities; that opened not the house of his prisoners?


###### Isaiah 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:18)


כׇּל־מַלְכֵ֥י גוֹיִ֖ם כֻּלָּ֑ם שָׁכְב֥וּ בְכָב֖וֹד אִ֥ישׁ בְּבֵיתֽוֹ׃


All the kings of the nations, even all of them, lie in glory, every one in his own house.


###### Isaiah 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:19)


וְאַתָּ֞ה הׇשְׁלַ֤כְתָּ מִֽקִּבְרְךָ֙ כְּנֵ֣צֶר נִתְעָ֔ב לְבֻ֥שׁ הֲרֻגִ֖ים מְטֹ֣עֲנֵי חָ֑רֶב יוֹרְדֵ֥י אֶל־אַבְנֵי־ב֖וֹר כְּפֶ֥גֶר מוּבָֽס׃


But thou art cast out of thy grave like an abhorred branch, dressed in the garment of the slain, thrust through with a sword; like them that go down to the stones of the pit as a carcass trodden under feet.


###### Isaiah 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:20)


לֹֽא־תֵחַ֤ד אִתָּם֙ בִּקְבוּרָ֔ה כִּי־אַרְצְךָ֥ שִׁחַ֖תָּ עַמְּךָ֣ הָרָ֑גְתָּ לֹא־יִקָּרֵ֥א לְעוֹלָ֖ם זֶ֥רַע מְרֵעִֽים׃


Thou shalt not be joined with them in burial, because thou hast destroyed thy land, and slain thy people: may the seed of evildoers nevermore be named.


###### Isaiah 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:21)


הָכִ֧ינוּ לְבָנָ֛יו מַטְבֵּ֖חַ בַּעֲוֺ֣ן אֲבוֹתָ֑ם בַּל־יָקֻ֙מוּ֙ וְיָ֣רְשׁוּ אָ֔רֶץ וּמָלְא֥וּ פְנֵי־תֵבֵ֖ל עָרִֽים׃


Prepare slaughter for his children for the iniquity of their fathers; that they do not rise, nor possess the land; for the face of the world would be filled with enemies.


###### Isaiah 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:22)


וְקַמְתִּ֣י עֲלֵיהֶ֔ם נְאֻ֖ם יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֑וֹת וְהִכְרַתִּ֨י לְבָבֶ֜ל שֵׁ֥ם וּשְׁאָ֛ר וְנִ֥ין וָנֶ֖כֶד נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


For I will rise up against them, says the Lord of hosts, and cut off from Bavel name, and remnant, and offspring and posterity, says the Lord.


###### Isaiah 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:23)


וְשַׂמְתִּ֛יהָ לְמוֹרַ֥שׁ קִפֹּ֖ד וְאַגְמֵי־מָ֑יִם וְטֵאטֵאתִ֙יהָ֙ בְּמַטְאֲטֵ֣א הַשְׁמֵ֔ד נְאֻ֖ם יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָאֽוֹת׃{ס}


I will also make it a possession for wild birds, and pools of water: and I will sweep it with the broom of destruction, says the Lord of hosts.


###### Isaiah 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:24)


נִשְׁבַּ֛ע יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת לֵאמֹ֑ר אִם־לֹ֞א כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר דִּמִּ֙יתִי֙ כֵּ֣ן הָיָ֔תָה וְכַאֲשֶׁ֥ר יָעַ֖צְתִּי הִ֥יא תָקֽוּם׃


The Lord of hosts has sworn, saying, Surely as I have thought, so has it come to pass; and as I have purposed, so shall it stand:


###### Isaiah 14:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:25)


לִשְׁבֹּ֤ר אַשּׁוּר֙ בְּאַרְצִ֔י וְעַל־הָרַ֖י אֲבוּסֶ֑נּוּ וְסָ֤ר מֵֽעֲלֵיהֶם֙ עֻלּ֔וֹ וְסֻ֨בֳּל֔וֹ מֵעַ֥ל שִׁכְמ֖וֹ יָסֽוּר׃


that I will break Ashshur in my land, and upon my mountains tread him under foot: then shall his yoke depart from off them, and his burden depart from off their shoulders.


###### Isaiah 14:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:26)


זֹ֛את הָעֵצָ֥ה הַיְּעוּצָ֖ה עַל־כׇּל־הָאָ֑רֶץ וְזֹ֛את הַיָּ֥ד הַנְּטוּיָ֖ה עַל־כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִֽם׃


This is the purpose that is purposed upon the whole earth: and this is the hand that is stretched out upon all the nations.


###### Isaiah 14:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:27)


כִּֽי־יְהֹוָ֧ה צְבָא֛וֹת יָעָ֖ץ וּמִ֣י יָפֵ֑ר וְיָד֥וֹ הַנְּטוּיָ֖ה וּמִ֥י יְשִׁיבֶֽנָּה׃{פ}


For the Lord of hosts has purposed, and who shall annul it? and his hand is stretched out, and who shall turn it back?


###### Isaiah 14:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:28)


בִּשְׁנַת־מ֖וֹת הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ אָחָ֑ז הָיָ֖ה הַמַּשָּׂ֥א הַזֶּֽה׃


In the year that king Aĥaz died was this burden.


###### Isaiah 14:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:29)


אַֽל־תִּשְׂמְחִ֤י פְלֶ֙שֶׁת֙ כֻּלֵּ֔ךְ כִּ֥י נִשְׁבַּ֖ר שֵׁ֣בֶט מַכֵּ֑ךְ כִּֽי־מִשֹּׁ֤רֶשׁ נָחָשׁ֙ יֵ֣צֵא צֶ֔פַע וּפִרְי֖וֹ שָׂרָ֥ף מְעוֹפֵֽף׃


Rejoice not thou, O Peleshet entire, because the rod of him that smote thee is broken: for out of the serpent’s root shall come forth a viper, and his fruit shall be a venomous flying serpent.


###### Isaiah 14:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:30)


וְרָעוּ֙ בְּכוֹרֵ֣י דַלִּ֔ים וְאֶבְיוֹנִ֖ים לָבֶ֣טַח יִרְבָּ֑צוּ וְהֵמַתִּ֤י בָרָעָב֙ שׇׁרְשֵׁ֔ךְ וּשְׁאֵרִיתֵ֖ךְ יַהֲרֹֽג׃


And the firstborn of the poor shall feed, and the needy shall lie down in safety: and I will kill thy root with famine, and he shall slay thy remnant.


###### Isaiah 14:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:31)


הֵילִ֤ילִֽי שַׁ֙עַר֙ זַֽעֲקִי־עִ֔יר נָמ֖וֹג פְּלֶ֣שֶׁת כֻּלֵּ֑ךְ כִּ֤י מִצָּפוֹן֙ עָשָׁ֣ן בָּ֔א וְאֵ֥ין בּוֹדֵ֖ד בְּמוֹעָדָֽיו׃


Howl, O gate; cry, O city; thou, entire Peleshet, melt in fear: for there shall come from the north a smoke, and there shall be no straggler in his ranks.


###### Isaiah 14:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/14:32)


וּמַֽה־יַּעֲנֶ֖ה מַלְאֲכֵי־ג֑וֹי כִּ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ יִסַּ֣ד צִיּ֔וֹן וּבָ֥הּ יֶחֱס֖וּ עֲנִיֵּ֥י עַמּֽוֹ׃{פ}


What shall one then answer the messengers of the nation? That the Lord has founded Żiyyon, and the poor of his people shall shelter in it.



## Chapter 15

###### Isaiah 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:1)


מַשָּׂ֖א מוֹאָ֑ב כִּ֠י בְּלֵ֞יל שֻׁדַּ֨ד עָ֤ר מוֹאָב֙ נִדְמָ֔ה כִּ֗י בְּלֵ֛יל שֻׁדַּ֥ד קִיר־מוֹאָ֖ב נִדְמָֽה׃


The burden of Mo᾽av. Because in the night ῾Ar of Mo᾽av is laid waste, and ruined; because in the night Qir of Mo᾽av is laid waste, and ruined;


###### Isaiah 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:2)


עָלָ֨ה הַבַּ֧יִת וְדִיבֹ֛ן הַבָּמ֖וֹת לְבֶ֑כִי עַל־נְב֞וֹ וְעַ֤ל מֵֽידְבָא֙ מוֹאָ֣ב יְיֵלִ֔יל בְּכׇל־רֹאשָׁ֣יו קׇרְחָ֔ה כׇּל־זָקָ֖ן גְּרוּעָֽה׃


he is gone up to the shrine, and Divon to the high places, to weep: Mo᾽av shall howl over Nevo, and over Medeva: on all their heads is baldness, and every beard is cut off.


###### Isaiah 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:3)


בְּחוּצֹתָ֖יו חָ֣גְרוּ שָׂ֑ק עַ֣ל גַּגּוֹתֶ֧יהָ וּבִרְחֹבֹתֶ֛יהָ כֻּלֹּ֥ה יְיֵלִ֖יל יֹרֵ֥ד בַּבֶּֽכִי׃


In their streets they gird themselves with sackcloth: on the tops of their houses, and in their streets, every one howls, weeping abundantly.


###### Isaiah 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:4)


וַתִּזְעַ֤ק חֶשְׁבּוֹן֙ וְאֶלְעָלֵ֔ה עַד־יַ֖הַץ נִשְׁמַ֣ע קוֹלָ֑ם עַל־כֵּ֗ן חֲלֻצֵ֤י מוֹאָב֙ יָרִ֔יעוּ נַפְשׁ֖וֹ יָ֥רְעָה לּֽוֹ׃


And Ḥeshbon cries, and ᾽᾽El῾ale: their voice is heard as far as Yahaż: therefore the armed soldiers of Mo᾽av cry out; his soul trembles.


###### Isaiah 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:5)


לִבִּי֙ לְמוֹאָ֣ב יִזְעָ֔ק בְּרִיחֶ֕הָ עַד־צֹ֖עַר עֶגְלַ֣ת שְׁלִשִׁיָּ֑ה כִּ֣י**׀** מַעֲלֵ֣ה הַלּוּחִ֗ית בִּבְכִי֙ יַֽעֲלֶה־בּ֔וֹ כִּ֚י דֶּ֣רֶךְ חוֹרֹנַ֔יִם זַעֲקַת־שֶׁ֖בֶר יְעֹעֵֽרוּ׃


My heart cries out for Mo᾽av; his fugitives shall flee to Żo῾ar, a heifer of three years old: for by the ascent of Luĥit, with weeping shall they ascend it; for in the way of Ḥoronayim they shall raise up a cry of destruction.


###### Isaiah 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:6)


כִּי־מֵ֥י נִמְרִ֖ים מְשַׁמּ֣וֹת יִֽהְי֑וּ כִּֽי־יָבֵ֤שׁ חָצִיר֙ כָּ֣לָה דֶ֔שֶׁא יֶ֖רֶק לֹ֥א הָיָֽה׃


For the waters of Nimrim shall be desolate: for the nay is withered away, the grass fails, there is no green thing.


###### Isaiah 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:7)


עַל־כֵּ֖ן יִתְרָ֣ה עָשָׂ֑ה וּ֨פְקֻדָּתָ֔ם עַ֛ל נַ֥חַל הָעֲרָבִ֖ים יִשָּׂאֽוּם׃


Therefore the abundance they have gotten, and that which they have laid up, shall the enemy carry away past the wadi of the willows.


###### Isaiah 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:8)


כִּי־הִקִּ֥יפָה הַזְּעָקָ֖ה אֶת־גְּב֣וּל מוֹאָ֑ב עַד־אֶגְלַ֙יִם֙ יִלְלָתָ֔הּ וּבְאֵ֥ר אֵילִ֖ים יִלְלָתָֽהּ׃


For the cry is gone round about the borders of Mo᾽av; its howling as far as Eglayim, and its roaring to Be᾽er-elim.


###### Isaiah 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/15:9)


כִּ֣י מֵ֤י דִימוֹן֙ מָ֣לְאוּ דָ֔ם כִּֽי־אָשִׁ֥ית עַל־דִּימ֖וֹן נוֹסָפ֑וֹת לִפְלֵיטַ֤ת מוֹאָב֙ אַרְיֵ֔ה וְלִשְׁאֵרִ֖ית אֲדָמָֽה׃


For the waters of Dimon shall be full of blood: for I will bring yet more upon Dimon: a lion will I bring upon him that escapes of Mo᾽av, and upon the remnant of the land.



## Chapter 16

###### Isaiah 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:1)


שִׁלְחוּ־כַ֥ר מֹשֵֽׁל־אֶ֖רֶץ מִסֶּ֣לַע מִדְבָּ֑רָה אֶל־הַ֖ר בַּת־צִיּֽוֹן׃


Send the lamb to the ruler of the land from Sela to the wilderness, to the mount of the daughter of Żiyyon.


###### Isaiah 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:2)


וְהָיָ֥ה כְעוֹף־נוֹדֵ֖ד קֵ֣ן מְשֻׁלָּ֑ח תִּֽהְיֶ֙ינָה֙ בְּנ֣וֹת מוֹאָ֔ב מַעְבָּרֹ֖ת לְאַרְנֽוֹן׃


For it shall be, that, as a wandering bird, as a scattered nest, the daughters of Mo᾽av shall be at the fords of Arnon.


###### Isaiah 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:3)


(הביאו) [הָבִ֤יאִי] עֵצָה֙ עֲשׂ֣וּ פְלִילָ֔ה שִׁ֧יתִי כַלַּ֛יִל צִלֵּ֖ךְ בְּת֣וֹךְ צׇהֳרָ֑יִם סַתְּרִי֙ נִדָּחִ֔ים נֹדֵ֖ד אַל־תְּגַלִּֽי׃


Take counsel, execute judgment; make thy shadow as the night in the midst of the noonday; hide the outcasts; betray not the wanderer.


###### Isaiah 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:4)


יָג֤וּרוּ בָךְ֙ נִדָּחַ֔י מוֹאָ֛ב הֱוִי־סֵ֥תֶר לָ֖מוֹ מִפְּנֵ֣י שׁוֹדֵ֑ד כִּֽי־אָפֵ֤ס הַמֵּץ֙ כָּ֣לָה שֹׁ֔ד תַּ֥מּוּ רֹמֵ֖ס מִן־הָאָֽרֶץ׃{ס}


Let my outcasts, the outcasts of Mo᾽av, dwell with thee; be thou a covert to them from the face of the spoiler: for the extortioner is at an end, the spoiler ceases, the oppressors are consumed out of the land.


###### Isaiah 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:5)


וְהוּכַ֤ן בַּחֶ֙סֶד֙ כִּסֵּ֔א וְיָשַׁ֥ב עָלָ֛יו בֶּאֱמֶ֖ת בְּאֹ֣הֶל דָּוִ֑ד שֹׁפֵ֛ט וְדֹרֵ֥שׁ מִשְׁפָּ֖ט וּמְהִ֥ר צֶֽדֶק׃


And in mercy a throne was established: and he sat upon it in truthfulness in the tabernacle of David, judging, and seeking judgment, and quick to do righteousness.


###### Isaiah 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:6)


שָׁמַ֥עְנוּ גְאוֹן־מוֹאָ֖ב גֵּ֣א מְאֹ֑ד גַּאֲוָת֧וֹ וּגְאוֹנ֛וֹ וְעֶבְרָת֖וֹ לֹא־כֵ֥ן בַּדָּֽיו׃


We have heard of the pride of Mo᾽av; he is very proud: even of his haughtiness, and his pride, and his wrath: false are his pratings.


###### Isaiah 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:7)


לָכֵ֗ן יְיֵלִ֥יל מוֹאָ֛ב לְמוֹאָ֖ב כֻּלֹּ֣ה יְיֵלִ֑יל לַאֲשִׁישֵׁ֧י קִיר־חֲרֶ֛שֶׂת תֶּהְגּ֖וּ אַךְ־נְכָאִֽים׃


Therefore shall Mo᾽av howl for Mo᾽av, every one shall howl: for the foundations of Qir-ĥareset shall you mourn; surely they are stricken.


###### Isaiah 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:8)


כִּ֣י שַׁדְמוֹת֩ חֶשְׁבּ֨וֹן אֻמְלָ֜ל גֶּ֣פֶן שִׂבְמָ֗ה בַּעֲלֵ֤י גוֹיִם֙ הָלְמ֣וּ שְׂרוּקֶּ֔יהָ עַד־יַעְזֵ֥ר נָגָ֖עוּ תָּ֣עוּ מִדְבָּ֑ר שְׁלֻ֣חוֹתֶ֔יהָ נִטְּשׁ֖וּ עָ֥בְרוּ יָֽם׃


For the fields of Ḥeshbon languish, and the vine of Sivma: have broken down its choice branches, they reached to Ya῾zer, they wandered into the wilderness: her offshoots were stretched out, they went over the sea.


###### Isaiah 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:9)


עַל־כֵּ֡ן אֶבְכֶּ֞ה בִּבְכִ֤י יַעְזֵר֙ גֶּ֣פֶן שִׂבְמָ֔ה אֲרַיָּ֙וֶךְ֙ דִּמְעָתִ֔י חֶשְׁבּ֖וֹן וְאֶלְעָלֵ֑ה כִּ֧י עַל־קֵיצֵ֛ךְ וְעַל־קְצִירֵ֖ךְ הֵידָ֥ד נָפָֽל׃


Therefore I will bewail with the weeping of Ya῾zer the vine of Sivma: I will water thee with my tears, O Ḥeshbon, and ᾽El῾ale: for on thy summer fruits and on thy harvest the battle cry has fallen.


###### Isaiah 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:10)


וְנֶאֱסַ֨ף שִׂמְחָ֤ה וָגִיל֙ מִן־הַכַּרְמֶ֔ל וּבַכְּרָמִ֥ים לֹֽא־יְרֻנָּ֖ן לֹ֣א יְרֹעָ֑ע יַ֗יִן בַּיְקָבִ֛ים לֹא־יִדְרֹ֥ךְ הַדֹּרֵ֖ךְ הֵידָ֥ד הִשְׁבַּֽתִּי׃


And gladness is taken away, and joy out of the plentiful field; and in the vineyards there shall be no singing, neither shall there be shouting: the treaders shall tread out no wine in their presses; I have made shouting at the vintage to cease.


###### Isaiah 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:11)


עַל־כֵּן֙ מֵעַ֣י לְמוֹאָ֔ב כַּכִּנּ֖וֹר יֶהֱמ֑וּ וְקִרְבִּ֖י לְקִ֥יר חָֽרֶשׂ׃


Wherefore my heart shall moan like a lyre for Mo᾽av, and my inward parts for Qir-ĥeres.


###### Isaiah 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:12)


וְהָיָ֧ה כִֽי־נִרְאָ֛ה כִּֽי־נִלְאָ֥ה מוֹאָ֖ב עַל־הַבָּמָ֑ה וּבָ֧א אֶל־מִקְדָּשׁ֛וֹ לְהִתְפַּלֵּ֖ל וְלֹ֥א יוּכָֽל׃{ס}


And it shall come to pass, when it is seen that Mo᾽av is weary on the high place, that he shall come to his sanctuary to pray; but he shall not prevail.


###### Isaiah 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:13)


זֶ֣ה הַדָּבָ֗ר אֲשֶׁ֨ר דִּבֶּ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶל־מוֹאָ֖ב מֵאָֽז׃


This is the word that the Lord has spoken concerning Mo᾽av long ago.


###### Isaiah 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/16:14)


וְעַתָּ֗ה דִּבֶּ֣ר יְהֹוָה֮ לֵאמֹר֒ בְּשָׁלֹ֤שׁ שָׁנִים֙ כִּשְׁנֵ֣י שָׂכִ֔יר וְנִקְלָה֙ כְּב֣וֹד מוֹאָ֔ב בְּכֹ֖ל הֶהָמ֣וֹן הָרָ֑ב וּשְׁאָ֥ר מְעַ֛ט מִזְעָ֖ר ל֥וֹא כַבִּֽיר׃{פ}


But now the Lord has spoken, saying, Within three years, as the years of a hireling, and the glory of Mo᾽av shall be contemned, with all that great multitude; and the remnant shall be very small and powerless.



## Chapter 17

###### Isaiah 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:1)


מַשָּׂ֖א דַּמָּ֑שֶׂק הִנֵּ֤ה דַמֶּ֙שֶׂק֙ מוּסָ֣ר מֵעִ֔יר וְהָיְתָ֖ה מְעִ֥י מַפָּלָֽה׃


The burden of Dammeseq. Behold, Dammeseq is taken away from being a city, and it shall be a heap of ruins.


###### Isaiah 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:2)


עֲזֻב֖וֹת עָרֵ֣י עֲרֹעֵ֑ר לַעֲדָרִ֣ים תִּֽהְיֶ֔ינָה וְרָבְצ֖וּ וְאֵ֥ין מַחֲרִֽיד׃


The cities of ῾Aro῾er are forsaken: they shall be for flocks, which shall lie down, and none shall make them afraid.


###### Isaiah 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:3)


וְנִשְׁבַּ֤ת מִבְצָר֙ מֵאֶפְרַ֔יִם וּמַמְלָכָ֥ה מִדַּמֶּ֖שֶׂק וּשְׁאָ֣ר אֲרָ֑ם כִּכְב֤וֹד בְּנֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ יִֽהְי֔וּ נְאֻ֖ם יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָאֽוֹת׃{פ}


The fortress also shall cease from Efrayim, and the kingdom from Dammeseq, and the remnant of Aram shall be as the glory of the children of Yisra᾽el, says the Lord of hosts.


###### Isaiah 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:4)


וְהָיָה֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא יִדַּ֖ל כְּב֣וֹד יַעֲקֹ֑ב וּמִשְׁמַ֥ן בְּשָׂר֖וֹ יֵרָזֶֽה׃


And on that day it shall come to pass, that the glory of Ya῾aqov shall be made thin, and the fatness of his flesh shall become lean.


###### Isaiah 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:5)


וְהָיָ֗ה כֶּֽאֱסֹף֙ קָצִ֣יר קָמָ֔ה וּזְרֹע֖וֹ שִׁבֳּלִ֣ים יִקְצ֑וֹר וְהָיָ֛ה כִּמְלַקֵּ֥ט שִׁבֳּלִ֖ים בְּעֵ֥מֶק רְפָאִֽים׃


And it shall be as when the harvester gathers the standing corn, and reaps the ears with his arm; and it shall be as he that gathers ears in the valley of Refa᾽im.


###### Isaiah 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:6)


וְנִשְׁאַר־בּ֤וֹ עֽוֹלֵלֹת֙ כְּנֹ֣קֶף זַ֔יִת שְׁנַ֧יִם שְׁלֹשָׁ֛ה גַּרְגְּרִ֖ים בְּרֹ֣אשׁ אָמִ֑יר אַרְבָּעָ֣ה חֲמִשָּׁ֗ה בִּסְעִפֶ֙יהָ֙ פֹּֽרִיָּ֔ה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


And gleanings shall be left of him, as in the beating of an olive tree, two or three berries in the top of the uppermost bough, four or five in its fruitful branches, says the Lord God of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:7)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא יִשְׁעֶ֥ה הָאָדָ֖ם עַל־עֹשֵׂ֑הוּ וְעֵינָ֕יו אֶל־קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל תִּרְאֶֽינָה׃


At that day shall a man look to his Maker, and his eyes shall have regard to the Holy One of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:8)


וְלֹ֣א יִשְׁעֶ֔ה אֶל־הַֽמִּזְבְּח֖וֹת מַעֲשֵׂ֣ה יָדָ֑יו וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשׂ֤וּ אֶצְבְּעֹתָיו֙ לֹ֣א יִרְאֶ֔ה וְהָאֲשֵׁרִ֖ים וְהָחַמָּנִֽים׃{ס}


And he shall not look to the altars, the work of his hands, neither shall he regard that which his fingers have made, either the asherim or the sun images.


###### Isaiah 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:9)


בַּיּ֨וֹם הַה֜וּא יִהְי֣וּ**׀** עָרֵ֣י מָעוּזּ֗וֹ כַּעֲזוּבַ֤ת הַחֹ֙רֶשׁ֙ וְהָ֣אָמִ֔יר אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָֽזְב֔וּ מִפְּנֵ֖י בְּנֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וְהָיְתָ֖ה שְׁמָמָֽה׃


On that day shall his strong cities be as the forsaken portion of the thicket and the uppermost branch, which they left because of the children of Yisra᾽el: and there shall be desolation.


###### Isaiah 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:10)


כִּ֤י שָׁכַ֙חַתְּ֙ אֱלֹהֵ֣י יִשְׁעֵ֔ךְ וְצ֥וּר מָעֻזֵּ֖ךְ לֹ֣א זָכָ֑רְתְּ עַל־כֵּ֗ן תִּטְּעִי֙ נִטְעֵ֣י נַעֲמָנִ֔ים וּזְמֹ֥רַת זָ֖ר תִּזְרָעֶֽנּוּ׃


Because thou hast forgotten the God of thy salvation, and hast not been mindful of the rock of thy strength, therefore though thou plant pleasant plants, and set it with slips of a strange god:


###### Isaiah 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:11)


בְּי֤וֹם נִטְעֵךְ֙ תְּשַׂגְשֵׂ֔גִי וּבַבֹּ֖קֶר זַרְעֵ֣ךְ תַּפְרִ֑יחִי נֵ֥ד קָצִ֛יר בְּי֥וֹם נַחֲלָ֖ה וּכְאֵ֥ב אָנֽוּשׁ׃{ס}


though in the day of thy planting thou make it grow, and in the morning thou make thy seed to flourish: yet the harvest shall disappear in the day of grief and of desperate pain.


###### Isaiah 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:12)


ה֗וֹי הֲמוֹן֙ עַמִּ֣ים רַבִּ֔ים כַּהֲמ֥וֹת יַמִּ֖ים יֶהֱמָי֑וּן וּשְׁא֣וֹן לְאֻמִּ֔ים כִּשְׁא֛וֹן מַ֥יִם כַּבִּירִ֖ים יִשָּׁאֽוּן׃


Woe to the multitude of many people, who make a noise like the noise of the seas; and to the rushing of nations, that make a rushing like the rushing of mighty waters!


###### Isaiah 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:13)


לְאֻמִּ֗ים כִּשְׁא֞וֹן מַ֤יִם רַבִּים֙ יִשָּׁא֔וּן וְגָ֥עַר בּ֖וֹ וְנָ֣ס מִמֶּרְחָ֑ק וְרֻדַּ֗ף כְּמֹ֤ץ הָרִים֙ לִפְנֵי־ר֔וּחַ וּכְגַלְגַּ֖ל לִפְנֵ֥י סוּפָֽה׃


The nations shall rush like the rushing of many waters: but he shall rebuke them, and they shall flee far off, and shall be chased as the chaff of the mountains before the wind, and like whirling dust before the storm.


###### Isaiah 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/17:14)


לְעֵ֥ת עֶ֙רֶב֙ וְהִנֵּ֣ה בַלָּהָ֔ה בְּטֶ֥רֶם בֹּ֖קֶר אֵינֶ֑נּוּ זֶ֚ה חֵ֣לֶק שׁוֹסֵ֔ינוּ וְגוֹרָ֖ל לְבֹזְזֵֽינוּ׃{פ}


And behold at evening, trouble; and before the morning they are no more. This is the portion of them that spoil us, and the lot of them that rob us.



## Chapter 18

###### Isaiah 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:1)


ה֥וֹי אֶ֖רֶץ צִלְצַ֣ל כְּנָפָ֑יִם אֲשֶׁ֥ר מֵעֵ֖בֶר לְנַהֲרֵי־כֽוּשׁ׃


O land of buzzing wings, which fly beyond the rivers of Kush:


###### Isaiah 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:2)


הַשֹּׁלֵ֨חַ בַּיָּ֜ם צִירִ֗ים וּבִכְלֵי־גֹ֘מֶא֮ עַל־פְּנֵי־מַ֒יִם֒ לְכ֣וּ**׀** מַלְאָכִ֣ים קַלִּ֗ים אֶל־גּוֹי֙ מְמֻשָּׁ֣ךְ וּמוֹרָ֔ט אֶל־עַ֥ם נוֹרָ֖א מִן־ה֣וּא וָהָ֑לְאָה גּ֚וֹי קַו־קָ֣ו וּמְבוּסָ֔ה אֲשֶׁר־בָּזְא֥וּ נְהָרִ֖ים אַרְצֽוֹ׃


that sendest ambassadors by the sea, even in vessels of papyrus upon the waters: Go, swift messengers, to a nation tall and smooth, to a people terrible from their beginning onward; a nation mighty and conquering, whose land the rivers have divided!


###### Isaiah 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:3)


כׇּל־יֹשְׁבֵ֥י תֵבֵ֖ל וְשֹׁ֣כְנֵי אָ֑רֶץ כִּנְשֹׂא־נֵ֤ס הָרִים֙ תִּרְא֔וּ וְכִתְקֹ֥עַ שׁוֹפָ֖ר תִּשְׁמָֽעוּ׃{ס}


All you inhabitants of the world, and dwellers on the earth, see, when he lifts up a banner on the mountains; and when he blows a shofar, hear!


###### Isaiah 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:4)


כִּי֩ כֹ֨ה אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֵלַ֔י (אשקוטה) [אֶשְׁקֳטָ֖ה] וְאַבִּ֣יטָה בִמְכוֹנִ֑י כְּחֹ֥ם צַח֙ עֲלֵי־א֔וֹר כְּעָ֥ב טַ֖ל בְּחֹ֥ם קָצִֽיר׃


For so the Lord said to me, I will take my rest, and I will look on in my dwelling place, like the clear heat in sunlight; and like the cloud of dew in the heat of harvest.


###### Isaiah 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:5)


כִּֽי־לִפְנֵ֤י קָצִיר֙ כְּתׇם־פֶּ֔רַח וּבֹ֥סֶר גֹּמֵ֖ל יִֽהְיֶ֣ה נִצָּ֑הֿ וְכָרַ֤ת הַזַּלְזַלִּים֙ בַּמַּזְמֵר֔וֹת וְאֶת־הַנְּטִישׁ֖וֹת הֵסִ֥יר הֵתַֽז׃


For before the harvest, when the blossom is past, and the bud is ripening into young grapes, he shall both cut off the sprigs with pruning hooks, and take away and cut down the branches.


###### Isaiah 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:6)


יֵעָזְב֤וּ יַחְדָּו֙ לְעֵ֣יט הָרִ֔ים וּֽלְבֶהֱמַ֖ת הָאָ֑רֶץ וְקָ֤ץ עָלָיו֙ הָעַ֔יִט וְכׇל־בֶּהֱמַ֥ת הָאָ֖רֶץ עָלָ֥יו תֶּחֱרָֽף׃{ס}


They shall be left together to the predatory birds of the mountains, and to the beasts of the earth: and the birds shall summer upon them, and all the beasts of the earth shall winter upon them.


###### Isaiah 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/18:7)


בָּעֵת֩ הַהִ֨יא יוּבַל־שַׁ֜י לַיהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֗וֹת עַ֚ם מְמֻשָּׁ֣ךְ וּמוֹרָ֔ט וּמֵעַ֥ם נוֹרָ֖א מִן־ה֣וּא וָהָ֑לְאָה גּ֣וֹי**׀** קַו־קָ֣ו וּמְבוּסָ֗ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר בָּזְא֤וּ נְהָרִים֙ אַרְצ֔וֹ אֶל־מְק֛וֹם שֵׁם־יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת הַר־צִיּֽוֹן׃{פ}


In that time shall a present be brought to the Lord of hosts, by a people tall and smooth, even by a people terrible from their beginning onward; a nation mighty and conquering, whose land the rivers have divided, to the place of the name of the Lord of hosts, the mount Żiyyon.



## Chapter 19

###### Isaiah 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:1)


מַשָּׂ֖א מִצְרָ֑יִם הִנֵּ֨ה יְהֹוָ֜ה רֹכֵ֨ב עַל־עָ֥ב קַל֙ וּבָ֣א מִצְרַ֔יִם וְנָע֞וּ אֱלִילֵ֤י מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ מִפָּנָ֔יו וּלְבַ֥ב מִצְרַ֖יִם יִמַּ֥ס בְּקִרְבּֽוֹ׃


The burden of Miżrayim. Behold, the Lord rides upon a swift cloud, and shall come into Miżrayim: and the idols of Miżrayim shall be moved at his presence, and the heart of Miżrayim shall melt in the midst of it.


###### Isaiah 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:2)


וְסִכְסַכְתִּ֤י מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ בְּמִצְרַ֔יִם וְנִלְחֲמ֥וּ אִישׁ־בְּאָחִ֖יו וְאִ֣ישׁ בְּרֵעֵ֑הוּ עִ֣יר בְּעִ֔יר מַמְלָכָ֖ה בְּמַמְלָכָֽה׃


And I will set Miżrayim against Miżrayim: and they shall fight every one against his brother, and every one against his neighbour; city against city, and kingdom against kingdom.


###### Isaiah 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:3)


וְנָבְקָ֤ה רוּחַ־מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ בְּקִרְבּ֔וֹ וַעֲצָת֖וֹ אֲבַלֵּ֑עַ וְדָרְשׁ֤וּ אֶל־הָֽאֱלִילִים֙ וְאֶל־הָ֣אִטִּ֔ים וְאֶל־הָאֹב֖וֹת וְאֶל־הַיִּדְּעֹנִֽים׃


And the spirit of Miżrayim shall be emptied in his midst; and I will destroy his counsel: and they shall seek to the idols, and to the necromancers, and to the mediums, and to the wizards.


###### Isaiah 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:4)


וְסִכַּרְתִּי֙ אֶת־מִצְרַ֔יִם בְּיַ֖ד אֲדֹנִ֣ים קָשֶׁ֑ה וּמֶ֤לֶךְ עַז֙ יִמְשׇׁל־בָּ֔ם נְאֻ֥ם הָאָד֖וֹן יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָאֽוֹת׃


And I will give over Miżrayim to the hand of a cruel lord; and a fierce king shall rule over them, says the Master, the Lord of hosts.


###### Isaiah 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:5)


וְנִשְּׁתוּ־מַ֖יִם מֵֽהַיָּ֑ם וְנָהָ֖ר יֶחֱרַ֥ב וְיָבֵֽשׁ׃


And the waters shall fail from the sea, and the river shall be wasted and dried up.


###### Isaiah 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:6)


וְהֶאֶזְנִ֣יחוּ נְהָר֔וֹת דָּלְל֥וּ וְחָרְב֖וּ יְאֹרֵ֣י מָצ֑וֹר קָנֶ֥ה וָס֖וּף קָמֵֽלוּ׃


And the rivers shall become foul; and the canals of Mażor shall be diminished and dried up: the reeds and rushes shall wither.


###### Isaiah 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:7)


עָר֥וֹת עַל־יְא֖וֹר עַל־פִּ֣י יְא֑וֹר וְכֹל֙ מִזְרַ֣ע יְא֔וֹר יִיבַ֥שׁ נִדַּ֖ף וְאֵינֶֽנּוּ׃


The meadows by the River, by the mouth of the River, and every thing sown by the River, shall wither, be driven away, and be no more.


###### Isaiah 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:8)


וְאָנוּ֙ הַדַּיָּגִ֔ים וְאָ֣בְל֔וּ כׇּל־מַשְׁלִיכֵ֥י בַיְא֖וֹר חַכָּ֑ה וּפֹרְשֵׂ֥י מִכְמֹ֛רֶת עַל־פְּנֵי־מַ֖יִם אֻמְלָֽלוּ׃


The fishers also shall mourn, and all they that cast angle into the River shall lament, and they that spread nets upon the waters shall be wretched.


###### Isaiah 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:9)


וּבֹ֛שׁוּ עֹבְדֵ֥י פִשְׁתִּ֖ים שְׂרִיק֑וֹת וְאֹרְגִ֖ים חוֹרָֽי׃


Moreover they that work in combed flax, and they that weave white cotton, shall be confounded.


###### Isaiah 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:10)


וְהָי֥וּ שָׁתֹתֶ֖יהָ מְדֻכָּאִ֑ים כׇּל־עֹ֥שֵׂי שֶׂ֖כֶר אַגְמֵי־נָֽפֶשׁ׃


And its foundations shall be broken; all that make dams shall be sad in their soul.


###### Isaiah 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:11)


אַךְ־אֱוִלִים֙ שָׂ֣רֵי צֹ֔עַן חַכְמֵי֙ יֹעֲצֵ֣י פַרְעֹ֔ה עֵצָ֖ה נִבְעָרָ֑ה אֵ֚יךְ תֹּאמְר֣וּ אֶל־פַּרְעֹ֔ה בֶּן־חֲכָמִ֥ים אֲנִ֖י בֶּן־מַלְכֵי־קֶֽדֶם׃


Surely the princes of Żo῾an are fools, the counsel of the wise counsellors of Par῾o is become brutish: how can you say to Par῾o, I am the son of the wise, the son of ancient kings?


###### Isaiah 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:12)


אַיָּם֙ אֵפ֣וֹא חֲכָמֶ֔יךָ וְיַגִּ֥ידוּ נָ֖א לָ֑ךְ וְיֵ֣דְע֔וּ מַה־יָּעַ֛ץ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת עַל־מִצְרָֽיִם׃


Where are they? where are thy wise men? and let them tell thee now, and let them know what the Lord of hosts has purposed concerning Miżrayim.


###### Isaiah 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:13)


נֽוֹאֲלוּ֙ שָׂ֣רֵי צֹ֔עַן נִשְּׁא֖וּ שָׂ֣רֵי נֹ֑ף הִתְע֥וּ אֶת־מִצְרַ֖יִם פִּנַּ֥ת שְׁבָטֶֽיהָ׃


The princes of Żo῾an are become fools, the princes of Nof are deceived; they have also made Miżrayim go astray, those that are the stay of its tribes.


###### Isaiah 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:14)


יְהֹוָ֛ה מָסַ֥ךְ בְּקִרְבָּ֖הּ ר֣וּחַ עִוְעִ֑ים וְהִתְע֤וּ אֶת־מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ בְּכׇֽל־מַעֲשֵׂ֔הוּ כְּהִתָּע֥וֹת שִׁכּ֖וֹר בְּקִיאֽוֹ׃


The Lord has mingled a spirit of confusion in the midst of her: and they have caused Miżrayim to err in all its work, as a drunken man staggers in his vomit.


###### Isaiah 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:15)


וְלֹא־יִהְיֶ֥ה לְמִצְרַ֖יִם מַעֲשֶׂ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֧ר יַעֲשֶׂ֛ה רֹ֥אשׁ וְזָנָ֖ב כִּפָּ֥ה וְאַגְמֽוֹן׃


Neither shall there be any work for Miżrayim which the head or tail, branch or rush, may do.


###### Isaiah 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:16)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא יִהְיֶ֥ה מִצְרַ֖יִם כַּנָּשִׁ֑ים וְחָרַ֣ד**׀** וּפָחַ֗ד מִפְּנֵי֙ תְּנוּפַת֙ יַד־יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת אֲשֶׁר־ה֖וּא מֵנִ֥יף עָלָֽיו׃


In that day shall Miżrayim be like women: and it shall be afraid and fear because of the shaking of the hand of the Lord of hosts, which he shakes over it.


###### Isaiah 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:17)


וְ֠הָיְתָ֠ה אַדְמַ֨ת יְהוּדָ֤ה לְמִצְרַ֙יִם֙ לְחׇגָּ֔א כֹּל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יַזְכִּ֥יר אֹתָ֛הּ אֵלָ֖יו יִפְחָ֑ד מִפְּנֵ֗י עֲצַת֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת אֲשֶׁר־ה֖וּא יוֹעֵ֥ץ עָלָֽיו׃{ס}


And the land of Yehuda shall be a trembling to Miżrayim, every one to whom one makes mention of that shall be afraid, because of the counsel of the Lord of hosts, which he has determined against it.


###### Isaiah 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:18)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֡וּא יִהְיוּ֩ חָמֵ֨שׁ עָרִ֜ים בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרַ֗יִם מְדַבְּרוֹת֙ שְׂפַ֣ת כְּנַ֔עַן וְנִשְׁבָּע֖וֹת לַיהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֑וֹת עִ֣יר הַהֶ֔רֶס יֵאָמֵ֖ר לְאֶחָֽת׃{ס}


In that day there shall be five cities in the land of Miżrayim, speaking the language of Kena῾an and swearing by the Lord of hosts; one shall be called, The city of destruction.


###### Isaiah 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:19)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא יִֽהְיֶ֤ה מִזְבֵּ֙חַ֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה בְּת֖וֹךְ אֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם וּמַצֵּבָ֥ה אֵצֶל־גְּבוּלָ֖הּ לַֽיהֹוָֽה׃


In that day shall there be an altar to the Lord in the midst of the land of Miżrayim, and a pillar at its border to the Lord.


###### Isaiah 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:20)


וְהָיָ֨ה לְא֥וֹת וּלְעֵ֛ד לַיהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם כִּֽי־יִצְעֲק֤וּ אֶל־יְהֹוָה֙ מִפְּנֵ֣י לֹחֲצִ֔ים וְיִשְׁלַ֥ח לָהֶ֛ם מוֹשִׁ֥יעַ וָרָ֖ב וְהִצִּילָֽם׃


And it shall be for a sign and for a witness to the Lord of hosts in the land of Miżrayim: for they shall cry to the Lord because of the oppressors, and he shall send them one that shall save them and plead for them, and he shall deliver them.


###### Isaiah 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:21)


וְנוֹדַ֤ע יְהֹוָה֙ לְמִצְרַ֔יִם וְיָדְע֥וּ מִצְרַ֛יִם אֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֑וּא וְעָֽבְדוּ֙ זֶ֣בַח וּמִנְחָ֔ה וְנָדְרוּ־נֵ֥דֶר לַיהֹוָ֖ה וְשִׁלֵּֽמוּ׃


And the Lord shall be known to Miżrayim, and Miżrayim shall know the Lord on that day, and shall do sacrifice and meal offering; and they shall vow a vow to the Lord, and perform it.


###### Isaiah 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:22)


וְנָגַ֧ף יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶת־מִצְרַ֖יִם נָגֹ֣ף וְרָפ֑וֹא וְשָׁ֙בוּ֙ עַד־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְנֶעְתַּ֥ר לָהֶ֖ם וּרְפָאָֽם׃{ס}


And the Lord shall smite Miżrayim: he shall smite and heal: and they shall return to the Lord, and he shall be entreated of them, and shall heal them.


###### Isaiah 19:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:23)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא תִּהְיֶ֨ה מְסִלָּ֤ה מִמִּצְרַ֙יִם֙ אַשּׁ֔וּרָה וּבָא־אַשּׁ֥וּר בְּמִצְרַ֖יִם וּמִצְרַ֣יִם בְּאַשּׁ֑וּר וְעָבְד֥וּ מִצְרַ֖יִם אֶת־אַשּֽׁוּר׃{ס}


In that day there shall be a highway out of Miżrayim to Ashshur, and Ashshur shall come into Miżrayim, and Miżrayim to Ashshur, and Miżrayim shall worship with Ashshur.


###### Isaiah 19:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:24)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא יִהְיֶ֤ה יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ שְׁלִ֣ישִׁיָּ֔ה לְמִצְרַ֖יִם וּלְאַשּׁ֑וּר בְּרָכָ֖ה בְּקֶ֥רֶב הָאָֽרֶץ׃


In that day shall Yisra᾽el be the third with Miżrayim and with Ashshur, a blessing in the midst of the land:


###### Isaiah 19:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/19:25)


אֲשֶׁ֧ר בֵּרְכ֛וֹ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת לֵאמֹ֑ר בָּר֨וּךְ עַמִּ֜י מִצְרַ֗יִם וּמַעֲשֵׂ֤ה יָדַי֙ אַשּׁ֔וּר וְנַחֲלָתִ֖י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


whom the Lord of hosts shall bless, saying, Blessed be Miżrayim my people, and Ashshur the work of my hands, and Yisra᾽el my inheritance.



## Chapter 20

###### Isaiah 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:1)


בִּשְׁנַ֨ת בֹּ֤א תַרְתָּן֙ אַשְׁדּ֔וֹדָה בִּשְׁלֹ֣חַ אֹת֔וֹ סַֽרְג֖וֹן מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֑וּר וַיִּלָּ֥חֶם בְּאַשְׁדּ֖וֹד וַֽיִּלְכְּדָֽהּ׃


In the year that Tartan came to Ashdod, (when Sargon the king of Ashshur sent him,) and fought against Ashdod, and took it;


###### Isaiah 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:2)


בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֗יא דִּבֶּ֣ר יְהֹוָה֮ בְּיַ֣ד יְשַֽׁעְיָ֣הוּ בֶן־אָמוֹץ֮ לֵאמֹר֒ לֵ֗ךְ וּפִתַּחְתָּ֤ הַשַּׂק֙ מֵעַ֣ל מׇתְנֶ֔יךָ וְנַעַלְךָ֥ תַחֲלֹ֖ץ מֵעַ֣ל רַגְלֶ֑ךָ וַיַּ֣עַשׂ כֵּ֔ן הָלֹ֖ךְ עָר֥וֹם וְיָחֵֽף׃{ס}


at the same time the Lord spoke by Yesha῾yahu the son of Amoż saying, Go and loose the sackcloth from off thy loins, and put off thy shoe from thy foot. And he did so, walking naked and barefoot.


###### Isaiah 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:3)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְהֹוָ֔ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר הָלַ֛ךְ עַבְדִּ֥י יְשַֽׁעְיָ֖הוּ עָר֣וֹם וְיָחֵ֑ף שָׁלֹ֤שׁ שָׁנִים֙ א֣וֹת וּמוֹפֵ֔ת עַל־מִצְרַ֖יִם וְעַל־כּֽוּשׁ׃


And the Lord said, Just as my servant Yesha῾yahu has walked naked and barefoot three years for a sign and a portent upon Miżrayim and upon Kush;


###### Isaiah 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:4)


כֵּ֣ן יִנְהַ֣ג מֶֽלֶךְ־אַ֠שּׁ֠וּר אֶת־שְׁבִ֨י מִצְרַ֜יִם וְאֶת־גָּל֥וּת כּ֛וּשׁ נְעָרִ֥ים וּזְקֵנִ֖ים עָר֣וֹם וְיָחֵ֑ף וַחֲשׂוּפַ֥י שֵׁ֖ת עֶרְוַ֥ת מִצְרָֽיִם׃


so shall the king of Ashshur lead away the prisoners of Miżrayim and the exiles of Kush, young and old, naked and barefoot, even with their buttocks uncovered, to the shame of Miżrayim.


###### Isaiah 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:5)


וְחַתּ֖וּ וָבֹ֑שׁוּ מִכּוּשׁ֙ מַבָּטָ֔ם וּמִן־מִצְרַ֖יִם תִּפְאַרְתָּֽם׃


And they shall be afraid and ashamed of Kush their expectation, and of Miżrayim their glory.


###### Isaiah 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/20:6)


וְ֠אָמַ֠ר יֹשֵׁ֨ב הָאִ֣י הַזֶּה֮ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַהוּא֒ הִנֵּה־כֹ֣ה מַבָּטֵ֗נוּ אֲשֶׁר־נַ֤סְנוּ שָׁם֙ לְעֶזְרָ֔ה לְהִ֨נָּצֵ֔ל מִפְּנֵ֖י מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֑וּר וְאֵ֖יךְ נִמָּלֵ֥ט אֲנָֽחְנוּ׃{פ}


And the inhabitant of this coast land shall say on that day, Behold, such is our expectation, whither we flee for help to be delivered from the king of Ashshur: and how shall we escape?



## Chapter 21

###### Isaiah 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:1)


מַשָּׂ֖א מִדְבַּר־יָ֑ם כְּסוּפ֤וֹת בַּנֶּ֙גֶב֙ לַחֲלֹ֔ף מִמִּדְבָּ֣ר בָּ֔א מֵאֶ֖רֶץ נוֹרָאָֽה׃


The burden of the desert of the sea. As storms in the south sweep on; so it comes from the desert, from a terrible land.


###### Isaiah 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:2)


חָז֥וּת קָשָׁ֖ה הֻגַּד־לִ֑י הַבּוֹגֵ֤ד׀בּוֹגֵד֙ וְהַשּׁוֹדֵ֣ד׀שׁוֹדֵ֔ד עֲלִ֤י עֵילָם֙ צוּרִ֣י מָדַ֔י כׇּל־אַנְחָתָ֖הֿ הִשְׁבַּֽתִּי׃


A grievous vision is declared to me; He who desires to be treacherous may be treacherous, and he who desires to spoil may spoil, Go up, O ῾Elam: besiege, O Maday; all its sighing have I made to cease.


###### Isaiah 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:3)


עַל־כֵּ֗ן מָלְא֤וּ מׇתְנַי֙ חַלְחָלָ֔ה צִירִ֣ים אֲחָז֔וּנִי כְּצִירֵ֖י יוֹלֵדָ֑ה נַעֲוֵ֣יתִי מִשְּׁמֹ֔עַ נִבְהַ֖לְתִּי מֵרְאֽוֹת׃


Therefore are my loins filled with anguish: pangs have taken hold upon me, as the pangs of a woman in travail: I was bowed down so that I could not hear; I was dismayed so that I could not see.


###### Isaiah 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:4)


תָּעָ֣ה לְבָבִ֔י פַּלָּצ֖וּת בִּֽעֲתָ֑תְנִי אֵ֚ת נֶ֣שֶׁף חִשְׁקִ֔י שָׂ֥ם לִ֖י לַחֲרָדָֽה׃


My heart reeled, fearfulness affrighted me: the night of my pleasure has he turned for me into terror.


###### Isaiah 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:5)


עָרֹ֧ךְ הַשֻּׁלְחָ֛ן צָפֹ֥ה הַצָּפִ֖ית אָכ֣וֹל שָׁתֹ֑ה ק֥וּמוּ הַשָּׂרִ֖ים מִשְׁח֥וּ מָגֵֽן׃{ס}


Prepare the table, spread the carpet, eat, drink: arise, princes, and anoint the shield.


###### Isaiah 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:6)


כִּ֣י כֹ֥ה אָמַ֛ר אֵלַ֖י אֲדֹנָ֑י לֵ֚ךְ הַעֲמֵ֣ד הַֽמְצַפֶּ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר יִרְאֶ֖ה יַגִּֽיד׃


For thus has the Lord said to me, Go, set a watchman, let him declare what he sees.


###### Isaiah 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:7)


וְרָ֣אָה רֶ֗כֶב צֶ֚מֶד פָּרָשִׁ֔ים רֶ֥כֶב חֲמ֖וֹר רֶ֣כֶב גָּמָ֑ל וְהִקְשִׁ֥יב קֶ֖שֶׁב רַב־קָֽשֶׁב׃


And if he should see riders, pairs of horsemen, riders on asses, riders on camels; he should hearken diligently with much heed:


###### Isaiah 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:8)


וַיִּקְרָ֖א אַרְיֵ֑ה עַל־מִצְפֶּ֣ה**׀** אֲדֹנָ֗י אָנֹכִ֞י עֹמֵ֤ד תָּמִיד֙ יוֹמָ֔ם וְעַ֨ל־מִשְׁמַרְתִּ֔י אָנֹכִ֥י נִצָּ֖ב כׇּל־הַלֵּילֽוֹת׃


and he cried like a lion: My Lord, I stand continually upon the watchtower in the daytime, and I stand on guard whole nights:


###### Isaiah 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:9)


וְהִנֵּה־זֶ֥ה בָא֙ רֶ֣כֶב אִ֔ישׁ צֶ֖מֶד פָּרָשִׁ֑ים וַיַּ֣עַן וַיֹּ֗אמֶר נָפְלָ֤ה נָֽפְלָה֙ בָּבֶ֔ל וְכׇל־פְּסִילֵ֥י אֱלֹהֶ֖יהָ שִׁבַּ֥ר לָאָֽרֶץ׃


and, behold, here come men riding, pairs of horsemen. And he answered and said, Bavel is fallen, is fallen; and all the carved images of her gods he has broken to the ground.


###### Isaiah 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:10)


מְדֻשָׁתִ֖י וּבֶן־גׇּרְנִ֑י אֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׁמַ֗עְתִּי מֵאֵ֨ת יְהֹוָ֧ה צְבָא֛וֹת אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל הִגַּ֥דְתִּי לָכֶֽם׃{פ}


O my trodden one, and child of my threshing floor; that which I have heard of the Lord of hosts, the God of Yisra᾽el, have I declared to you.


###### Isaiah 21:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:11)


מַשָּׂ֖א דּוּמָ֑ה אֵלַי֙ קֹרֵ֣א מִשֵּׂעִ֔יר שֹׁמֵר֙ מַה־מִּלַּ֔יְלָה שֹׁמֵ֖ר מַה־מִּלֵּֽיל׃


The burden of Duma. One calls to me out of Se῾ir, Watchman, what of the night? Watchman, what of the night?


###### Isaiah 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:12)


אָמַ֣ר שֹׁמֵ֔ר אָתָ֥ה בֹ֖קֶר וְגַם־לָ֑יְלָה אִם־תִּבְעָי֥וּן בְּעָ֖יוּ שֻׁ֥בוּ אֵתָֽיוּ׃{פ}


The watchman said, The morning comes, and also the night: if you will inquire, inquire: return, come.


###### Isaiah 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:13)


מַשָּׂ֖א בַּעְרָ֑ב בַּיַּ֤עַר בַּעְרַב֙ תָּלִ֔ינוּ אֹרְח֖וֹת דְּדָנִֽים׃


The burden upon ῾Arav. In the forest, in ῾Arav shall you lodge, O travelling companies of Dedanim.


###### Isaiah 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:14)


לִקְרַ֥את צָמֵ֖א הֵתָ֣יוּ מָ֑יִם יֹֽשְׁבֵי֙ אֶ֣רֶץ תֵּימָ֔א בְּלַחְמ֖וֹ קִדְּמ֥וּ נֹדֵֽד׃


O inhabitants of the land of Tema, bring water to him that is thirsty, meet the fugitive with bread.


###### Isaiah 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:15)


כִּֽי־מִפְּנֵ֥י חֲרָב֖וֹת נָדָ֑דוּ מִפְּנֵ֣י**׀** חֶ֣רֶב נְטוּשָׁ֗ה וּמִפְּנֵי֙ קֶ֣שֶׁת דְּרוּכָ֔ה וּמִפְּנֵ֖י כֹּ֥בֶד מִלְחָמָֽה׃{ס}


For they have fled from the swords, from the drawn sword, and from the bent bow, and from the grievousness of war.


###### Isaiah 21:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:16)


כִּי־כֹ֛ה אָמַ֥ר אֲדֹנָ֖י אֵלָ֑י בְּע֤וֹד שָׁנָה֙ כִּשְׁנֵ֣י שָׂכִ֔יר וְכָלָ֖ה כׇּל־כְּב֥וֹד קֵדָֽר׃


For thus the Lord said to me, Within a year, according to the years of a hireling, and all the glory of Qedar shall fail:


###### Isaiah 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/21:17)
