וּשְׁאָ֧ר מִסְפַּר־קֶ֛שֶׁת גִּבּוֹרֵ֥י בְנֵֽי־קֵדָ֖ר יִמְעָ֑טוּ כִּ֛י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵֽי־יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל דִּבֵּֽר׃{ס}


And the residue of the number of archers, the mighty men of the children of Qedar, shall be diminished: for the Lord God of Yisra᾽el has spoken it.



## Chapter 22

###### Isaiah 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:1)


מַשָּׂ֖א גֵּ֣יא חִזָּי֑וֹן מַה־לָּ֣ךְ אֵפ֔וֹא כִּי־עָלִ֥ית כֻּלָּ֖ךְ לַגַּגּֽוֹת׃


The burden of Ge-ĥizzayon (the Valley of Vision.) What ails thee now, that thou art wholly gone up to the housetops?


###### Isaiah 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:2)


תְּשֻׁא֣וֹת**׀** מְלֵאָ֗ה עִ֚יר הֽוֹמִיָּ֔ה קִרְיָ֖ה עַלִּיזָ֑ה חֲלָלַ֙יִךְ֙ לֹ֣א חַלְלֵי־חֶ֔רֶב וְלֹ֖א מֵתֵ֥י מִלְחָמָֽה׃


Thou that art full of uproar, a tumultuous city, a joyous city: thy slain men are not slain with the sword, nor dead in battle.


###### Isaiah 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:3)


כׇּל־קְצִינַ֥יִךְ נָֽדְדוּ־יַ֖חַד מִקֶּ֣שֶׁת אֻסָּ֑רוּ כׇּל־נִמְצָאַ֙יִךְ֙ אֻסְּר֣וּ יַחְדָּ֔ו מֵרָח֖וֹק בָּרָֽחוּ׃


All thy rulers are fled together, they are bound by the bowmen: all that are found in thee are bound together; they have fled from far.


###### Isaiah 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:4)


עַל־כֵּ֥ן אָמַ֛רְתִּי שְׁע֥וּ מִנִּ֖י אֲמָרֵ֣ר בַּבֶּ֑כִי אַל־תָּאִ֣יצוּ לְנַֽחֲמֵ֔נִי עַל־שֹׁ֖ד בַּת־עַמִּֽי׃


Therefore said I, Look away from me; I will weep bitterly, labour not to comfort me, because of the spoiling of the daughter of my people.


###### Isaiah 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:5)


כִּ֣י יוֹם֩ מְהוּמָ֨ה וּמְבוּסָ֜ה וּמְבוּכָ֗ה לַֽאדֹנָ֧י יֱהֹוִ֛ה צְבָא֖וֹת בְּגֵ֣י חִזָּי֑וֹן מְקַרְקַ֥ר קִ֖ר וְשׁ֥וֹעַ אֶל־הָהָֽר׃


For it is a day of trouble, and of trampling, and of confusion by the Lord God of hosts in Ge-ĥizzayon (the Valley of Vision), a breaking down of walls and a shouting to the mountains.


###### Isaiah 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:6)


וְעֵילָם֙ נָשָׂ֣א אַשְׁפָּ֔ה בְּרֶ֥כֶב אָדָ֖ם פָּרָשִׁ֑ים וְקִ֥יר עֵרָ֖ה מָגֵֽן׃


And ῾Elam bore the quiver with chariots of men and horsemen, and Qir uncovered the shield.


###### Isaiah 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:7)


וַיְהִ֥י מִבְחַר־עֲמָקַ֖יִךְ מָ֣לְאוּ רָ֑כֶב וְהַפָּ֣רָשִׁ֔ים שֹׁ֖ת שָׁ֥תוּ הַשָּֽׁעְרָה׃


And it came to pass, when thy choicest valleys were full of chariots, and the horsemen set themselves in array at the gate,


###### Isaiah 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:8)


וַיְגַ֕ל אֵ֖ת מָסַ֣ךְ יְהוּדָ֑ה וַתַּבֵּט֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא אֶל־נֶ֖שֶׁק בֵּ֥ית הַיָּֽעַר׃


and he stripped the covering of Yehuda, that thou didst look on that day to the armour of the house of the forest.


###### Isaiah 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:9)


וְאֵ֨ת בְּקִיעֵ֧י עִיר־דָּוִ֛ד רְאִיתֶ֖ם כִּי־רָ֑בּוּ וַֽתְּקַבְּצ֔וּ אֶת־מֵ֥י הַבְּרֵכָ֖ה הַתַּחְתּוֹנָֽה׃


You saw also the breaches of the city of David, that they are many: and you gathered together the waters of the lower pool.


###### Isaiah 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:10)


וְאֶת־בָּתֵּ֥י יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם סְפַרְתֶּ֑ם וַתִּתְצוּ֙ הַבָּ֣תִּ֔ים לְבַצֵּ֖ר הַחוֹמָֽה׃


And you numbered the houses of Yerushalayim, and the houses you broke down to fortify the wall.


###### Isaiah 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:11)


וּמִקְוָ֣ה**׀** עֲשִׂיתֶ֗ם בֵּ֚ין הַחֹ֣מֹתַ֔יִם לְמֵ֖י הַבְּרֵכָ֣ה הַיְשָׁנָ֑ה וְלֹ֤א הִבַּטְתֶּם֙ אֶל־עֹשֶׂ֔יהָ וְיֹצְרָ֥הּ מֵרָח֖וֹק לֹ֥א רְאִיתֶֽם׃


You made also a pond between the two walls for the water of the old pool: but you did not look to him who made it, nor did you see him that fashioned it long ago.


###### Isaiah 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:12)


וַיִּקְרָ֗א אֲדֹנָ֧י יֱהֹוִ֛ה צְבָא֖וֹת בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֑וּא לִבְכִי֙ וּלְמִסְפֵּ֔ד וּלְקׇרְחָ֖ה וְלַחֲגֹ֥ר שָֽׂק׃


And on that day did the Lord God of hosts call to weeping, and to mourning, and to baldness, and to girding with sackcloth:


###### Isaiah 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:13)


וְהִנֵּ֣ה**׀** שָׂשׂ֣וֹן וְשִׂמְחָ֗ה הָרֹ֤ג׀בָּקָר֙ וְשָׁחֹ֣ט צֹ֔אן אָכֹ֥ל בָּשָׂ֖ר וְשָׁת֣וֹת יָ֑יִן אָכ֣וֹל וְשָׁת֔וֹ כִּ֥י מָחָ֖ר נָמֽוּת׃


and behold joy and gladness, slaying oxen, and killing sheep, eating meat, and drinking wine: let us eat and drink; for to morrow we shall die.


###### Isaiah 22:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:14)


וְנִגְלָ֥ה בְאׇזְנָ֖י יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֑וֹת אִם־יְ֠כֻפַּ֠ר הֶעָוֺ֨ן הַזֶּ֤ה לָכֶם֙ עַד־תְּמֻת֔וּן אָמַ֛ר אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִ֖ה צְבָאֽוֹת׃{פ}


And it was revealed in my ears, by the Lord of hosts: Surely this iniquity shall not be forgiven you till you die, says the Lord God of hosts.


###### Isaiah 22:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:15)


כֹּ֥ה אָמַ֛ר אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִ֖ה צְבָא֑וֹת לֶךְ־בֹּא֙ אֶל־הַסֹּכֵ֣ן הַזֶּ֔ה עַל־שֶׁבְנָ֖א אֲשֶׁ֥ר עַל־הַבָּֽיִת׃


Thus says the Lord God of hosts, Go, get thee to this steward, to Shevna who is over the house.


###### Isaiah 22:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:16)


מַה־לְּךָ֥ פֹה֙ וּמִ֣י לְךָ֣ פֹ֔ה כִּֽי־חָצַ֧בְתָּ לְּךָ֛ פֹּ֖ה קָ֑בֶר חֹצְבִ֤י מָרוֹם֙ קִבְר֔וֹ חֹקְקִ֥י בַסֶּ֖לַע מִשְׁכָּ֥ן לֽוֹ׃


Say to him, What hast thou here? and whom hast thou here, that thou hast hewn thee out a tomb here, hewing out a tomb on high, carving a habitation for himself in a rock?


###### Isaiah 22:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:17)


הִנֵּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ מְטַלְטֶלְךָ֔ טַלְטֵלָ֖ה גָּ֑בֶר וְעֹטְךָ֖ עָטֹֽה׃


Behold, the Lord will thrust thee about with a mighty throw, and will seize thee firmly.


###### Isaiah 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:18)


צָנ֤וֹף יִצְנׇפְךָ֙ צְנֵפָ֔ה כַּדּ֕וּר אֶל־אֶ֖רֶץ רַחֲבַ֣ת יָדָ֑יִם שָׁ֣מָּה תָמ֗וּת וְשָׁ֙מָּה֙ מַרְכְּב֣וֹת כְּבוֹדֶ֔ךָ קְל֖וֹן בֵּ֥ית אֲדֹנֶֽיךָ׃


He will violently roll and toss thee like a ball into a large country: there shalt thou die, and there shall be the chariots of thy glory, O shame of thy master’s house.


###### Isaiah 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:19)


וַהֲדַפְתִּ֖יךָ מִמַּצָּבֶ֑ךָ וּמִמַּעֲמָֽדְךָ֖ יֶהֶרְסֶֽךָ׃


And I will drive thee from thy station, and from thy state shall he pull thee down.


###### Isaiah 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:20)


וְהָיָ֖ה בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֑וּא וְקָרָ֣אתִי לְעַבְדִּ֔י לְאֶלְיָקִ֖ים בֶּן־חִלְקִיָּֽהוּ׃


And it shall come to pass on that day, that I will call my servant Elyaqim the son of Ḥilqiyyahu.


###### Isaiah 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:21)


וְהִלְבַּשְׁתִּ֣יו כֻּתׇּנְתֶּ֗ךָ וְאַבְנֵֽטְךָ֙ אֲחַזְּקֶ֔נּוּ וּמֶֽמְשַׁלְתְּךָ֖ אֶתֵּ֣ן בְּיָד֑וֹ וְהָיָ֥ה לְאָ֛ב לְיוֹשֵׁ֥ב יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וּלְבֵ֥ית יְהוּדָֽה׃


And I will clothe him with thy robe, and strengthen him with thy girdle, and I will commit thy government into his hand: and he shall be a father to the inhabitants of Yerushalayim and to the house of Yehuda.


###### Isaiah 22:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:22)


וְנָתַתִּ֛י מַפְתֵּ֥חַ בֵּית־דָּוִ֖ד עַל־שִׁכְמ֑וֹ וּפָתַח֙ וְאֵ֣ין סֹגֵ֔ר וְסָגַ֖ר וְאֵ֥ין פֹּתֵֽחַ׃


And the key of the house of David will I lay upon his shoulder; so he shall open, and none shall shut: and he shall shut, and none shall open.


###### Isaiah 22:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:23)


וּתְקַעְתִּ֥יו יָתֵ֖ד בְּמָק֣וֹם נֶאֱמָ֑ן וְהָיָ֛ה לְכִסֵּ֥א כָב֖וֹד לְבֵ֥ית אָבִֽיו׃


And I will fasten him as a tent peg in a sure place; and he shall be for a glorious throne to his father’s house.


###### Isaiah 22:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:24)


וְתָל֨וּ עָלָ֜יו כֹּ֣ל**׀** כְּב֣וֹד בֵּית־אָבִ֗יו הַצֶּֽאֱצָאִים֙ וְהַצְּפִע֔וֹת כֹּ֖ל כְּלֵ֣י הַקָּטָ֑ן מִכְּלֵי֙ הָאַגָּנ֔וֹת וְעַ֖ד כׇּל־כְּלֵ֥י הַנְּבָלִֽים׃


And they shall hang upon him all the glory of his father’s house, the offspring and the issue, all vessels of small quantity, from the vessels of cups, even to all the vessels of flagons.


###### Isaiah 22:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/22:25)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא נְאֻם֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת תָּמוּשׁ֙ הַיָּתֵ֔ד הַתְּקוּעָ֖ה בְּמָק֣וֹם נֶאֱמָ֑ן וְנִגְדְּעָ֣ה וְנָפְלָ֗ה וְנִכְרַת֙ הַמַּשָּׂ֣א אֲשֶׁר־עָלֶ֔יהָ כִּ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבֵּֽר׃{פ}


On that day, says the Lord of hosts, shall the tent peg that is fastened in the sure place be removed, and be cut down, and fall; and the burden that was upon it shall be cut off: for the Lord has spoken it.



## Chapter 23

###### Isaiah 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:1)


מַשָּׂ֖א צֹ֑ר הֵילִ֣ילוּ**׀** אֳנִיּ֣וֹת תַּרְשִׁ֗ישׁ כִּֽי־שֻׁדַּ֤ד מִבַּ֙יִת֙ מִבּ֔וֹא מֵאֶ֥רֶץ כִּתִּ֖ים נִגְלָה־לָֽמוֹ׃


The burden of Żor. Howl, O ships of Tarshish; for it is laid waste, so that there is no house, no entering in: from the land of Kittim it is revealed to them.


###### Isaiah 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:2)


דֹּ֖מּוּ יֹ֣שְׁבֵי אִ֑י סֹחֵ֥ר צִיד֛וֹן עֹבֵ֥ר יָ֖ם מִלְאֽוּךְ׃


Be still, O inhabitants of the coastland; thou whom the merchants of Żidon, that pass over the sea, have replenished.


###### Isaiah 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:3)


וּבְמַ֤יִם רַבִּים֙ זֶ֣רַע שִׁחֹ֔ר קְצִ֥יר יְא֖וֹר תְּבוּאָתָ֑הּ וַתְּהִ֖י סְחַ֥ר גּוֹיִֽם׃


And by great waters the seed of Shiĥor, the harvest of the River, is her revenue; and she is a mart of nations.


###### Isaiah 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:4)


בּ֣וֹשִׁי צִיד֔וֹן כִּֽי־אָמַ֣ר יָ֔ם מָע֥וֹז הַיָּ֖ם לֵאמֹ֑ר לֹא־חַ֣לְתִּי וְלֹֽא־יָלַ֗דְתִּי וְלֹ֥א גִדַּ֛לְתִּי בַּחוּרִ֖ים רוֹמַ֥מְתִּי בְתוּלֽוֹת׃


Be thou ashamed, O Żidon: for the sea has spoken, even the strength of the sea, saying, I have not travailed, nor brought forth children, nor did I nourish up young men, nor bring up virgins.


###### Isaiah 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:5)


כַּאֲשֶׁר־שֵׁ֖מַע לְמִצְרָ֑יִם יָחִ֖ילוּ כְּשֵׁ֥מַע צֹֽר׃


When the report comes to Miżrayim, they shall tremble at the report of Żor.


###### Isaiah 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:6)


עִבְר֖וּ תַּרְשִׁ֑ישָׁה הֵילִ֖ילוּ יֹ֥שְׁבֵי אִֽי׃


Pass over to Tarshish; howl, inhabitants of the coastland.


###### Isaiah 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:7)


הֲזֹ֥את לָכֶ֖ם עַלִּיזָ֑ה מִֽימֵי־קֶ֤דֶם קַדְמָתָהּ֙ יֹבִל֣וּהָ רַגְלֶ֔יהָ מֵרָח֖וֹק לָגֽוּר׃


Is this your joyous city, whose antiquity is of ancient days? her own feet shall carry her afar off to sojourn.


###### Isaiah 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:8)


מִ֚י יָעַ֣ץ זֹ֔את עַל־צֹ֖ר הַמַּעֲטִירָ֑ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר סֹחֲרֶ֙יהָ֙ שָׂרִ֔ים כִּנְעָנֶ֖יהָ נִכְבַּדֵּי־אָֽרֶץ׃


Who has taken this counsel against Żor, the crowning city, whose merchants are princes, whose traffickers are the honourable men of the earth?


###### Isaiah 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:9)


יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת יְעָצָ֑הּ לְחַלֵּל֙ גְּא֣וֹן כׇּל־צְבִ֔י לְהָקֵ֖ל כׇּל־נִכְבַּדֵּי־אָֽרֶץ׃


The Lord of hosts has purposed it, to stain the pride of all glory, and to bring into contempt all the honourable of the earth.


###### Isaiah 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:10)


עִבְרִ֥י אַרְצֵ֖ךְ כַּיְאֹ֑ר בַּת־תַּרְשִׁ֕ישׁ אֵ֖ין מֵ֥זַח עֽוֹד׃


Overflow thy land like the River, O daughter of Tarshish: there is no wharf any more.


###### Isaiah 23:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:11)


יָדוֹ֙ נָטָ֣ה עַל־הַיָּ֔ם הִרְגִּ֖יז מַמְלָכ֑וֹת יְהֹוָה֙ צִוָּ֣ה אֶל־כְּנַ֔עַן לַשְׁמִ֖ד מָעֻזְנֶֽיהָ׃


He stretched out his hand over the sea, he shook the kingdoms: the Lord has given a commandment against the merchant city, to destroy its strongholds.


###### Isaiah 23:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:12)


וַיֹּ֕אמֶר לֹא־תוֹסִ֥יפִי ע֖וֹד לַעְל֑וֹז הַֽמְעֻשָּׁקָ֞ה בְּתוּלַ֣ת בַּת־צִיד֗וֹן (כתיים) [כִּתִּים֙] ק֣וּמִי עֲבֹ֔רִי גַּם־שָׁ֖ם לֹא־יָנ֥וּחַֽ לָֽךְ׃


And he said, Thou shalt no more rejoice, O thou oppressed virgin, daughter of Żidon: arise, pass over to Kittim; there also shalt thou have no rest.


###### Isaiah 23:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:13)


הֵ֣ן**׀** אֶ֣רֶץ כַּשְׂדִּ֗ים זֶ֤ה הָעָם֙ לֹ֣א הָיָ֔ה אַשּׁ֖וּר יְסָדָ֣הּ לְצִיִּ֑ים הֵקִ֣ימוּ (בחיניו) [בַחוּנָ֗יו] עֽוֹרְרוּ֙ אַרְמְנוֹתֶ֔יהָ שָׂמָ֖הּ לְמַפֵּלָֽה׃


(Behold the land of Kasdim; this people was not till Ashshur founded it for desert dwellers; they set up its towers, they destroyed its palaces; they have brought it to ruin.)


###### Isaiah 23:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:14)


הֵילִ֖ילוּ אֳנִיּ֣וֹת תַּרְשִׁ֑ישׁ כִּ֥י שֻׁדַּ֖ד מָעֻזְּכֶֽן׃{ס}


Howl, O ships of Tarshish: for your strength is laid waste.


###### Isaiah 23:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:15)


וְהָיָה֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא וְנִשְׁכַּ֤חַת צֹר֙ שִׁבְעִ֣ים שָׁנָ֔ה כִּימֵ֖י מֶ֣לֶךְ אֶחָ֑ד מִקֵּ֞ץ שִׁבְעִ֤ים שָׁנָה֙ יִהְיֶ֣ה לְצֹ֔ר כְּשִׁירַ֖ת הַזּוֹנָֽה׃


And it shall come to pass on that day, that Żor shall be forgotten seventy years, according to the days of one king: after the end of years, according to the days of one king: after the end of seventy years, it shall fare with Żor as in the song of the harlot.


###### Isaiah 23:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:16)


קְחִ֥י כִנּ֛וֹר סֹ֥בִּי עִ֖יר זוֹנָ֣ה נִשְׁכָּחָ֑ה הֵיטִ֤יבִי נַגֵּן֙ הַרְבִּי־שִׁ֔יר לְמַ֖עַן תִּזָּכֵֽרִי׃


Take a lyre, go about the city, thou harlot that hast been forgotten; make sweet melody, sing many songs, that thou mayst be remembered.


###### Isaiah 23:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:17)


וְהָיָ֞ה מִקֵּ֣ץ**׀** שִׁבְעִ֣ים שָׁנָ֗ה יִפְקֹ֤ד יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־צֹ֔ר וְשָׁבָ֖ה לְאֶתְנַנָּ֑הֿ וְזָ֥נְתָ֛ה אֶת־כׇּל־מַמְלְכ֥וֹת הָאָ֖רֶץ עַל־פְּנֵ֥י הָאֲדָמָֽה׃


And it shall come to pass after the end of seventy years, that the Lord will visit Żor, and she shall turn to her hire, and shall play the harlot with all the kingdoms of the world up­­on the face of the earth.


###### Isaiah 23:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/23:18)


וְהָיָ֨ה סַחְרָ֜הּ וְאֶתְנַנָּ֗הּ קֹ֚דֶשׁ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה לֹ֥א יֵאָצֵ֖ר וְלֹ֣א יֵחָסֵ֑ן כִּ֣י לַיֹּשְׁבִ֞ים לִפְנֵ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ יִהְיֶ֣ה סַחְרָ֔הּ לֶאֱכֹ֥ל לְשׇׂבְעָ֖ה וְלִמְכַסֶּ֥ה עָתִֽיק׃{פ}


And her merchandise and her hire shall be holiness to the Lord: it shall not be treasured nor laid up; for her gain shall be for them that dwell before the Lord, to eat sufficiently, and for stately clothing.



## Chapter 24

###### Isaiah 24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:1)


הִנֵּ֧ה יְהֹוָ֛ה בּוֹקֵ֥ק הָאָ֖רֶץ וּבֽוֹלְקָ֑הּ וְעִוָּ֣ה פָנֶ֔יהָ וְהֵפִ֖יץ יֹשְׁבֶֽיהָ׃


Behold, the Lord makes the earth empty, and makes it waste, and turns it upside down, and scatters abroad its inhabitants.


###### Isaiah 24:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:2)


וְהָיָ֤ה כָעָם֙ כַּכֹּהֵ֔ן כַּעֶ֙בֶד֙ כַּֽאדֹנָ֔יו כַּשִּׁפְחָ֖ה כַּגְּבִרְתָּ֑הּ כַּקּוֹנֶה֙ כַּמּוֹכֵ֔ר כַּמַּלְוֶה֙ כַּלֹּוֶ֔ה כַּנֹּשֶׁ֕ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֖ר נֹשֶׁ֥א בֽוֹ׃


And it shall be as with the people, so with the priest; as with the servant, so with his master; as with the maid, so with her mistress; as with the buyer, so with the seller; as with the lender, so with the borrower, as with the creditor, so with the debtor.


###### Isaiah 24:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:3)


הִבּ֧וֹק׀תִּבּ֛וֹק הָאָ֖רֶץ וְהִבּ֣וֹז׀תִּבֹּ֑ז כִּ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה דִּבֶּ֖ר אֶת־הַדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּֽה׃


The land shall be utterly emptied, and utterly spoiled: for the Lord has spoken this word.


###### Isaiah 24:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:4)


אָבְלָ֤ה נָֽבְלָה֙ הָאָ֔רֶץ אֻמְלְלָ֥ה נָבְלָ֖ה תֵּבֵ֑ל אֻמְלָ֖לוּ מְר֥וֹם עַם־הָאָֽרֶץ׃


The earth mourns and fades away, the world languishes and fades away, the lofty people of the earth languish.


###### Isaiah 24:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:5)


וְהָאָ֥רֶץ חָנְפָ֖ה תַּ֣חַת יֹשְׁבֶ֑יהָ כִּֽי־עָבְר֤וּ תוֹרֹת֙ חָ֣לְפוּ חֹ֔ק הֵפֵ֖רוּ בְּרִ֥ית עוֹלָֽם׃


The earth also is defiled under its inhabitants; because they have transgressed the Torot, changed the ordinance, broken the everlasting covenant.


###### Isaiah 24:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:6)


עַל־כֵּ֗ן אָלָה֙ אָ֣כְלָה אֶ֔רֶץ וַֽיֶּאְשְׁמ֖וּ יֹ֣שְׁבֵי בָ֑הּ עַל־כֵּ֗ן חָרוּ֙ יֹ֣שְׁבֵי אֶ֔רֶץ וְנִשְׁאַ֥ר אֱנ֖וֹשׁ מִזְעָֽר׃


Therefore has a curse devoured the earth, and they that dwell therein are found guilty: therefore the inhabitants of the earth are parched, and few men left.


###### Isaiah 24:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:7)


אָבַ֥ל תִּיר֖וֹשׁ אֻמְלְלָה־גָ֑פֶן נֶאֶנְח֖וּ כׇּל־שִׂמְחֵי־לֵֽב׃


The new wine mourns, the vine languishes, all the merryhearted do sigh.


###### Isaiah 24:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:8)


שָׁבַת֙ מְשׂ֣וֹשׂ תֻּפִּ֔ים חָדַ֖ל שְׁא֣וֹן עַלִּיזִ֑ים שָׁבַ֖ת מְשׂ֥וֹשׂ כִּנּֽוֹר׃


The mirth of tambourines ceases, the noise of them that rejoice ends, the joy of the lyre is stilled.


###### Isaiah 24:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:9)


בַּשִּׁ֖יר לֹ֣א יִשְׁתּוּ־יָ֑יִן יֵמַ֥ר שֵׁכָ֖ר לְשֹׁתָֽיו׃


They shall not drink wine with a song; strong drink shall be bitter to them that drink it.


###### Isaiah 24:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:10)


נִשְׁבְּרָ֖ה קִרְיַת־תֹּ֑הוּ סֻגַּ֥ר כׇּל־בַּ֖יִת מִבּֽוֹא׃


The city of confusion is broken down: every house is shut up, that no man may come in.


###### Isaiah 24:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:11)


צְוָחָ֥ה עַל־הַיַּ֖יִן בַּחוּצ֑וֹת עָֽרְבָה֙ כׇּל־שִׂמְחָ֔ה גָּלָ֖ה מְשׂ֥וֹשׂ הָאָֽרֶץ׃


There is a crying for wine in the streets; all joy is darkened, the mirth of the land is gone.


###### Isaiah 24:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:12)


נִשְׁאַ֥ר בָּעִ֖יר שַׁמָּ֑ה וּשְׁאִיָּ֖ה יֻכַּת־שָֽׁעַר׃


In the city is left desolation, and the gate is smitten with destruction.


###### Isaiah 24:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:13)


כִּ֣י כֹ֥ה יִֽהְיֶ֛ה בְּקֶ֥רֶב הָאָ֖רֶץ בְּת֣וֹךְ הָעַמִּ֑ים כְּנֹ֣קֶף זַ֔יִת כְּעוֹלֵלֹ֖ת אִם־כָּלָ֥ה בָצִֽיר׃


For thus it shall be in the midst of the land among the people, even as the beating of an olive tree, and as the gleaning grapes when the vintage is done.


###### Isaiah 24:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:14)


הֵ֛מָּה יִשְׂא֥וּ קוֹלָ֖ם יָרֹ֑נּוּ בִּגְא֣וֹן יְהֹוָ֔ה צָהֲל֖וּ מִיָּֽם׃


They shall lift up their voice, they shall sing; for the majesty of the Lord shall they shout more than at the sea.


###### Isaiah 24:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:15)


עַל־כֵּ֥ן בָּאֻרִ֖ים כַּבְּד֣וּ יְהֹוָ֑ה בְּאִיֵּ֣י הַיָּ֔ם שֵׁ֥ם יְהֹוָ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


Wherefore glorify the Lord in the regions of light, even the name of the Lord God of Yisra᾽el in the isles of the sea.


###### Isaiah 24:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:16)


מִכְּנַ֨ף הָאָ֜רֶץ זְמִרֹ֤ת שָׁמַ֙עְנוּ֙ צְבִ֣י לַצַּדִּ֔יק וָאֹמַ֛ר רָזִי־לִ֥י רָֽזִי־לִ֖י א֣וֹי לִ֑י בֹּגְדִ֣ים בָּגָ֔דוּ וּבֶ֥גֶד בּוֹגְדִ֖ים בָּגָֽדוּ׃


From the uttermost part of the earth have we heard songs, glory to the righteous. But I said, My leanness, my leanness, woe to me! traitors have dealt treacherously; traitors have dealt very treacherously.


###### Isaiah 24:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:17)


פַּ֥חַד וָפַ֖חַת וָפָ֑ח עָלֶ֖יךָ יוֹשֵׁ֥ב הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Fear, and the pit, and the trap, are upon thee, O inhabitant of the land.


###### Isaiah 24:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:18)


וְ֠הָיָ֠ה הַנָּ֞ס מִקּ֤וֹל הַפַּ֙חַד֙ יִפֹּ֣ל אֶל־הַפַּ֔חַת וְהָֽעוֹלֶה֙ מִתּ֣וֹךְ הַפַּ֔חַת יִלָּכֵ֖ד בַּפָּ֑ח כִּֽי־אֲרֻבּ֤וֹת מִמָּרוֹם֙ נִפְתָּ֔חוּ וַֽיִּרְעֲשׁ֖וּ מ֥וֹסְדֵי אָֽרֶץ׃


And it shall come to pass, that he who flees from the noise of the fear shall fall into the pit; and he that comes up out of the midst of the pit shall be taken in the trap: for the windows from on high are open, and the foundations of the earth do shake.


###### Isaiah 24:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:19)


רֹ֥עָה הִֽתְרֹעֲעָ֖ה הָאָ֑רֶץ פּ֤וֹר הִֽתְפּוֹרְרָה֙ אֶ֔רֶץ מ֥וֹט הִֽתְמוֹטְטָ֖ה אָֽרֶץ׃


The earth is utterly broken down, the earth crumbles away, the earth is violently shaken.


###### Isaiah 24:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:20)


נ֣וֹעַ תָּנ֤וּעַ אֶ֙רֶץ֙ כַּשִּׁכּ֔וֹר וְהִֽתְנוֹדְדָ֖ה כַּמְּלוּנָ֑ה וְכָבַ֤ד עָלֶ֙יהָ֙ פִּשְׁעָ֔הּ וְנָפְלָ֖ה וְלֹא־תֹסִ֥יף קֽוּם׃{ס}


The earth shall reel to and fro like a drunkard, and shall be removed like a frail hut; and its transgression is heavy upon it; and it shall fall, and not rise again.


###### Isaiah 24:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:21)


וְהָיָה֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא יִפְקֹ֧ד יְהֹוָ֛ה עַל־צְבָ֥א הַמָּר֖וֹם בַּמָּר֑וֹם וְעַל־מַלְכֵ֥י הָאֲדָמָ֖ה עַל־הָאֲדָמָֽה׃


And it shall come to pass on that day, that the Lord shall punish the host of the high ones on high, and the kings of the earth upon the earth.


###### Isaiah 24:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:22)


וְאֻסְּפ֨וּ אֲסֵפָ֤ה אַסִּיר֙ עַל־בּ֔וֹר וְסֻגְּר֖וּ עַל־מַסְגֵּ֑ר וּמֵרֹ֥ב יָמִ֖ים יִפָּקֵֽדוּ׃


And they shall be gathered together, as prisoners are gathered in the pit, and shall be shut up in the prison, and after many days shall they be punished.


###### Isaiah 24:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/24:23)


וְחָֽפְרָה֙ הַלְּבָנָ֔ה וּבוֹשָׁ֖ה הַחַמָּ֑ה כִּֽי־מָלַ֞ךְ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֗וֹת בְּהַ֤ר צִיּוֹן֙ וּבִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם וְנֶ֥גֶד זְקֵנָ֖יו כָּבֽוֹד׃{פ}


Then the moon shall be confounded, and the sun ashamed, when the Lord of hosts shall reign in mount Żiyyon, and in Yerushalayim, and before his elders will be his glory.



## Chapter 25

###### Isaiah 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:1)


יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהַי֙ אַתָּ֔ה אֲרֽוֹמִמְךָ֙ אוֹדֶ֣ה שִׁמְךָ֔ כִּ֥י עָשִׂ֖יתָ פֶּ֑לֶא עֵצ֥וֹת מֵרָחֹ֖ק אֱמ֥וּנָה אֹֽמֶן׃


O Lord, Thou art my God; I will exalt Thee, I will praise Thy name; for Thou hast done wonderful things – counsels of old in faithfulness and truth.


###### Isaiah 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:2)


כִּ֣י שַׂ֤מְתָּ מֵעִיר֙ לַגָּ֔ל קִרְיָ֥ה בְצוּרָ֖ה לְמַפֵּלָ֑ה אַרְמ֤וֹן זָרִים֙ מֵעִ֔יר לְעוֹלָ֖ם לֹ֥א יִבָּנֶֽה׃


For thou hast made of a city a heap; of a fortified city a ruin: a palace of strangers to be no city; it shall never be built.


###### Isaiah 25:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:3)


עַל־כֵּ֖ן יְכַבְּד֣וּךָ עַם־עָ֑ז קִרְיַ֛ת גּוֹיִ֥ם עָרִיצִ֖ים יִירָאֽוּךָ׃


Therefore shall the strong people glorify Thee, the city of the terrible nations shall fear Thee.


###### Isaiah 25:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:4)


כִּי־הָיִ֨יתָ מָע֥וֹז לַדָּ֛ל מָע֥וֹז לָאֶבְי֖וֹן בַּצַּר־ל֑וֹ מַחְסֶ֤ה מִזֶּ֙רֶם֙ צֵ֣ל מֵחֹ֔רֶב כִּ֛י ר֥וּחַ עָרִיצִ֖ים כְּזֶ֥רֶם קִֽיר׃


For Thou hast been a strength to the poor, a strength to the needy in his distress, a refuge from the storm, a shadow from the heat, when the blast of tyrants was like a storm against the wall.


###### Isaiah 25:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:5)


כְּחֹ֣רֶב בְּצָי֔וֹן שְׁא֥וֹן זָרִ֖ים תַּכְנִ֑יעַ חֹ֚רֶב בְּצֵ֣ל עָ֔ב זְמִ֥יר עָרִיצִ֖ים יַעֲנֶֽה׃{פ}


Thou shalt bring down the noise of strangers, as the heat in a dry place; as the heat is removed by the shadow of a cloud, so the song of tyrants shall be brought low.


###### Isaiah 25:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:6)


וְעָשָׂה֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה צְבָא֜וֹת לְכׇל־הָֽעַמִּים֙ בָּהָ֣ר הַזֶּ֔ה מִשְׁתֵּ֥ה שְׁמָנִ֖ים מִשְׁתֵּ֣ה שְׁמָרִ֑ים שְׁמָנִים֙ מְמֻ֣חָיִ֔ם שְׁמָרִ֖ים מְזֻקָּקִֽים׃


And in this mountain shall the Lord of hosts make to all the peoples a feast of fat things, a feast of wines on the lees, of fat things full of marrow, of wines on the lees well refined.


###### Isaiah 25:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:7)


וּבִלַּע֙ בָּהָ֣ר הַזֶּ֔ה פְּנֵֽי־הַלּ֥וֹט׀הַלּ֖וֹט עַל־כׇּל־הָעַמִּ֑ים וְהַמַּסֵּכָ֥ה הַנְּסוּכָ֖ה עַל־כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִֽם׃


And he will destroy in this mountain the covering that is cast over all the people, and the veil that is spread over all the nations.


###### Isaiah 25:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:8)


בִּלַּ֤ע הַמָּ֙וֶת֙ לָנֶ֔צַח וּמָחָ֨ה אֲדֹנָ֧י יֱהֹוִ֛ה דִּמְעָ֖ה מֵעַ֣ל כׇּל־פָּנִ֑ים וְחֶרְפַּ֣ת עַמּ֗וֹ יָסִיר֙ מֵעַ֣ל כׇּל־הָאָ֔רֶץ כִּ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבֵּֽר׃{פ}


He will destroy death for ever; and the Lord God will wipe away tears from off all faces; and the insult of his people shall he take away from off all the earth: for the Lord has spoken it.


###### Isaiah 25:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:9)


וְאָמַר֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא הִנֵּ֨ה אֱלֹהֵ֥ינוּ זֶ֛ה קִוִּ֥ינוּ ל֖וֹ וְיוֹשִׁיעֵ֑נוּ זֶ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ קִוִּ֣ינוּ ל֔וֹ נָגִ֥ילָה וְנִשְׂמְחָ֖ה בִּישׁוּעָתֽוֹ׃


And it shall be said on that day, Lo, this is our God; we have waited for him, that he should save us: this is the Lord; we have waited for him, we will be glad and rejoice in his salvation.


###### Isaiah 25:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:10)


כִּי־תָנ֥וּחַ יַד־יְהֹוָ֖ה בָּהָ֣ר הַזֶּ֑ה וְנָד֤וֹשׁ מוֹאָב֙ תַּחְתָּ֔יו כְּהִדּ֥וּשׁ מַתְבֵּ֖ן (במי) [בְּמ֥וֹ] מַדְמֵנָֽה׃


For in this mountain shall the hand of the Lord rest, and Mo᾽av shall be trodden down in his own place, even as straw is trodden down in the dunghill.


###### Isaiah 25:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:11)


וּפֵרַ֤שׂ יָדָיו֙ בְּקִרְבּ֔וֹ כַּאֲשֶׁ֛ר יְפָרֵ֥שׂ הַשֹּׂחֶ֖ה לִשְׂח֑וֹת וְהִשְׁפִּיל֙ גַּאֲוָת֔וֹ עִ֖ם אׇרְבּ֥וֹת יָדָֽיו׃


And he shall spread out his hands in the midst of it, as he that swims spreads out his hands to swim: and he shall bring down his pride together with the spoils of his hands.


###### Isaiah 25:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/25:12)


וּמִבְצַ֞ר מִשְׂגַּ֣ב חוֹמֹתֶ֗יךָ הֵשַׁ֥ח הִשְׁפִּ֛יל הִגִּ֥יעַ לָאָ֖רֶץ עַד־עָפָֽר׃{ס}


And the lofty fortress of thy walls shall he bring down, lay low, and bring to the ground, even to the dust.



## Chapter 26

###### Isaiah 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:1)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא יוּשַׁ֥ר הַשִּׁיר־הַזֶּ֖ה בְּאֶ֣רֶץ יְהוּדָ֑ה עִ֣יר עׇז־לָ֔נוּ יְשׁוּעָ֥ה יָשִׁ֖ית חוֹמ֥וֹת וָחֵֽל׃


On that day shall this song be sung in the land of Yehuda; We have a strong city; he sets up salvation as walls and bulwarks.


###### Isaiah 26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:2)


פִּתְח֖וּ שְׁעָרִ֑ים וְיָבֹ֥א גוֹי־צַדִּ֖יק שֹׁמֵ֥ר אֱמֻנִֽים׃


Open the gates, that the righteous nation that keeps faithfulness may enter in:


###### Isaiah 26:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:3)


יֵ֣צֶר סָמ֔וּךְ תִּצֹּ֖ר שָׁל֣וֹם׀שָׁל֑וֹם כִּ֥י בְךָ֖ בָּטֽוּחַ׃


Thou wilt keep him in perfect peace, whose mind is stayed on Thee: because he trusts in Thee.


###### Isaiah 26:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:4)


בִּטְח֥וּ בַיהֹוָ֖ה עֲדֵי־עַ֑ד כִּ֚י בְּיָ֣הּ יְהֹוָ֔ה צ֖וּר עוֹלָמִֽים׃


Trust in the Lord for ever: for the Lord God is an eternal Rock.


###### Isaiah 26:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:5)


כִּ֤י הֵשַׁח֙ יֹשְׁבֵ֣י מָר֔וֹם קִרְיָ֖ה נִשְׂגָּבָ֑ה יַשְׁפִּילֶ֤נָּה יַשְׁפִּילָהּ֙ עַד־אֶ֔רֶץ יַגִּיעֶ֖נָּה עַד־עָפָֽר׃


For he brings down them that dwell on high; the lofty city, he lays it low; he lays it low, even to the ground; he brings it to the dust.


###### Isaiah 26:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:6)


תִּרְמְסֶ֖נָּה רָ֑גֶל רַגְלֵ֥י עָנִ֖י פַּעֲמֵ֥י דַלִּֽים׃


The foot shall trample it down, even the feet of the poor, and the steps of the needy.


###### Isaiah 26:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:7)


אֹ֥רַח לַצַּדִּ֖יק מֵישָׁרִ֑ים יָשָׁ֕ר מַעְגַּ֥ל צַדִּ֖יק תְּפַלֵּֽס׃


The way of the just is uprightness: Thou, most upright, dost make level the path of the just.


###### Isaiah 26:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:8)


אַ֣ף אֹ֧רַח מִשְׁפָּטֶ֛יךָ יְהֹוָ֖ה קִוִּינ֑וּךָ לְשִׁמְךָ֥ וּלְזִכְרְךָ֖ תַּאֲוַת־נָֽפֶשׁ׃


Indeed, in the way of Thy judgments, O Lord, have we waited for Thee; the desire of our soul is to Thy name, and to the remembrance of Thee.


###### Isaiah 26:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:9)


נַפְשִׁ֤י אִוִּיתִ֙ךָ֙ בַּלַּ֔יְלָה אַף־רוּחִ֥י בְקִרְבִּ֖י אֲשַׁחֲרֶ֑ךָּ כִּ֞י כַּאֲשֶׁ֤ר מִשְׁפָּטֶ֙יךָ֙ לָאָ֔רֶץ צֶ֥דֶק לָמְד֖וּ יֹשְׁבֵ֥י תֵבֵֽל׃


With my soul have I desired Thee in the night; with my spirit within me I seek Thee: for when Thy judgments are on the earth, the inhabitants of the world learn righteousness.


###### Isaiah 26:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:10)


יֻחַ֤ן רָשָׁע֙ בַּל־לָמַ֣ד צֶ֔דֶק בְּאֶ֥רֶץ נְכֹח֖וֹת יְעַוֵּ֑ל וּבַל־יִרְאֶ֖ה גֵּא֥וּת יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


Let favour be shown to the wicked, yet will he not learn righteousness: in the land of uprightness will he deal unjustly, and will not behold the majesty of the Lord.


###### Isaiah 26:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:11)


יְהֹוָ֛ה רָ֥מָה יָדְךָ֖ בַּל־יֶחֱזָי֑וּן יֶחֱז֤וּ וְיֵבֹ֙שׁוּ֙ קִנְאַת־עָ֔ם אַף־אֵ֖שׁ צָרֶ֥יךָ תֹאכְלֵֽם׃{ס}


Lord, when Thy hand is lifted up, they will not see; but they shall see with shame Thy zeal for the people, the fire which shall devour Thy enemies.


###### Isaiah 26:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:12)


יְהֹוָ֕ה תִּשְׁפֹּ֥ת שָׁל֖וֹם לָ֑נוּ כִּ֛י גַּ֥ם כׇּֽל־מַעֲשֵׂ֖ינוּ פָּעַ֥לְתָּ לָּֽנוּ׃{ס}


Lord, Thou wilt ordain peace for us: for Thou also hast performed all our works for us.


###### Isaiah 26:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:13)


יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ בְּעָל֥וּנוּ אֲדֹנִ֖ים זוּלָתֶ֑ךָ לְבַד־בְּךָ֖ נַזְכִּ֥יר שְׁמֶֽךָ׃


O Lord our God, other masters besides Thee have had dominion over us: but by Thee only will we make mention of Thy name.


###### Isaiah 26:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:14)


מֵתִים֙ בַּל־יִ֣חְי֔וּ רְפָאִ֖ים בַּל־יָקֻ֑מוּ לָכֵ֤ן פָּקַ֙דְתָּ֙ וַתַּשְׁמִידֵ֔ם וַתְּאַבֵּ֥ד כׇּל־זֵ֖כֶר לָֽמוֹ׃


They are dead, they shall not live; the shades of the dead shall not rise: therefore hast Thou visited and destroyed them, and made all their memory to perish.


###### Isaiah 26:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:15)


יָסַ֤פְתָּ לַגּוֹי֙ יְהֹוָ֔ה יָסַ֥פְתָּ לַגּ֖וֹי נִכְבָּ֑דְתָּ רִחַ֖קְתָּ כׇּל־קַצְוֵי־אָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


Thou hast increased the nation, O Lord, Thou hast increased the nation: Thou art glorified: Thou hast enlarged all the borders of the land.


###### Isaiah 26:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:16)


יְהֹוָ֖ה בַּצַּ֣ר פְּקָד֑וּךָ צָק֣וּן לַ֔חַשׁ מוּסָרְךָ֖ לָֽמוֹ׃


Lord, in trouble have they besought Thee, they poured out a silent prayer when Thy chastening was upon them.


###### Isaiah 26:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:17)


כְּמ֤וֹ הָרָה֙ תַּקְרִ֣יב לָלֶ֔דֶת תָּחִ֥יל תִּזְעַ֖ק בַּחֲבָלֶ֑יהָ כֵּ֛ן הָיִ֥ינוּ מִפָּנֶ֖יךָ יְהֹוָֽה׃


Like a woman with child, that draws near the time of her delivery, is in pain, and cries out in her pangs; so have we been in Thy sight, O Lord.


###### Isaiah 26:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:18)


הָרִ֣ינוּ חַ֔לְנוּ כְּמ֖וֹ יָלַ֣דְנוּ ר֑וּחַ יְשׁוּעֹת֙ בַּל־נַ֣עֲשֶׂה אֶ֔רֶץ וּבַֽל־יִפְּל֖וּ יֹשְׁבֵ֥י תֵבֵֽל׃


We have been with child, we have been in pain, we have as it were brought forth wind; we have not wrought any deliverance in the earth; and the inhabitants of the world have not fallen.


###### Isaiah 26:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:19)


יִֽחְי֣וּ מֵתֶ֔יךָ נְבֵלָתִ֖י יְקוּמ֑וּן הָקִ֨יצוּ וְרַנְּנ֜וּ שֹׁכְנֵ֣י עָפָ֗ר כִּ֣י טַ֤ל אוֹרֹת֙ טַלֶּ֔ךָ וָאָ֖רֶץ רְפָאִ֥ים תַּפִּֽיל׃{פ}


The dead men of Thy people shall live, my dead body shall arise. Awake and sing, you that dwell in dust: for Thy dew is as the dew on herbs, and the earth shall cast out the shades of the dead.


###### Isaiah 26:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:20)


לֵ֤ךְ עַמִּי֙ בֹּ֣א בַחֲדָרֶ֔יךָ וּֽסְגֹ֥ר (דלתיך) [דְּלָֽתְךָ֖] בַּעֲדֶ֑ךָ חֲבִ֥י כִמְעַט־רֶ֖גַע עַד־(יעבור)־[יַעֲבׇר־]זָֽעַם׃


Come, my people, enter thou into thy chambers, and shut thy doors about thee: hide thyself for a little moment, until the indignation be overpast.


###### Isaiah 26:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/26:21)


כִּֽי־הִנֵּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ יֹצֵ֣א מִמְּקוֹמ֔וֹ לִפְקֹ֛ד עֲוֺ֥ן יֹֽשֵׁב־הָאָ֖רֶץ עָלָ֑יו וְגִלְּתָ֤ה הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ אֶת־דָּמֶ֔יהָ וְלֹא־תְכַסֶּ֥ה ע֖וֹד עַל־הֲרוּגֶֽיהָ׃{פ}


For, behold, the Lord comes out of his place to punish the inhabitants of the earth for their iniquity: the earth also shall disclose her blood, and shall no more cover her slain.



## Chapter 27

###### Isaiah 27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:1)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֡וּא יִפְקֹ֣ד יְהֹוָה֩ בְּחַרְבּ֨וֹ הַקָּשָׁ֜ה וְהַגְּדוֹלָ֣ה וְהַחֲזָקָ֗ה עַ֤ל לִוְיָתָן֙ נָחָ֣שׁ בָּרִ֔חַ וְעַל֙ לִוְיָתָ֔ן נָחָ֖שׁ עֲקַלָּת֑וֹן וְהָרַ֥ג אֶת־הַתַּנִּ֖ין אֲשֶׁ֥ר בַּיָּֽם׃{ס}


On that day the Lord with his sore and great and strong sword shall punish Livyatan the flying serpent, and Livyatan that crooked serpent; and he shall slay the crocodile that is in the sea.


###### Isaiah 27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:2)


בַּיּ֖וֹם הַה֑וּא כֶּ֥רֶם חֶ֖מֶר עַנּוּ־לָֽהּ׃


On that day sing to her, A vineyard of foaming wine.


###### Isaiah 27:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:3)


אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ נֹֽצְרָ֔הּ לִרְגָעִ֖ים אַשְׁקֶ֑נָּה פֶּ֚ן יִפְקֹ֣ד עָלֶ֔יהָ לַ֥יְלָה וָי֖וֹם אֶצֳּרֶֽנָּה׃


I the Lord do keep it; I will water it every moment: lest any hurt it, I will keep it night and day.


###### Isaiah 27:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:4)


חֵמָ֖ה אֵ֣ין לִ֑י מִֽי־יִתְּנֵ֜נִי שָׁמִ֥יר שַׁ֙יִת֙ בַּמִּלְחָמָ֔ה אֶפְשְׂעָ֥ה בָ֖הּ אֲצִיתֶ֥נָּה יָּֽחַד׃


Fury is not in me: who would set the thorns and weeds against me in battle? I would go through them, I would burn them together.


###### Isaiah 27:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:5)


א֚וֹ יַחֲזֵ֣ק בְּמָעוּזִּ֔י יַעֲשֶׂ֥ה שָׁל֖וֹם לִ֑י שָׁל֖וֹם יַעֲשֶׂה־לִּֽי׃


Or let him take hold of my strength, that he may make peace with me; and he shall make peace with me.


###### Isaiah 27:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:6)


הַבָּאִים֙ יַשְׁרֵ֣שׁ יַֽעֲקֹ֔ב יָצִ֥יץ וּפָרַ֖ח יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל וּמָלְא֥וּ פְנֵי־תֵבֵ֖ל תְּנוּבָֽה׃{פ}


In days to come Ya῾aqov shall take root: Yisra᾽el shall blossom and bud, and the face of the world shall be filled with fruit.


###### Isaiah 27:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:7)


הַכְּמַכַּ֥ת מַכֵּ֖הוּ הִכָּ֑הוּ אִם־כְּהֶ֥רֶג הֲרֻגָ֖יו הֹרָֽג׃


Has he smitten him, as he smote those that smote him? or is he slain according to the slaughter of them that are slain by him?


###### Isaiah 27:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:8)


בְּסַאסְּאָ֖ה בְּשַׁלְחָ֣הּ תְּרִיבֶ֑נָּה הָגָ֛ה בְּרוּח֥וֹ הַקָּשָׁ֖ה בְּי֥וֹם קָדִֽים׃


By measure, by exile, thou didst contend with them; he removed her by his rough blast in the day of the east wind.


###### Isaiah 27:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:9)


לָכֵ֗ן בְּזֹאת֙ יְכֻפַּ֣ר עֲוֺֽן־יַעֲקֹ֔ב וְזֶ֕ה כׇּל־פְּרִ֖י הָסִ֣ר חַטָּאת֑וֹ בְּשׂוּמ֣וֹ**׀** כׇּל־אַבְנֵ֣י מִזְבֵּ֗חַ כְּאַבְנֵי־גִר֙ מְנֻפָּצ֔וֹת לֹא־יָקֻ֥מוּ אֲשֵׁרִ֖ים וְחַמָּנִֽים׃


By this therefore shall the iniquity of Ya῾aqov be atoned; and this is all the fruit to take away his sin; when he makes all the stones of the altar as chalkstones that are beaten in sunder, the asherim and sun images shall not remain standing.


###### Isaiah 27:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:10)


כִּ֣י עִ֤יר בְּצוּרָה֙ בָּדָ֔ד נָוֶ֕ה מְשֻׁלָּ֥ח וְנֶעֱזָ֖ב כַּמִּדְבָּ֑ר שָׁ֣ם יִרְעֶ֥ה עֵ֛גֶל וְשָׁ֥ם יִרְבָּ֖ץ וְכִלָּ֥ה סְעִפֶֽיהָ׃


Yet the fortified city shall be solitary, and the habitation forsaken, and left like a wilderness: there shall the calf feed, and there shall he lie down, and consume its branches.


###### Isaiah 27:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:11)


בִּיבֹ֤שׁ קְצִירָהּ֙ תִּשָּׁבַ֔רְנָה נָשִׁ֕ים בָּא֖וֹת מְאִיר֣וֹת אוֹתָ֑הּ כִּ֣י לֹ֤א עַם־בִּינוֹת֙ ה֔וּא עַל־כֵּן֙ לֹא־יְרַחֲמֶ֣נּוּ עֹשֵׂ֔הוּ וְיֹצְר֖וֹ לֹ֥א יְחֻנֶּֽנּוּ׃{פ}


When its boughs are withered, they shall be broken off: the women shall come, and set them on fire: for it is a people of no understanding: therefore he that made them will not have mercy on them, and he that formed them will show them no favour.


###### Isaiah 27:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:12)


וְהָיָה֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא יַחְבֹּ֧ט יְהֹוָ֛ה מִשִּׁבֹּ֥לֶת הַנָּהָ֖ר עַד־נַ֣חַל מִצְרָ֑יִם וְאַתֶּ֧ם תְּלֻקְּט֛וּ לְאַחַ֥ד אֶחָ֖ד בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{פ}


And it shall come to pass in that day, that the Lord shall beat out his harvest from the strongly flowing river as far as the wadi of Miżrayim, and you shall be gathered up one by one, O children of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 27:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/27:13)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא יִתָּקַע֮ בְּשׁוֹפָ֣ר גָּדוֹל֒ וּבָ֗אוּ הָאֹֽבְדִים֙ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ אַשּׁ֔וּר וְהַנִּדָּחִ֖ים בְּאֶ֣רֶץ מִצְרָ֑יִם וְהִשְׁתַּחֲו֧וּ לַיהֹוָ֛ה בְּהַ֥ר הַקֹּ֖דֶשׁ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{פ}


And it shall come to pass on that day, that a great shofar shall be blown, and they shall come who were lost in the land of Ashshur, and the outcasts in the land of Miżrayim, and shall worship the Lord in the holy mountain at Yerushalayim.



## Chapter 28

###### Isaiah 28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:1)


ה֗וֹי עֲטֶ֤רֶת גֵּאוּת֙ שִׁכֹּרֵ֣י אֶפְרַ֔יִם וְצִ֥יץ נֹבֵ֖ל צְבִ֣י תִפְאַרְתּ֑וֹ אֲשֶׁ֛ר עַל־רֹ֥אשׁ גֵּֽיא־שְׁמָנִ֖ים הֲל֥וּמֵי יָֽיִן׃


Woe to the crown of pride of the drunkards Efrayim, and to the fading flower of his glorious beauty which is at the head of the rich valley of those who are overcome with wine!


###### Isaiah 28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:2)


הִנֵּ֨ה חָזָ֤ק וְאַמִּץ֙ לַֽאדֹנָ֔י כְּזֶ֥רֶם בָּרָ֖ד שַׂ֣עַר קָ֑טֶב כְּ֠זֶ֠רֶם מַ֣יִם כַּבִּירִ֥ים שֹׁטְפִ֛ים הִנִּ֥יחַ לָאָ֖רֶץ בְּיָֽד׃


Behold, the Lord has one who is mighty and strong, as a tempest of hail and a destroying storm, as a flood of mighty waters overflowing; he will cast down to the earth with might.


###### Isaiah 28:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:3)


בְּרַגְלַ֖יִם תֵּרָמַ֑סְנָה עֲטֶ֥רֶת גֵּא֖וּת שִׁכּוֹרֵ֥י אֶפְרָֽיִם׃


The crown of pride of the drunkards of Efrayim shall be trodden under feet:


###### Isaiah 28:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:4)


וְֽהָ֨יְתָ֜ה צִיצַ֤ת נֹבֵל֙ צְבִ֣י תִפְאַרְתּ֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֥ר עַל־רֹ֖אשׁ גֵּ֣יא שְׁמָנִ֑ים כְּבִכּוּרָהּ֙ בְּטֶ֣רֶם קַ֔יִץ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִרְאֶ֤ה הָרֹאֶה֙ אוֹתָ֔הּ בְּעוֹדָ֥הּ בְּכַפּ֖וֹ יִבְלָעֶֽנָּה׃{ס}


and the fading flower of the glorious beauty, which is at the head of the rich valley, shall be as the first ripe fig before the summer; which when one sees, while it is yet in his hand he swallows it up.


###### Isaiah 28:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:5)


בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֗וּא יִֽהְיֶה֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת לַעֲטֶ֣רֶת צְבִ֔י וְלִצְפִירַ֖ת תִּפְאָרָ֑ה לִשְׁאָ֖ר עַמּֽוֹ׃


In that day shall the Lord of hosts be for a crown of glory, and for a diadem of beauty, to the residue of his people,


###### Isaiah 28:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:6)


וּלְר֖וּחַ מִשְׁפָּ֑ט לַיּוֹשֵׁב֙ עַל־הַמִּשְׁפָּ֔ט וְלִ֨גְבוּרָ֔ה מְשִׁיבֵ֥י מִלְחָמָ֖ה שָֽׁעְרָה׃{ס}


and for a spirit of judgment to him that sits in judgment, and for strength to them that turn back the battle to the gate.


###### Isaiah 28:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:7)


וְגַם־אֵ֙לֶּה֙ בַּיַּ֣יִן שָׁג֔וּ וּבַשֵּׁכָ֖ר תָּע֑וּ כֹּהֵ֣ן וְנָבִיא֩ שָׁג֨וּ בַשֵּׁכָ֜ר נִבְלְע֣וּ מִן־הַיַּ֗יִן תָּעוּ֙ מִן־הַשֵּׁכָ֔ר שָׁגוּ֙ בָּרֹאֶ֔ה פָּק֖וּ פְּלִילִיָּֽה׃


But they also reel through wine, and stagger through strong drink; the priest and the prophet reel through strong drink, they are confused by wine, they stagger through strong drink; they err in vision, they stumble in judgment.


###### Isaiah 28:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:8)


כִּ֚י כׇּל־שֻׁלְחָנ֔וֹת מָלְא֖וּ קִ֣יא צֹאָ֑ה בְּלִ֖י מָקֽוֹם׃{פ}


For all tables are full of vomit and filth, so that there is no place clean.


###### Isaiah 28:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:9)


אֶת־מִי֙ יוֹרֶ֣ה דֵעָ֔ה וְאֶת־מִ֖י יָבִ֣ין שְׁמוּעָ֑ה גְּמוּלֵי֙ מֵחָלָ֔ב עַתִּיקֵ֖י מִשָּׁדָֽיִם׃


Whom shall one teach knowledge? and whom shall one make to understand doctrine? those that are weaned from the milk, and removed from the breasts?


###### Isaiah 28:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:10)


כִּ֣י צַ֤ו לָצָו֙ צַ֣ו לָצָ֔ו קַ֥ו לָקָ֖ו קַ֣ו לָקָ֑ו זְעֵ֥יר שָׁ֖ם זְעֵ֥יר שָֽׁם׃


For it is precept upon precept, precept upon precept; line upon line, line upon line; here a little, and there a little:


###### Isaiah 28:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:11)


כִּ֚י בְּלַעֲגֵ֣י שָׂפָ֔ה וּבְלָשׁ֖וֹן אַחֶ֑רֶת יְדַבֵּ֖ר אֶל־הָעָ֥ם הַזֶּֽה׃


for with stammering lips and another tongue shall one speak to this people.


###### Isaiah 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:12)


אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** אָמַ֣ר אֲלֵיהֶ֗ם זֹ֤את הַמְּנוּחָה֙ הָנִ֣יחוּ לֶעָיֵ֔ף וְזֹ֖את הַמַּרְגֵּעָ֑ה וְלֹ֥א אָב֖וּא שְׁמֽוֹעַ׃


When he said to them, This is the rest; cause the weary to rest and this is the refreshing: they would not hear.


###### Isaiah 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:13)


וְהָיָ֨ה לָהֶ֜ם דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֗ה צַ֣ו לָצָ֞ו צַ֤ו לָצָו֙ קַ֤ו לָקָו֙ קַ֣ו לָקָ֔ו זְעֵ֥יר שָׁ֖ם זְעֵ֣יר שָׁ֑ם לְמַ֨עַן יֵלְכ֜וּ וְכָשְׁל֤וּ אָחוֹר֙ וְנִשְׁבָּ֔רוּ וְנוֹקְשׁ֖וּ וְנִלְכָּֽדוּ׃{פ}


Therefore the word of the Lord was to them precept upon precept, precept upon precept; line upon line, line upon line; here a little, and there a little; that they might go, and fall backward, and be broken, and snared, and taken.


###### Isaiah 28:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:14)


לָכֵ֛ן שִׁמְע֥וּ דְבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אַנְשֵׁ֣י לָצ֑וֹן מֹֽשְׁלֵי֙ הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֖ר בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Wherefore hear the word of the Lord, scornful men, that rule this people which is in Yerushalayim.


###### Isaiah 28:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:15)


כִּ֣י אֲמַרְתֶּ֗ם כָּרַ֤תְנֽוּ בְרִית֙ אֶת־מָ֔וֶת וְעִם־שְׁא֖וֹל עָשִׂ֣ינוּ חֹזֶ֑ה (שיט) [שׁ֣וֹט] שׁוֹטֵ֤ף כִּי־[יַֽעֲבֹר֙] (עבר) לֹ֣א יְבוֹאֵ֔נוּ כִּ֣י שַׂ֧מְנוּ כָזָ֛ב מַחְסֵ֖נוּ וּבַשֶּׁ֥קֶר נִסְתָּֽרְנוּ׃{פ}


Because you have said, We have made a covenant with death, and with She᾽ol are we at agreement; when the overflowing scourge shall pass through, it shall not come to us: for we have made lies our refuge, and under falsehood have we hid ourselves:


###### Isaiah 28:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:16)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה הִנְנִ֛י יִסַּ֥ד בְּצִיּ֖וֹן אָ֑בֶן אֶ֣בֶן בֹּ֜חַן פִּנַּ֤ת יִקְרַת֙ מוּסָ֣ד מוּסָּ֔ד הַֽמַּאֲמִ֖ין לֹ֥א יָחִֽישׁ׃


therefore thus says the Lord God, Behold, I lay in Żiyyon for a foundation a stone, a tried stone, a precious corner stone, a sure foundation: he that believes shall not make haste.


###### Isaiah 28:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:17)


וְשַׂמְתִּ֤י מִשְׁפָּט֙ לְקָ֔ו וּצְדָקָ֖ה לְמִשְׁקָ֑לֶת וְיָעָ֤ה בָרָד֙ מַחְסֵ֣ה כָזָ֔ב וְסֵ֥תֶר מַ֖יִם יִשְׁטֹֽפוּ׃{ס}


Judgment also will I lay by a line, and righteousness by a plummet: and the hail shall sweep away the refuge of lies, and waters shall overflow the hiding place.


###### Isaiah 28:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:18)


וְכֻפַּ֤ר בְּרִֽיתְכֶם֙ אֶת־מָ֔וֶת וְחָזוּתְכֶ֥ם אֶת־שְׁא֖וֹל לֹ֣א תָק֑וּם שׁ֤וֹט שׁוֹטֵף֙ כִּ֣י יַֽעֲבֹ֔ר וִהְיִ֥יתֶם ל֖וֹ לְמִרְמָֽס׃


And your covenant with death shall be annulled, and your agreement with She᾽ol shall not stand; when the overflowing scourge shall pass through, then you shall be trodden down by it.


###### Isaiah 28:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:19)


מִדֵּ֤י עׇבְרוֹ֙ יִקַּ֣ח אֶתְכֶ֔ם כִּֽי־בַבֹּ֧קֶר בַּבֹּ֛קֶר יַעֲבֹ֖ר בַּיּ֣וֹם וּבַלָּ֑יְלָה וְהָיָ֥ה רַק־זְוָעָ֖ה הָבִ֥ין שְׁמוּעָֽה׃


As often as it goes forth it shall take you: for morning by morning shall it pass over, by day and by night: and the mere understanding of the report shall bring terror.


###### Isaiah 28:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:20)


כִּֽי־קָצַ֥ר הַמַּצָּ֖ע מֵהִשְׂתָּרֵ֑עַ וְהַמַּסֵּכָ֥ה צָ֖רָה כְּהִתְכַּנֵּֽס׃


For the bed is too short for a man to stretch himself; and the covering too narrow for him to wrap himself up.


###### Isaiah 28:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:21)


כִּ֤י כְהַר־פְּרָצִים֙ יָק֣וּם יְהֹוָ֔ה כְּעֵ֖מֶק בְּגִבְע֣וֹן יִרְגָּ֑ז לַעֲשׂ֤וֹת מַעֲשֵׂ֙הוּ֙ זָ֣ר מַעֲשֵׂ֔הוּ וְלַֽעֲבֹד֙ עֲבֹ֣דָת֔וֹ נׇכְרִיָּ֖ה עֲבֹדָתֽוֹ׃


For the Lord shall rise up as in mount Perażim, he shall be full of anger as in the valley of Giv῾on, that he may do his work, though strange be his work; and bring to pass his act, though strange be his act.


###### Isaiah 28:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:22)


וְעַתָּה֙ אַל־תִּתְלוֹצָ֔צוּ פֶּֽן־יֶחְזְק֖וּ מֽוֹסְרֵיכֶ֑ם כִּֽי־כָלָ֨ה וְנֶחֱרָצָ֜ה שָׁמַ֗עְתִּי מֵאֵ֨ת אֲדֹנָ֧י יֱהֹוִ֛ה צְבָא֖וֹת עַל־כׇּל־הָאָֽרֶץ׃{פ}


Now therefore be not mockers, lest your bands be made strong: for I have heard from the Lord God of hosts that destruction is decreed upon the whole land.


###### Isaiah 28:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:23)


הַאֲזִ֥ינוּ וְשִׁמְע֖וּ קוֹלִ֑י הַקְשִׁ֥יבוּ וְשִׁמְע֖וּ אִמְרָתִֽי׃


Give ear, and hear my voice; hearken, and hear my speech.


###### Isaiah 28:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:24)


הֲכֹ֣ל הַיּ֔וֹם יַחֲרֹ֥שׁ הַחֹרֵ֖שׁ לִזְרֹ֑עַ יְפַתַּ֥ח וִֽישַׂדֵּ֖ד אַדְמָתֽוֹ׃


Does the plowman all day plow to sow? open and harrow his ground?


###### Isaiah 28:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:25)


הֲלוֹא֙ אִם־שִׁוָּ֣ה פָנֶ֔יהָ וְהֵפִ֥יץ קֶ֖צַח וְכַמֹּ֣ן יִזְרֹ֑ק וְשָׂ֨ם חִטָּ֤ה שׂוֹרָה֙ וּשְׂעֹרָ֣ה נִסְמָ֔ן וְכֻסֶּ֖מֶת גְּבֻלָתֽוֹ׃


When he has prepared a smooth surface, does he not then scatter the black seeds, and cast the cummin, and throw in wheat by rows, and barley in the marked spot, and spelt along its border.


###### Isaiah 28:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:26)


וְיִסְּר֥וֹ לַמִּשְׁפָּ֖ט אֱלֹהָ֥יו יוֹרֶֽנּוּ׃


And he strictly raises it according to the method prescribed; his God instructs him.


###### Isaiah 28:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:27)


כִּ֣י לֹ֤א בֶחָרוּץ֙ י֣וּדַשׁ קֶ֔צַח וְאוֹפַ֣ן עֲגָלָ֔ה עַל־כַּמֹּ֖ן יוּסָּ֑ב כִּ֧י בַמַּטֶּ֛ה יֵחָ֥בֶט קֶ֖צַח וְכַמֹּ֥ן בַּשָּֽׁבֶט׃


For the black seeds are not threshed with a threshing instrument, neither is a cart wheel turned about upon the cummin; but the black crop is beaten out with a staff, and the cummin with a rod.


###### Isaiah 28:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:28)


לֶ֣חֶם יוּדָ֔ק כִּ֛י לֹ֥א לָנֶ֖צַח אָד֣וֹשׁ יְדוּשֶׁ֑נּוּ וְ֠הָמַ֠ם גִּלְגַּ֧ל עֶגְלָת֛וֹ וּפָרָשָׁ֖יו לֹ֥א יְדֻקֶּֽנּוּ׃


Is the bread corn crushed? No, he will not ever be threshing it; and when he drives the wheel of his cart and his horses over it, he does not crush it.


###### Isaiah 28:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/28:29)


גַּם־זֹ֕את מֵעִ֛ם יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת יָצָ֑אָה הִפְלִ֣א עֵצָ֔ה הִגְדִּ֖יל תּוּשִׁיָּֽה׃{פ}


This also came from the Lord of hosts. He is wonderful in counsel, and excellent in wisdom.



## Chapter 29

###### Isaiah 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:1)


ה֚וֹי אֲרִיאֵ֣ל אֲרִיאֵ֔ל קִרְיַ֖ת חָנָ֣ה דָוִ֑ד סְפ֥וּ שָׁנָ֛ה עַל־שָׁנָ֖ה חַגִּ֥ים יִנְקֹֽפוּ׃


Ho Ari᾽el, Ari᾽el, the city where David encamped! add year to year; let the feasts come round.


###### Isaiah 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:2)


וַהֲצִיק֖וֹתִי לַאֲרִיאֵ֑ל וְהָיְתָ֤ה תַֽאֲנִיָּה֙ וַאֲנִיָּ֔ה וְהָ֥יְתָה לִּ֖י כַּאֲרִיאֵֽל׃


Yet I will distress Ari᾽el, and there shall be heaviness and sorrow: yet it shall be to me as an Ari᾽el (altar hearth).


###### Isaiah 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:3)


וְחָנִ֥יתִי כַדּ֖וּר עָלָ֑יִךְ וְצַרְתִּ֤י עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ מֻצָּ֔ב וַהֲקִימֹתִ֥י עָלַ֖יִךְ מְצֻרֹֽת׃


And I will camp against thee round about, and will lay siege against thee with a mound, and I will raise siege works against thee.


###### Isaiah 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:4)


וְשָׁפַלְתְּ֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ תְּדַבֵּ֔רִי וּמֵעָפָ֖ר תִּשַּׁ֣ח אִמְרָתֵ֑ךְ וְֽ֠הָיָ֠ה כְּא֤וֹב מֵאֶ֙רֶץ֙ קוֹלֵ֔ךְ וּמֵעָפָ֖ר אִמְרָתֵ֥ךְ תְּצַפְצֵֽף׃


And thou shalt be brought down, and shalt speak out of the ground, and thy speech shall be low out of the dust, and thy voice shall be, as of a medium, out of the ground, and thy speech shall chirp out of the dust.


###### Isaiah 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:5)


וְהָיָ֛ה כְּאָבָ֥ק דַּ֖ק הֲמ֣וֹן זָרָ֑יִךְ וּכְמֹ֤ץ עֹבֵר֙ הֲמ֣וֹן עָרִיצִ֔ים וְהָיָ֖ה לְפֶ֥תַע פִּתְאֹֽם׃


Yet the multitude of thy strangers shall be like small dust, and the multitude of the terrible ones shall be like chaff that passes away: and it shall be in an instant suddenly.


###### Isaiah 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:6)


מֵעִ֨ם יְהֹוָ֤ה צְבָאוֹת֙ תִּפָּקֵ֔ד בְּרַ֥עַם וּבְרַ֖עַשׁ וְק֣וֹל גָּד֑וֹל סוּפָה֙ וּסְעָרָ֔ה וְלַ֖הַב אֵ֥שׁ אוֹכֵלָֽה׃


Thou shalt be visited by the Lord of hosts with thunder, and with earthquake, and great noise, with storm and tempest, and the flame of devouring fire.


###### Isaiah 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:7)


וְהָיָ֗ה כַּֽחֲלוֹם֙ חֲז֣וֹן לַ֔יְלָה הֲמוֹן֙ כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֔ם הַצֹּבְאִ֖ים עַל־אֲרִיאֵ֑ל וְכׇל־צֹבֶ֙יהָ֙ וּמְצֹ֣דָתָ֔הּ וְהַמְּצִיקִ֖ים לָֽהּ׃


And the multitude of all the nations that fight against Ari᾽el, even all that fight against her and her stronghold, and that distress her, shall be as a dream of a night vision.


###### Isaiah 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:8)


וְהָיָ֡ה כַּאֲשֶׁר֩ יַחֲלֹ֨ם הָרָעֵ֜ב וְהִנֵּ֣ה אוֹכֵ֗ל וְהֵקִיץ֮ וְרֵיקָ֣ה נַפְשׁוֹ֒ וְכַאֲשֶׁ֨ר יַחֲלֹ֤ם הַצָּמֵא֙ וְהִנֵּ֣ה שֹׁתֶ֔ה וְהֵקִיץ֙ וְהִנֵּ֣ה עָיֵ֔ף וְנַפְשׁ֖וֹ שׁוֹקֵקָ֑ה כֵּ֣ן יִֽהְיֶ֗ה הֲמוֹן֙ כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֔ם הַצֹּבְאִ֖ים עַל־הַ֥ר צִיּֽוֹן׃{פ}


It shall even be as when a hungry man dreams, and, behold, he eats; but he awakes, and his soul is empty: or as when a thirsty man dreams, and, behold, he drinks; but he awakes, and, behold, he is faint, and his soul longs for water: so shall the multitude of all the nations be, that fight against mount Żiyyon.


###### Isaiah 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:9)


הִתְמַהְמְה֣וּ וּתְמָ֔הוּ הִשְׁתַּעַשְׁע֖וּ וָשֹׁ֑עוּ שָׁכְר֣וּ וְלֹא־יַ֔יִן נָע֖וּ וְלֹ֥א שֵׁכָֽר׃


Amaze yourselves and be amazed; blind yourselves and be blind; be drunk, but not with wine; stagger, but not with strong drink.


###### Isaiah 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:10)


כִּֽי־נָסַ֨ךְ עֲלֵיכֶ֤ם יְהֹוָה֙ ר֣וּחַ תַּרְדֵּמָ֔ה וַיְעַצֵּ֖ם אֶת־עֵינֵיכֶ֑ם אֶת־הַנְּבִיאִ֛ים וְאֶת־רָאשֵׁיכֶ֥ם הַחֹזִ֖ים כִּסָּֽה׃


For the Lord has poured out upon you the spirit of deep sleep, and has closed your eyes, the prophets, and has covered your heads, the seers.


###### Isaiah 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:11)


וַתְּהִ֨י לָכֶ֜ם חָז֣וּת הַכֹּ֗ל כְּדִבְרֵי֮ הַסֵּ֣פֶר הֶחָתוּם֒ אֲשֶֽׁר־יִתְּנ֣וּ אֹת֗וֹ אֶל־יוֹדֵ֥עַ (הספר) [סֵ֛פֶר] לֵאמֹ֖ר קְרָ֣א נָא־זֶ֑ה וְאָמַר֙ לֹ֣א אוּכַ֔ל כִּ֥י חָת֖וּם הֽוּא׃


And the vision of all this is become to you as the words of a book that is sealed, which men deliver to one that is learned, saying, Read this, I pray thee: and he says, I cannot; for it is sealed:


###### Isaiah 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:12)


וְנִתַּ֣ן הַסֵּ֗פֶר עַל֩ אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹא־יָדַ֥ע סֵ֛פֶר לֵאמֹ֖ר קְרָ֣א נָא־זֶ֑ה וְאָמַ֕ר לֹ֥א יָדַ֖עְתִּי סֵֽפֶר׃{ס}


and the book is delivered to him that is not learned, saying, Read this, I pray thee: and he says, I am not learned.


###### Isaiah 29:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:13)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֲדֹנָ֗י יַ֚עַן כִּ֤י נִגַּשׁ֙ הָעָ֣ם הַזֶּ֔ה בְּפִ֤יו וּבִשְׂפָתָיו֙ כִּבְּד֔וּנִי וְלִבּ֖וֹ רִחַ֣ק מִמֶּ֑נִּי וַתְּהִ֤י יִרְאָתָם֙ אֹתִ֔י מִצְוַ֥ת אֲנָשִׁ֖ים מְלֻמָּדָֽה׃


And the Lord said, Since this people draw near, and with their mouth and with their lips do honour me, but have removed their heart far from me, and their fear towards me is as a commandment of men learned by rote;


###### Isaiah 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:14)


לָכֵ֗ן הִנְנִ֥י יוֹסִ֛ף לְהַפְלִ֥יא אֶת־הָעָם־הַזֶּ֖ה הַפְלֵ֣א וָפֶ֑לֶא וְאָֽבְדָה֙ חׇכְמַ֣ת חֲכָמָ֔יו וּבִינַ֥ת נְבֹנָ֖יו תִּסְתַּתָּֽר׃{ס}


therefore, behold, I will proceed to do a marvellous work among this people, even a marvellous work and a wonder: for the wisdom of their wise men shall perish, and the understanding of their prudent men shall be hid.


###### Isaiah 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:15)


ה֛וֹי הַמַּעֲמִיקִ֥ים מֵיְהֹוָ֖ה לַסְתִּ֣ר עֵצָ֑ה וְהָיָ֤ה בְמַחְשָׁךְ֙ מַֽעֲשֵׂיהֶ֔ם וַיֹּ֣אמְר֔וּ מִ֥י רֹאֵ֖נוּ וּמִ֥י יֹֽדְעֵֽנוּ׃


Woe to them that seek deep to hide their counsel from the Lord, and their works are in the dark, and they say, Who sees us? and who knows us?


###### Isaiah 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:16)


הַ֨פְכְּכֶ֔ם אִם־כְּחֹ֥מֶר הַיֹּצֵ֖ר יֵחָשֵׁ֑ב כִּֽי־יֹאמַ֨ר מַעֲשֶׂ֤ה לְעֹשֵׂ֙הוּ֙ לֹ֣א עָשָׂ֔נִי וְיֵ֛צֶר אָמַ֥ר לְיֹצְר֖וֹ לֹ֥א הֵבִֽין׃


O your perverseness! shall the potter be esteemed as the clay? for shall the work say of him that made it, He made me not? or shall the thing framed say of him that framed it, He had no understanding?


###### Isaiah 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:17)


הֲלוֹא־עוֹד֙ מְעַ֣ט מִזְעָ֔ר וְשָׁ֥ב לְבָנ֖וֹן לַכַּרְמֶ֑ל וְהַכַּרְמֶ֖ל לַיַּ֥עַר יֵחָשֵֽׁב׃


Is it not yet a very little while, and Levanon shall be turned into a fruitful field, and the fruitful field shall be regarded as a forest?


###### Isaiah 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:18)


וְשָׁמְע֧וּ בַיּוֹם־הַה֛וּא הַחֵרְשִׁ֖ים דִּבְרֵי־סֵ֑פֶר וּמֵאֹ֣פֶל וּמֵחֹ֔שֶׁךְ עֵינֵ֥י עִוְרִ֖ים תִּרְאֶֽינָה׃


And on that day shall the deaf hear the words of a book, and the eyes of the blind shall see out of obscurity, and out of darkness.


###### Isaiah 29:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:19)


וְיָסְפ֧וּ עֲנָוִ֛ים בַּֽיהֹוָ֖ה שִׂמְחָ֑ה וְאֶבְיוֹנֵ֣י אָדָ֔ם בִּקְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל יָגִֽילוּ׃


The meek also shall increase their joy in the Lord, and the poorest among men shall rejoice in the Holy One of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 29:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:20)


כִּי־אָפֵ֥ס עָרִ֖יץ וְכָ֣לָה לֵ֑ץ וְנִכְרְת֖וּ כׇּל־שֹׁ֥קְדֵי אָֽוֶן׃


For the terrible one is brought to nought, and the scorner is consumed, and all that watch for iniquity are cut off:


###### Isaiah 29:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:21)


מַחֲטִיאֵ֤י אָדָם֙ בְּדָבָ֔ר וְלַמּוֹכִ֥יחַ בַּשַּׁ֖עַר יְקֹשׁ֑וּן וַיַּטּ֥וּ בַתֹּ֖הוּ צַדִּֽיק׃{פ}


that make a man an offender for a word, and ensnare the mentor in the gate, and turn aside the just for a thing of nought.


###### Isaiah 29:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:22)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֶל־בֵּ֣ית יַעֲקֹ֔ב אֲשֶׁ֥ר פָּדָ֖ה אֶת־אַבְרָהָ֑ם לֹֽא־עַתָּ֤ה יֵבוֹשׁ֙ יַעֲקֹ֔ב וְלֹ֥א עַתָּ֖ה פָּנָ֥יו יֶחֱוָֽרוּ׃


Therefore thus says the Lord, who redeemed Avraham, concerning the house of Ya῾aqov, Ya῾aqov shall not now be ashamed, neither shall his face now grow pale;


###### Isaiah 29:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:23)


כִּ֣י בִ֠רְאֹת֠וֹ יְלָדָ֞יו מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יָדַ֛י בְּקִרְבּ֖וֹ יַקְדִּ֣ישֽׁוּ שְׁמִ֑י וְהִקְדִּ֙ישׁוּ֙ אֶת־קְד֣וֹשׁ יַֽעֲקֹ֔ב וְאֶת־אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל יַעֲרִֽיצוּ׃


when he sees his children, the work of my hands, in the midst of him, sanctifying my name; for they shall sanctify the Holy One of Ya῾aqov, and shall reverence the God of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 29:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/29:24)


וְיָדְע֥וּ תֹֽעֵי־ר֖וּחַ בִּינָ֑ה וְרוֹגְנִ֖ים יִלְמְדוּ־לֶֽקַח׃{ס}


They also that erred in spirit shall come to understanding, and they that murmured shall learn doctrine.



## Chapter 30

###### Isaiah 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:1)


ה֣וֹי בָּנִ֤ים סֽוֹרְרִים֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה לַעֲשׂ֤וֹת עֵצָה֙ וְלֹ֣א מִנִּ֔י וְלִנְסֹ֥ךְ מַסֵּכָ֖ה וְלֹ֣א רוּחִ֑י לְמַ֛עַן סְפ֥וֹת חַטָּ֖את עַל־חַטָּֽאת׃


Woe to the rebellious children, says the Lord, that take counsel, but not of me; and that prepare a plan but not of my spirit, that they may add sin to sin:


###### Isaiah 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:2)


הַהֹֽלְכִים֙ לָרֶ֣דֶת מִצְרַ֔יִם וּפִ֖י לֹ֣א שָׁאָ֑לוּ לָעוֹז֙ בְּמָע֣וֹז פַּרְעֹ֔ה וְלַחְס֖וֹת בְּצֵ֥ל מִצְרָֽיִם׃


that walk to go down into Miż­rayim, and have not asked at my mouth; to strengthen themselves in the strength of Par῾o, and to trust in the shadow of Miżrayim!


###### Isaiah 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:3)


וְהָיָ֥ה לָכֶ֛ם מָע֥וֹז פַּרְעֹ֖ה לְבֹ֑שֶׁת וְהֶחָס֥וּת בְּצֵל־מִצְרַ֖יִם לִכְלִמָּֽה׃


Therefore shall the strength of Par῾o be your shame, and the trust in the shadow of Miżrayim your confusion.


###### Isaiah 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:4)


כִּֽי־הָי֥וּ בְצֹ֖עַן שָׂרָ֑יו וּמַלְאָכָ֖יו חָנֵ֥ס יַגִּֽיעוּ׃


For his princes were at Żo῾an, and his ambassadors came to Ḥanes.


###### Isaiah 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:5)


כֹּ֣ל הֹבִ֔אישׁ עַל־עַ֖ם לֹא־יוֹעִ֣ילוּ לָ֑מוֹ לֹ֤א לְעֵ֙זֶר֙ וְלֹ֣א לְהוֹעִ֔יל כִּ֥י לְבֹ֖שֶׁת וְגַם־לְחֶרְפָּֽה׃{ס}


They were all ashamed of a people that could not profit them, nor be a help nor profit, but a shame, and also a reproach.


###### Isaiah 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:6)


מַשָּׂ֖א בַּהֲמ֣וֹת נֶ֑גֶב בְּאֶ֩רֶץ֩ צָרָ֨ה וְצוּקָ֜ה לָבִ֧יא וָלַ֣יִשׁ מֵהֶ֗ם אֶפְעֶה֙ וְשָׂרָ֣ף מְעוֹפֵ֔ף יִשְׂאוּ֩ עַל־כֶּ֨תֶף עֲיָרִ֜ים חֵילֵהֶ֗ם וְעַל־דַּבֶּ֤שֶׁת גְּמַלִּים֙ אֽוֹצְרֹתָ֔ם עַל־עַ֖ם לֹ֥א יוֹעִֽילוּ׃


The burden of the beasts of the south: into the land of trouble and anguish, from whence come the young and old lion, the viper and the venomous flying serpent, they will carry their riches upon the shoulders of young asses, and their treasurers upon the humps of camels, to a people that shall not profit them.


###### Isaiah 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:7)


וּמִצְרַ֕יִם הֶ֥בֶל וָרִ֖יק יַעְזֹ֑רוּ לָכֵן֙ קָרָ֣אתִי לָזֹ֔את רַ֥הַב הֵ֖ם שָֽׁבֶת׃


For Miżrayim shall help in vain, and to no purpose: therefore have I called this people, Boasters in sitting still.


###### Isaiah 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:8)


עַתָּ֗ה בּ֣וֹא כׇתְבָ֥הּ עַל־ל֛וּחַ אִתָּ֖ם וְעַל־סֵ֣פֶר חֻקָּ֑הּ וּתְהִי֙ לְי֣וֹם אַחֲר֔וֹן לָעַ֖ד עַד־עוֹלָֽם׃


Now go, write it before them on a tablet, and inscribe it in a book, that it may be for the time to come for ever and ever:


###### Isaiah 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:9)


כִּ֣י עַ֤ם מְרִי֙ ה֔וּא בָּנִ֖ים כֶּחָשִׁ֑ים בָּנִ֕ים לֹא־אָב֥וּ שְׁמ֖וֹעַ תּוֹרַ֥ת יְהֹוָֽה׃


that this is a rebellious people, lying children, children that will not hear the Tora of the Lord:


###### Isaiah 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:10)


אֲשֶׁ֨ר אָמְר֤וּ לָרֹאִים֙ לֹ֣א תִרְא֔וּ וְלַ֣חֹזִ֔ים לֹ֥א תֶחֱזוּ־לָ֖נוּ נְכֹח֑וֹת דַּבְּרוּ־לָ֣נוּ חֲלָק֔וֹת חֲז֖וּ מַהֲתַלּֽוֹת׃


who say to the seers, See not; and to the prophets, Prophesy not to us right things, speak to us smooth things, prophesy delusions:


###### Isaiah 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:11)


ס֚וּרוּ מִנֵּי־דֶ֔רֶךְ הַטּ֖וּ מִנֵּי־אֹ֑רַח הַשְׁבִּ֥יתוּ מִפָּנֵ֖ינוּ אֶת־קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


get you out of the way, turn aside out of the path, cause the Holy One of Yisra᾽el to cease from before us.


###### Isaiah 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:12)


לָכֵ֗ן כֹּ֤ה אָמַר֙ קְד֣וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל יַ֥עַן מׇאׇסְכֶ֖ם בַּדָּבָ֣ר הַזֶּ֑ה וַֽתִּבְטְחוּ֙ בְּעֹ֣שֶׁק וְנָל֔וֹז וַתִּֽשָּׁעֲנ֖וּ עָלָֽיו׃


Wherefore thus says the Holy One of Yisra᾽el, Because you despise this word, and trust in oppression and perverseness, and depend on that:


###### Isaiah 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:13)


לָכֵ֗ן יִהְיֶ֤ה לָכֶם֙ הֶעָוֺ֣ן הַזֶּ֔ה כְּפֶ֣רֶץ נֹפֵ֔ל נִבְעֶ֖ה בְּחוֹמָ֣ה נִשְׂגָּבָ֑ה אֲשֶׁר־פִּתְאֹ֥ם לְפֶ֖תַע יָב֥וֹא שִׁבְרָֽהּ׃


therefore this iniquity shall be to you as a breach ready to fall, bulging out in a high wall, whose breaking comes suddenly in an instant.


###### Isaiah 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:14)


וּ֠שְׁבָרָ֠הּ כְּשֵׁ֨בֶר נֵ֧בֶל יוֹצְרִ֛ים כָּת֖וּת לֹ֣א יַחְמֹ֑ל וְלֹֽא־יִמָּצֵ֤א בִמְכִתָּתוֹ֙ חֶ֔רֶשׂ לַחְתּ֥וֹת אֵשׁ֙ מִיָּק֔וּד וְלַחְשֹׂ֥ף מַ֖יִם מִגֶּֽבֶא׃{ס}


And he shall break it as the breaking of the potters’ vessel that is broken in pieces; he shall not spare: so that there shall not be found in the bursting of it a shred sufficient for taking fire from the hearth, or for taking water out of the cistern.


###### Isaiah 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:15)


כִּ֣י כֹֽה־אָמַר֩ אֲדֹנָ֨י יֱהֹוִ֜ה קְד֣וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֗ל בְּשׁוּבָ֤ה וָנַ֙חַת֙ תִּוָּ֣שֵׁע֔וּן בְּהַשְׁקֵט֙ וּבְבִטְחָ֔ה תִּֽהְיֶ֖ה גְּבוּרַתְכֶ֑ם וְלֹ֖א אֲבִיתֶֽם׃


For thus says the Lord God, the Holy One of Yisra᾽el; In ease and rest shall you be saved; in quietness and in confidence shall be your strength: and you did not wish it.


###### Isaiah 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:16)


וַתֹּ֨אמְר֥וּ לֹא־כִ֛י עַל־ס֥וּס נָנ֖וּס עַל־כֵּ֣ן תְּנוּס֑וּן וְעַל־קַ֣ל נִרְכָּ֔ב עַל־כֵּ֖ן יִקַּ֥לּוּ רֹדְפֵיכֶֽם׃


But you said, No; for we will flee upon horses; therefore shall you flee; and, We will ride upon the swift; therefore shall they that pursue you be swift.


###### Isaiah 30:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:17)


אֶ֣לֶף אֶחָ֗ד מִפְּנֵי֙ גַּעֲרַ֣ת אֶחָ֔ד מִפְּנֵ֛י גַּעֲרַ֥ת חֲמִשָּׁ֖ה תָּנֻ֑סוּ עַ֣ד אִם־נוֹתַרְתֶּ֗ם כַּתֹּ֙רֶן֙ עַל־רֹ֣אשׁ הָהָ֔ר וְכַנֵּ֖ס עַל־הַגִּבְעָֽה׃


One thousand shall flee at the rebuke of one; at the rebuke of five shall you flee: till you be left like a flagstaff upon the top of a mountain, and as a banner on a hill.


###### Isaiah 30:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:18)


וְלָכֵ֞ן יְחַכֶּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ לַחֲנַנְכֶ֔ם וְלָכֵ֥ן יָר֖וּם לְרַחֶמְכֶ֑ם כִּֽי־אֱלֹהֵ֤י מִשְׁפָּט֙ יְהֹוָ֔ה אַשְׁרֵ֖י כׇּל־ח֥וֹכֵי לֽוֹ׃{פ}


And therefore will the Lord wait, that he may be gracious to you, and therefore will he be exalted, that he may have mercy upon you: for the Lord is a God of judgment: happy are all they that wait for him.


###### Isaiah 30:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:19)


כִּי־עַ֛ם בְּצִיּ֥וֹן יֵשֵׁ֖ב בִּירוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם בָּכ֣וֹ לֹֽא־תִבְכֶּ֗ה חָנ֤וֹן יׇחְנְךָ֙ לְק֣וֹל זַעֲקֶ֔ךָ כְּשׇׁמְעָת֖וֹ עָנָֽךְ׃


For a people that dwell in Żiyyon, even in Yerushalayim: thou shalt weep no more: he will be very gracious to thee at the voice of thy cry; when he shall hear it, he will answer thee.


###### Isaiah 30:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:20)


וְנָתַ֨ן לָכֶ֧ם אֲדֹנָ֛י לֶ֥חֶם צָ֖ר וּמַ֣יִם לָ֑חַץ וְלֹא־יִכָּנֵ֥ף עוֹד֙ מוֹרֶ֔יךָ וְהָי֥וּ עֵינֶ֖יךָ רֹא֥וֹת אֶת־מוֹרֶֽיךָ׃


And the Lord shall give you the bread of adversity, and the water of affliction; yet thy teacher shall not withdraw himself any more, but thy eyes shall see thy teacher:


###### Isaiah 30:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:21)


וְאׇזְנֶ֙יךָ֙ תִּשְׁמַ֣עְנָה דָבָ֔ר מֵאַחֲרֶ֖יךָ לֵאמֹ֑ר זֶ֤ה הַדֶּ֙רֶךְ֙ לְכ֣וּ ב֔וֹ כִּ֥י תַאֲמִ֖ינוּ וְכִ֥י תַשְׂמְאִֽילוּ׃


and thy ears shall hear a word behind thee, saying, This is the way, walk in it, when you turn to the right hand, and when you turn to the left.


###### Isaiah 30:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:22)


וְטִמֵּאתֶ֗ם אֶת־צִפּוּי֙ פְּסִילֵ֣י כַסְפֶּ֔ךָ וְאֶת־אֲפֻדַּ֖ת מַסֵּכַ֣ת זְהָבֶ֑ךָ תִּזְרֵם֙ כְּמ֣וֹ דָוָ֔ה צֵ֖א תֹּ֥אמַר לֽוֹ׃


You shall defile also the silver plating of thy carved idols, and the gold binding of thy molten images: thou shalt cast them away as a menstruous thing; thou shalt say to it, Get thee hence.


###### Isaiah 30:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:23)


וְנָתַן֩ מְטַ֨ר זַרְעֲךָ֜ אֲשֶׁר־תִּזְרַ֣ע אֶת־הָאֲדָמָ֗ה וְלֶ֙חֶם֙ תְּבוּאַ֣ת הָאֲדָמָ֔ה וְהָיָ֥ה דָשֵׁ֖ן וְשָׁמֵ֑ן יִרְעֶ֥ה מִקְנֶ֛יךָ בַּיּ֥וֹם הַה֖וּא כַּ֥ר נִרְחָֽב׃


Then shall he give the rain for thy seed, that thou shalt sow the ground with that; and bread of the increase of the earth, and it shall be fat and plenteous: in that day shall thy cattle feed in large pastures.


###### Isaiah 30:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:24)


וְהָאֲלָפִ֣ים וְהָעֲיָרִ֗ים עֹֽבְדֵי֙ הָאֲדָמָ֔ה בְּלִ֥יל חָמִ֖יץ יֹאכֵ֑לוּ אֲשֶׁר־זֹרֶ֥ה בָרַ֖חַת וּבַמִּזְרֶֽה׃


The oxen likewise and the young asses that till the ground shall eat clean provender, which has been winnowed with the shovel and with the fan.


###### Isaiah 30:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:25)


וְהָיָ֣ה**׀** עַל־כׇּל־הַ֣ר גָּבֹ֗הַּ וְעַל֙ כׇּל־גִּבְעָ֣ה נִשָּׂאָ֔ה פְּלָגִ֖ים יִבְלֵי־מָ֑יִם בְּיוֹם֙ הֶ֣רֶג רָ֔ב בִּנְפֹ֖ל מִגְדָּלִֽים׃


And there shall be upon every high mountain, and upon every high hill, rivers and streams of waters on the day of the great slaughter, when the towers fall.


###### Isaiah 30:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:26)


וְהָיָ֤ה אוֹר־הַלְּבָנָה֙ כְּא֣וֹר הַחַמָּ֔ה וְא֤וֹר הַֽחַמָּה֙ יִֽהְיֶ֣ה שִׁבְעָתַ֔יִם כְּא֖וֹר שִׁבְעַ֣ת הַיָּמִ֑ים בְּי֗וֹם חֲבֹ֤שׁ יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־שֶׁ֣בֶר עַמּ֔וֹ וּמַ֥חַץ מַכָּת֖וֹ יִרְפָּֽא׃{פ}


Moreover the light of the moon shall be as the light of the sun, and the light of the sun shall be sevenfold, as the light of seven days, on the day that the Lord binds up the breach of his people, and heals the stroke of their wound.


###### Isaiah 30:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:27)


הִנֵּ֤ה שֵׁם־יְהֹוָה֙ בָּ֣א מִמֶּרְחָ֔ק בֹּעֵ֣ר אַפּ֔וֹ וְכֹ֖בֶד מַשָּׂאָ֑הֿ שְׂפָתָיו֙ מָ֣לְאוּ זַ֔עַם וּלְשׁוֹנ֖וֹ כְּאֵ֥שׁ אֹכָֽלֶת׃


Behold the name of the Lord comes from far; burning with his anger and in a thick column of smoke: his lips are full of indignation, and his tongue as a devouring fire:


###### Isaiah 30:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:28)


וְרוּח֞וֹ כְּנַ֤חַל שׁוֹטֵף֙ עַד־צַוָּ֣אר יֶחֱצֶ֔ה לַהֲנָפָ֥ה גוֹיִ֖ם בְּנָ֣פַת שָׁ֑וְא וְרֶ֣סֶן מַתְעֶ֔ה עַ֖ל לְחָיֵ֥י עַמִּֽים׃


and his breath is as an overflowing stream, that reaches to the neck, to sift the nations with the sieve of vanity: and to put a bridle on the jaws of the people, causing them to err.


###### Isaiah 30:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:29)


הַשִּׁיר֙ יִהְיֶ֣ה לָכֶ֔ם כְּלֵ֖יל הִתְקַדֶּשׁ־חָ֑ג וְשִׂמְחַ֣ת לֵבָ֗ב כַּהוֹלֵךְ֙ בֶּחָלִ֔יל לָב֥וֹא בְהַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶל־צ֥וּר יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


You shall have a song, as in the night when a holy solemnity is kept; and gladness of heart, as when one goes with a flute to come into the mountain of the Lord, to the Rock of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 30:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:30)


וְהִשְׁמִ֨יעַ יְהֹוָ֜ה אֶת־ה֣וֹד קוֹל֗וֹ וְנַ֤חַת זְרוֹעוֹ֙ יַרְאֶ֔ה בְּזַ֣עַף אַ֔ף וְלַ֖הַב אֵ֣שׁ אוֹכֵלָ֑ה נֶ֥פֶץ וָזֶ֖רֶם וְאֶ֥בֶן בָּרָֽד׃


And the Lord shall cause his glorious voice to be heard, and shall show the descending blow of his arm, with the indignation of his anger, and with the flame of a devouring fire, with cloudburst, and tempest, and hailstones.


###### Isaiah 30:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:31)


כִּֽי־מִקּ֥וֹל יְהֹוָ֖ה יֵחַ֣ת אַשּׁ֑וּר בַּשֵּׁ֖בֶט יַכֶּֽה׃


For through the voice of the Lord shall Ashshur be beaten down; with a rod will he smite him.


###### Isaiah 30:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:32)


וְהָיָ֗ה כֹּ֚ל מַֽעֲבַר֙ מַטֵּ֣ה מֽוּסָדָ֔הֿ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יָנִ֤יחַ יְהֹוָה֙ עָלָ֔יו בְּתֻפִּ֖ים וּבְכִנֹּר֑וֹת וּבְמִלְחֲמ֥וֹת תְּנוּפָ֖ה נִלְחַם־[בָּֽם] (בה)׃


And in every place where the appointed staff shall pass, which the Lord shall lay upon him, it shall be with tambourines and lyres: and in battle of shaking will he fight with them.


###### Isaiah 30:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/30:33)


כִּֽי־עָר֤וּךְ מֵֽאֶתְמוּל֙ תׇּפְתֶּ֔ה גַּם־[הִ֛יא] (הוא) לַמֶּ֥לֶךְ הוּכָ֖ן הֶעְמִ֣יק הִרְחִ֑ב מְדֻרָתָ֗הּ אֵ֤שׁ וְעֵצִים֙ הַרְבֵּ֔ה נִשְׁמַ֤ת יְהֹוָה֙ כְּנַ֣חַל גׇּפְרִ֔ית בֹּעֲרָ֖ה בָּֽהּ׃{פ}


For its hearth is ordained of old; yes, for the king it is prepared: he has made it deep and large: its bonfire is of much fire and wood; the breath of the Lord, like a stream of brimstone, kindles it.



## Chapter 31

###### Isaiah 31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:1)


ה֣וֹי הַיֹּרְדִ֤ים מִצְרַ֙יִם֙ לְעֶזְרָ֔ה עַל־סוּסִ֖ים יִשָּׁעֵ֑נוּ וַיִּבְטְח֨וּ עַל־רֶ֜כֶב כִּ֣י רָ֗ב וְעַ֤ל פָּרָשִׁים֙ כִּֽי־עָצְמ֣וּ מְאֹ֔ד וְלֹ֤א שָׁעוּ֙ עַל־קְד֣וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וְאֶת־יְהֹוָ֖ה לֹ֥א דָרָֽשׁוּ׃


Woe to them that go down to Miżrayim for help; and depend on horses, and trust in chariots, because they are many; and in horsemen, because they are very strong; but they look not to the Holy One of Yisra᾽el, neither seek the Lord!


###### Isaiah 31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:2)


וְגַם־ה֤וּא חָכָם֙ וַיָּ֣בֵא רָ֔ע וְאֶת־דְּבָרָ֖יו לֹ֣א הֵסִ֑יר וְקָם֙ עַל־בֵּ֣ית מְרֵעִ֔ים וְעַל־עֶזְרַ֖ת פֹּ֥עֲלֵי אָֽוֶן׃


Yet he also is wise, and will bring evil, and will not call back his words: but will arise against the house of the evildoers, and against the help of them that work iniquity.


###### Isaiah 31:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:3)


וּמִצְרַ֤יִם אָדָם֙ וְֽלֹא־אֵ֔ל וְסוּסֵיהֶ֥ם בָּשָׂ֖ר וְלֹא־ר֑וּחַ וַיהֹוָ֞ה יַטֶּ֣ה יָד֗וֹ וְכָשַׁ֤ל עוֹזֵר֙ וְנָפַ֣ל עָזֻ֔ר וְיַחְדָּ֖ו כֻּלָּ֥ם יִכְלָיֽוּן׃{ס}


Now Miżrayim is man, and not God; and their horses flesh, and not spirit. When the Lord shall stretch out his hand, the helper shall stumble, and the one who is helped shall fall down, and they shall all perish together.


###### Isaiah 31:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:4)


כִּ֣י כֹ֣ה אָֽמַר־יְהֹוָ֣ה׀אֵלַ֡י כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר יֶהְגֶּה֩ הָאַרְיֵ֨ה וְהַכְּפִ֜יר עַל־טַרְפּ֗וֹ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִקָּרֵ֤א עָלָיו֙ מְלֹ֣א רֹעִ֔ים מִקּוֹלָם֙ לֹ֣א יֵחָ֔ת וּמֵהֲמוֹנָ֖ם לֹ֣א יַעֲנֶ֑ה כֵּ֗ן יֵרֵד֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֔וֹת לִצְבֹּ֥א עַל־הַר־צִיּ֖וֹן וְעַל־גִּבְעָתָֽהּ׃


For thus has the Lord spoken to me, Like the lion and the young lion roaring on their prey, though a multitude of shepherds is called out against him, he will not be afraid of their voice, nor abase himself for the noise of them: so shall the Lord of hosts come down to fight for mount Żiyyon, and for the hill thereof.


###### Isaiah 31:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:5)


כְּצִפֳּרִ֣ים עָפ֔וֹת כֵּ֗ן יָגֵ֛ן יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת עַל־יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם גָּנ֥וֹן וְהִצִּ֖יל פָּסֹ֥חַ וְהִמְלִֽיט׃


As birds hovering, so will the Lord of hosts defend Yerushalayim; he will defend it and deliver it; he will pass over it and spare it.


###### Isaiah 31:6
