[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:6)


שׁ֗וּבוּ לַאֲשֶׁ֛ר הֶעְמִ֥יקוּ סָרָ֖ה בְּנֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Turn to him from whom you have deeply revolted, O children of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 31:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:7)


כִּ֚י בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא יִמְאָס֗וּן אִ֚ישׁ אֱלִילֵ֣י כַסְפּ֔וֹ וֶאֱלִילֵ֖י זְהָב֑וֹ אֲשֶׁ֨ר עָשׂ֥וּ לָכֶ֛ם יְדֵיכֶ֖ם חֵֽטְא׃


For on that day every man shall cast away his idols of silver, and his idols of gold, which your own hands have made to you for a sin.


###### Isaiah 31:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:8)


וְנָפַ֤ל אַשּׁוּר֙ בְּחֶ֣רֶב לֹא־אִ֔ישׁ וְחֶ֥רֶב לֹא־אָדָ֖ם תֹּאכְלֶ֑נּוּ וְנָ֥ס לוֹ֙ מִפְּנֵי־חֶ֔רֶב וּבַחוּרָ֖יו לָמַ֥ס יִהְיֽוּ׃


Then shall Ashshur fall with the sword, not of a mighty man; and the sword, not of a mean man, shall devour him: but he shall flee from the sword, and his young men shall become bondslaves.


###### Isaiah 31:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/31:9)


וְסַלְעוֹ֙ מִמָּג֣וֹר יַעֲב֔וֹר וְחַתּ֥וּ מִנֵּ֖ס שָׂרָ֑יו נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֗ה אֲשֶׁר־א֥וּר לוֹ֙ בְּצִיּ֔וֹן וְתַנּ֥וּר ל֖וֹ בִּירוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃{פ}


And his rock shall pass away in terror, and his princes desert the banner in fear, says the Lord, whose hearth is in Żiyyon, and whose furnace is in Yerushalayim.



## Chapter 32

###### Isaiah 32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:1)


הֵ֥ן לְצֶ֖דֶק יִמְלׇךְ־מֶ֑לֶךְ וּלְשָׂרִ֖ים לְמִשְׁפָּ֥ט יָשֹֽׂרוּ׃


Behold, a king shall reign in righteousness, and as for princes, they shall rule in judgment.


###### Isaiah 32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:2)


וְהָיָה־אִ֥ישׁ כְּמַחֲבֵא־ר֖וּחַ וְסֵ֣תֶר זָ֑רֶם כְּפַלְגֵי־מַ֣יִם בְּצָי֔וֹן כְּצֵ֥ל סֶלַע־כָּבֵ֖ד בְּאֶ֥רֶץ עֲיֵפָֽה׃


And a man shall be as hiding place from the wind, and a covert from the tempest; as rivers of water in a dry place, as the shadow of a great rock in a weary land.


###### Isaiah 32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:3)


וְלֹ֥א תִשְׁעֶ֖ינָה עֵינֵ֣י רֹאִ֑ים וְאׇזְנֵ֥י שֹׁמְעִ֖ים תִּקְשַֽׁבְנָה׃


And the eyes of them that see shall not be blind, and the ears of them that hear shall hearken.


###### Isaiah 32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:4)


וּלְבַ֥ב נִמְהָרִ֖ים יָבִ֣ין לָדָ֑עַת וּלְשׁ֣וֹן עִלְּגִ֔ים תְּמַהֵ֖ר לְדַבֵּ֥ר צָחֽוֹת׃


The heart also of the rash shall understand knowledge, and the tongue of the stammerers shall be ready to speak plainly.


###### Isaiah 32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:5)


לֹא־יִקָּרֵ֥א ע֛וֹד לְנָבָ֖ל נָדִ֑יב וּלְכִילַ֕י לֹ֥א יֵאָמֵ֖ר שֽׁוֹעַ׃


The vile person shall be no more called liberal, nor the churl said to be bountiful.


###### Isaiah 32:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:6)


כִּ֤י נָבָל֙ נְבָלָ֣ה יְדַבֵּ֔ר וְלִבּ֖וֹ יַעֲשֶׂה־אָ֑וֶן לַעֲשׂ֣וֹת חֹ֗נֶף וּלְדַבֵּ֤ר אֶל־יְהֹוָה֙ תּוֹעָ֔ה לְהָרִיק֙ נֶ֣פֶשׁ רָעֵ֔ב וּמַשְׁקֶ֥ה צָמֵ֖א יַחְסִֽיר׃


For the vile person speaks villainy, and his heart works iniquity, to practise hypocrisy, and to utter error against the Lord, to make empty the soul of the hungry, and he will cause the drink of the thirsty to fail.


###### Isaiah 32:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:7)


וְכֵלַ֖י כֵּלָ֣יו רָעִ֑ים ה֚וּא זִמּ֣וֹת יָעָ֔ץ לְחַבֵּ֤ל (ענוים) [עֲנִיִּים֙] בְּאִמְרֵי־שֶׁ֔קֶר וּבְדַבֵּ֥ר אֶבְי֖וֹן מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


The instruments also of the churl are evil: he devises wicked devices to destroy the poor with lying words, and the needy when he speaks right.


###### Isaiah 32:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:8)


וְנָדִ֖יב נְדִיב֣וֹת יָעָ֑ץ וְה֖וּא עַל־נְדִיב֥וֹת יָקֽוּם׃{ס}


But the liberal devises liberal things; and by liberal things shall he stand.


###### Isaiah 32:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:9)


נָשִׁים֙ שַׁאֲנַנּ֔וֹת קֹ֖מְנָה שְׁמַ֣עְנָה קוֹלִ֑י בָּנוֹת֙ בֹּֽטְח֔וֹת הַאְזֵ֖נָּה אִמְרָתִֽי׃


Rise up, you women that are at ease, hear my voice; you complacent daughters, give ear to my speech.


###### Isaiah 32:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:10)


יָמִים֙ עַל־שָׁנָ֔ה תִּרְגַּ֖זְנָה בֹּטְח֑וֹת כִּ֚י כָּלָ֣ה בָצִ֔יר אֹ֖סֶף בְּלִ֥י יָבֽוֹא׃


In a year and some days, you shall be troubled, you complacent women; for the vintage shall fail, the gathering shall not come.


###### Isaiah 32:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:11)


חִרְדוּ֙ שַֽׁאֲנַנּ֔וֹת רְגָ֖זָה בֹּטְח֑וֹת פְּשֹׁ֣טָֽה וְעֹ֔רָה וַחֲג֖וֹרָה עַל־חֲלָצָֽיִם׃


Tremble, you women that are at ease; be troubled, you complacent ones: strip, and make yourselves bare, and gird sackcloth upon your loins.


###### Isaiah 32:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:12)


עַל־שָׁדַ֖יִם סֹפְדִ֑ים עַל־שְׂדֵי־חֶ֕מֶד עַל־גֶּ֖פֶן פֹּרִיָּֽה׃


Striking the breasts, for the pleasant fields, for the fruitful vine.


###### Isaiah 32:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:13)


עַ֚ל אַדְמַ֣ת עַמִּ֔י ק֥וֹץ שָׁמִ֖יר תַּעֲלֶ֑ה כִּ֚י עַל־כׇּל־בָּתֵּ֣י מָשׂ֔וֹשׂ קִרְיָ֖ה עַלִּיזָֽה׃


Upon the land of my people shall come up thorns and briers; yes, upon all the houses of joy in the joyous city:


###### Isaiah 32:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:14)


כִּֽי־אַרְמ֣וֹן נֻטָּ֔שׁ הֲמ֥וֹן עִ֖יר עֻזָּ֑ב עֹ֣פֶל וָבַ֜חַן הָיָ֨ה בְעַ֤ד מְעָרוֹת֙ עַד־עוֹלָ֔ם מְשׂ֥וֹשׂ פְּרָאִ֖ים מִרְעֵ֥ה עֲדָרִֽים׃


because the palaces shall be forsaken; the city with its stir shall be deserted; the fort and tower shall be for dens for ever, a joy of wild asses, a pasture of flocks;


###### Isaiah 32:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:15)


עַד־יֵ֨עָרֶ֥ה עָלֵ֛ינוּ ר֖וּחַ מִמָּר֑וֹם וְהָיָ֤ה מִדְבָּר֙ לַכַּרְמֶ֔ל (וכרמל) [וְהַכַּרְמֶ֖ל] לַיַּ֥עַר יֵחָשֵֽׁב׃


until a spirit be poured upon us from on high, and the wilderness be a fruitful field, and the fruitful field be counted for a forest.


###### Isaiah 32:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:16)


וְשָׁכַ֥ן בַּמִּדְבָּ֖ר מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּצְדָקָ֖ה בַּכַּרְמֶ֥ל תֵּשֵֽׁב׃


Then judgment shall dwell in the wilderness, and righteousness remain in the fruitful field.


###### Isaiah 32:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:17)


וְהָיָ֛ה מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה הַצְּדָקָ֖ה שָׁל֑וֹם וַֽעֲבֹדַת֙ הַצְּדָקָ֔ה הַשְׁקֵ֥ט וָבֶ֖טַח עַד־עוֹלָֽם׃


And the work of righteousness shall be peace; and the effect of righteousness, quietness and assurance for ever.


###### Isaiah 32:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:18)


וְיָשַׁ֥ב עַמִּ֖י בִּנְוֵ֣ה שָׁל֑וֹם וּֽבְמִשְׁכְּנוֹת֙ מִבְטַחִ֔ים וּבִמְנוּחֹ֖ת שַׁאֲנַנּֽוֹת׃


And my people shall dwell in a peaceable habitation, and in secure dwellings, and in quiet resting places;


###### Isaiah 32:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:19)


וּבָרַ֖ד בְּרֶ֣דֶת הַיָּ֑עַר וּבַשִּׁפְלָ֖ה תִּשְׁפַּ֥ל הָעִֽיר׃


and it shall hail in the downfall of the forest, and the city shall descend into the valley.


###### Isaiah 32:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/32:20)


אַשְׁרֵיכֶ֕ם זֹרְעֵ֖י עַל־כׇּל־מָ֑יִם מְשַׁלְּחֵ֥י רֶגֶל־הַשּׁ֖וֹר וְהַחֲמֽוֹר׃{ס}


Blessed are you that sow beside all waters, that let the feet of the ox and the ass range freely.



## Chapter 33

###### Isaiah 33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:1)


ה֣וֹי שׁוֹדֵ֗ד וְאַתָּה֙ לֹ֣א שָׁד֔וּד וּבוֹגֵ֖ד וְלֹא־בָ֣גְדוּ ב֑וֹ כַּהֲתִֽמְךָ֤ שׁוֹדֵד֙ תּוּשַּׁ֔ד כַּנְּלֹתְךָ֥ לִבְגֹּ֖ד יִבְגְּדוּ־בָֽךְ׃{ס}


Woe to the spoiler, and thou wast not spoiled; and the traitor, and they dealt not treacherously with thee! when thou shalt cease to spoil, thou shalt be spoiled; and when thou shalt make an end of betraying, they shall betray thee.


###### Isaiah 33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:2)


יְהֹוָ֥ה חׇנֵּ֖נוּ לְךָ֣ קִוִּ֑ינוּ הֱיֵ֤ה זְרֹעָם֙ לַבְּקָרִ֔ים אַף־יְשׁוּעָתֵ֖נוּ בְּעֵ֥ת צָרָֽה׃


O Lord, be gracious to us; we have waited for Thee: be Thou their arm every morning, our salvation also in the time of trouble.


###### Isaiah 33:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:3)


מִקּ֣וֹל הָמ֔וֹן נָדְד֖וּ עַמִּ֑ים מֵר֣וֹמְמֻתֶ֔ךָ נָפְצ֖וּ גּוֹיִֽם׃


At the noise of the tumult the people fled; at the lifting up of Thyself the nations were scattered.


###### Isaiah 33:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:4)


וְאֻסַּ֣ף שְׁלַלְכֶ֔ם אֹ֖סֶף הֶחָסִ֑יל כְּמַשַּׁ֥ק גֵּבִ֖ים שֹׁקֵ֥ק בּֽוֹ׃


And your spoil shall be gathered like the gathering of the caterpillar: as the running of locusts shall he rove about upon it.


###### Isaiah 33:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:5)


נִשְׂגָּ֣ב יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּ֥י שֹׁכֵ֖ן מָר֑וֹם מִלֵּ֣א צִיּ֔וֹן מִשְׁפָּ֖ט וּצְדָקָֽה׃


The Lord is exalted; for he dwells on high: he has filled Żiyyon with judgment and righteousness.


###### Isaiah 33:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:6)


וְהָיָה֙ אֱמוּנַ֣ת עִתֶּ֔יךָ חֹ֥סֶן יְשׁוּעֹ֖ת חׇכְמַ֣ת וָדָ֑עַת יִרְאַ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה הִ֥יא אוֹצָרֽוֹ׃{פ}


And he shall be the stability of thy times, a store of salvation, wisdom and know­ledge: the fear of the Lord is his treasure.


###### Isaiah 33:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:7)


הֵ֚ן אֶרְאֶלָּ֔ם צָעֲק֖וּ חֻ֑צָה מַלְאֲכֵ֣י שָׁל֔וֹם מַ֖ר יִבְכָּיֽוּן׃


Behold, the mighty ones shall cry outside: ambassadors of peace shall weep bitterly.


###### Isaiah 33:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:8)


נָשַׁ֣מּוּ מְסִלּ֔וֹת שָׁבַ֖ת עֹבֵ֣ר אֹ֑רַח הֵפֵ֤ר בְּרִית֙ מָאַ֣ס עָרִ֔ים לֹ֥א חָשַׁ֖ב אֱנֽוֹשׁ׃


The highways lie waste, the wayfaring man ceases: he has broken the covenant, he has despised the cities, he regards no man.


###### Isaiah 33:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:9)


אָבַ֤ל אֻמְלְלָה֙ אָ֔רֶץ הֶחְפִּ֥יר לְבָנ֖וֹן קָמַ֑ל הָיָ֤ה הַשָּׁרוֹן֙ כָּֽעֲרָבָ֔ה וְנֹעֵ֥ר בָּשָׁ֖ן וְכַרְמֶֽל׃{ס}


The earth mourns and languishes: Levanon is ashamed and withers: the Sharon is like the ῾Arava; and Bashan and Karmel shake off their leaves.


###### Isaiah 33:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:10)


עַתָּ֥ה אָק֖וּם יֹאמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה עַתָּה֙ אֵֽרוֹמָ֔ם עַתָּ֖ה אֶנָּשֵֽׂא׃


Now will I rise, says the Lord; now will I be exalted; now will I lift up myself.


###### Isaiah 33:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:11)


תַּהֲר֥וּ חֲשַׁ֖שׁ תֵּ֣לְדוּ קַ֑שׁ רוּחֲכֶ֕ם אֵ֖שׁ תֹּאכַלְכֶֽם׃


You shall conceive chaff, you shall bring forth stubble: your breath is a fire that shall devour you.


###### Isaiah 33:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:12)


וְהָי֥וּ עַמִּ֖ים מִשְׂרְפ֣וֹת שִׂ֑יד קוֹצִ֥ים כְּסוּחִ֖ים בָּאֵ֥שׁ יִצַּֽתּוּ׃{פ}


And the people shall be as the burnings of lime: as thorns cut down that are burned in the fire.


###### Isaiah 33:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:13)


שִׁמְע֥וּ רְחוֹקִ֖ים אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָשִׂ֑יתִי וּדְע֥וּ קְרוֹבִ֖ים גְּבֻרָתִֽי׃


Hear, you that are far off, what I have done; and, you that are near, acknowledge my might.


###### Isaiah 33:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:14)


פָּחֲד֤וּ בְצִיּוֹן֙ חַטָּאִ֔ים אָחֲזָ֥ה רְעָדָ֖ה חֲנֵפִ֑ים מִ֣י**׀** יָג֣וּר לָ֗נוּ אֵ֚שׁ אֽוֹכֵלָ֔ה מִֽי־יָג֥וּר לָ֖נוּ מוֹקְדֵ֥י עוֹלָֽם׃


The sinners in Żiyyon are afraid; fearfulness has surprised the flatterers. Who among us shall dwell with the devouring fire? who among us shall dwell with everlasting burnings?


###### Isaiah 33:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:15)


הֹלֵ֣ךְ צְדָק֔וֹת וְדֹבֵ֖ר מֵישָׁרִ֑ים מֹאֵ֞ס בְּבֶ֣צַע מַעֲשַׁקּ֗וֹת נֹעֵ֤ר כַּפָּיו֙ מִתְּמֹ֣ךְ בַּשֹּׁ֔חַד אֹטֵ֤ם אׇזְנוֹ֙ מִשְּׁמֹ֣עַ דָּמִ֔ים וְעֹצֵ֥ם עֵינָ֖יו מֵרְא֥וֹת בְּרָֽע׃


He that walks righteously, and speaks uprightly; he that despises the gain of oppressions, that shakes his hands from holding of bribes, that stops his ears from hearing of blood, and shuts his eyes from seeing evil;


###### Isaiah 33:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:16)


ה֚וּא מְרוֹמִ֣ים יִשְׁכֹּ֔ן מְצָד֥וֹת סְלָעִ֖ים מִשְׂגַּבּ֑וֹ לַחְמ֣וֹ נִתָּ֔ן מֵימָ֖יו נֶאֱמָנִֽים׃


he shall dwell on high: his place of defence shall be the fortresses of rocks: bread shall be given him; his water shall be sure.


###### Isaiah 33:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:17)


מֶ֥לֶךְ בְּיׇפְי֖וֹ תֶּחֱזֶ֣ינָה עֵינֶ֑יךָ תִּרְאֶ֖ינָה אֶ֥רֶץ מַרְחַקִּֽים׃


Thy eyes shall see the king in his beauty: they shall behold the land that is very far off.


###### Isaiah 33:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:18)


לִבְּךָ֖ יֶהְגֶּ֣ה אֵימָ֑ה אַיֵּ֤ה סֹפֵר֙ אַיֵּ֣ה שֹׁקֵ֔ל אַיֵּ֖ה סֹפֵ֥ר אֶת־הַמִּגְדָּלִֽים׃


Thy heart shall muse on terror. Where is the scribe? where is the receiver? where is he that counted the towers?


###### Isaiah 33:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:19)


אֶת־עַ֥ם נוֹעָ֖ז לֹ֣א תִרְאֶ֑ה עַ֣ם עִמְקֵ֤י שָׂפָה֙ מִשְּׁמ֔וֹעַ נִלְעַ֥ג לָשׁ֖וֹן אֵ֥ין בִּינָֽה׃


Thou shalt not see the fierce people, the people of a deeper speech than thou canst understand; of a stammering tongue, that thou canst not apprehend.


###### Isaiah 33:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:20)


חֲזֵ֣ה צִיּ֔וֹן קִרְיַ֖ת מוֹעֲדֵ֑נוּ עֵינֶ֩יךָ֩ תִרְאֶ֨ינָה יְרוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם נָוֶ֣ה שַׁאֲנָ֗ן אֹ֤הֶל בַּל־יִצְעָן֙ בַּל־יִסַּ֤ע יְתֵדֹתָיו֙ לָנֶ֔צַח וְכׇל־חֲבָלָ֖יו בַּל־יִנָּתֵֽקוּ׃


Look upon Żiyyon, the city of our solemnities: thy eyes shall see Yerushalayim a quiet habitation, a tent that shall not be taken down; its pegs shall not be removed for ever, nor shall any of its cords be broken.


###### Isaiah 33:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:21)


כִּ֣י אִם־שָׁ֞ם אַדִּ֤יר יְהֹוָה֙ לָ֔נוּ מְקוֹם־נְהָרִ֥ים יְאֹרִ֖ים רַחֲבֵ֣י יָדָ֑יִם בַּל־תֵּ֤לֶךְ בּוֹ֙ אֳנִי־שַׁ֔יִט וְצִ֥י אַדִּ֖יר לֹ֥א יַעַבְרֶֽנּוּ׃


But there the Lord in majesty will be for us a place of broad rivers and streams; wherein no galley with oars can go, neither shall gallant ship be able to pass by it.


###### Isaiah 33:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:22)


כִּ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ שֹׁפְטֵ֔נוּ יְהֹוָ֖ה מְחֹקְקֵ֑נוּ יְהֹוָ֥ה מַלְכֵּ֖נוּ ה֥וּא יוֹשִׁיעֵֽנוּ׃


For the Lord is our judge, the Lord is our lawgiver, the Lord is our king; he will save us.


###### Isaiah 33:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:23)


נִטְּשׁ֖וּ חֲבָלָ֑יִךְ בַּל־יְחַזְּק֤וּ כֵן־תׇּרְנָם֙ בַּל־פָּ֣רְשׂוּ נֵ֔ס אָ֣ז חֻלַּ֤ק עַד־שָׁלָל֙ מַרְבֶּ֔ה פִּסְחִ֖ים בָּ֥זְזוּ בַֽז׃


Thy tacklings are loosed; they could not strengthen the socket of their mast, they could not spread the sail: then is the prey of a great spoil divided; the lame take the prey.


###### Isaiah 33:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/33:24)


וּבַל־יֹאמַ֥ר שָׁכֵ֖ן חָלִ֑יתִי הָעָ֛ם הַיֹּשֵׁ֥ב בָּ֖הּ נְשֻׂ֥א עָוֺֽן׃{ס}


And the inhabitant shall not say, I am sick: the people that dwell therein shall be forgiven their iniquity.



## Chapter 34

###### Isaiah 34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:1)


קִרְב֤וּ גוֹיִם֙ לִשְׁמֹ֔עַ וּלְאֻמִּ֖ים הַקְשִׁ֑יבוּ תִּשְׁמַ֤ע הָאָ֙רֶץ֙ וּמְלֹאָ֔הּ תֵּבֵ֖ל וְכׇל־צֶאֱצָאֶֽיהָ׃


Come near, O nations, to hear; and hearken, O people: let the earth hear, and all that is in it; the world, and all things that come out of it.


###### Isaiah 34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:2)


כִּ֣י קֶ֤צֶף לַֽיהֹוָה֙ עַל־כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֔ם וְחֵמָ֖ה עַל־כׇּל־צְבָאָ֑ם הֶחֱרִימָ֖ם נְתָנָ֥ם לַטָּֽבַח׃


For the Lord has indignation against all the nations, and fury against all their armies: he has utterly destroyed them, he has delivered them to the slaughter.


###### Isaiah 34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:3)


וְחַלְלֵיהֶ֣ם יֻשְׁלָ֔כוּ וּפִגְרֵיהֶ֖ם יַעֲלֶ֣ה בׇאְשָׁ֑ם וְנָמַ֥סּוּ הָרִ֖ים מִדָּמָֽם׃


Their slain also shall be cast out, and their stench shall come up out of their carcasses, and the mountains shall be melted with their blood.


###### Isaiah 34:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:4)


וְנָמַ֙קּוּ֙ כׇּל־צְבָ֣א הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם וְנָגֹ֥לּוּ כַסֵּ֖פֶר הַשָּׁמָ֑יִם וְכׇל־צְבָאָ֣ם יִבּ֔וֹל כִּנְבֹ֤ל עָלֶה֙ מִגֶּ֔פֶן וּכְנֹבֶ֖לֶת מִתְּאֵנָֽה׃


And all the host of heaven shall rot away, and the heavens shall be rolled together like a scroll: and all their host shall fall down, as the leaf falls off from the vine, and as a falling fig from the fig tree.


###### Isaiah 34:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:5)


כִּֽי־רִוְּתָ֥ה בַשָּׁמַ֖יִם חַרְבִּ֑י הִנֵּה֙ עַל־אֱד֣וֹם תֵּרֵ֔ד וְעַל־עַ֥ם חֶרְמִ֖י לְמִשְׁפָּֽט׃


For my sword is sated in heaven: behold, it shall come down upon Edom, and upon the people of my curse, to judgment.


###### Isaiah 34:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:6)


חֶ֣רֶב לַיהֹוָ֞ה מָלְאָ֥ה דָם֙ הֻדַּ֣שְׁנָה מֵחֵ֔לֶב מִדַּ֤ם כָּרִים֙ וְעַתּוּדִ֔ים מֵחֵ֖לֶב כִּלְי֣וֹת אֵילִ֑ים כִּ֣י זֶ֤בַח לַֽיהֹוָה֙ בְּבׇצְרָ֔ה וְטֶ֥בַח גָּד֖וֹל בְּאֶ֥רֶץ אֱדֽוֹם׃


The sword of the Lord is filled with blood, it is made fat with fatness: with the blood of lambs and goats, with the fat of the kidneys of rams: for the Lord has a sacrifice in Bożra, and a great slaughter in the land of Edom.


###### Isaiah 34:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:7)


וְיָרְד֤וּ רְאֵמִים֙ עִמָּ֔ם וּפָרִ֖ים עִם־אַבִּירִ֑ים וְרִוְּתָ֤ה אַרְצָם֙ מִדָּ֔ם וַעֲפָרָ֖ם מֵחֵ֥לֶב יְדֻשָּֽׁן׃


And wild oxen shall come down with them, and bullocks with bulls; and their land shall be soaked with blood, and their dust made fat with fatness.


###### Isaiah 34:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:8)


כִּ֛י י֥וֹם נָקָ֖ם לַֽיהֹוָ֑ה שְׁנַ֥ת שִׁלּוּמִ֖ים לְרִ֥יב צִיּֽוֹן׃


For it is the day of the Lord’s vengeance, and the year of recompenses for the cause of Żiyyon.


###### Isaiah 34:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:9)


וְנֶהֶפְכ֤וּ נְחָלֶ֙יהָ֙ לְזֶ֔פֶת וַעֲפָרָ֖הּ לְגׇפְרִ֑ית וְהָיְתָ֣ה אַרְצָ֔הּ לְזֶ֖פֶת בֹּעֵרָֽה׃


And its streams shall be turned into pitch, and its dust into brimstone, and its land shall become burning pitch.


###### Isaiah 34:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:10)


לַ֤יְלָה וְיוֹמָם֙ לֹ֣א תִכְבֶּ֔ה לְעוֹלָ֖ם יַעֲלֶ֣ה עֲשָׁנָ֑הּ מִדּ֤וֹר לָדוֹר֙ תֶּחֱרָ֔ב לְנֵ֣צַח נְצָחִ֔ים אֵ֥ין עֹבֵ֖ר בָּֽהּ׃


It shall not be quenched night or day; its smoke shall go up for ever: from generation to generation it shall lie waste; none shall pass through it for ever and ever.


###### Isaiah 34:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:11)


וִירֵשׁ֙וּהָ֙ קָאַ֣ת וְקִפּ֔וֹד וְיַנְשׁ֥וֹף וְעֹרֵ֖ב יִשְׁכְּנוּ־בָ֑הּ וְנָטָ֥ה עָלֶ֛יהָ קַֽו־תֹֿ֖הוּ וְאַבְנֵי־בֹֽהוּ׃


But wild birds of night shall possess it; the owl also and the raven shall dwell in it: and he shall stretch out upon it the line of confusion, and the plummet of emptiness.


###### Isaiah 34:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:12)


חֹרֶ֥יהָ וְאֵֽין־שָׁ֖ם מְלוּכָ֣ה יִקְרָ֑אוּ וְכׇל־שָׂרֶ֖יהָ יִ֥הְיוּ אָֽפֶס׃


They shall call its nobles to the kingdom, but none shall be there, and all her princes shall be nothing.


###### Isaiah 34:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:13)


וְעָלְתָ֤ה אַרְמְנֹתֶ֙יהָ֙ סִירִ֔ים קִמּ֥וֹשׂ וָח֖וֹחַ בְּמִבְצָרֶ֑יהָ וְהָֽיְתָה֙ נְוֵ֣ה תַנִּ֔ים חָצִ֖יר לִבְנ֥וֹת יַעֲנָֽה׃


And thorns shall come up in her palaces, nettles and brambles in her fortresses: and it shall be a habitation of wild dogs, and a court for owls.


###### Isaiah 34:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:14)


וּפָגְשׁ֤וּ צִיִּים֙ אֶת־אִיִּ֔ים וְשָׂעִ֖יר עַל־רֵעֵ֣הוּ יִקְרָ֑א אַךְ־שָׁם֙ הִרְגִּ֣יעָה לִּילִ֔ית וּמָצְאָ֥ה לָ֖הּ מָנֽוֹחַ׃


The wild creatures of the desert also meet with the jackals, the scops owl shall cry to his fellow; the tawny owl also shall rest there, and find for herself a place of rest.


###### Isaiah 34:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:15)


שָׁ֣מָּה קִנְּנָ֤ה קִפּוֹז֙ וַתְּמַלֵּ֔ט וּבָקְעָ֖ה וְדָגְרָ֣ה בְצִלָּ֑הּ אַךְ־שָׁ֛ם נִקְבְּצ֥וּ דַיּ֖וֹת אִשָּׁ֥ה רְעוּתָֽהּ׃


There shall the great owl make her nest, and lay, and hatch, and brood under her shadow: there shall the kites also be gathered, every one with her mate.


###### Isaiah 34:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:16)


דִּרְשׁ֨וּ מֵעַל־סֵ֤פֶר יְהֹוָה֙ וּֽקְרָ֔אוּ אַחַ֤ת מֵהֵ֙נָּה֙ לֹ֣א נֶעְדָּ֔רָה אִשָּׁ֥ה רְעוּתָ֖הּ לֹ֣א פָקָ֑דוּ כִּי־פִי֙ ה֣וּא צִוָּ֔ה וְרוּח֖וֹ ה֥וּא קִבְּצָֽן׃


Seek out of the book of the Lord, and read: none of these shall be missing, none shall want her mate; for my speech has commanded, and his spirit has gathered them.


###### Isaiah 34:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/34:17)


וְהֽוּא־הִפִּ֤יל לָהֶן֙ גּוֹרָ֔ל וְיָד֛וֹ חִלְּקַ֥תָּה לָהֶ֖ם בַּקָּ֑ו עַד־עוֹלָם֙ יִֽירָשׁ֔וּהָ לְד֥וֹר וָד֖וֹר יִשְׁכְּנוּ־בָֽהּ׃{ס}


And he has cast the lot for them, and his hand has divided it unto them by line: they shall possess it for ever, from generation to generation shall they dwell therein.



## Chapter 35

###### Isaiah 35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:1)


יְשֻׂשׂ֥וּם מִדְבָּ֖ר וְצִיָּ֑ה וְתָגֵ֧ל עֲרָבָ֛ה וְתִפְרַ֖ח כַּחֲבַצָּֽלֶת׃


The wilderness and the arid land shall be glad; and the desert shall rejoice, and blossom like the tulip.


###### Isaiah 35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:2)


פָּרֹ֨חַ תִּפְרַ֜ח וְתָגֵ֗ל אַ֚ף גִּילַ֣ת וְרַנֵּ֔ן כְּב֤וֹד הַלְּבָנוֹן֙ נִתַּן־לָ֔הּ הֲדַ֥ר הַכַּרְמֶ֖ל וְהַשָּׁר֑וֹן הֵ֛מָּה יִרְא֥וּ כְבוֹד־יְהֹוָ֖ה הֲדַ֥ר אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃{פ}


It shall blossom abundantly, and rejoice even with joy and singing: the glory of the Levanon shall be given to it, the excellency of the Karmel and the Sharon, they shall see the glory of the Lord, and the excellency of our God.


###### Isaiah 35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:3)


חַזְּק֖וּ יָדַ֣יִם רָפ֑וֹת וּבִרְכַּ֥יִם כֹּשְׁל֖וֹת אַמֵּֽצוּ׃


Strengthen the weak hands, and confirm the feeble knees.


###### Isaiah 35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:4)


אִמְרוּ֙ לְנִמְהֲרֵי־לֵ֔ב חִזְק֖וּ אַל־תִּירָ֑אוּ הִנֵּ֤ה אֱלֹֽהֵיכֶם֙ נָקָ֣ם יָב֔וֹא גְּמ֣וּל אֱלֹהִ֔ים ה֥וּא יָב֖וֹא וְיֹשַׁעֲכֶֽם׃


Say to them that are of a fearful heart, Be strong, fear not: behold, your God will come with vengeance, even with a divine recompense; he will come and save you.


###### Isaiah 35:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:5)


אָ֥ז תִּפָּקַ֖חְנָה עֵינֵ֣י עִוְרִ֑ים וְאׇזְנֵ֥י חֵרְשִׁ֖ים תִּפָּתַֽחְנָה׃


Then the eyes of the blind shall be opened, and the ears of the deaf shall be unstopped.


###### Isaiah 35:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:6)


אָ֣ז יְדַלֵּ֤ג כָּֽאַיָּל֙ פִּסֵּ֔חַ וְתָרֹ֖ן לְשׁ֣וֹן אִלֵּ֑ם כִּֽי־נִבְקְע֤וּ בַמִּדְבָּר֙ מַ֔יִם וּנְחָלִ֖ים בָּעֲרָבָֽה׃


Then shall the lame man leap as a hart and the tongue of the dumb sing: for in the wilderness shall waters break out, and streams in the desert.


###### Isaiah 35:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:7)


וְהָיָ֤ה הַשָּׁרָב֙ לַאֲגַ֔ם וְצִמָּא֖וֹן לְמַבּ֣וּעֵי מָ֑יִם בִּנְוֵ֤ה תַנִּים֙ רִבְצָ֔הּ חָצִ֖יר לְקָנֶ֥ה וָגֹֽמֶא׃


And the parched ground shall become a pool, and the thirsty land springs of water: the habitation of jackals shall become a pasture for cattle, an enclosure of reeds and rushes.


###### Isaiah 35:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:8)


וְהָיָה־שָׁ֞ם מַסְל֣וּל וָדֶ֗רֶךְ וְדֶ֤רֶךְ הַקֹּ֙דֶשׁ֙ יִקָּ֣רֵא לָ֔הּ לֹא־יַעַבְרֶ֥נּוּ טָמֵ֖א וְהוּא־לָ֑מוֹ הֹלֵ֥ךְ דֶּ֛רֶךְ וֶאֱוִילִ֖ים לֹ֥א יִתְעֽוּ׃


And a highway shall be there, and a way, and it shall be called The way of holiness: the unclean shall not pass over it; and he shall be to them a guide, and fools shall not err in it.


###### Isaiah 35:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:9)


לֹא־יִהְיֶ֨ה שָׁ֜ם אַרְיֵ֗ה וּפְרִ֤יץ חַיּוֹת֙ בַּֽל־יַעֲלֶ֔נָּה לֹ֥א תִמָּצֵ֖א שָׁ֑ם וְהָלְכ֖וּ גְּאוּלִֽים׃


No lion shall be there, nor any ravenous beast shall go up on it, they shall not be found there; but the redeemed shall walk there:


###### Isaiah 35:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/35:10)


וּפְדוּיֵ֨י יְהֹוָ֜ה יְשֻׁב֗וּן וּבָ֤אוּ צִיּוֹן֙ בְּרִנָּ֔ה וְשִׂמְחַ֥ת עוֹלָ֖ם עַל־רֹאשָׁ֑ם שָׂשׂ֤וֹן וְשִׂמְחָה֙ יַשִּׂ֔יגוּ וְנָ֖סוּ יָג֥וֹן וַאֲנָחָֽה׃{ס}


and the ransomed of the Lord shall return, and come to Żiyyon with songs and everlasting joy upon their heads: they shall obtain joy and gladness, and sorrow and sighing shall flee away.



## Chapter 36

###### Isaiah 36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:1)


וַיְהִ֡י בְּאַרְבַּע֩ עֶשְׂרֵ֨ה שָׁנָ֜ה לַמֶּ֣לֶךְ חִזְקִיָּ֗הוּ עָלָ֞ה סַנְחֵרִ֤יב מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁוּר֙ עַ֣ל כׇּל־עָרֵ֧י יְהוּדָ֛ה הַבְּצֻר֖וֹת וַֽיִּתְפְּשֵֽׂם׃


Now it came to pass in the fourteenth year of king Ḥizqiyyahu, that Sanĥeriv king of Ashshur came up against all the fortified cities of Yehuda, and took them.


###### Isaiah 36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:2)


וַיִּשְׁלַ֥ח מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֣וּר**׀** אֶת־רַבְשָׁקֵ֨ה מִלָּכִ֧ישׁ יְרוּשָׁלַ֛͏ְמָה אֶל־הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ חִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ בְּחֵ֣יל כָּבֵ֑ד וַֽיַּעֲמֹ֗ד בִּתְעָלַת֙ הַבְּרֵכָ֣ה הָעֶלְיוֹנָ֔ה בִּמְסִלַּ֖ת שְׂדֵ֥ה כוֹבֵֽס׃


And the king of Ashshur sent Ravshaqe from Lakhish to Yerushalayim to king Ḥizqiyyahu with a great army. And he stood by the aqueduct of the upper pool in the highway of the washer’s field.


###### Isaiah 36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:3)


וַיֵּצֵ֥א אֵלָ֛יו אֶלְיָקִ֥ים בֶּן־חִלְקִיָּ֖הוּ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עַל־הַבָּ֑יִת וְשֶׁבְנָא֙ הַסֹּפֵ֔ר וְיוֹאָ֥ח בֶּן־אָסָ֖ף הַמַּזְכִּֽיר׃


Then Elyaqim, Ḥilqiyyahu’s son, who was over the house, came out to him, and Shevna the scribe, and Yo᾽aĥ Asaf’s son, the recorder.


###### Isaiah 36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:4)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֲלֵיהֶם֙ רַבְשָׁקֵ֔ה אִמְרוּ־נָ֖א אֶל־חִזְקִיָּ֑הוּ כֹּֽה־אָמַ֞ר הַמֶּ֤לֶךְ הַגָּדוֹל֙ מֶ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֔וּר מָ֧ה הַבִּטָּח֛וֹן הַזֶּ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר בָּטָֽחְתָּ׃


And Ravshaqe said to them, Say now to Ḥizqiyyahu, Thus says the great king, the king of Ashshur, What confidence is this in which thou trustest?


###### Isaiah 36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:5)


אָמַ֙רְתִּי֙ אַךְ־דְּבַר־שְׂפָתַ֔יִם עֵצָ֥ה וּגְבוּרָ֖ה לַמִּלְחָמָ֑ה עַתָּה֙ עַל־מִ֣י בָטַ֔חְתָּ כִּ֥י מָרַ֖דְתָּ בִּֽי׃


I said, A mere word with the lips is thy counsel and strength for war: now on whom dost thou trust, that thou rebellest against me?


###### Isaiah 36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:6)


הִנֵּ֣ה בָטַ֡חְתָּ עַל־מִשְׁעֶ֩נֶת֩ הַקָּנֶ֨ה הָרָצ֤וּץ הַזֶּה֙ עַל־מִצְרַ֔יִם אֲשֶׁ֨ר יִסָּמֵ֥ךְ אִישׁ֙ עָלָ֔יו וּבָ֥א בְכַפּ֖וֹ וּנְקָבָ֑הּ כֵּ֚ן פַּרְעֹ֣ה מֶלֶךְ־מִצְרַ֔יִם לְכׇֽל־הַבֹּטְחִ֖ים עָלָֽיו׃


Lo, thou trustest in the staff of this broken reed, in Miżrayim; on which if a man lean, it will go into his arm, and pierce it: so is Par῾o king of Miżrayim to all that trust in him.


###### Isaiah 36:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:7)


וְכִֽי־תֹאמַ֣ר אֵלַ֔י אֶל־יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ בָּטָ֑חְנוּ הֲלוֹא־ה֗וּא אֲשֶׁ֨ר הֵסִ֤יר חִזְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ אֶת־בָּמֹתָ֣יו וְאֶת־מִזְבְּחֹתָ֔יו וַיֹּ֤אמֶר לִיהוּדָה֙ וְלִיר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם לִפְנֵ֛י הַמִּזְבֵּ֥חַ הַזֶּ֖ה תִּֽשְׁתַּחֲוֽוּ׃


But if thou say to me, We trust in the Lord our God: is it not he, whose high places and whose altars Ḥizqiyyahu has taken away, and said to Yehuda and to Yerushalayim, You shall worship before this altar?


###### Isaiah 36:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:8)


וְעַתָּה֙ הִתְעָ֣רֶב נָ֔א אֶת־אֲדֹנִ֖י הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֑וּר וְאֶתְּנָ֤ה לְךָ֙ אַלְפַּ֣יִם סוּסִ֔ים אִם־תּוּכַ֕ל לָ֥תֶת לְךָ֖ רֹכְבִ֥ים עֲלֵיהֶֽם׃


Now therefore make a wager, I pray thee, with my master the king of Ashshur, and I will give thee two thousand horses, if thou be able on thy part to set riders upon them.


###### Isaiah 36:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:9)


וְאֵ֣יךְ תָּשִׁ֗יב אֵ֠ת פְּנֵ֨י פַחַ֥ת אַחַ֛ד עַבְדֵ֥י אֲדֹנִ֖י הַקְּטַנִּ֑ים וַתִּבְטַ֤ח לְךָ֙ עַל־מִצְרַ֔יִם לְרֶ֖כֶב וּלְפָרָשִֽׁים׃


How then wilt thou turn away the face of one officer of the least of my master’s servants, and put thy trust in Miżrayim for chariots and for horsemen?


###### Isaiah 36:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:10)


וְעַתָּה֙ הֲמִבַּלְעֲדֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה עָלִ֛יתִי עַל־הָאָ֥רֶץ הַזֹּ֖את לְהַשְׁחִיתָ֑הּ יְהֹוָה֙ אָמַ֣ר אֵלַ֔י עֲלֵ֛ה אֶל־הָאָ֥רֶץ הַזֹּ֖את וְהַשְׁחִיתָֽהּ׃{ס}


And am I now come up without the Lord against this land to destroy it? it is the Lord who said to me, Go up against this land, and destroy it.


###### Isaiah 36:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:11)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר אֶלְיָקִים֩ וְשֶׁבְנָ֨א וְיוֹאָ֜ח אֶל־רַבְשָׁקֵ֗ה דַּבֶּר־נָ֤א אֶל־עֲבָדֶ֙יךָ֙ אֲרָמִ֔ית כִּ֥י שֹׁמְעִ֖ים אֲנָ֑חְנוּ וְאַל־תְּדַבֵּ֤ר אֵלֵ֙ינוּ֙ יְהוּדִ֔ית בְּאׇזְנֵ֣י הָעָ֔ם אֲשֶׁ֖ר עַל־הַחוֹמָֽה׃


Then said Elyaqim and Shevna and Yo᾽aĥ to Ravshaqe, Speak, I pray thee, to thy servants in the tongue of Aram: for we understand it: and speak not to us in the language of Yehuda, in the hearing of the people that are on the wall.


###### Isaiah 36:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:12)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר רַבְשָׁקֵ֗ה הַאֶ֨ל אֲדֹנֶ֤יךָ וְאֵלֶ֙יךָ֙ שְׁלָחַ֣נִי אֲדֹנִ֔י לְדַבֵּ֖ר אֶת־הַדְּבָרִ֣ים הָאֵ֑לֶּה הֲלֹ֣א עַל־הָאֲנָשִׁ֗ים הַיֹּֽשְׁבִים֙ עַל־הַ֣חוֹמָ֔ה לֶאֱכֹ֣ל אֶת־[צוֹאָתָ֗ם] (חראיהם) וְלִשְׁתּ֛וֹת אֶת־[מֵימֵ֥י רַגְלֵיהֶ֖ם] (שיניהם) עִמָּכֶֽם׃


But Ravshaqe said, Has my master sent me to thy master and to thee to speak these words? has he not sent me to the men that sit upon the wall, that they may eat their own dung, and drink their own urine with you?


###### Isaiah 36:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:13)


וַֽיַּעֲמֹד֙ רַבְשָׁקֵ֔ה וַיִּקְרָ֥א בְקוֹל־גָּד֖וֹל יְהוּדִ֑ית וַיֹּ֕אמֶר שִׁמְע֗וּ אֶת־דִּבְרֵ֛י הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ הַגָּד֖וֹל מֶ֥לֶךְ אַשּֽׁוּר׃


Then Ravshaqe stood, and cried with a loud voice in the language of Yehuda, and said, Hear the words of the great king, the king of Ashshur.


###### Isaiah 36:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:14)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר הַמֶּ֔לֶךְ אַל־יַשִּׁ֥א לָכֶ֖ם חִזְקִיָּ֑הוּ כִּ֥י לֹֽא־יוּכַ֖ל לְהַצִּ֥יל אֶתְכֶֽם׃


Thus says the king, Let not Ḥizqiyyahu deceive you: for he shall not be able to deliver you.


###### Isaiah 36:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:15)


וְאַל־יַבְטַ֨ח אֶתְכֶ֤ם חִזְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ אֶל־יְהֹוָ֣ה לֵאמֹ֔ר הַצֵּ֥ל יַצִּילֵ֖נוּ יְהֹוָ֑ה לֹ֤א תִנָּתֵן֙ הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֔את בְּיַ֖ד מֶ֥לֶךְ אַשּֽׁוּר׃


Neither let Ḥizqiyyahu make you trust in the Lord, saying, The Lord will surely deliver us: this city shall not be delivered into the hand of the king of Ashshur.


###### Isaiah 36:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:16)


אַֽל־תִּשְׁמְע֖וּ אֶל־חִזְקִיָּ֑הוּ{פ}
כִּי֩ כֹ֨ה אָמַ֜ר הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ אַשּׁ֗וּר עֲשֽׂוּ־אִתִּ֤י בְרָכָה֙ וּצְא֣וּ אֵלַ֔י וְאִכְל֤וּ אִישׁ־גַּפְנוֹ֙ וְאִ֣ישׁ תְּאֵנָת֔וֹ וּשְׁת֖וּ אִ֥ישׁ מֵי־בוֹרֽוֹ׃


Hearken not to Ḥizqiyyahu: for thus says the king of Ashshur, Make an agreement with me, and come out to me: and then you may eat every one of his own vine, and every one of his fig tree, and drink every one the waters of his own cistern;


###### Isaiah 36:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:17)


עַד־בֹּאִ֕י וְלָקַחְתִּ֥י אֶתְכֶ֖ם אֶל־אֶ֣רֶץ כְּאַרְצְכֶ֑ם אֶ֤רֶץ דָּגָן֙ וְתִיר֔וֹשׁ אֶ֥רֶץ לֶ֖חֶם וּכְרָמִֽים׃


until I come and take you away to a land like your own land, a land of corn and wine, a land of bread and vineyards.


###### Isaiah 36:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:18)


פֶּן־יַסִּ֨ית אֶתְכֶ֤ם חִזְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר יְהֹוָ֖ה יַצִּילֵ֑נוּ הַהִצִּ֜ילוּ אֱלֹהֵ֤י הַגּוֹיִם֙ אִ֣ישׁ אֶת־אַרְצ֔וֹ מִיַּ֖ד מֶ֥לֶךְ אַשּֽׁוּר׃


Beware lest Ḥizqiyyahu persuade you, saying, The Lord will deliver us. Has any of the gods of the nations delivered his land out of the hand of the king of Ashshur?


###### Isaiah 36:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:19)


אַיֵּ֞ה אֱלֹהֵ֤י חֲמָת֙ וְאַרְפָּ֔ד אַיֵּ֖ה אֱלֹהֵ֣י סְפַרְוָ֑יִם וְכִֽי־הִצִּ֥ילוּ אֶת־שֹׁמְר֖וֹן מִיָּדִֽי׃


Where are the gods of Ḥamat and Arpad? where are the gods of Sefarvayim? and have they delivered Shomeron out of my hand?


###### Isaiah 36:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:20)


מִ֗י בְּכׇל־אֱלֹהֵ֤י הָאֲרָצוֹת֙ הָאֵ֔לֶּה אֲשֶׁר־הִצִּ֥ילוּ אֶת־אַרְצָ֖ם מִיָּדִ֑י כִּֽי־יַצִּ֧יל יְהֹוָ֛ה אֶת־יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם מִיָּדִֽי׃


Which of the gods of these lands, have delivered their land out of my hand, that the Lord should deliver Yerushalayim out of my hand?


###### Isaiah 36:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:21)


וַֽיַּחֲרִ֔ישׁוּ וְלֹא־עָנ֥וּ אֹת֖וֹ דָּבָ֑ר כִּֽי־מִצְוַ֨ת הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ הִ֛יא לֵאמֹ֖ר לֹ֥א תַעֲנֻֽהוּ׃


But they held their peace, and answered him not a word: for the king’s commandment was, saying, Answer him not.


###### Isaiah 36:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/36:22)


וַיָּבֹ֣א אֶלְיָקִ֣ים בֶּן־חִלְקִיָּ֣הוּ אֲשֶׁר־עַל־הַ֠בַּ֠יִת וְשֶׁבְנָ֨א הַסֹּפֵ֜ר וְיוֹאָ֨ח בֶּן־אָסָ֧ף הַמַּזְכִּ֛יר אֶל־חִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ קְרוּעֵ֣י בְגָדִ֑ים וַיַּגִּ֣ידוּ ל֔וֹ אֵ֖ת דִּבְרֵ֥י רַבְשָׁקֵֽה׃{ס}


Then Elyaqim, the son of Ḥilqiyyahu, that was over the household, and Shevna the scribe, and Yo᾽aĥ, the son of Asaf, the recorder, came to Ḥizqiyyahu with their clothes rent, and told him the words of Ravshaqe.



## Chapter 37

###### Isaiah 37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:1)


וַֽיְהִ֗י כִּשְׁמֹ֙עַ֙ הַמֶּ֣לֶךְ חִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ וַיִּקְרַ֖ע אֶת־בְּגָדָ֑יו וַיִּתְכַּ֣ס בַּשָּׂ֔ק וַיָּבֹ֖א בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


And it came to pass, when king Ḥizqiyyahu heard it, that he rent his clothes, and covered himself with sackcloth, and went into the house of the Lord.


###### Isaiah 37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:2)


וַ֠יִּשְׁלַ֠ח אֶת־אֶלְיָקִ֨ים אֲשֶׁר־עַל־הַבַּ֜יִת וְאֵ֣ת**׀** שֶׁבְנָ֣א הַסּוֹפֵ֗ר וְאֵת֙ זִקְנֵ֣י הַכֹּהֲנִ֔ים מִתְכַּסִּ֖ים בַּשַּׂקִּ֑ים אֶל־יְשַׁעְיָ֥הוּ בֶן־אָמ֖וֹץ הַנָּבִֽיא׃


And he sent Elyaqim, who was over the household, and Shevna the scribe, and the elders of the priests covered with sackcloth, to Yesha῾yahu the prophet the son of Amoż.


###### Isaiah 37:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:3)


וַיֹּאמְר֣וּ אֵלָ֗יו כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר חִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ יוֹם־צָרָ֧ה וְתוֹכֵחָ֛ה וּנְאָצָ֖ה הַיּ֣וֹם הַזֶּ֑ה כִּ֣י בָ֤אוּ בָנִים֙ עַד־מַשְׁבֵּ֔ר וְכֹ֥חַ אַ֖יִן לְלֵדָֽה׃


And they said to him, Thus says Ḥizqiyyahu, This day is a day of trouble, and of reviling, and of blasphemy: for the children are come to the birth, and there is not strength to bring forth.


###### Isaiah 37:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:4)


אוּלַ֡י יִשְׁמַע֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֱלֹהֶ֜יךָ אֵ֣ת**׀** דִּבְרֵ֣י רַבְשָׁקֵ֗ה אֲשֶׁר֩ שְׁלָח֨וֹ מֶלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֤וּר׀אֲדֹנָיו֙ לְחָרֵף֙ אֱלֹהִ֣ים חַ֔י וְהוֹכִ֙יחַ֙ בַּדְּבָרִ֔ים אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׁמַ֖ע יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ וְנָשָׂ֣אתָ תְפִלָּ֔ה בְּעַ֥ד הַשְּׁאֵרִ֖ית הַנִּמְצָאָֽה׃


It may be the Lord thy God will hear the words of Ravshaqe, whom the king of Ashshur his master has sent to taunt the living God, and to revile with words as the Lord thy God has heard: wherefore send up a prayer for the remnant that is left.


###### Isaiah 37:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:5)


וַיָּבֹ֗אוּ עַבְדֵ֛י הַמֶּ֥לֶךְ חִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ אֶֽל־יְשַׁעְיָֽהוּ׃


So the servants of king Ḥizqiyyahu came to Yesha῾yahu.


###### Isaiah 37:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:6)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר אֲלֵיהֶם֙ יְשַֽׁעְיָ֔הוּ כֹּ֥ה תֹאמְר֖וּן אֶל־אֲדֹנֵיכֶ֑ם כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אַל־תִּירָא֙ מִפְּנֵ֤י הַדְּבָרִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר שָׁמַ֔עְתָּ אֲשֶׁ֧ר גִּדְּפ֛וּ נַעֲרֵ֥י מֶלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֖וּר אוֹתִֽי׃


And Yesha῾yahu said to them, Thus shall you say to your master, Thus says the Lord, Be not afraid of the words that thou hast heard, with which the servants of the king of Ashshur have blasphemed me.


###### Isaiah 37:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:7)


הִנְנִ֨י נוֹתֵ֥ן בּוֹ֙ ר֔וּחַ וְשָׁמַ֥ע שְׁמוּעָ֖ה וְשָׁ֣ב אֶל־אַרְצ֑וֹ וְהִפַּלְתִּ֥יו בַּחֶ֖רֶב בְּאַרְצֽוֹ׃

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:35)


וְגַנּוֹתִ֛י עַל־הָעִ֥יר הַזֹּ֖את לְהֽוֹשִׁיעָ֑הּ לְמַֽעֲנִ֔י וּלְמַ֖עַן דָּוִ֥ד עַבְדִּֽי׃{ס}


For I will defend this city to save it for my own sake, and for my servant David’s sake.


###### Isaiah 37:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:36)


וַיֵּצֵ֣א**׀** מַלְאַ֣ךְ יְהֹוָ֗ה וַיַּכֶּה֙ בְּמַחֲנֵ֣ה אַשּׁ֔וּר מֵאָ֛ה וּשְׁמֹנִ֥ים וַחֲמִשָּׁ֖ה אָ֑לֶף וַיַּשְׁכִּ֣ימוּ בַבֹּ֔קֶר וְהִנֵּ֥ה כֻלָּ֖ם פְּגָרִ֥ים מֵתִֽים׃


Then the angel of the Lord went forth, and smote in the camp of Ashshur a hundred and eighty five thousand: and when they arose early in the morning, behold, they were all dead corpses.


###### Isaiah 37:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:37)


וַיִּסַּ֣ע וַיֵּ֔לֶךְ וַיָּ֖שׇׁב סַנְחֵרִ֣יב מֶלֶךְ־אַשּׁ֑וּר וַיֵּ֖שֶׁב בְּנִֽינְוֵֽה׃


So Sanĥeriv king of Ashshur departed, and went and returned, and dwelt at Nineve.


###### Isaiah 37:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/37:38)


וַיְהִי֩ ה֨וּא מִֽשְׁתַּחֲוֶ֜ה בֵּ֣ית**׀** נִסְרֹ֣ךְ אֱלֹהָ֗יו וְֽאַדְרַמֶּ֨לֶךְ וְשַׂרְאֶ֤צֶר בָּנָיו֙ הִכֻּ֣הוּ בַחֶ֔רֶב וְהֵ֥מָּה נִמְלְט֖וּ אֶ֣רֶץ אֲרָרָ֑ט וַיִּמְלֹ֛ךְ אֵסַר־חַדֹּ֥ן בְּנ֖וֹ תַּחְתָּֽיו׃{ס}


And it came to pass, as he was worshipping in the house of Nisrokh his god, that Adrammelekh and Shar᾽eżer his sons smote him with the sword; and they escaped into the land of Ararat: and Esar-ĥaddon his son reigned in his stead.



## Chapter 38

###### Isaiah 38:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:1)


בַּיָּמִ֣ים הָהֵ֔ם חָלָ֥ה חִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ לָמ֑וּת וַיָּב֣וֹא אֵ֠לָ֠יו יְשַֽׁעְיָ֨הוּ בֶן־אָמ֜וֹץ הַנָּבִ֗יא וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֵלָ֜יו כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ צַ֣ו לְבֵיתֶ֔ךָ כִּ֛י מֵ֥ת אַתָּ֖ה וְלֹ֥א תִֽחְיֶֽה׃


In those days Ḥizqiyyahu fell mortally sick. And Yesha῾yahu the prophet, the son of Amoż came to him, and said to him, Thus says the Lord, Set thy house in order: for thou shalt die, and not live.


###### Isaiah 38:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:2)


וַיַּסֵּ֧ב חִזְקִיָּ֛הוּ פָּנָ֖יו אֶל־הַקִּ֑יר וַיִּתְפַּלֵּ֖ל אֶל־יְהֹוָֽה׃


Then Ḥizqiyyahu turned his face toward the wall, and prayed to the Lord,


###### Isaiah 38:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:3)


וַיֹּאמַ֗ר אָנָּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ זְכׇר־נָ֞א אֵ֣ת אֲשֶׁ֧ר הִתְהַלַּ֣כְתִּי לְפָנֶ֗יךָ בֶּֽאֱמֶת֙ וּבְלֵ֣ב שָׁלֵ֔ם וְהַטּ֥וֹב בְּעֵינֶ֖יךָ עָשִׂ֑יתִי וַיֵּ֥בְךְּ חִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ בְּכִ֥י גָדֽוֹל׃{ס}


and said, Remember now, O Lord, I beseech Thee, how I have walked before Thee in truth and with a perfect heart, and have done that which is good in Thy sight. And Ḥizqiyyahu wept sore.


###### Isaiah 38:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:4)


וַֽיְהִי֙ דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶֽל־יְשַֽׁעְיָ֖הוּ לֵאמֹֽר׃


Then came the word of the Lord to Yesha῾yahu, saying,


###### Isaiah 38:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:5)


הָל֞וֹךְ וְאָמַרְתָּ֣ אֶל־חִזְקִיָּ֗הוּ כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ אֱלֹהֵי֙ דָּוִ֣ד אָבִ֔יךָ שָׁמַ֙עְתִּי֙ אֶת־תְּפִלָּתֶ֔ךָ רָאִ֖יתִי אֶת־דִּמְעָתֶ֑ךָ הִנְנִי֙ יוֹסִ֣ף עַל־יָמֶ֔יךָ חֲמֵ֥שׁ עֶשְׂרֵ֖ה שָׁנָֽה׃


Go, and say to Ḥizqiyyahu, Thus says the Lord, the God of David thy father, I have heard thy prayer, I have seen thy tears: behold, I will add to thy days fifteen years.


###### Isaiah 38:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:6)


וּמִכַּ֤ף מֶֽלֶךְ־אַשּׁוּר֙ אַצִּ֣ילְךָ֔ וְאֵ֖ת הָעִ֣יר הַזֹּ֑את וְגַנּוֹתִ֖י עַל־הָעִ֥יר הַזֹּֽאת׃


And I will deliver thee and this city out of the hand of the king of Ashshur: and I will defend this city.


###### Isaiah 38:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:7)


וְזֶה־לְּךָ֥ הָא֖וֹת מֵאֵ֣ת יְהֹוָ֑ה אֲשֶׁר֙ יַעֲשֶׂ֣ה יְהֹוָ֔ה אֶת־הַדָּבָ֥ר הַזֶּ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר דִּבֵּֽר׃


And this shall be a sign to thee from the Lord, that the Lord will do this thing that he has spoken:


###### Isaiah 38:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:8)


הִנְנִ֣י מֵשִׁ֣יב אֶת־צֵ֣ל הַֽמַּעֲל֡וֹת אֲשֶׁ֣ר יָרְדָה֩ בְמַעֲל֨וֹת אָחָ֥ז בַּשֶּׁ֛מֶשׁ אֲחֹרַנִּ֖ית עֶ֣שֶׂר מַעֲל֑וֹת וַתָּ֤שׇׁב הַשֶּׁ֙מֶשׁ֙ עֶ֣שֶׂר מַעֲל֔וֹת בַּֽמַּעֲל֖וֹת אֲשֶׁ֥ר יָרָֽדָה׃{ס}


behold, I will turn the shadow of the dial, which is gone down in the sun dial of Aĥaz, ten degrees backward. So the sun returned ten degrees, by which it had gone down on the dial.


###### Isaiah 38:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:9)


מִכְתָּ֖ב לְחִזְקִיָּ֣הוּ מֶלֶךְ־יְהוּדָ֑ה בַּחֲלֹת֕וֹ וַיְחִ֖י מֵחׇלְיֽוֹ׃


The writing of Ḥizqiyyahu king of Yehuda when he had been sick, and was recovered of his sickness:


###### Isaiah 38:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:10)


אֲנִ֣י אָמַ֗רְתִּי בִּדְמִ֥י יָמַ֛י אֵלֵ֖כָה בְּשַׁעֲרֵ֣י שְׁא֑וֹל פֻּקַּ֖דְתִּי יֶ֥תֶר שְׁנוֹתָֽי׃


I said in the noontide of my days, I shall go to the gates of She᾽ol: I am deprived of the residue of my years.


###### Isaiah 38:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:11)


אָמַ֙רְתִּי֙ לֹא־אֶרְאֶ֣ה יָ֔הּ יָ֖הּ בְּאֶ֣רֶץ הַחַיִּ֑ים לֹא־אַבִּ֥יט אָדָ֛ם ע֖וֹד עִם־י֥וֹשְׁבֵי חָֽדֶל׃


I said, I shall not see the Lord, the Lord, in the land of the living: I shall behold man no more with the inhabitants of the world.


###### Isaiah 38:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:12)


דּוֹרִ֗י נִסַּ֧ע וְנִגְלָ֛ה מִנִּ֖י כְּאֹ֣הֶל רֹעִ֑י קִפַּ֨דְתִּי כָאֹרֵ֤ג חַיַּי֙ מִדַּלָּ֣ה יְבַצְּעֵ֔נִי מִיּ֥וֹם עַד־לַ֖יְלָה תַּשְׁלִימֵֽנִי׃


My dwelling is plucked up and is removed from me like a shepherd’s tent: I have rolled up my life like a weaver: he will cut me off from the loom: from day to night Thou dost make an end of me.


###### Isaiah 38:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:13)


שִׁוִּ֤יתִי עַד־בֹּ֙קֶר֙ כָּאֲרִ֔י כֵּ֥ן יְשַׁבֵּ֖ר כׇּל־עַצְמוֹתָ֑י מִיּ֥וֹם עַד־לַ֖יְלָה תַּשְׁלִימֵֽנִי׃


I wait for morning; as a lion, even so he breaks all my bones: from day to night dost thou make an end of me.


###### Isaiah 38:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:14)


כְּס֤וּס עָגוּר֙ כֵּ֣ן אֲצַפְצֵ֔ף אֶהְגֶּ֖ה כַּיּוֹנָ֑ה דַּלּ֤וּ עֵינַי֙ לַמָּר֔וֹם אֲדֹנָ֖י עָֽשְׁקָה־לִּ֥י עׇרְבֵֽנִי׃


Like a swift or a crane, even so do I chirp: I moan like a dove: my eyes fail with looking upwards: O Lord, I am oppressed, be Thou my security.


###### Isaiah 38:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:15)


מָֽה־אֲדַבֵּ֥ר וְאָֽמַר־לִ֖י וְה֣וּא עָשָׂ֑ה אֶדַּדֶּ֥ה כׇל־שְׁנוֹתַ֖י עַל־מַ֥ר נַפְשִֽׁי׃


What shall I say? he has both spoken to me, and himself has done it: I shall walk softly all my years for the bitterness of my soul.


###### Isaiah 38:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:16)


אֲדֹנָ֖י עֲלֵיהֶ֣ם יִֽחְי֑וּ וּלְכׇל־בָּהֶן֙ חַיֵּ֣י רוּחִ֔י וְתַחֲלִימֵ֖נִי וְהַחֲיֵֽנִי׃


O Lord, with these things men live! and in all these is the life of my spirit: wherefore restore me and make me live.


###### Isaiah 38:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:17)


הִנֵּ֥ה לְשָׁל֖וֹם מַר־לִ֣י מָ֑ר וְאַתָּ֞ה חָשַׁ֤קְתָּ נַפְשִׁי֙ מִשַּׁ֣חַת בְּלִ֔י כִּ֥י הִשְׁלַ֛כְתָּ אַחֲרֵ֥י גֵוְךָ֖ כׇּל־חֲטָאָֽי׃


Behold, for in peace I had great bitterness: but Thou hast in love to my soul delivered it, from the pit of destruction: for Thou hast cast all my sins behind Thy back.


###### Isaiah 38:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:18)


כִּ֣י לֹ֥א שְׁא֛וֹל תּוֹדֶ֖ךָּ מָ֣וֶת יְהַלְלֶ֑ךָּ לֹא־יְשַׂבְּר֥וּ יוֹרְדֵי־ב֖וֹר אֶל־אֲמִתֶּֽךָ׃


For She᾽ol cannot praise Thee, death cannot celebrate Thee: they that go down into the pit cannot hope for Thy truth.


###### Isaiah 38:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:19)


חַ֥י חַ֛י ה֥וּא יוֹדֶ֖ךָ כָּמ֣וֹנִי הַיּ֑וֹם אָ֣ב לְבָנִ֔ים יוֹדִ֖יעַ אֶל־אֲמִתֶּֽךָ׃


The living, the living, he shall praise Thee, as I do this day: the father to the children shall make known Thy truth.


###### Isaiah 38:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:20)


יְהֹוָ֖ה לְהוֹשִׁיעֵ֑נִי וּנְגִנוֹתַ֧י נְנַגֵּ֛ן כׇּל־יְמֵ֥י חַיֵּ֖ינוּ עַל־בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


The Lord was ready to save me: therefore we will sing my songs to the stringed instruments all the days of our life in the house of the Lord.


###### Isaiah 38:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:21)


וַיֹּ֣אמֶר יְשַֽׁעְיָ֔הוּ יִשְׂא֖וּ דְּבֶ֣לֶת תְּאֵנִ֑ים וְיִמְרְח֥וּ עַֽל־הַשְּׁחִ֖ין וְיֶֽחִי׃


For Yesha῾yahu had said, Let them take a cake of figs, and lay it for a plaster upon the festering place, and he shall recover.


###### Isaiah 38:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/38:22)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר חִזְקִיָּ֖הוּ מָ֣ה א֑וֹת כִּ֥י אֶעֱלֶ֖ה בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


Ḥizqiyyahu also had said, What is the sign that I shall go up to the house of the Lord?



## Chapter 39

###### Isaiah 39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:1)


בָּעֵ֣ת הַהִ֡יא שָׁלַ֡ח מְרֹדַ֣ךְ בַּ֠לְאֲדָ֠ן בֶּֽן־בַּלְאֲדָ֧ן מֶלֶךְ־בָּבֶ֛ל סְפָרִ֥ים וּמִנְחָ֖ה אֶל־חִזְקִיָּ֑הוּ וַיִּשְׁמַ֕ע כִּ֥י חָלָ֖ה וַֽיֶּחֱזָֽק׃


At that time Merodakh-bal᾽adan, the son of Bal᾽adan, king of Bavel, sent letters and a present to Ḥizqiyyahu: for he had heard that he had been sick, and was recovered.


###### Isaiah 39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:2)


וַיִּשְׂמַ֣ח עֲלֵיהֶם֮ חִזְקִיָּ֒הוּ֒ וַיַּרְאֵ֣ם אֶת־בֵּ֣ית נְכֹתֹ֡ה אֶת־הַכֶּסֶף֩ וְאֶת־הַזָּהָ֨ב וְאֶת־הַבְּשָׂמִ֜ים וְאֵ֣ת**׀** הַשֶּׁ֣מֶן הַטּ֗וֹב וְאֵת֙ כׇּל־בֵּ֣ית כֵּלָ֔יו וְאֵ֛ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֥ר נִמְצָ֖א בְּאֹצְרֹתָ֑יו לֹא־הָיָ֣ה דָבָ֗ר אֲ֠שֶׁ֠ר לֹֽא־הֶרְאָ֧ם חִזְקִיָּ֛הוּ בְּבֵית֖וֹ וּבְכׇל־מֶמְשַׁלְתּֽוֹ׃{ס}


And Ḥizqiyyahu was glad of them, and showed them the house of his treasures, the silver, and the gold, and the spices, and the precious ointment, and all the house of his armour, and all that was found in his treasures: there was nothing in his house, nor in all his dominion, that Ḥizqiyyahu did not show them.


###### Isaiah 39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:3)


וַיָּבֹא֙ יְשַֽׁעְיָ֣הוּ הַנָּבִ֔יא אֶל־הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ חִזְקִיָּ֑הוּ וַיֹּ֨אמֶר אֵלָ֜יו מָ֥ה אָמְר֣וּ**׀** הָאֲנָשִׁ֣ים הָאֵ֗לֶּה וּמֵאַ֙יִן֙ יָבֹ֣אוּ אֵלֶ֔יךָ וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ חִזְקִיָּ֔הוּ מֵאֶ֧רֶץ רְחוֹקָ֛ה בָּ֥אוּ אֵלַ֖י מִבָּבֶֽל׃


Then Yesha῾yahu the prophet came to king Ḥizqiyyahu, and said to him, What said these men? and from where did they come to thee? And Ḥizqiyyahu said, They are come from a far country to me, from Bavel.


###### Isaiah 39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:4)


וַיֹּ֕אמֶר מָ֥ה רָא֖וּ בְּבֵיתֶ֑ךָ וַיֹּ֣אמֶר חִזְקִיָּ֗הוּ אֵ֣ת כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֤ר בְּבֵיתִי֙ רָא֔וּ לֹא־הָיָ֥ה דָבָ֛ר אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־הִרְאִיתִ֖ים בְּאוֹצְרֹתָֽי׃


Then said he, What have they seen in thy house? And Ḥizqiyyahu answered, All that is in my house have they seen: there is nothing among my treasures that I did not show them.


###### Isaiah 39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:5)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר יְשַֽׁעְיָ֖הוּ אֶל־חִזְקִיָּ֑הוּ שְׁמַ֖ע דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָאֽוֹת׃


Then said Yesha῾yahu to Ḥizqiyyahu, Hear the word of the Lord of hosts:


###### Isaiah 39:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:6)


הִנֵּה֮ יָמִ֣ים בָּאִים֒ וְנִשָּׂ֣א**׀** כׇּל־אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּבֵיתֶ֗ךָ וַאֲשֶׁ֨ר אָצְר֧וּ אֲבֹתֶ֛יךָ עַד־הַיּ֥וֹם הַזֶּ֖ה בָּבֶ֑ל לֹֽא־יִוָּתֵ֥ר דָּבָ֖ר אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


Behold, days are coming that all that is in thy house, and that which thy fathers have laid up in store until this day, shall be carried to Bavel: nothing shall be left, says the Lord.


###### Isaiah 39:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:7)


וּמִבָּנֶ֜יךָ אֲשֶׁ֨ר יֵצְא֧וּ מִמְּךָ֛ אֲשֶׁ֥ר תּוֹלִ֖יד יִקָּ֑חוּ וְהָיוּ֙ סָרִיסִ֔ים בְּהֵיכַ֖ל מֶ֥לֶךְ בָּבֶֽל׃


And of thy sons that shall issue from thee, which thou shalt beget, shall they take away; and they shall be eunuchs in the palace of the king of Bavel.


###### Isaiah 39:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/39:8)


וַיֹּ֤אמֶר חִזְקִיָּ֙הוּ֙ אֶֽל־יְשַׁעְיָ֔הוּ ט֥וֹב דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֖ה אֲשֶׁ֣ר דִּבַּ֑רְתָּ וַיֹּ֕אמֶר כִּ֥י יִהְיֶ֛ה שָׁל֥וֹם וֶאֱמֶ֖ת בְּיָמָֽי׃{פ}


Then Ḥizqiyyahu said to Yesha῾yahu, Good is the word of the Lord which thou hast spoken. He said moreover, But there shall be peace and truth in my days.



## Chapter 40

###### Isaiah 40:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:1)


נַחֲמ֥וּ נַחֲמ֖וּ עַמִּ֑י יֹאמַ֖ר אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


Comfort my people, comfort them, says your God.


###### Isaiah 40:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:2)


דַּבְּר֞וּ עַל־לֵ֤ב יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ וְקִרְא֣וּ אֵלֶ֔יהָ כִּ֤י מָֽלְאָה֙ צְבָאָ֔הּ כִּ֥י נִרְצָ֖ה עֲוֺנָ֑הּ כִּ֤י לָֽקְחָה֙ מִיַּ֣ד יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּפְלַ֖יִם בְּכׇל־חַטֹּאתֶֽיהָ׃{ס}


Speak comfortably to Yerushalayim, and cry to her, that her war service is ended, that her iniquity is pardoned: for she has received of the Lord’s hand double for all her sins.


###### Isaiah 40:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:3)


ק֣וֹל קוֹרֵ֔א בַּמִּדְבָּ֕ר פַּנּ֖וּ דֶּ֣רֶךְ יְהֹוָ֑ה יַשְּׁרוּ֙ בָּעֲרָבָ֔ה מְסִלָּ֖ה לֵאלֹהֵֽינוּ׃


A voice cries, Prepare in the wilderness the way of the Lord, make straight in the desert a highway for our God.


###### Isaiah 40:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:4)


כׇּל־גֶּיא֙ יִנָּשֵׂ֔א וְכׇל־הַ֥ר וְגִבְעָ֖ה יִשְׁפָּ֑לוּ וְהָיָ֤ה הֶֽעָקֹב֙ לְמִישׁ֔וֹר וְהָרְכָסִ֖ים לְבִקְעָֽה׃


Every valley shall be exalted, and every mountain and hill shall be made low: and the crooked shall be made straight, and the rough places plain:


###### Isaiah 40:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:5)


וְנִגְלָ֖ה כְּב֣וֹד יְהֹוָ֑ה וְרָא֤וּ כׇל־בָּשָׂר֙ יַחְדָּ֔ו כִּ֛י פִּ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבֵּֽר׃{פ}


and the glory of the Lord shall be revealed, and all flesh shall see it together: for the mouth of the Lord has spoken it.


###### Isaiah 40:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:6)


ק֚וֹל אֹמֵ֣ר קְרָ֔א וְאָמַ֖ר מָ֣ה אֶקְרָ֑א כׇּל־הַבָּשָׂ֣ר חָצִ֔יר וְכׇל־חַסְדּ֖וֹ כְּצִ֥יץ הַשָּׂדֶֽה׃


A voice says, Cry, and he said what should I cry? All flesh is grass, and all its grace is as the flower of the field:


###### Isaiah 40:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:7)


יָבֵ֤שׁ חָצִיר֙ נָ֣בֵֽל־צִ֔יץ כִּ֛י ר֥וּחַ יְהֹוָ֖ה נָ֣שְׁבָה בּ֑וֹ אָכֵ֥ן חָצִ֖יר הָעָֽם׃


the grass withers, the flower fades: when the breath of the Lord blows upon it: surely the people is like grass.


###### Isaiah 40:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:8)


יָבֵ֥שׁ חָצִ֖יר נָ֣בֵֽל צִ֑יץ וּדְבַר־אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ יָק֥וּם לְעוֹלָֽם׃{ס}


The grass withers, the flower fades: but the word of our God shall stand for ever.


###### Isaiah 40:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:9)


עַ֣ל הַר־גָּבֹ֤הַּ עֲלִי־לָךְ֙ מְבַשֶּׂ֣רֶת צִיּ֔וֹן הָרִ֤ימִי בַכֹּ֙חַ֙ קוֹלֵ֔ךְ מְבַשֶּׂ֖רֶת יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם הָרִ֙ימִי֙ אַל־תִּירָ֔אִי אִמְרִי֙ לְעָרֵ֣י יְהוּדָ֔ה הִנֵּ֖ה אֱלֹהֵיכֶֽם׃


Thou that bringest good tidings to Żiyyon, get thee up into the high mountain; Thou that bringest good tidings to Yerushalayim, lift up thy voice with strength; lift it up, be not afraid; say to the cities of Yehuda, Behold your God!


###### Isaiah 40:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:10)


הִנֵּ֨ה אֲדֹנָ֤י יֱהֹוִה֙ בְּחָזָ֣ק יָב֔וֹא וּזְרֹע֖וֹ מֹ֣שְׁלָה ל֑וֹ הִנֵּ֤ה שְׂכָרוֹ֙ אִתּ֔וֹ וּפְעֻלָּת֖וֹ לְפָנָֽיו׃


Behold, the Lord God will come with might, and his arm shall rule for him: behold, his reward is with him, and his hire before him,


###### Isaiah 40:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:11)


כְּרֹעֶה֙ עֶדְר֣וֹ יִרְעֶ֔ה בִּזְרֹעוֹ֙ יְקַבֵּ֣ץ טְלָאִ֔ים וּבְחֵיק֖וֹ יִשָּׂ֑א עָל֖וֹת יְנַהֵֽל׃{ס}


He shall feed his flock like a shepherd: he shall gather the lambs with his arm, and carry them in his bosom, and shall gently lead those that are with young.


###### Isaiah 40:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:12)


מִי־מָדַ֨ד בְּשׇׁעֳל֜וֹ מַ֗יִם וְשָׁמַ֙יִם֙ בַּזֶּ֣רֶת תִּכֵּ֔ן וְכָ֥ל בַּשָּׁלִ֖שׁ עֲפַ֣ר הָאָ֑רֶץ וְשָׁקַ֤ל בַּפֶּ֙לֶס֙ הָרִ֔ים וּגְבָע֖וֹת בְּמֹאזְנָֽיִם׃


Who has measured the waters in the hollow of his hand, and meted out heaven with the span, and comprehended the dust of the earth in a measure, and weighed the mountains in scales, and the hills in a balance?


###### Isaiah 40:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:13)


מִֽי־תִכֵּ֥ן אֶת־ר֖וּחַ יְהֹוָ֑ה וְאִ֖ישׁ עֲצָת֥וֹ יוֹדִיעֶֽנּוּ׃


Who has directed the spirit of the Lord, or being his counsellor has taught him?


###### Isaiah 40:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:14)


אֶת־מִ֤י נוֹעָץ֙ וַיְבִינֵ֔הוּ וַֽיְלַמְּדֵ֖הוּ בְּאֹ֣רַח מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וַיְלַמְּדֵ֣הוּ דַ֔עַת וְדֶ֥רֶךְ תְּבוּנ֖וֹת יוֹדִיעֶֽנּוּ׃


With whom took he counsel, and who instructed him, and taught him in the path of judgment, and taught him knowledge, and showed to him the way of understanding?


###### Isaiah 40:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:15)


הֵ֤ן גּוֹיִם֙ כְּמַ֣ר מִדְּלִ֔י וּכְשַׁ֥חַק מֹאזְנַ֖יִם נֶחְשָׁ֑בוּ הֵ֥ן אִיִּ֖ים כַּדַּ֥ק יִטּֽוֹל׃


Behold, the nations are as a drop of a bucket, and are counted as the small dust of the balance: behold, he takes up the isles like fine dust.


###### Isaiah 40:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:16)


וּלְבָנ֕וֹן אֵ֥ין דֵּ֖י בָּעֵ֑ר וְחַ֨יָּת֔וֹ אֵ֥ין דֵּ֖י עוֹלָֽה׃{פ}


And Levanon is not sufficient for fuel; nor are its beasts sufficient for a burnt offering.


###### Isaiah 40:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:17)


כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֖ם כְּאַ֣יִן נֶגְדּ֑וֹ מֵאֶ֥פֶס וָתֹ֖הוּ נֶחְשְׁבוּ־לֽוֹ׃


All nations before him are as nothing; and they are counted to him less than nothing, and vanity.


###### Isaiah 40:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:18)


וְאֶל־מִ֖י תְּדַמְּי֣וּן אֵ֑ל וּמַה־דְּמ֖וּת תַּ֥עַרְכוּ לֽוֹ׃


To whom then will you liken God? or what likeness will you compare to him?


###### Isaiah 40:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:19)


הַפֶּ֙סֶל֙ נָסַ֣ךְ חָרָ֔שׁ וְצֹרֵ֖ף בַּזָּהָ֣ב יְרַקְּעֶ֑נּוּ וּרְתֻק֥וֹת כֶּ֖סֶף צוֹרֵֽף׃


As for the image, a craftsman casts it, and a refiner plates it with gold, he forges silver chains.


###### Isaiah 40:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:20)


הַֽמְסֻכָּ֣ן תְּרוּמָ֔ה עֵ֥ץ לֹא־יִרְקַ֖ב יִבְחָ֑ר חָרָ֤שׁ חָכָם֙ יְבַקֶּשׁ־ל֔וֹ לְהָכִ֥ין פֶּ֖סֶל לֹ֥א יִמּֽוֹט׃{ס}


He that is too impoverished for such a gift, chooses a tree that will not rot; he seeks to him a cunning workman to prepare a carved idol, that shall not be moved.


###### Isaiah 40:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:21)


הֲל֤וֹא תֵֽדְעוּ֙ הֲל֣וֹא תִשְׁמָ֔עוּ הֲל֛וֹא הֻגַּ֥ד מֵרֹ֖אשׁ לָכֶ֑ם הֲלוֹא֙ הֲבִ֣ינוֹתֶ֔ם מוֹסְד֖וֹת הָאָֽרֶץ׃


Do you not know? do you not hear? has it not been told you from the beginning? have you not understood from the foundations of the earth?


###### Isaiah 40:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:22)


הַיֹּשֵׁב֙ עַל־ח֣וּג הָאָ֔רֶץ וְיֹשְׁבֶ֖יהָ כַּחֲגָבִ֑ים הַנּוֹטֶ֤ה כַדֹּק֙ שָׁמַ֔יִם וַיִּמְתָּחֵ֥ם כָּאֹ֖הֶל לָשָֽׁבֶת׃


It is he that sits upon the circle of the earth, and its inhabitants are as grasshoppers; that stretches out the heavens as a curtain, and spreads them out as a tent to dwell in:


###### Isaiah 40:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:23)


הַנּוֹתֵ֥ן רוֹזְנִ֖ים לְאָ֑יִן שֹׁ֥פְטֵי אֶ֖רֶץ כַּתֹּ֥הוּ עָשָֽׂה׃


that brings princes to nothing; he makes the judges of the earth as vanity.


###### Isaiah 40:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:24)


אַ֣ף בַּל־נִטָּ֗עוּ אַ֚ף בַּל־זֹרָ֔עוּ אַ֛ף בַּל־שֹׁרֵ֥שׁ בָּאָ֖רֶץ גִּזְעָ֑ם וְגַם־נָשַׁ֤ף בָּהֶם֙ וַיִּבָ֔שׁוּ וּסְעָרָ֖ה כַּקַּ֥שׁ תִּשָּׂאֵֽם׃{ס}


Scarcely are they planted; scarcely are they sown; scarcely has their stock taken root in the earth: he merely blows upon them and they wither; and the storm wind takes them away as stubble.


###### Isaiah 40:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:25)


וְאֶל־מִ֥י תְדַמְּי֖וּנִי וְאֶשְׁוֶ֑ה יֹאמַ֖ר קָדֽוֹשׁ׃


To whom then will you liken me, that I should be his equal, says the Holy One.


###### Isaiah 40:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:26)


שְׂאוּ־מָר֨וֹם עֵינֵיכֶ֤ם וּרְאוּ֙ מִֽי־בָרָ֣א אֵ֔לֶּה הַמּוֹצִ֥יא בְמִסְפָּ֖ר צְבָאָ֑ם לְכֻלָּם֙ בְּשֵׁ֣ם יִקְרָ֔א מֵרֹ֤ב אוֹנִים֙ וְאַמִּ֣יץ כֹּ֔חַ אִ֖ישׁ לֹ֥א נֶעְדָּֽר׃{ס}


Lift up your eyes on high, and behold who has created these things, that brings out their host by number: he calls them all by names; because of the greatness of his might, and because he is strong in power, not one is missing.


###### Isaiah 40:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:27)


לָ֤מָּה תֹאמַר֙ יַעֲקֹ֔ב וּתְדַבֵּ֖ר יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל נִסְתְּרָ֤ה דַרְכִּי֙ מֵיְהֹוָ֔ה וּמֵאֱלֹהַ֖י מִשְׁפָּטִ֥י יַעֲבֽוֹר׃


Why sayst thou, O Ya῾aqov, and speakest, O Yisra᾽el, My way is hid from the Lord, and my judgment is passed over from my God?


###### Isaiah 40:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:28)


הֲל֨וֹא יָדַ֜עְתָּ אִם־לֹ֣א שָׁמַ֗עְתָּ אֱלֹהֵ֨י עוֹלָ֤ם׀יְהֹוָה֙ בּוֹרֵא֙ קְצ֣וֹת הָאָ֔רֶץ לֹ֥א יִיעַ֖ף וְלֹ֣א יִיגָ֑ע אֵ֥ין חֵ֖קֶר לִתְבוּנָתֽוֹ׃


Hast thou not known? hast thou not heard, that the everlasting God, the Lord, the Creator of the ends of the earth, faints not, nor is he weary? there is no searching of his understanding.


###### Isaiah 40:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:29)


נֹתֵ֥ן לַיָּעֵ֖ף כֹּ֑חַ וּלְאֵ֥ין אוֹנִ֖ים עׇצְמָ֥ה יַרְבֶּֽה׃


He gives power to the faint; and to the power­less he increases strength.


###### Isaiah 40:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:30)


וְיִעֲפ֥וּ נְעָרִ֖ים וְיִגָ֑עוּ וּבַחוּרִ֖ים כָּשׁ֥וֹל יִכָּשֵֽׁלוּ׃


Even the youths shall faint and be weary, and the young men shall utterly fall:


###### Isaiah 40:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/40:31)


וְקוֹיֵ֤ יְהֹוָה֙ יַחֲלִ֣יפוּ כֹ֔חַ יַעֲל֥וּ אֵ֖בֶר כַּנְּשָׁרִ֑ים יָר֙וּצוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א יִיגָ֔עוּ יֵלְכ֖וּ וְלֹ֥א יִיעָֽפוּ׃{ס}


but they that wait upon the Lord shall renew their strength; they shall mount up with wings as eagles; they shall run, and not be weary; they shall walk, and not faint.



## Chapter 41

###### Isaiah 41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:1)


הַחֲרִ֤ישׁוּ אֵלַי֙ אִיִּ֔ים וּלְאֻמִּ֖ים יַחֲלִ֣יפוּ כֹ֑חַ יִגְּשׁוּ֙ אָ֣ז יְדַבֵּ֔רוּ יַחְדָּ֖ו לַמִּשְׁפָּ֥ט נִקְרָֽבָה׃


Keep silence before me, O islands; and let the people renew their strength: let them come near; then let them speak: let us come near together to judgment.


###### Isaiah 41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:2)


מִ֤י הֵעִיר֙ מִמִּזְרָ֔ח צֶ֖דֶק יִקְרָאֵ֣הוּ לְרַגְל֑וֹ יִתֵּ֨ן לְפָנָ֤יו גּוֹיִם֙ וּמְלָכִ֣ים יַ֔רְדְּ יִתֵּ֤ן כֶּֽעָפָר֙ חַרְבּ֔וֹ כְּקַ֥שׁ נִדָּ֖ף קַשְׁתּֽוֹ׃


Who raised up one from the east whom righteousness met wherever he set his foot, gave the nations before him, and made him rule over his kings; his sword makes them as dust, his bow as driven stubble.


###### Isaiah 41:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:3)


יִרְדְּפֵ֖ם יַעֲב֣וֹר שָׁל֑וֹם אֹ֥רַח בְּרַגְלָ֖יו לֹ֥א יָבֽוֹא׃


That he pursued them, and passed on safely; even by a path that his feet have not trodden.


###### Isaiah 41:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:4)


מִֽי־פָעַ֣ל וְעָשָׂ֔ה קֹרֵ֥א הַדֹּר֖וֹת מֵרֹ֑אשׁ אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ רִאשׁ֔וֹן וְאֶת־אַחֲרֹנִ֖ים אֲנִי־הֽוּא׃


Who has wrought and done it? He who calls the generations from the beginning; I the Lord, the first; and with the last, I am he.


###### Isaiah 41:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:5)


רָא֤וּ אִיִּים֙ וְיִרָ֔אוּ קְצ֥וֹת הָאָ֖רֶץ יֶחֱרָ֑דוּ קָרְב֖וּ וַיֶּאֱתָיֽוּן׃


The isles saw it, and feared; the ends of the earth trembled; they drew near, and came.


###### Isaiah 41:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:6)


אִ֥ישׁ אֶת־רֵעֵ֖הוּ יַעְזֹ֑רוּ וּלְאָחִ֖יו יֹאמַ֥ר חֲזָֽק׃


They helped every one his neighbour; and every one said to his brother, Be of good courage.


###### Isaiah 41:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:7)


וַיְחַזֵּ֤ק חָרָשׁ֙ אֶת־צֹרֵ֔ף מַחֲלִ֥יק פַּטִּ֖ישׁ אֶת־ה֣וֹלֶם פָּ֑עַם אֹמֵ֤ר לַדֶּ֙בֶק֙ ט֣וֹב ה֔וּא וַיְחַזְּקֵ֥הוּ בְמַסְמְרִ֖ים לֹ֥א יִמּֽוֹט׃{ס}


So the carpenter encouraged the goldsmith, and he that smoothes with the hammer him that smote the anvil, saying of the soldering, It is good: and he fastened it with nails, that it should not be moved.


###### Isaiah 41:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:8)


וְאַתָּה֙ יִשְׂרָאֵ֣ל עַבְדִּ֔י יַעֲקֹ֖ב אֲשֶׁ֣ר בְּחַרְתִּ֑יךָ זֶ֖רַע אַבְרָהָ֥ם אֹהֲבִֽי׃


But thou, Yisra᾽el, art my servant, Ya῾aqov whom I have chosen, the seed of Avraham my friend.


###### Isaiah 41:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:9)


אֲשֶׁ֤ר הֶחֱזַקְתִּ֙יךָ֙ מִקְצ֣וֹת הָאָ֔רֶץ וּמֵאֲצִילֶ֖יהָ קְרָאתִ֑יךָ וָאֹ֤מַר לְךָ֙ עַבְדִּי־אַ֔תָּה בְּחַרְתִּ֖יךָ וְלֹ֥א מְאַסְתִּֽיךָ׃


Thou whom I have taken from the ends of the earth, and called thee from its farthest corners, and said to thee, Thou art my servant; I have chosen thee, and not cast thee away.


###### Isaiah 41:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:10)


אַל־תִּירָא֙ כִּ֣י עִמְּךָ־אָ֔נִי אַל־תִּשְׁתָּ֖ע כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י אֱלֹהֶ֑יךָ אִמַּצְתִּ֙יךָ֙ אַף־עֲזַרְתִּ֔יךָ אַף־תְּמַכְתִּ֖יךָ בִּימִ֥ין צִדְקִֽי׃


Fear not; for I am with thee: be not dismayed; for I am thy God: I will strengthen thee; indeed, I will help thee; moreover, I will uphold thee with the right hand of my righteousness.


###### Isaiah 41:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:11)


הֵ֤ן יֵבֹ֙שׁוּ֙ וְיִכָּ֣לְמ֔וּ כֹּ֖ל הַנֶּחֱרִ֣ים בָּ֑ךְ יִהְי֥וּ כְאַ֛יִן וְיֹאבְד֖וּ אַנְשֵׁ֥י רִיבֶֽךָ׃


Behold, all they that were incensed against thee shall be ashamed and confounded: they shall be as nothing; and they that strive with thee shall perish.


###### Isaiah 41:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:12)


תְּבַקְשֵׁם֙ וְלֹ֣א תִמְצָאֵ֔ם אַנְשֵׁ֖י מַצֻּתֶ֑ךָ יִהְי֥וּ כְאַ֛יִן וּכְאֶ֖פֶס אַנְשֵׁ֥י מִלְחַמְתֶּֽךָ׃


Thou shalt seek them, and shalt not find them, those that contend with thee: they that war against thee shall be as nothing, and as a thing of nought.


###### Isaiah 41:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:13)


כִּ֗י אֲנִ֛י יְהֹוָ֥ה אֱלֹהֶ֖יךָ מַחֲזִ֣יק יְמִינֶ֑ךָ הָאֹמֵ֥ר לְךָ֛ אַל־תִּירָ֖א אֲנִ֥י עֲזַרְתִּֽיךָ׃{ס}


For I, the Lord thy God will hold thy right hand, saying to thee, Fear not; I will help thee.


###### Isaiah 41:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:14)


אַל־תִּֽירְאִי֙ תּוֹלַ֣עַת יַעֲקֹ֔ב מְתֵ֖י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אֲנִ֤י עֲזַרְתִּיךְ֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְגֹאֲלֵ֖ךְ קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


Fear not, thou worm Ya῾aqov, O men of Yisra᾽el; I will help thee, says the Lord, and thy redeemer, the Holy One of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 41:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:15)


הִנֵּ֣ה שַׂמְתִּ֗יךְ לְמוֹרַג֙ חָר֣וּץ חָדָ֔שׁ בַּ֖עַל פִּיפִיּ֑וֹת תָּד֤וּשׁ הָרִים֙ וְתָדֹ֔ק וּגְבָע֖וֹת כַּמֹּ֥ץ תָּשִֽׂים׃


Behold, I will make of thee a new sharp threshing instrument having teeth: thou shalt thresh the mountains, and beat them small, and shalt make the hills as chaff.


###### Isaiah 41:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:16)


תִּזְרֵם֙ וְר֣וּחַ תִּשָּׂאֵ֔ם וּסְעָרָ֖ה תָּפִ֣יץ אוֹתָ֑ם וְאַתָּה֙ תָּגִ֣יל בַּֽיהֹוָ֔ה בִּקְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל תִּתְהַלָּֽל׃{ס}


Thou shalt fan them, and the wind shall carry them away, and the storm wind shall scatter them: and thou shalt rejoice in the Lord, and shalt glory in the Holy One of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 41:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:17)


הָעֲנִיִּ֨ים וְהָאֶבְיוֹנִ֜ים מְבַקְשִׁ֥ים מַ֙יִם֙ וָאַ֔יִן לְשׁוֹנָ֖ם בַּצָּמָ֣א נָשָׁ֑תָּה אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ אֶעֱנֵ֔ם אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֹ֥א אֶעֶזְבֵֽם׃


The poor and needy seek water, and there is none, and their tongue is parched for thirst; I the Lord will answer them, I the God of Yisra᾽el will not forsake them.


###### Isaiah 41:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:18)


אֶפְתַּ֤ח עַל־שְׁפָיִים֙ נְהָר֔וֹת וּבְת֥וֹךְ בְּקָע֖וֹת מַעְיָנ֑וֹת אָשִׂ֤ים מִדְבָּר֙ לַאֲגַם־מַ֔יִם וְאֶ֥רֶץ צִיָּ֖ה לְמוֹצָ֥אֵי מָֽיִם׃


I will open rivers on high places, and fountains in the midst of the valleys: I will make the wilderness a pool of water, and the dry land springs of water.


###### Isaiah 41:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:19)


אֶתֵּ֤ן בַּמִּדְבָּר֙ אֶ֣רֶז שִׁטָּ֔ה וַהֲדַ֖ס וְעֵ֣ץ שָׁ֑מֶן אָשִׂ֣ים בָּעֲרָבָ֗ה בְּר֛וֹשׁ תִּדְהָ֥ר וּתְאַשּׁ֖וּר יַחְדָּֽו׃


I will plant in the wilderness the cedar, the shitta tree, and the myrtle, and the oil tree; I will set in the ῾Arava cypress, maple, and box tree together:


###### Isaiah 41:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:20)


לְמַ֧עַן יִרְא֣וּ וְיֵדְע֗וּ וְיָשִׂ֤ימוּ וְיַשְׂכִּ֙ילוּ֙ יַחְדָּ֔ו כִּ֥י יַד־יְהֹוָ֖ה עָ֣שְׂתָה זֹּ֑את וּקְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בְּרָאָֽהּ׃{פ}


that they may see, and know, and consider, and understand together, that the hand of the Lord has done this, and the Holy One of Yisra᾽el has created it.


###### Isaiah 41:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:21)


קָרְב֥וּ רִֽיבְכֶ֖ם יֹאמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה הַגִּ֙ישׁוּ֙ עֲצֻמ֣וֹתֵיכֶ֔ם יֹאמַ֖ר מֶ֥לֶךְ יַעֲקֹֽב׃


Produce your cause, says the Lord; bring forth your strong reasons, says the King of Ya῾aqov.


###### Isaiah 41:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:22)


יַגִּ֙ישׁוּ֙ וְיַגִּ֣ידוּ לָ֔נוּ אֵ֖ת אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּקְרֶ֑ינָה הָרִאשֹׁנ֣וֹת**׀** מָ֣ה הֵ֗נָּה הַגִּ֜ידוּ וְנָשִׂ֤ימָה לִבֵּ֙נוּ֙ וְנֵדְעָ֣ה אַחֲרִיתָ֔ן א֥וֹ הַבָּא֖וֹת הַשְׁמִיעֻֽנוּ׃


Let them bring them forth, and tell us what shall happen: let them relate the former things, what they were, that we may consider them, and know the latter end of them; or declare us things to come.


###### Isaiah 41:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:23)


הַגִּ֙ידוּ֙ הָאֹתִיּ֣וֹת לְאָח֔וֹר וְנֵ֣דְעָ֔ה כִּ֥י אֱלֹהִ֖ים אַתֶּ֑ם אַף־תֵּיטִ֣יבוּ וְתָרֵ֔עוּ וְנִשְׁתָּ֖עָה (ונרא) [וְנִרְאֶ֥ה] יַחְדָּֽו׃


Declare the things that are to come hereafter, that we may know that you are gods: indeed, do good, or do evil, that we may be dismayed, and behold it together.


###### Isaiah 41:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:24)


הֵן־אַתֶּ֣ם מֵאַ֔יִן וּפׇעׇלְכֶ֖ם מֵאָ֑פַע תּוֹעֵבָ֖ה יִבְחַ֥ר בָּכֶֽם׃{פ}


Behold, you are of nothing, and your work of nought: an abomination is he that chooses you.


###### Isaiah 41:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:25)


הַעִיר֤וֹתִי מִצָּפוֹן֙ וַיַּ֔את מִמִּזְרַח־שֶׁ֖מֶשׁ יִקְרָ֣א בִשְׁמִ֑י וְיָבֹ֤א סְגָנִים֙ כְּמוֹ־חֹ֔מֶר וּכְמ֥וֹ יוֹצֵ֖ר יִרְמׇס־טִֽיט׃


I have raised up one from the north, and he is come: from the rising of the sun, and he shall call upon my name: and he shall come upon princes as upon mortar, and as the potter treads clay.


###### Isaiah 41:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:26)


מִֽי־הִגִּ֤יד מֵרֹאשׁ֙ וְנֵדָ֔עָה וּמִלְּפָנִ֖ים וְנֹאמַ֣ר צַדִּ֑יק אַ֣ף אֵין־מַגִּ֗יד אַ֚ף אֵ֣ין מַשְׁמִ֔יעַ אַ֥ף אֵין־שֹׁמֵ֖עַ אִמְרֵיכֶֽם׃


Who has declared from the beginning, that we may know? and beforetime, that we may say, He is right? there is none that told, none that declared, not one even that heard your words.


###### Isaiah 41:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:27)


רִאשׁ֥וֹן לְצִיּ֖וֹן הִנֵּ֣ה הִנָּ֑ם וְלִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם מְבַשֵּׂ֥ר אֶתֵּֽן׃


A harbinger to Żiyyon will I give: Behold, behold them; and to Yerushalayim a messenger of good tidings.


###### Isaiah 41:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:28)


וְאֵ֙רֶא֙ וְאֵ֣ין אִ֔ישׁ וּמֵאֵ֖לֶּה וְאֵ֣ין יוֹעֵ֑ץ וְאֶשְׁאָלֵ֖ם וְיָשִׁ֥יבוּ דָבָֽר׃


For I behold, and there is no man; among them, and there is no counsellor, that, when I ask of them, could answer a word.


###### Isaiah 41:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/41:29)


הֵ֣ן כֻּלָּ֔ם אָ֥וֶן אֶ֖פֶס מַעֲשֵׂיהֶ֑ם ר֥וּחַ וָתֹ֖הוּ נִסְכֵּיהֶֽם׃{פ}


Behold, they are all vanity; their works are nothing: their molten idols are wind and confusion.



## Chapter 42

###### Isaiah 42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:1)


הֵ֤ן עַבְדִּי֙ אֶתְמׇךְ־בּ֔וֹ בְּחִירִ֖י רָצְתָ֣ה נַפְשִׁ֑י נָתַ֤תִּי רוּחִי֙ עָלָ֔יו מִשְׁפָּ֖ט לַגּוֹיִ֥ם יוֹצִֽיא׃


Behold my servant, whom I uphold: my elect, in whom my soul delights: I have put my spirit upon him: he shall bring forth judgment to the nations.


###### Isaiah 42:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:2)


לֹ֥א יִצְעַ֖ק וְלֹ֣א יִשָּׂ֑א וְלֹא־יַשְׁמִ֥יעַ בַּח֖וּץ קוֹלֽוֹ׃


He shall not cry, nor lift up his voice, nor cause his voice to be heard in the street.


###### Isaiah 42:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:3)


קָנֶ֤ה רָצוּץ֙ לֹ֣א יִשְׁבּ֔וֹר וּפִשְׁתָּ֥ה כֵהָ֖ה לֹ֣א יְכַבֶּ֑נָּה לֶאֱמֶ֖ת יוֹצִ֥יא מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


A bruised reed shall he not break, and the dimly burning flax shall he not quench: he shall bring forth judgment unto truth.


###### Isaiah 42:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:4)


לֹ֤א יִכְהֶה֙ וְלֹ֣א יָר֔וּץ עַד־יָשִׂ֥ים בָּאָ֖רֶץ מִשְׁפָּ֑ט וּלְתוֹרָת֖וֹ אִיִּ֥ים יְיַחֵֽלוּ׃{פ}


He shall not fail nor be discouraged, till he have set judgment in the earth: and the isles shall wait for his Tora.


###### Isaiah 42:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:5)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר הָאֵ֣ל׀יְהֹוָ֗ה בּוֹרֵ֤א הַשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ וְנ֣וֹטֵיהֶ֔ם רֹקַ֥ע הָאָ֖רֶץ וְצֶאֱצָאֶ֑יהָ נֹתֵ֤ן נְשָׁמָה֙ לָעָ֣ם עָלֶ֔יהָ וְר֖וּחַ לַהֹלְכִ֥ים בָּֽהּ׃


Thus says God the Lord, he that created the heavens, and stretched them out; he that spread forth the earth, and that which comes out of it; he that gives breath to the people upon it, and spirit to them that walk therein:


###### Isaiah 42:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:6)


אֲנִ֧י יְהֹוָ֛ה קְרָאתִ֥יךָֽ בְצֶ֖דֶק וְאַחְזֵ֣ק בְּיָדֶ֑ךָ וְאֶצׇּרְךָ֗ וְאֶתֶּנְךָ֛ לִבְרִ֥ית עָ֖ם לְא֥וֹר גּוֹיִֽם׃


I the Lord have called thee in righteousness, and will hold thy hand, and will keep thee, and give thee for a covenant of the people, for a light of the nations;


###### Isaiah 42:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:7)


לִפְקֹ֖חַ עֵינַ֣יִם עִוְר֑וֹת לְהוֹצִ֤יא מִמַּסְגֵּר֙ אַסִּ֔יר מִבֵּ֥ית כֶּ֖לֶא יֹ֥שְׁבֵי חֹֽשֶׁךְ׃


to open the blind eyes, to bring out the prisoners from the prison, and them that sit in darkness out of the prison house.


###### Isaiah 42:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:8)


אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה ה֣וּא שְׁמִ֑י וּכְבוֹדִי֙ לְאַחֵ֣ר לֹֽא־אֶתֵּ֔ן וּתְהִלָּתִ֖י לַפְּסִילִֽים׃


I am the Lord: that is my name: and my glory will I not give to another, neither my praise to carved idols.


###### Isaiah 42:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:9)


הָרִאשֹׁנ֖וֹת הִנֵּה־בָ֑אוּ וַֽחֲדָשׁוֹת֙ אֲנִ֣י מַגִּ֔יד בְּטֶ֥רֶם תִּצְמַ֖חְנָה אַשְׁמִ֥יעַ אֶתְכֶֽם׃{פ}


Behold, the former things are come to pass, and new things do I declare: before they spring forth I tell you of them.


###### Isaiah 42:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:10)


שִׁ֤ירוּ לַֽיהֹוָה֙ שִׁ֣יר חָדָ֔שׁ תְּהִלָּת֖וֹ מִקְצֵ֣ה הָאָ֑רֶץ יוֹרְדֵ֤י הַיָּם֙ וּמְלֹא֔וֹ אִיִּ֖ים וְיֹשְׁבֵיהֶֽם׃


Sing to the Lord a new song, and his praise from the end of the earth, you that go down to the sea, and all that is therein; the isles, and their inhabitants.


###### Isaiah 42:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:11)
