יִשְׂא֤וּ מִדְבָּר֙ וְעָרָ֔יו חֲצֵרִ֖ים תֵּשֵׁ֣ב קֵדָ֑ר יָרֹ֙נּוּ֙ יֹ֣שְׁבֵי סֶ֔לַע מֵרֹ֥אשׁ הָרִ֖ים יִצְוָֽחוּ׃


Let the wilderness and its cities lift up their voice, the villages that Qedar inhabits: let the inhabitants of Sela sing, let them shout from the top of the mountains.


###### Isaiah 42:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:12)


יָשִׂ֥ימוּ לַיהֹוָ֖ה כָּב֑וֹד וּתְהִלָּת֖וֹ בָּאִיִּ֥ים יַגִּֽידוּ׃


Let them give glory to the Lord, and declare his praise in the islands.


###### Isaiah 42:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:13)


יְהֹוָה֙ כַּגִּבּ֣וֹר יֵצֵ֔א כְּאִ֥ישׁ מִלְחָמ֖וֹת יָעִ֣יר קִנְאָ֑ה יָרִ֙יעַ֙ אַף־יַצְרִ֔יחַ עַל־אֹיְבָ֖יו יִתְגַּבָּֽר׃{ס}


The Lord shall go forth as a mighty man, he shall stir up ardour like a man of war: he shall cry, indeed, roar; he shall show himself mighty against his foes.


###### Isaiah 42:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:14)


הֶחֱשֵׁ֙יתִי֙ מֵֽעוֹלָ֔ם אַחֲרִ֖ישׁ אֶתְאַפָּ֑ק כַּיּוֹלֵדָ֣ה אֶפְעֶ֔ה אֶשֹּׁ֥ם וְאֶשְׁאַ֖ף יָֽחַד׃


I have a long time held my peace; I have been still, and refrained myself: now will I cry like a woman in travail; I will gasp and pant together.


###### Isaiah 42:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:15)


אַחֲרִ֤יב הָרִים֙ וּגְבָע֔וֹת וְכׇל־עֶשְׂבָּ֖ם אוֹבִ֑ישׁ וְשַׂמְתִּ֤י נְהָרוֹת֙ לָאִיִּ֔ים וַאֲגַמִּ֖ים אוֹבִֽישׁ׃


I will make waste mountains and hills, and dry up all their herbs; and I will make the rivers islands, and I will dry up the pools.


###### Isaiah 42:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:16)


וְהוֹלַכְתִּ֣י עִוְרִ֗ים בְּדֶ֙רֶךְ֙ לֹ֣א יָדָ֔עוּ בִּנְתִיב֥וֹת לֹא־יָדְע֖וּ אַדְרִיכֵ֑ם אָשִׂים֩ מַחְשָׁ֨ךְ לִפְנֵיהֶ֜ם לָא֗וֹר וּמַֽעֲקַשִּׁים֙ לְמִישׁ֔וֹר אֵ֚לֶּה הַדְּבָרִ֔ים עֲשִׂיתִ֖ם וְלֹ֥א עֲזַבְתִּֽים׃


And I will bring the blind by a way that they knew not; I will lead them in paths that they have not known: I will make darkness light before them, and crooked things straight. These are the things which I have done, and I have not forsaken them.


###### Isaiah 42:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:17)


נָסֹ֤גוּ אָחוֹר֙ יֵבֹ֣שׁוּ בֹ֔שֶׁת הַבֹּטְחִ֖ים בַּפָּ֑סֶל הָאֹמְרִ֥ים לְמַסֵּכָ֖ה אַתֶּ֥ם אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃{פ}


They shall be turned back, they shall be greatly ashamed, that trust in carved idols, that say to the molten images, You are our gods.


###### Isaiah 42:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:18)


הַחֵרְשִׁ֖ים שְׁמָ֑עוּ וְהַעִוְרִ֖ים הַבִּ֥יטוּ לִרְאֽוֹת׃


Hear, O deaf; and look, O blind, that you may see.


###### Isaiah 42:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:19)


מִ֤י עִוֵּר֙ כִּ֣י אִם־עַבְדִּ֔י וְחֵרֵ֖שׁ כְּמַלְאָכִ֣י אֶשְׁלָ֑ח מִ֤י עִוֵּר֙ כִּמְשֻׁלָּ֔ם וְעִוֵּ֖ר כְּעֶ֥בֶד יְהֹוָֽה׃


Who is blind, but my servant? or deaf, as my messenger that I sent? who is blind as he that is perfect, and blind as the Lord’s servant?


###### Isaiah 42:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:20)


(ראית) [רָא֥וֹת] רַבּ֖וֹת וְלֹ֣א תִשְׁמֹ֑ר פָּק֥וֹחַ אׇזְנַ֖יִם וְלֹ֥א יִשְׁמָֽע׃


Seeing many things, but thou observest not; opening the ears, but he hears not.


###### Isaiah 42:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:21)


יְהֹוָ֥ה חָפֵ֖ץ לְמַ֣עַן צִדְק֑וֹ יַגְדִּ֥יל תּוֹרָ֖ה וְיַאְדִּֽיר׃


The Lord was well pleased for his righteousness’ sake, to magnify Tora, and to make it glorious.


###### Isaiah 42:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:22)


וְהוּא֮ עַם־בָּז֣וּז וְשָׁסוּי֒ הָפֵ֤חַ בַּחוּרִים֙ כֻּלָּ֔ם וּבְבָתֵּ֥י כְלָאִ֖ים הׇחְבָּ֑אוּ הָי֤וּ לָבַז֙ וְאֵ֣ין מַצִּ֔יל מְשִׁסָּ֖ה וְאֵין־אֹמֵ֥ר הָשַֽׁב׃


Therefore this is a people robbed and spoiled; they are all of them snared in holes, and they are hid in prison houses: they are for a prey, and none delivers; for a spoil, and none says, Restore.


###### Isaiah 42:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:23)


מִ֥י בָכֶ֖ם יַאֲזִ֣ין זֹ֑את יַקְשִׁ֥ב וְיִשְׁמַ֖ע לְאָחֽוֹר׃


Who among you will give ear to this? who will hearken and hear for the time to come?


###### Isaiah 42:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:24)


מִֽי־נָתַ֨ן (למשוסה) [לִמְשִׁסָּ֧ה] יַעֲקֹ֛ב וְיִשְׂרָאֵ֥ל לְבֹזְזִ֖ים הֲל֣וֹא יְהֹוָ֑ה ז֚וּ חָטָ֣אנוּ ל֔וֹ וְלֹֽא־אָב֤וּ בִדְרָכָיו֙ הָל֔וֹךְ וְלֹ֥א שָׁמְע֖וּ בְּתוֹרָתֽוֹ׃


Who gave Ya῾aqov for a spoil, and Yisra᾽el to the robbers? did not the Lord, he against whom we have sinned, and in whose ways they would not walk, and unto whose Tora they were not obedient?


###### Isaiah 42:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/42:25)


וַיִּשְׁפֹּ֤ךְ עָלָיו֙ חֵמָ֣ה אַפּ֔וֹ וֶעֱז֖וּז מִלְחָמָ֑ה וַתְּלַהֲטֵ֤הוּ מִסָּבִיב֙ וְלֹ֣א יָדָ֔ע וַתִּבְעַר־בּ֖וֹ וְלֹא־יָשִׂ֥ים עַל־לֵֽב׃


Therefore he has poured upon him the fury of his anger, and the strength of battle: and it has set him on fire round about, yet he knew not; and it burned him, yet he laid it not to heart.



## Chapter 43

###### Isaiah 43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:1)


וְעַתָּ֞ה כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ בֹּרַאֲךָ֣ יַעֲקֹ֔ב וְיֹצֶרְךָ֖ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אַל־תִּירָא֙ כִּ֣י גְאַלְתִּ֔יךָ קָרָ֥אתִי בְשִׁמְךָ֖ לִי־אָֽתָּה׃


But now thus says the Lord that created thee, O Ya῾aqov, and he that formed thee, O Yisra᾽el, Fear not: for I have redeemed thee, I have called thee by thy name; thou art mine.


###### Isaiah 43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:2)


כִּֽי־תַעֲבֹ֤ר בַּמַּ֙יִם֙ אִתְּךָ־אָ֔נִי וּבַנְּהָר֖וֹת לֹ֣א יִשְׁטְפ֑וּךָ כִּֽי־תֵלֵ֤ךְ בְּמוֹ־אֵשׁ֙ לֹ֣א תִכָּוֶ֔ה וְלֶהָבָ֖ה לֹ֥א תִבְעַר־בָּֽךְ׃


When thou passest through the waters, I will be with thee; and through the rivers, they shall not overflow thee: when thou walkest through the fire, thou shalt not be burned; neither shall the flame kindle upon thee.


###### Isaiah 43:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:3)


כִּ֗י אֲנִי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל מוֹשִׁיעֶ֑ךָ נָתַ֤תִּי כׇפְרְךָ֙ מִצְרַ֔יִם כּ֥וּשׁ וּסְבָ֖א תַּחְתֶּֽיךָ׃


For I am the Lord thy God, the Holy One of Yisra᾽el, thy deliverer: I gave Miżrayim for thy ransom, Kush and Seva instead of thee.


###### Isaiah 43:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:4)


מֵאֲשֶׁ֨ר יָקַ֧רְתָּ בְעֵינַ֛י נִכְבַּ֖דְתָּ וַאֲנִ֣י אֲהַבְתִּ֑יךָ וְאֶתֵּ֤ן אָדָם֙ תַּחְתֶּ֔יךָ וּלְאֻמִּ֖ים תַּ֥חַת נַפְשֶֽׁךָ׃


Since thou wast precious in my sight, thou wast honoured, and I have loved thee: therefore will I give men for thee, and people for thy life.


###### Isaiah 43:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:5)


אַל־תִּירָ֖א כִּ֣י אִתְּךָ־אָ֑נִי מִמִּזְרָח֙ אָבִ֣יא זַרְעֶ֔ךָ וּמִֽמַּעֲרָ֖ב אֲקַבְּצֶֽךָּ׃


Fear not: for I am with thee: I will bring thy seed from the east, and gather thee from the west;


###### Isaiah 43:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:6)


אֹמַ֤ר לַצָּפוֹן֙ תֵּ֔נִי וּלְתֵימָ֖ן אַל־תִּכְלָ֑אִי הָבִ֤יאִי בָנַי֙ מֵרָח֔וֹק וּבְנוֹתַ֖י מִקְצֵ֥ה הָאָֽרֶץ׃


I will say to the north, Give up; and to the south, Keep not back: bring my sons from far, and my daughters from the ends of the earth;


###### Isaiah 43:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:7)


כֹּ֚ל הַנִּקְרָ֣א בִשְׁמִ֔י וְלִכְבוֹדִ֖י בְּרָאתִ֑יו יְצַרְתִּ֖יו אַף־עֲשִׂיתִֽיו׃


every one that is called by my name: for I have created him for my glory; I have formed him; yea, I have made him.


###### Isaiah 43:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:8)


הוֹצִ֥יא עַם־עִוֵּ֖ר וְעֵינַ֣יִם יֵ֑שׁ וְחֵרְשִׁ֖ים וְאׇזְנַ֥יִם לָֽמוֹ׃


Bring forth the blind people that have eyes, and the deaf that have ears.


###### Isaiah 43:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:9)


כׇּֽל־הַגּוֹיִ֞ם נִקְבְּצ֣וּ יַחְדָּ֗ו וְיֵאָֽסְפוּ֙ לְאֻמִּ֔ים מִ֤י בָהֶם֙ יַגִּ֣יד זֹ֔את וְרִאשֹׁנ֖וֹת יַשְׁמִיעֻ֑נוּ יִתְּנ֤וּ עֵֽדֵיהֶם֙ וְיִצְדָּ֔קוּ וְיִשְׁמְע֖וּ וְיֹאמְר֥וּ אֱמֶֽת׃


Let all the nations be gathered together, and let the peoples be assembled: who among them can declare this, and announce to us former things? let them bring forth their witnesses, that they may be justified: or let them hear, and say, It is truth.


###### Isaiah 43:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:10)


אַתֶּ֤ם עֵדַי֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְעַבְדִּ֖י אֲשֶׁ֣ר בָּחָ֑רְתִּי לְמַ֣עַן תֵּ֠דְע֠וּ וְתַאֲמִ֨ינוּ לִ֤י וְתָבִ֙ינוּ֙ כִּֽי־אֲנִ֣י ה֔וּא לְפָנַי֙ לֹא־נ֣וֹצַר אֵ֔ל וְאַחֲרַ֖י לֹ֥א יִהְיֶֽה׃{ס}


You are my witnesses, says the Lord, and my servant whom I have chosen: that you may know and believe me, and understand that I am he: before me there was no God formed, neither shall there be after me.


###### Isaiah 43:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:11)


אָנֹכִ֥י אָנֹכִ֖י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְאֵ֥ין מִבַּלְעָדַ֖י מוֹשִֽׁיעַ׃


I, even I, am the Lord; and beside me there is no deliverer.


###### Isaiah 43:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:12)


אָנֹכִ֞י הִגַּ֤דְתִּי וְהוֹשַׁ֙עְתִּי֙ וְהִשְׁמַ֔עְתִּי וְאֵ֥ין בָּכֶ֖ם זָ֑ר וְאַתֶּ֥ם עֵדַ֛י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֖ה וַאֲנִי־אֵֽל׃


I have declared, and have saved, and I have announced, and there was no strange god among you: therefore you are my witnesses, says the Lord, and I am God.


###### Isaiah 43:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:13)


גַּם־מִיּוֹם֙ אֲנִ֣י ה֔וּא וְאֵ֥ין מִיָּדִ֖י מַצִּ֑יל אֶפְעַ֖ל וּמִ֥י יְשִׁיבֶֽנָּה׃{ס}


Yea, from the first I am he; and there is none that can deliver out of my hand: I will work, and who shall reverse it?


###### Isaiah 43:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:14)


כֹּֽה־אָמַ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה גֹּאַלְכֶ֖ם קְד֣וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל לְמַעַנְכֶ֞ם שִׁלַּ֣חְתִּי בָבֶ֗לָה וְהוֹרַדְתִּ֤י בָֽרִיחִים֙ כֻּלָּ֔ם וְכַשְׂדִּ֖ים בָּאֳנִיּ֥וֹת רִנָּתָֽם׃


Thus says the Lord, your redeemer, the Holy One of Yisra᾽el; For your sake I have sent to Bavel, and will bring down all of them as fugitives, the Kasdim, in the ships of their song.


###### Isaiah 43:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:15)


אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה קְדֽוֹשְׁכֶ֑ם בּוֹרֵ֥א יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל מַלְכְּכֶֽם׃{ס}


I am the Lord, your Holy One, the Creator of Yisra᾽el, your King.


###### Isaiah 43:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:16)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הַנּוֹתֵ֥ן בַּיָּ֖ם דָּ֑רֶךְ וּבְמַ֥יִם עַזִּ֖ים נְתִיבָֽה׃


Thus says the Lord, who makes a way in the sea, and a path in the mighty waters;


###### Isaiah 43:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:17)


הַמּוֹצִ֥יא רֶכֶב־וָס֖וּס חַ֣יִל וְעִזּ֑וּז יַחְדָּ֤ו יִשְׁכְּבוּ֙ בַּל־יָק֔וּמוּ דָּעֲכ֖וּ כַּפִּשְׁתָּ֥ה כָבֽוּ׃


who brings out chariot and horse, army and power; they lie down together, they shall not rise: they are extinct, they are quenched like a wick.


###### Isaiah 43:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:18)


אַֽל־תִּזְכְּר֖וּ רִאשֹׁנ֑וֹת וְקַדְמֹנִיּ֖וֹת אַל־תִּתְבֹּנָֽנוּ׃


Remember not the former things, neither consider the things of old.


###### Isaiah 43:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:19)


הִנְנִ֨י עֹשֶׂ֤ה חֲדָשָׁה֙ עַתָּ֣ה תִצְמָ֔ח הֲל֖וֹא תֵּדָע֑וּהָ אַ֣ף אָשִׂ֤ים בַּמִּדְבָּר֙ דֶּ֔רֶךְ בִּישִׁמ֖וֹן נְהָרֽוֹת׃


Behold, I will do a new thing; now it shall spring forth; shall you not know it? I will even make a way in the wilderness, and rivers in the desert.


###### Isaiah 43:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:20)


תְּכַבְּדֵ֙נִי֙ חַיַּ֣ת הַשָּׂדֶ֔ה תַּנִּ֖ים וּבְנ֣וֹת יַעֲנָ֑ה כִּֽי־נָתַ֨תִּי בַמִּדְבָּ֜ר מַ֗יִם נְהָרוֹת֙ בִּֽישִׁימֹ֔ן לְהַשְׁק֖וֹת עַמִּ֥י בְחִירִֽי׃


The beast of the field shall honour me, the jackals and the owls: because I give waters in the wilderness, and rivers in the desert, to give drink to my people, my chosen.


###### Isaiah 43:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:21)


עַם־זוּ֙ יָצַ֣רְתִּי לִ֔י תְּהִלָּתִ֖י יְסַפֵּֽרוּ׃{ס}


This people have I formed for myself; they shall relate my praise.


###### Isaiah 43:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:22)


וְלֹא־אֹתִ֥י קָרָ֖אתָ יַעֲקֹ֑ב כִּֽי־יָגַ֥עְתָּ בִּ֖י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


But thou hast not called upon me, O Ya῾aqov; but thou hast been weary of me, O Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 43:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:23)


לֹֽא־הֵבֵ֤יאתָ לִּי֙ שֵׂ֣ה עֹלֹתֶ֔יךָ וּזְבָחֶ֖יךָ לֹ֣א כִבַּדְתָּ֑נִי לֹ֤א הֶעֱבַדְתִּ֙יךָ֙ בְּמִנְחָ֔ה וְלֹ֥א הוֹגַעְתִּ֖יךָ בִּלְבוֹנָֽה׃


Thou hast not brought me the small cattle of thy burnt offerings: nor hast thou honoured me with thy sacrifices. I have not burdened thee with a meal offering, nor wearied thee with incense.


###### Isaiah 43:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:24)


לֹא־קָנִ֨יתָ לִּ֤י בַכֶּ֙סֶף֙ קָנֶ֔ה וְחֵ֥לֶב זְבָחֶ֖יךָ לֹ֣א הִרְוִיתָ֑נִי אַ֗ךְ הֶעֱבַדְתַּ֙נִי֙ בְּחַטֹּאותֶ֔יךָ הוֹגַעְתַּ֖נִי בַּעֲוֺנֹתֶֽיךָ׃


Thou hast bought me no sweet cane with money, nor hast thou sated me with the fat of thy sacrifices: but thou hast burdened me with thy sins, thou hast wearied me with thy iniquities.


###### Isaiah 43:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:25)


אָנֹכִ֨י אָנֹכִ֥י ה֛וּא מֹחֶ֥ה פְשָׁעֶ֖יךָ לְמַעֲנִ֑י וְחַטֹּאתֶ֖יךָ לֹ֥א אֶזְכֹּֽר׃


I, even I, am he that blots out thy transgressions for my own sake, and will not remember thy sins.


###### Isaiah 43:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:26)


הַזְכִּירֵ֕נִי נִשָּׁפְטָ֖ה יָ֑חַד סַפֵּ֥ר אַתָּ֖ה לְמַ֥עַן תִּצְדָּֽק׃


Put me in remembrance: let us plead together: declare thou, that thou mayst be justified.


###### Isaiah 43:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:27)


אָבִ֥יךָ הָרִאשׁ֖וֹן חָטָ֑א וּמְלִיצֶ֖יךָ פָּ֥שְׁעוּ בִֽי׃


Thy first father has sinned, and thy teachers have transgressed against me.


###### Isaiah 43:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/43:28)


וַאֲחַלֵּ֖ל שָׂ֣רֵי קֹ֑דֶשׁ וְאֶתְּנָ֤ה לַחֵ֙רֶם֙ יַֽעֲקֹ֔ב וְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לְגִדּוּפִֽים׃{פ}


Therefore I have profaned the princes of the sanctuary, and have given Ya῾aqov to destruction, and Yisra᾽el to reviling.



## Chapter 44

###### Isaiah 44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:1)


וְעַתָּ֥ה שְׁמַ֖ע יַעֲקֹ֣ב עַבְדִּ֑י וְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בָּחַ֥רְתִּי בֽוֹ׃


Yet now hear, O Ya῾aqov my servant; and Yisra᾽el whom I have chosen:


###### Isaiah 44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:2)


כֹּה־אָמַ֨ר יְהֹוָ֥ה עֹשֶׂ֛ךָ וְיֹצֶרְךָ֥ מִבֶּ֖טֶן יַעְזְרֶ֑ךָּ אַל־תִּירָא֙ עַבְדִּ֣י יַעֲקֹ֔ב וִישֻׁר֖וּן בָּחַ֥רְתִּי בֽוֹ׃


thus says the Lord that made thee, and formed thee from the womb, who will help thee; Fear not, O Ya῾aqov, my servant; and thou, Yeshurun, whom I have chosen.


###### Isaiah 44:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:3)


כִּ֤י אֶצׇּק־מַ֙יִם֙ עַל־צָמֵ֔א וְנֹזְלִ֖ים עַל־יַבָּשָׁ֑ה אֶצֹּ֤ק רוּחִי֙ עַל־זַרְעֶ֔ךָ וּבִרְכָתִ֖י עַל־צֶאֱצָאֶֽיךָ׃


For I will pour water upon the thirsty land, and floods upon the dry ground: I will pour my spirit upon thy seed, and my blessing upon thy offspring:


###### Isaiah 44:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:4)


וְצָמְח֖וּ בְּבֵ֣ין חָצִ֑יר כַּעֲרָבִ֖ים עַל־יִבְלֵי־מָֽיִם׃


and they shall spring up as among the grass, as willows by the water courses.


###### Isaiah 44:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:5)


זֶ֤ה יֹאמַר֙ לַיהֹוָ֣ה אָ֔נִי וְזֶ֖ה יִקְרָ֣א בְשֵֽׁם־יַעֲקֹ֑ב וְזֶ֗ה יִכְתֹּ֤ב יָדוֹ֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה וּבְשֵׁ֥ם יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל יְכַנֶּֽה׃{פ}


One shall say, I am the Lord’s; and another shall call himself by the name of Ya῾aqov; and another shall subscribe with his hand to the Lord, and surname himself by the name of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 44:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:6)


כֹּה־אָמַ֨ר יְהֹוָ֧ה מֶלֶךְ־יִשְׂרָאֵ֛ל וְגֹאֲל֖וֹ יְהֹוָ֣ה צְבָא֑וֹת אֲנִ֤י רִאשׁוֹן֙ וַאֲנִ֣י אַחֲר֔וֹן וּמִבַּלְעָדַ֖י אֵ֥ין אֱלֹהִֽים׃


Thus says the Lord the King of Yisra᾽el, and his redeemer the Lord of hosts; I am the first, and I am the last; and beside me there is no God.


###### Isaiah 44:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:7)


וּמִי־כָמ֣וֹנִי יִקְרָ֗א וְיַגִּידֶ֤הָ וְיַעְרְכֶ֙הָ֙ לִ֔י מִשּׂוּמִ֖י עַם־עוֹלָ֑ם וְאֹתִיּ֛וֹת וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר תָּבֹ֖אנָה יַגִּ֥ידוּ לָֽמוֹ׃


And who is like me? let him proclaim it, let him declare it, and set it in order for me, from when I appointed the eternal people; let them relate for themselves the things that will come, and are to be.


###### Isaiah 44:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:8)


אַֽל־תִּפְחֲדוּ֙ וְאַל־תִּרְה֔וּ הֲלֹ֥א מֵאָ֛ז הִשְׁמַעְתִּ֥יךָ וְהִגַּ֖דְתִּי וְאַתֶּ֣ם עֵדָ֑י הֲיֵ֤שׁ אֱל֙וֹהַּ֙ מִבַּלְעָדַ֔י וְאֵ֥ין צ֖וּר בַּל־יָדָֽעְתִּי׃


Fear not, neither be afraid: have not I told thee from that time, and have declared it? for you are my witnesses. Is there a God beside me? indeed there is no Rock; I know not any.


###### Isaiah 44:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:9)


יֹצְרֵי־פֶ֤סֶל כֻּלָּם֙ תֹּ֔הוּ וַחֲמוּדֵיהֶ֖ם בַּל־יוֹעִ֑ילוּ וְעֵדֵיהֶ֣ם הֵ֗ׄמָּׄהׄ בַּל־יִרְא֛וּ וּבַל־יֵדְע֖וּ לְמַ֥עַן יֵבֹֽשׁוּ׃


They that make a carved idol are all of them vanity; and the things they delight in, do not profit; and their witnesses see not, nor know; that they may be ashamed.


###### Isaiah 44:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:10)


מִֽי־יָצַ֥ר אֵ֖ל וּפֶ֣סֶל נָסָ֑ךְ לְבִלְתִּ֖י הוֹעִֽיל׃


Who has fashioned a god, or cast an idol? it is profitable for nothing.


###### Isaiah 44:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:11)


הֵ֤ן כׇּל־חֲבֵרָיו֙ יֵבֹ֔שׁוּ וְחָרָשִׁ֥ים הֵ֖מָּה מֵאָדָ֑ם יִֽתְקַבְּצ֤וּ כֻלָּם֙ יַעֲמֹ֔דוּ יִפְחֲד֖וּ יֵבֹ֥שׁוּ יָֽחַד׃


Behold, all his fellows shall be ashamed: and the workmen, they are but men: let them all be gathered together, let them stand up; they shall fear, they shall be ashamed together.


###### Isaiah 44:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:12)


חָרַ֤שׁ בַּרְזֶל֙ מַֽעֲצָ֔ד וּפָעַל֙ בַּפֶּחָ֔ם וּבַמַּקָּב֖וֹת יִצְּרֵ֑הוּ וַיִּפְעָלֵ֙הוּ֙ בִּזְר֣וֹעַ כֹּח֔וֹ גַּם־רָעֵב֙ וְאֵ֣ין כֹּ֔חַ לֹא־שָׁ֥תָה מַ֖יִם וַיִּיעָֽף׃


The ironsmith makes an axe, and works in the coals, and fashions it with hammers, works it with the strength of his arms: if he is hungry, his strength fails: if he drinks no water, he is faint.


###### Isaiah 44:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:13)


חָרַ֣שׁ עֵצִים֮ נָ֣טָה קָו֒ יְתָאֲרֵ֣הוּ בַשֶּׂ֔רֶד יַעֲשֵׂ֙הוּ֙ בַּמַּקְצֻע֔וֹת וּבַמְּחוּגָ֖ה יְתׇאֳרֵ֑הוּ וַֽיַּעֲשֵׂ֙הוּ֙ כְּתַבְנִ֣ית אִ֔ישׁ כְּתִפְאֶ֥רֶת אָדָ֖ם לָשֶׁ֥בֶת בָּֽיִת׃


The carpenter stretches out his rule; he marks it out with a pencil; he fits it with chisels, and he marks it out with the compass, and makes it after the figure of a man, according to the beauty of a man; that it may remain in the house.


###### Isaiah 44:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:14)


לִכְרׇת־ל֣וֹ אֲרָזִ֔ים וַיִּקַּ֤ח תִּרְזָה֙ וְאַלּ֔וֹן וַיְאַמֶּץ־ל֖וֹ בַּעֲצֵי־יָ֑עַר נָטַ֥ע אֹ֖רֶן וְגֶ֥שֶׁם יְגַדֵּֽל׃


He hews him down cedars, and takes the pine and the oak, which he strengthens for himself among the trees of the forest: he plants a forest tree and the rain nourishes it.


###### Isaiah 44:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:15)


וְהָיָ֤ה לְאָדָם֙ לְבָעֵ֔ר וַיִּקַּ֤ח מֵהֶם֙ וַיָּ֔חׇם אַף־יַשִּׂ֖יק וְאָ֣פָה לָ֑חֶם אַף־יִפְעַל־אֵל֙ וַיִּשְׁתָּ֔חוּ עָשָׂ֥הוּ פֶ֖סֶל וַיִּסְגׇּד־לָֽמוֹ׃


Then shall it be for a man for fuel: for he will take of it to warm himself; indeed, he kindles it, and bakes bread! or else he makes a god, and prostrates himself to it; he makes of it a carved idol, and bows himself down before it.


###### Isaiah 44:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:16)


חֶצְיוֹ֙ שָׂרַ֣ף בְּמוֹ־אֵ֔שׁ עַל־חֶצְיוֹ֙ בָּשָׂ֣ר יֹאכֵ֔ל יִצְלֶ֥ה צָלִ֖י וְיִשְׂבָּ֑ע אַף־יָחֹם֙ וְיֹאמַ֣ר הֶאָ֔ח חַמּוֹתִ֖י רָאִ֥יתִי אֽוּר׃


Half of it he burns in the fire; with this half of it he eats meat; he roasts the roast, and is satisfied: indeed, he warms himself, and says, Aha, I am warm, I have seen the fire:


###### Isaiah 44:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:17)


וּשְׁאֵ֣רִית֔וֹ לְאֵ֥ל עָשָׂ֖ה לְפִסְל֑וֹ (יסגוד) [יִסְגׇּד־]ל֤וֹ וְיִשְׁתַּ֙חוּ֙ וְיִתְפַּלֵּ֣ל אֵלָ֔יו וְיֹאמַר֙ הַצִּילֵ֔נִי כִּ֥י אֵלִ֖י אָֽתָּה׃


and of the rest of it he makes a god, his carved idol: he falls down to it, and worships it, and prays to it, and says, Deliver me; for thou art my god.


###### Isaiah 44:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:18)


לֹ֥א יָדְע֖וּ וְלֹ֣א יָבִ֑ינוּ כִּ֣י טַ֤ח מֵֽרְאוֹת֙ עֵינֵיהֶ֔ם מֵהַשְׂכִּ֖יל לִבֹּתָֽם׃


They have not known nor understood: for he has shut their eyes, that they cannot see; and their hearts, that they cannot understand.


###### Isaiah 44:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:19)


וְלֹֽא־יָשִׁ֣יב אֶל־לִבּ֗וֹ וְלֹ֨א דַ֥עַת וְלֹֽא־תְבוּנָה֮ לֵאמֹר֒ חֶצְי֞וֹ שָׂרַ֣פְתִּי בְמוֹ־אֵ֗שׁ וְ֠אַ֠ף אָפִ֤יתִי עַל־גֶּחָלָיו֙ לֶ֔חֶם אֶצְלֶ֥ה בָשָׂ֖ר וְאֹכֵ֑ל וְיִתְרוֹ֙ לְתוֹעֵבָ֣ה אֶעֱשֶׂ֔ה לְב֥וּל עֵ֖ץ אֶסְגּֽוֹד׃


And none considers in his heart, neither is there knowledge nor understanding to say, I have burned half of it in the fire; I have even baked bread upon its embers; I have roasted meat, and eaten it: and shall I make the rest of it an abomination? shall I fall down to worship the stock of a tree?


###### Isaiah 44:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:20)


רֹעֶ֣ה אֵ֔פֶר לֵ֥ב הוּתַ֖ל הִטָּ֑הוּ וְלֹא־יַצִּ֤יל אֶת־נַפְשׁוֹ֙ וְלֹ֣א יֹאמַ֔ר הֲל֥וֹא שֶׁ֖קֶר בִּימִינִֽי׃{ס}


He feeds on ashes: a deceived heart has turned him aside, that he cannot deliver his soul, nor say, Is there not a lie in my right hand?


###### Isaiah 44:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:21)


זְכׇר־אֵ֣לֶּה יַֽעֲקֹ֔ב וְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל כִּ֣י עַבְדִּי־אָ֑תָּה יְצַרְתִּ֤יךָ עֶֽבֶד־לִי֙ אַ֔תָּה יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֹ֥א תִנָּשֵֽׁנִי׃


Remember these, O Ya῾aqov and Yisra᾽el; thou art my servant: I have formed thee; thou art my own servant: O Yisra᾽el thou shalt not be forgotten by me.


###### Isaiah 44:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:22)


מָחִ֤יתִי כָעָב֙ פְּשָׁעֶ֔יךָ וְכֶעָנָ֖ן חַטֹּאותֶ֑יךָ שׁוּבָ֥ה אֵלַ֖י כִּ֥י גְאַלְתִּֽיךָ׃


I have blotted out, as a thick cloud, thy transgressions, and, as a cloud, thy sins: return to me; for I have redeemed thee.


###### Isaiah 44:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:23)


רׇנּ֨וּ שָׁמַ֜יִם כִּֽי־עָשָׂ֣ה יְהֹוָ֗ה הָרִ֙יעוּ֙ תַּחְתִּיּ֣וֹת אָ֔רֶץ פִּצְח֤וּ הָרִים֙ רִנָּ֔ה יַ֖עַר וְכׇל־עֵ֣ץ בּ֑וֹ כִּֽי־גָאַ֤ל יְהֹוָה֙ יַעֲקֹ֔ב וּבְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל יִתְפָּאָֽר׃{ס}


Sing, O heavens; for the Lord has done it: shout, you lower parts of the earth: break forth into singing, you mountains, O forest, and every tree in it: for the Lord has redeemed Ya῾aqov, and glorified himself in Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 44:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:24)


כֹּֽה־אָמַ֤ר יְהֹוָה֙ גֹּאֲלֶ֔ךָ וְיֹצֶרְךָ֖ מִבָּ֑טֶן אָנֹכִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ עֹ֣שֶׂה כֹּ֔ל נֹטֶ֤ה שָׁמַ֙יִם֙ לְבַדִּ֔י רֹקַ֥ע הָאָ֖רֶץ (מי אתי) [מֵאִתִּֽי]׃


Thus says the Lord, thy redeemer, and he that formed thee from the womb, I am the Lord that makes all things; that stretches the heavens, alone; that spreads abroad the earth by myself;


###### Isaiah 44:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:25)


מֵפֵר֙ אֹת֣וֹת בַּדִּ֔ים וְקֹסְמִ֖ים יְהוֹלֵ֑ל מֵשִׁ֧יב חֲכָמִ֛ים אָח֖וֹר וְדַעְתָּ֥ם יְסַכֵּֽל׃


that frustrates the omens of imposters, and makes diviners mad; that turns wise men backward, and makes their knowledge foolish;


###### Isaiah 44:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:26)


מֵקִים֙ דְּבַ֣ר עַבְדּ֔וֹ וַעֲצַ֥ת מַלְאָכָ֖יו יַשְׁלִ֑ים הָאֹמֵ֨ר לִירוּשָׁלַ֜͏ִם תּוּשָׁ֗ב וּלְעָרֵ֤י יְהוּדָה֙ תִּבָּנֶ֔ינָה וְחׇרְבוֹתֶ֖יהָ אֲקוֹמֵֽם׃


that confirms the word of his servant, and performs the counsel of his messengers; that says to Yerushalayim, Thou shalt be inhabited; and to the cities of Yehuda, They shall be rebuilt, and their ruins I shall raise up.


###### Isaiah 44:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:27)


הָאֹמֵ֥ר לַצּוּלָ֖ה חֳרָ֑בִי וְנַהֲרֹתַ֖יִךְ אוֹבִֽישׁ׃


That says to the deep, Be dry, and I will dry up thy rivers:


###### Isaiah 44:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/44:28)


הָאֹמֵ֤ר לְכ֙וֹרֶשׁ֙ רֹעִ֔י וְכׇל־חֶפְצִ֖י יַשְׁלִ֑ם וְלֵאמֹ֤ר לִירוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ תִּבָּנֶ֔ה וְהֵיכָ֖ל תִּוָּסֵֽד׃{פ}


that says of Koresh, He is my shepherd, and shall perform all my pleasure: and saying to Yerushalayim, Thou shalt be rebuilt; and to the temple, Thy foundation shall be laid.



## Chapter 45

###### Isaiah 45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:1)


כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָה֮ לִמְשִׁיחוֹ֮ לְכ֣וֹרֶשׁ אֲשֶׁר־הֶחֱזַ֣קְתִּי בִימִינ֗וֹ לְרַד־לְפָנָיו֙ גּוֹיִ֔ם וּמׇתְנֵ֥י מְלָכִ֖ים אֲפַתֵּ֑חַ לִפְתֹּ֤חַ לְפָנָיו֙ דְּלָתַ֔יִם וּשְׁעָרִ֖ים לֹ֥א יִסָּגֵֽרוּ׃


Thus says the Lord to his anointed, to Koresh, whose right hand I have held, that I may subdue nations before him, and loose the loins of kings; that I may open before him doors and gates which shall not be shut;


###### Isaiah 45:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:2)


אֲנִי֙ לְפָנֶ֣יךָ אֵלֵ֔ךְ וַהֲדוּרִ֖ים (אושר) [אֲיַשֵּׁ֑ר] דַּלְת֤וֹת נְחוּשָׁה֙ אֲשַׁבֵּ֔ר וּבְרִיחֵ֥י בַרְזֶ֖ל אֲגַדֵּֽעַ׃


I will go before thee, and make the mountainous places level: I will break in pieces the gates of brass, and cut in sunder the bars of iron:


###### Isaiah 45:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:3)


וְנָתַתִּ֤י לְךָ֙ אוֹצְר֣וֹת חֹ֔שֶׁךְ וּמַטְמֻנֵ֖י מִסְתָּרִ֑ים לְמַ֣עַן תֵּדַ֗ע כִּֽי־אֲנִ֧י יְהֹוָ֛ה הַקּוֹרֵ֥א בְשִׁמְךָ֖ אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


and I will give thee the treasures of darkness, and hidden riches of secret places, that thou mayst know that I, the Lord, who call thee by thy name, am the God of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 45:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:4)


לְמַ֙עַן֙ עַבְדִּ֣י יַעֲקֹ֔ב וְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל בְּחִירִ֑י וָאֶקְרָ֤א לְךָ֙ בִּשְׁמֶ֔ךָ אֲכַנְּךָ֖ וְלֹ֥א יְדַעְתָּֽנִי׃


For Ya῾aqov my servant’s sake, and Yisra᾽el my elect, I have even called thee by thy name: I have surnamed thee, though thou hast not known me.


###### Isaiah 45:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:5)


אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ וְאֵ֣ין ע֔וֹד זוּלָתִ֖י אֵ֣ין אֱלֹהִ֑ים אֲאַזֶּרְךָ֖ וְלֹ֥א יְדַעְתָּֽנִי׃


I am the Lord, and there is none else, there is no God beside me: I girded thee, though thou hast not known me:


###### Isaiah 45:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:6)


לְמַ֣עַן יֵדְע֗וּ מִמִּזְרַח־שֶׁ֙מֶשׁ֙ וּמִמַּ֣עֲרָבָ֔הֿ כִּי־אֶ֖פֶס בִּלְעָדָ֑י אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה וְאֵ֥ין עֽוֹד׃


that they may know from the rising of the sun, and from the west, that there is none beside me. I am the Lord, and there is none else,


###### Isaiah 45:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:7)


יוֹצֵ֥ר אוֹר֙ וּבוֹרֵ֣א חֹ֔שֶׁךְ עֹשֶׂ֥ה שָׁל֖וֹם וּב֣וֹרֵא רָ֑ע אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה עֹשֶׂ֥ה כׇל־אֵֽלֶּה׃{פ}


I form the light, and create darkness: I make peace, and create evil: I, the Lord, do all these things.


###### Isaiah 45:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:8)


הַרְעִ֤יפוּ שָׁמַ֙יִם֙ מִמַּ֔עַל וּשְׁחָקִ֖ים יִזְּלוּ־צֶ֑דֶק תִּפְתַּח־אֶ֣רֶץ וְיִפְרוּ־יֶ֗שַׁע וּצְדָקָ֤ה תַצְמִ֙יחַ֙ יַ֔חַד אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּרָאתִֽיו׃{ס}


Shower, O heavens, from above, and let the skies pour down righteousness: let the earth open, and let them bring forth salvation, and let it cause righteousness to spring up also; I, the Lord, have created it.


###### Isaiah 45:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:9)


ה֗וֹי רָ֚ב אֶת־יֹ֣צְר֔וֹ חֶ֖רֶשׂ אֶת־חַרְשֵׂ֣י אֲדָמָ֑ה הֲיֹאמַ֨ר חֹ֤מֶר לְיֹֽצְרוֹ֙ מַֽה־תַּעֲשֶׂ֔ה וּפׇעׇלְךָ֖ אֵין־יָדַ֥יִם לֽוֹ׃{ס}


Woe to him that strives with his maker! Let the potsherd strive with the potsherds of the earth. Shall the clay say to him that fashions it, What makest thou? as for thy work, it has no hands?


###### Isaiah 45:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:10)


ה֛וֹי אֹמֵ֥ר לְאָ֖ב מַה־תּוֹלִ֑יד וּלְאִשָּׁ֖ה מַה־תְּחִילִֽין׃{ס}


Woe to him that says to his father, What begettest thou? or to the woman, With what art thou in travail?


###### Isaiah 45:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:11)


כֹּה־אָמַ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וְיֹצְר֑וֹ הָאֹתִיּ֣וֹת שְׁאָל֔וּנִי עַל־בָּנַ֛י וְעַל־פֹּ֥עַל יָדַ֖י תְּצַוֻּֽנִי׃


Thus says the Lord, the Holy One of Yisra᾽el, and his Maker, Ask me of things to come; concerning my sons, and concerning the work of my hands command me.


###### Isaiah 45:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:12)


אָֽנֹכִי֙ עָשִׂ֣יתִי אֶ֔רֶץ וְאָדָ֖ם עָלֶ֣יהָ בָרָ֑אתִי אֲנִ֗י יָדַי֙ נָט֣וּ שָׁמַ֔יִם וְכׇל־צְבָאָ֖ם צִוֵּֽיתִי׃


I have made the earth, and created man upon it: I, even my hands, have stretched out the heavens, and all their host have I commanded.


###### Isaiah 45:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:13)


אָֽנֹכִי֙ הַעִירֹתִ֣הֽוּ בְצֶ֔דֶק וְכׇל־דְּרָכָ֖יו אֲיַשֵּׁ֑ר הֽוּא־יִבְנֶ֤ה עִירִי֙ וְגָלוּתִ֣י יְשַׁלֵּ֔חַ לֹ֤א בִמְחִיר֙ וְלֹ֣א בְשֹׁ֔חַד אָמַ֖ר יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָאֽוֹת׃{ס}


I have raised him up in righteousness, and I will make straight all his ways: he shall build my city, and he shall let go my captives, not for price nor reward, says the Lord of hosts.


###### Isaiah 45:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:14)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה יְגִ֨יעַ מִצְרַ֥יִם וּֽסְחַר־כּוּשׁ֮ וּסְבָאִים֮ אַנְשֵׁ֣י מִדָּה֒ עָלַ֤יִךְ יַעֲבֹ֙רוּ֙ וְלָ֣ךְ יִֽהְי֔וּ אַחֲרַ֣יִךְ יֵלֵ֔כוּ בַּזִּקִּ֖ים יַעֲבֹ֑רוּ וְאֵלַ֤יִךְ יִֽשְׁתַּחֲווּ֙ אֵלַ֣יִךְ יִתְפַּלָּ֔לוּ אַ֣ךְ בָּ֥ךְ אֵ֛ל וְאֵ֥ין ע֖וֹד אֶ֥פֶס אֱלֹהִֽים׃


Thus says the Lord, The labour of Miżrayim, and the merchandise of Kush and of the Seva᾽im, men of stature, shall come over to thee, and they shall be thine: they shall come after thee; in chains they shall come over, and they shall fall down to thee, they shall make supplication to thee, saying, Surely God is in thee; and there is none else, there is no other God.


###### Isaiah 45:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:15)


אָכֵ֕ן אַתָּ֖ה אֵ֣ל מִסְתַּתֵּ֑ר אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל מוֹשִֽׁיעַ׃


Verily Thou art a God who hidest Thyself, Thou are the God of Yisra᾽el who savest them.


###### Isaiah 45:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:16)


בּ֥וֹשׁוּ וְגַֽם־נִכְלְמ֖וּ כֻּלָּ֑ם יַחְדָּו֙ הָלְכ֣וּ בַכְּלִמָּ֔ה חָרָשֵׁ֖י צִירִֽים׃


They shall be ashamed, and also confounded, all of them: they shall go to confusion together that are makers of idols.


###### Isaiah 45:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:17)


יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ נוֹשַׁ֣ע בַּֽיהֹוָ֔ה תְּשׁוּעַ֖ת עוֹלָמִ֑ים לֹא־תֵבֹ֥שׁוּ וְלֹא־תִכָּלְמ֖וּ עַד־ע֥וֹלְמֵי עַֽד׃{פ}


But Yisra᾽el shall be saved in the Lord with an everlasting salvation: you shall not be ashamed nor confounded to all eternity.


###### Isaiah 45:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:18)


כִּ֣י כֹ֣ה אָֽמַר־יְ֠הֹוָ֠ה בּוֹרֵ֨א הַשָּׁמַ֜יִם ה֣וּא הָאֱלֹהִ֗ים יֹצֵ֨ר הָאָ֤רֶץ וְעֹשָׂהּ֙ ה֣וּא כֽוֹנְנָ֔הּ לֹא־תֹ֥הוּ בְרָאָ֖הּ לָשֶׁ֣בֶת יְצָרָ֑הּ אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה וְאֵ֥ין עֽוֹד׃


For thus says the Lord that created the heavens; God himself that formed the earth and made it; he has established it, he did not create it a waste land, he formed it to be inhabited: I am the Lord; and there is none else.


###### Isaiah 45:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:19)


לֹ֧א בַסֵּ֣תֶר דִּבַּ֗רְתִּי בִּמְקוֹם֙ אֶ֣רֶץ חֹ֔שֶׁךְ לֹ֥א אָמַ֛רְתִּי לְזֶ֥רַע יַעֲקֹ֖ב תֹּ֣הוּ בַקְּשׁ֑וּנִי אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ דֹּבֵ֣ר צֶ֔דֶק מַגִּ֖יד מֵישָׁרִֽים׃


I have not spoken in secret, in a place of a land of darkness: I said not to the seed of Ya῾aqov, Seek me in an empty waste: I the Lord speak righteousness, I declare things that are right.


###### Isaiah 45:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:20)


הִקָּבְצ֥וּ וָבֹ֛אוּ הִֽתְנַגְּשׁ֥וּ יַחְדָּ֖ו פְּלִיטֵ֣י הַגּוֹיִ֑ם לֹ֣א יָדְע֗וּ הַנֹּֽשְׂאִים֙ אֶת־עֵ֣ץ פִּסְלָ֔ם וּמִֽתְפַּלְלִ֔ים אֶל־אֵ֖ל לֹ֥א יוֹשִֽׁיעַ׃


Assemble yourselves and come; draw near together, you that are escaped of the nations: they have no knowledge, those that carry about their wooden carved idol, and pray to a god that cannot save.


###### Isaiah 45:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:21)


הַגִּ֣ידוּ וְהַגִּ֔ישׁוּ אַ֥ף יִֽוָּעֲצ֖וּ יַחְדָּ֑ו מִ֣י הִשְׁמִ֩יעַ֩ זֹ֨את מִקֶּ֜דֶם מֵאָ֣ז הִגִּידָ֗הּ הֲל֨וֹא אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ וְאֵֽין־ע֤וֹד אֱלֹהִים֙ מִבַּלְעָדַ֔י אֵל־צַדִּ֣יק וּמוֹשִׁ֔יעַ אַ֖יִן זוּלָתִֽי׃


Declare and bring them near; let them take counsel together: who declared this from ancient time? who told it from that time? did not I the Lord? and there is no God else beside me; a just God and a deliverer; there is none beside me.


###### Isaiah 45:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:22)


פְּנוּ־אֵלַ֥י וְהִוָּשְׁע֖וּ כׇּל־אַפְסֵי־אָ֑רֶץ כִּ֥י אֲנִי־אֵ֖ל וְאֵ֥ין עֽוֹד׃


Look to me, and be saved, all the ends of the earth: for I am God, and there is none else.


###### Isaiah 45:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:23)


בִּ֣י נִשְׁבַּ֔עְתִּי יָצָ֨א מִפִּ֧י צְדָקָ֛ה דָּבָ֖ר וְלֹ֣א יָשׁ֑וּב כִּי־לִי֙ תִּכְרַ֣ע כׇּל־בֶּ֔רֶךְ תִּשָּׁבַ֖ע כׇּל־לָשֽׁוֹן׃


I have sworn by myself, a word of righteousness is gone out of my mouth, a word that shall not be reversed, That to me every knee shall bow, every tongue shall swear.


###### Isaiah 45:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:24)


אַ֧ךְ בַּיהֹוָ֛ה לִ֥י אָמַ֖ר צְדָק֣וֹת וָעֹ֑ז עָדָיו֙ יָב֣וֹא וְיֵבֹ֔שׁוּ כֹּ֖ל הַנֶּחֱרִ֥ים בּֽוֹ׃


Surely, he said to me, in the Lord there is righteousness and strength: even to him shall men come; and all that are incensed against him shall be ashamed.


###### Isaiah 45:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/45:25)


בַּיהֹוָ֛ה יִצְדְּק֥וּ וְיִֽתְהַלְל֖וּ כׇּל־זֶ֥רַע יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


In the Lord shall all the seed of Yisra᾽el be justified, and shall glory.



## Chapter 46

###### Isaiah 46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:1)


כָּרַ֥ע בֵּל֙ קֹרֵ֣ס נְב֔וֹ הָיוּ֙ עֲצַבֵּיהֶ֔ם לַחַיָּ֖ה וְלַבְּהֵמָ֑ה נְשֻׂאֹתֵיכֶ֣ם עֲמוּס֔וֹת מַשָּׂ֖א לַעֲיֵפָֽה׃


Bel bows down, Nevo stoops, their idols were upon the beasts, and upon the cattle; the things you carry are laden as a burden on the weary beast.


###### Isaiah 46:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:2)


קָרְס֤וּ כָֽרְעוּ֙ יַחְדָּ֔ו לֹ֥א יָכְל֖וּ מַלֵּ֣ט מַשָּׂ֑א וְנַפְשָׁ֖ם בַּשְּׁבִ֥י הָלָֽכָה׃{פ}


They stoop, they bow down together; they could not deliver the burden, but themselves are gone into captivity.


###### Isaiah 46:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:3)


שִׁמְע֤וּ אֵלַי֙ בֵּ֣ית יַעֲקֹ֔ב וְכׇל־שְׁאֵרִ֖ית בֵּ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל הַֽעֲמֻסִים֙ מִנִּי־בֶ֔טֶן הַנְּשֻׂאִ֖ים מִנִּי־רָֽחַם׃


Hearken to me, O house of Ya῾aqov, and all the remnant of the house of Yisra᾽el, who are borne by me from birth, who are carried from the womb:


###### Isaiah 46:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:4)


וְעַד־זִקְנָה֙ אֲנִ֣י ה֔וּא וְעַד־שֵׂיבָ֖ה אֲנִ֣י אֶסְבֹּ֑ל אֲנִ֤י עָשִׂ֙יתִי֙ וַאֲנִ֣י אֶשָּׂ֔א וַאֲנִ֥י אֶסְבֹּ֖ל וַאֲמַלֵּֽט׃{ס}


and even to your old age I am the same; and even to hoar hairs will I carry you: I have made, and I will bear; and I will carry, and will deliver you.


###### Isaiah 46:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:5)


לְמִ֥י תְדַמְּי֖וּנִי וְתַשְׁו֑וּ וְתַמְשִׁל֖וּנִי וְנִדְמֶֽה׃


To whom will you liken me, and make me equal, and compare me, that we may be like?


###### Isaiah 46:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:6)


הַזָּלִ֤ים זָהָב֙ מִכִּ֔יס וְכֶ֖סֶף בַּקָּנֶ֣ה יִשְׁקֹ֑לוּ יִשְׂכְּר֤וּ צוֹרֵף֙ וְיַעֲשֵׂ֣הוּ אֵ֔ל יִסְגְּד֖וּ אַף־יִֽשְׁתַּחֲוֽוּ׃


They that scatter gold out of the bag, and weigh silver in the balance, and hire a goldsmith; and he makes it a god; they fall down, indeed, they prostrate themselves.


###### Isaiah 46:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:7)


יִ֠שָּׂאֻ֠הוּ עַל־כָּתֵ֨ף יִסְבְּלֻ֜הוּ וְיַנִּיחֻ֤הוּ תַחְתָּיו֙ וְיַֽעֲמֹ֔ד מִמְּקוֹמ֖וֹ לֹ֣א יָמִ֑ישׁ אַף־יִצְעַ֤ק אֵלָיו֙ וְלֹ֣א יַעֲנֶ֔ה מִצָּרָת֖וֹ לֹ֥א יוֹשִׁיעֶֽנּוּ׃{ס}


They bear him upon the shoulder, they carry him, and set him in his place, and he stands; from his place he cannot move: even if one shall cry out to him, yet can he not answer, nor save him out of his trouble.


###### Isaiah 46:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:8)


זִכְרוּ־זֹ֖את וְהִתְאֹשָׁ֑שׁוּ הָשִׁ֥יבוּ פוֹשְׁעִ֖ים עַל־לֵֽב׃


Remember this, and show yourselves men: bring it again to mind, O transgressors.


###### Isaiah 46:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:9)


זִכְר֥וּ רִאשֹׁנ֖וֹת מֵעוֹלָ֑ם כִּ֣י אָנֹכִ֥י אֵל֙ וְאֵ֣ין ע֔וֹד אֱלֹהִ֖ים וְאֶ֥פֶס כָּמֽוֹנִי׃


Remember the former things of old: for I am God, and there is none else; I am God, and there is none like me,


###### Isaiah 46:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:10)


מַגִּ֤יד מֵֽרֵאשִׁית֙ אַחֲרִ֔ית וּמִקֶּ֖דֶם אֲשֶׁ֣ר לֹא־נַֽעֲשׂ֑וּ אֹמֵר֙ עֲצָתִ֣י תָק֔וּם וְכׇל־חֶפְצִ֖י אֶעֱשֶֽׂה׃


declaring the end from the beginning, and from ancient times the things that are not yet done, saying, My counsel shall stand, and I will do all my pleasure:


###### Isaiah 46:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:11)


קֹרֵ֤א מִמִּזְרָח֙ עַ֔יִט מֵאֶ֥רֶץ מֶרְחָ֖ק אִ֣ישׁ (עצתו) [עֲצָתִ֑י] אַף־דִּבַּ֙רְתִּי֙ אַף־אֲבִיאֶ֔נָּה יָצַ֖רְתִּי אַף־אֶעֱשֶֽׂנָּה׃{ס}


calling an eagle from the east, the man that executes my counsel from a far country: I have spoken it, I will also bring it to pass; I have purposed it. I will also do it.


###### Isaiah 46:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:12)


שִׁמְע֥וּ אֵלַ֖י אַבִּ֣ירֵי לֵ֑ב הָרְחוֹקִ֖ים מִצְּדָקָֽה׃


Hearken to me, you stout-hearted, that are far from righteous­ness:


###### Isaiah 46:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/46:13)


קֵרַ֤בְתִּי צִדְקָתִי֙ לֹ֣א תִרְחָ֔ק וּתְשׁוּעָתִ֖י לֹ֣א תְאַחֵ֑ר וְנָתַתִּ֤י בְצִיּוֹן֙ תְּשׁוּעָ֔ה לְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל תִּפְאַרְתִּֽי׃{ס}


I bring near my righteousness; it shall not be far off, and my salvation shall not tarry: and I will place salvation in Żiyyon for Yisra᾽el my glory.



## Chapter 47

###### Isaiah 47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:1)


רְדִ֣י**׀** וּשְׁבִ֣י עַל־עָפָ֗ר בְּתוּלַת֙ בַּת־בָּבֶ֔ל שְׁבִי־לָאָ֥רֶץ אֵין־כִּסֵּ֖א בַּת־כַּשְׂדִּ֑ים כִּ֣י לֹ֤א תוֹסִ֙יפִי֙ יִקְרְאוּ־לָ֔ךְ רַכָּ֖ה וַעֲנֻגָּֽה׃


Come down, and sit in the dust, O virgin daughter of Bavel, sit on the ground: without a throne, O daughter of Kasdim: for thou shalt no more be called tender and delicate.


###### Isaiah 47:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:2)


קְחִ֥י רֵחַ֖יִם וְטַ֣חֲנִי קָ֑מַח גַּלִּ֨י צַמָּתֵ֧ךְ חֶשְׂפִּי־שֹׁ֛בֶל גַּלִּי־שׁ֖וֹק עִבְרִ֥י נְהָרֽוֹת׃


Take the millstones, and grind meal: uncover thy locks, tuck up the train, uncover the leg, pass over the rivers.


###### Isaiah 47:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:3)


תִּגָּל֙ עֶרְוָתֵ֔ךְ גַּ֥ם תֵּרָאֶ֖ה חֶרְפָּתֵ֑ךְ נָקָ֣ם אֶקָּ֔ח וְלֹ֥א אֶפְגַּ֖ע אָדָֽם׃{פ}


Thy nakedness shall be uncovered, yea, thy shame shall be seen: I will take vengeance, and I will spare no man.


###### Isaiah 47:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:4)


גֹּאֲלֵ֕נוּ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת שְׁמ֑וֹ קְד֖וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


As for our redeemer, the Lord of hosts is his name, the Holy One of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 47:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:5)


שְׁבִ֥י דוּמָ֛ם וּבֹ֥אִי בַחֹ֖שֶׁךְ בַּת־כַּשְׂדִּ֑ים כִּ֣י לֹ֤א תוֹסִ֙יפִי֙ יִקְרְאוּ־לָ֔ךְ גְּבֶ֖רֶת מַמְלָכֽוֹת׃


Sit thou silent, and get thee into darkness, O daughter of Kasdim: for thou shalt no more be called, The mistress of kingdoms.


###### Isaiah 47:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:6)


קָצַ֣פְתִּי עַל־עַמִּ֗י חִלַּ֙לְתִּי֙ נַחֲלָתִ֔י וָאֶתְּנֵ֖ם בְּיָדֵ֑ךְ לֹא־שַׂ֤מְתְּ לָהֶם֙ רַחֲמִ֔ים עַל־זָקֵ֕ן הִכְבַּ֥דְתְּ עֻלֵּ֖ךְ מְאֹֽד׃


I was angry with my people, I have profaned my inheritance, and given them into thy hand: thou didst show them no mercy; upon the aged hast thou very heavily laid thy yoke.


###### Isaiah 47:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:7)


וַתֹּ֣אמְרִ֔י לְעוֹלָ֖ם אֶהְיֶ֣ה גְבָ֑רֶת עַ֣ד לֹא־שַׂ֥מְתְּ אֵ֙לֶּה֙ עַל־לִבֵּ֔ךְ לֹ֥א זָכַ֖רְתְּ אַחֲרִיתָֽהּ׃{פ}


And thou didst say I shall be mistress for ever: so that thou didst not lay these things to thy heart, neither didst remember the latter end of it.


###### Isaiah 47:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:8)


וְעַתָּ֞ה שִׁמְעִי־זֹ֤את עֲדִינָה֙ הַיּוֹשֶׁ֣בֶת לָבֶ֔טַח הָאֹֽמְרָה֙ בִּלְבָבָ֔הּ אֲנִ֖י וְאַפְסִ֣י ע֑וֹד לֹ֤א אֵשֵׁב֙ אַלְמָנָ֔ה וְלֹ֥א אֵדַ֖ע שְׁכֽוֹל׃


Therefore hear now this, thou that art given to pleasures, that dwellest securely, that sayst in thy heart, I am, and none else beside me; I shall not sit as a widow, neither shall I know the loss of children.


###### Isaiah 47:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:9)


וְתָבֹ֩אנָה֩ לָּ֨ךְ שְׁתֵּי־אֵ֥לֶּה רֶ֛גַע בְּי֥וֹם אֶחָ֖ד שְׁכ֣וֹל וְאַלְמֹ֑ן כְּתֻמָּם֙ בָּ֣אוּ עָלַ֔יִךְ בְּרֹ֣ב כְּשָׁפַ֔יִךְ בְּעׇצְמַ֥ת חֲבָרַ֖יִךְ מְאֹֽד׃


But these two things shall come to thee in a moment in one day, the loss of children, and widowhood: they shall come upon thee in their full measure for the multitude of thy sorceries, and for the great abundance of thy enchantments.


###### Isaiah 47:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:10)


וַתִּבְטְחִ֣י בְרָעָתֵ֗ךְ אָמַרְתְּ֙ אֵ֣ין רֹאָ֔נִי חׇכְמָתֵ֥ךְ וְדַעְתֵּ֖ךְ הִ֣יא שׁוֹבְבָ֑תֶךְ וַתֹּאמְרִ֣י בְלִבֵּ֔ךְ אֲנִ֖י וְאַפְסִ֥י עֽוֹד׃


For thou hast trusted in thy wickedness: thou hast said, None sees me. Thy wisdom and thy knowledge, it has perverted thee; and thou hast said in thy heart, I am, and none else beside me.


###### Isaiah 47:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:11)


וּבָ֧א עָלַ֣יִךְ רָעָ֗ה לֹ֤א תֵֽדְעִי֙ שַׁחְרָ֔הּ וְתִפֹּ֤ל עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ הֹוָ֔ה לֹ֥א תוּכְלִ֖י כַּפְּרָ֑הּ וְתָבֹ֨א עָלַ֧יִךְ פִּתְאֹ֛ם שֹׁאָ֖ה לֹ֥א תֵדָֽעִי׃


Therefore shall evil come upon thee: thou shalt not know how to charm it away: and mischief shall fall upon thee; thou shalt not be able to put it off: and ruin shall come upon thee suddenly, which thou shalt not know.


###### Isaiah 47:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:12)


עִמְדִי־נָ֤א בַחֲבָרַ֙יִךְ֙ וּבְרֹ֣ב כְּשָׁפַ֔יִךְ בַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר יָגַ֖עַתְּ מִנְּעוּרָ֑יִךְ אוּלַ֛י תּוּכְלִ֥י הוֹעִ֖יל אוּלַ֥י תַּעֲרֽוֹצִי׃


Stand now with thy enchantments, and with the multitude of thy sorceries, wherein thou hast laboured from thy youth; perhaps thou shalt be able to profit, perhaps thou shalt inspire terror.


###### Isaiah 47:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:13)


נִלְאֵ֖ית בְּרֹ֣ב עֲצָתָ֑יִךְ יַעַמְדוּ־נָ֨א וְיוֹשִׁיעֻ֜ךְ (הברו) [הֹבְרֵ֣י] שָׁמַ֗יִם הַחֹזִים֙ בַּכּ֣וֹכָבִ֔ים מֽוֹדִעִים֙ לֶחֳדָשִׁ֔ים מֵאֲשֶׁ֥ר יָבֹ֖אוּ עָלָֽיִךְ׃


Thou art wearied in the multitude of thy counsels. Let now the astrologers, the stargazers, the monthly prognosticators, stand up, and save thee from these things that shall come upon thee.


###### Isaiah 47:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:14)


הִנֵּ֨ה הָי֤וּ כְקַשׁ֙ אֵ֣שׁ שְׂרָפָ֔תַם לֹא־יַצִּ֥ילוּ אֶת־נַפְשָׁ֖ם מִיַּ֣ד לֶהָבָ֑ה אֵין־גַּחֶ֣לֶת לַחְמָ֔ם א֖וּר לָשֶׁ֥בֶת נֶגְדּֽוֹ׃


Behold, they shall be as stubble; the fire shall burn them; they shall not deliver themselves from the power of the flame: there shall not be a coal to warm at, nor fire to sit before it.


###### Isaiah 47:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/47:15)


כֵּ֥ן הָיוּ־לָ֖ךְ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יָגָ֑עַתְּ סֹחֲרַ֣יִךְ מִנְּעוּרַ֗יִךְ אִ֤ישׁ לְעֶבְרוֹ֙ תָּע֔וּ אֵ֖ין מוֹשִׁיעֵֽךְ׃{ס}


Thus shall those be to thee with whom thou hast laboured, with whom thou hast traded, from thy youth: they shall wander every one to his own quarter, none shall save thee.



## Chapter 48

###### Isaiah 48:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:1)


שִׁמְעוּ־זֹ֣את בֵּֽית־יַעֲקֹ֗ב הַנִּקְרָאִים֙ בְּשֵׁ֣ם יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל וּמִמֵּ֥י יְהוּדָ֖ה יָצָ֑אוּ הַֽנִּשְׁבָּעִ֣ים**׀** בְּשֵׁ֣ם יְהֹוָ֗ה וּבֵאלֹהֵ֤י יִשְׂרָאֵל֙ יַזְכִּ֔ירוּ לֹ֥א בֶאֱמֶ֖ת וְלֹ֥א בִצְדָקָֽה׃


Hear this, O house of Ya῾aqov, who are called by the name of Yisra᾽el, and are come forth out of the waters of Yehuda, who swear by the name of the Lord, and make mention of the God of Yisra᾽el, but not in truth, nor in righteousness.


###### Isaiah 48:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:2)


כִּֽי־מֵעִ֤יר הַקֹּ֙דֶשׁ֙ נִקְרָ֔אוּ וְעַל־אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל נִסְמָ֑כוּ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת שְׁמֽוֹ׃{ס}


For they call themselves of the holy city, and stay themselves upon the God of Yisra᾽el, The Lord of hosts is his name.


###### Isaiah 48:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:3)


הָרִֽאשֹׁנוֹת֙ מֵאָ֣ז הִגַּ֔דְתִּי וּמִפִּ֥י יָצְא֖וּ וְאַשְׁמִיעֵ֑ם פִּתְאֹ֥ם עָשִׂ֖יתִי וַתָּבֹֽאנָה׃


I have declared the former things from the beginning; and they went out of my mouth, and I reported them; I did them suddenly, and they came to pass.


###### Isaiah 48:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:4)


מִדַּעְתִּ֕י כִּ֥י קָשֶׁ֖ה אָ֑תָּה וְגִ֤יד בַּרְזֶל֙ עׇרְפֶּ֔ךָ וּמִצְחֲךָ֖ נְחוּשָֽׁה׃


Because I know that thou art obstinate, and thy neck is an iron sinew, and thy forehead brass;


###### Isaiah 48:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:5)


וָאַגִּ֤יד לְךָ֙ מֵאָ֔ז בְּטֶ֥רֶם תָּב֖וֹא הִשְׁמַעְתִּ֑יךָ פֶּן־תֹּאמַר֙ עׇצְבִּ֣י עָשָׂ֔ם וּפִסְלִ֥י וְנִסְכִּ֖י צִוָּֽם׃


I have even from the beginning declared it to thee; before it came to pass I showed it thee: lest thou shouldst say, My idol has done them, and my carved idol, and my molten image has commanded them.


###### Isaiah 48:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:6)


שָׁמַ֤עְתָּֽ חֲזֵה֙ כֻּלָּ֔הּ וְאַתֶּ֖ם הֲל֣וֹא תַגִּ֑ידוּ הִשְׁמַעְתִּ֤יךָ חֲדָשׁוֹת֙ מֵעַ֔תָּה וּנְצֻר֖וֹת וְלֹ֥א יְדַעְתָּֽם׃


Thou hast heard, now see all this; and will you not declare it? I have shown thee new things from this time, hidden things, which thou didst not know.


###### Isaiah 48:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:7)


עַתָּ֤ה נִבְרְאוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א מֵאָ֔ז וְלִפְנֵי־י֖וֹם וְלֹ֣א שְׁמַעְתָּ֑ם פֶּן־תֹּאמַ֖ר הִנֵּ֥ה יְדַעְתִּֽין׃


They are created now, and not from the beginning; and before this day thou heardest them not; lest thou shouldst say, Behold, I knew them.


###### Isaiah 48:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:8)


גַּ֣ם לֹֽא־שָׁמַ֗עְתָּ גַּ֚ם לֹ֣א יָדַ֔עְתָּ גַּ֕ם מֵאָ֖ז לֹא־פִתְּחָ֣ה אׇזְנֶ֑ךָ כִּ֤י יָדַ֙עְתִּי֙ בָּג֣וֹד תִּבְגּ֔וֹד וּפֹשֵׁ֥עַ מִבֶּ֖טֶן קֹ֥רָא לָֽךְ׃


Thou didst neither hear nor know; nor from old was thy ear opened: for I knew that thou wouldst deal very treacherously, and wast called a transgressor from birth.


###### Isaiah 48:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:9)


לְמַ֤עַן שְׁמִי֙ אַאֲרִ֣יךְ אַפִּ֔י וּתְהִלָּתִ֖י אֶחֱטׇם־לָ֑ךְ לְבִלְתִּ֖י הַכְרִיתֶֽךָ׃


For my name’s sake will I defer my anger, and for my praise will I refrain for thee, that I cut thee not off.


###### Isaiah 48:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:10)


הִנֵּ֥ה צְרַפְתִּ֖יךָ וְלֹ֣א בְכָ֑סֶף בְּחַרְתִּ֖יךָ בְּכ֥וּר עֹֽנִי׃


Behold, I have refined thee, but not into silver; I have tried thee in the furnace of affliction.


###### Isaiah 48:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:11)


לְמַעֲנִ֧י לְמַעֲנִ֛י אֶעֱשֶׂ֖ה כִּ֣י אֵ֣יךְ יֵחָ֑ל וּכְבוֹדִ֖י לְאַחֵ֥ר לֹא־אֶתֵּֽן׃{פ}


For my own sake, for my own sake, will I do it: for how should my name be profaned? and I will not give my glory to another.


###### Isaiah 48:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:12)


שְׁמַ֤ע אֵלַי֙ יַֽעֲקֹ֔ב וְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל מְקֹרָאִ֑י אֲנִי־הוּא֙ אֲנִ֣י רִאשׁ֔וֹן אַ֖ף אֲנִ֥י אַחֲרֽוֹן׃


Hearken to me, O Ya῾aqov and Yisra᾽el whom I called; I am he; I am the first, I also am the last.


###### Isaiah 48:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:13)


אַף־יָדִי֙ יָ֣סְדָה אֶ֔רֶץ וִימִינִ֖י טִפְּחָ֣ה שָׁמָ֑יִם קֹרֵ֥א אֲנִ֛י אֲלֵיהֶ֖ם יַעַמְד֥וּ יַחְדָּֽו׃


My hand also has laid the foundation of the earth, and my right hand has spanned the heavens: when I call to them, they stand up together.


###### Isaiah 48:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:14)


הִקָּבְצ֤וּ כֻלְּכֶם֙ וּֽשְׁמָ֔עוּ מִ֥י בָהֶ֖ם הִגִּ֣יד אֶת־אֵ֑לֶּה יְהֹוָ֣ה אֲהֵב֔וֹ יַעֲשֶׂ֤ה חֶפְצוֹ֙ בְּבָבֶ֔ל וּזְרֹע֖וֹ כַּשְׂדִּֽים׃


All of you, assemble yourselves, and hear; which among them has declared these things? He whom the Lord loves, he will do his pleasure on Bavel, and his arm shall be on the Kasdim.


###### Isaiah 48:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:15)


אֲנִ֥י אֲנִ֛י דִּבַּ֖רְתִּי אַף־קְרָאתִ֑יו הֲבִאֹתִ֖יו וְהִצְלִ֥יחַ דַּרְכּֽוֹ׃


I, even I, have spoken; indeed, I have called him: I have brought him, and he shall succeed in his way.


###### Isaiah 48:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:16)


קִרְב֧וּ אֵלַ֣י שִׁמְעוּ־זֹ֗את לֹ֤א מֵרֹאשׁ֙ בַּסֵּ֣תֶר דִּבַּ֔רְתִּי מֵעֵ֥ת הֱיוֹתָ֖הּ שָׁ֣ם אָ֑נִי וְעַתָּ֗ה אֲדֹנָ֧י יֱהֹוִ֛ה שְׁלָחַ֖נִי וְרוּחֽוֹ׃{פ}


Come near to me, hear this; I have not spoken in secret from the beginning; from the time that it was, there have I been: and now the Lord God, and his spirit, has sent me.


###### Isaiah 48:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:17)


כֹּה־אָמַ֧ר יְהֹוָ֛ה גֹּאַלְךָ֖ קְד֣וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל אֲנִ֨י יְהֹוָ֤ה אֱלֹהֶ֙יךָ֙ מְלַמֶּדְךָ֣ לְהוֹעִ֔יל מַדְרִֽיכְךָ֖ בְּדֶ֥רֶךְ תֵּלֵֽךְ׃


Thus says the Lord, thy redeemer, the Holy One of Yisra᾽el; I am the Lord thy God who teaches thee for thy profit, who leads thee by the way that thou shouldst go.


###### Isaiah 48:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:18)


ל֥וּא הִקְשַׁ֖בְתָּ לְמִצְוֺתָ֑י וַיְהִ֤י כַנָּהָר֙ שְׁלוֹמֶ֔ךָ וְצִדְקָתְךָ֖ כְּגַלֵּ֥י הַיָּֽם׃


O that thou hadst hearkened to my commandments! then had thy peace been as a river, and thy righteousness as the waves of the sea:


###### Isaiah 48:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:19)


וַיְהִ֤י כַחוֹל֙ זַרְעֶ֔ךָ וְצֶאֱצָאֵ֥י מֵעֶ֖יךָ כִּמְעֹתָ֑יו לֹא־יִכָּרֵ֧ת וְֽלֹא־יִשָּׁמֵ֛ד שְׁמ֖וֹ מִלְּפָנָֽי׃{ס}


thy seed also had been as the sand, and the offspring of thy bowels like its pebbles; his name should not have been cut off nor destroyed from before me.


###### Isaiah 48:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:20)


צְא֣וּ מִבָּבֶל֮ בִּרְח֣וּ מִכַּשְׂדִּים֒ בְּק֣וֹל רִנָּ֗ה הַגִּ֤ידוּ הַשְׁמִ֙יעוּ֙ זֹ֔את הוֹצִיא֖וּהָ עַד־קְצֵ֣ה הָאָ֑רֶץ אִמְר֕וּ גָּאַ֥ל יְהֹוָ֖ה עַבְדּ֥וֹ יַעֲקֹֽב׃


Go forth out of Bavel, flee from the Kasdim, with a voice of singing declare, tell this, utter it even to the end of the earth; say, The Lord has redeemed his servant Ya῾aqov.


###### Isaiah 48:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:21)


וְלֹ֣א צָמְא֗וּ בׇּֽחֳרָבוֹת֙ הוֹלִיכָ֔ם מַ֥יִם מִצּ֖וּר הִזִּ֣יל לָ֑מוֹ וַיִּ֨בְקַע־צ֔וּר וַיָּזֻ֖בוּ מָֽיִם׃


And they thirsted not when he led them through the deserts: he caused water to flow out of the rock for them: he split the rock, and the water gushed out.


###### Isaiah 48:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/48:22)


אֵ֣ין שָׁל֔וֹם אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה לָרְשָׁעִֽים׃{פ}


There is no peace, says the Lord, for the wicked.



## Chapter 49

###### Isaiah 49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:1)


שִׁמְע֤וּ אִיִּים֙ אֵלַ֔י וְהַקְשִׁ֥יבוּ לְאֻמִּ֖ים מֵרָח֑וֹק יְהֹוָה֙ מִבֶּ֣טֶן קְרָאָ֔נִי מִמְּעֵ֥י אִמִּ֖י הִזְכִּ֥יר שְׁמִֽי׃


Listen, O isles, to me; and hearken, O people, from far; The Lord has called me from birth; from the bowels of my mother has he made mention of my name.


###### Isaiah 49:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:2)


וַיָּ֤שֶׂם פִּי֙ כְּחֶ֣רֶב חַדָּ֔ה בְּצֵ֥ל יָד֖וֹ הֶחְבִּיאָ֑נִי וַיְשִׂימֵ֙נִי֙ לְחֵ֣ץ בָּר֔וּר בְּאַשְׁפָּת֖וֹ הִסְתִּירָֽנִי׃


And he has made my mouth like a sharp sword; in the shadow of his hand has he hid me, and made me a polished shaft; in his quiver has he hid me;


###### Isaiah 49:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:3)


וַיֹּ֥אמֶר לִ֖י עַבְדִּי־אָ֑תָּה יִשְׂרָאֵ֕ל אֲשֶׁר־בְּךָ֖ אֶתְפָּאָֽר׃


and said to me, Thou art my servant, Yisra᾽el, in whom I will be glorified.


###### Isaiah 49:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:4)


וַאֲנִ֤י אָמַ֙רְתִּי֙ לְרִ֣יק יָגַ֔עְתִּי לְתֹ֥הוּ וְהֶ֖בֶל כֹּחִ֣י כִלֵּ֑יתִי אָכֵן֙ מִשְׁפָּטִ֣י אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה וּפְעֻלָּתִ֖י אֶת־אֱלֹהָֽי׃{ס}


Then I said, I have laboured in vain, I have spent my strength for nought, and in vain: yet surely my judgment is with the Lord, and my reward with my God.


###### Isaiah 49:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:5)


וְעַתָּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה יֽוֹצְרִ֤י מִבֶּ֙טֶן֙ לְעֶ֣בֶד ל֔וֹ לְשׁוֹבֵ֤ב יַֽעֲקֹב֙ אֵלָ֔יו וְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל (לא) [ל֣וֹ] יֵאָסֵ֑ף וְאֶכָּבֵד֙ בְּעֵינֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה וֵאלֹהַ֖י הָיָ֥ה עֻזִּֽי׃


And now, says the Lord that formed me from the womb to be his servant to bring Ya῾aqov back to him, that Yisra᾽el should be gathered to him, and I was honoured in the eyes of the Lord, and my God was my strength.


###### Isaiah 49:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:6)


וַיֹּ֗אמֶר נָקֵ֨ל מִֽהְיוֹתְךָ֥ לִי֙ עֶ֔בֶד לְהָקִים֙ אֶת־שִׁבְטֵ֣י יַעֲקֹ֔ב (ונצירי) [וּנְצוּרֵ֥י] יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לְהָשִׁ֑יב וּנְתַתִּ֙יךָ֙ לְא֣וֹר גּוֹיִ֔ם לִהְי֥וֹת יְשׁוּעָתִ֖י עַד־קְצֵ֥ה הָאָֽרֶץ׃{ס}


And he said, It is too slight a thing that thou shouldst be my servant to raise up the tribes of Ya῾aqov, and to restore the preserved of Yisra᾽el: I will also give thee for a light to the nations, that my salvation may be to the end of the earth.


###### Isaiah 49:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:7)


כֹּ֣ה אָֽמַר־יְהֹוָה֩ גֹּאֵ֨ל יִשְׂרָאֵ֜ל קְדוֹשׁ֗וֹ לִבְזֹה־נֶ֜פֶשׁ לִמְתָ֤עֵֽב גּוֹי֙ לְעֶ֣בֶד מֹשְׁלִ֔ים מְלָכִים֙ יִרְא֣וּ וָקָ֔מוּ שָׂרִ֖ים וְיִֽשְׁתַּחֲו֑וּ לְמַ֤עַן יְהֹוָה֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר נֶאֱמָ֔ן קְדֹ֥שׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל וַיִּבְחָרֶֽךָּ׃{ס}


Thus says the Lord, the redeemer of Yisra᾽el, his Holy One, to him whom man despises, to him whom the nation abhors, to a servant of rulers, Kings shall see and arise, princes also shall prostrate themselves, because of the Lord who is faithful, and the Holy One of Yisra᾽el, who has chosen thee.


###### Isaiah 49:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:8)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה בְּעֵ֤ת רָצוֹן֙ עֲנִיתִ֔יךָ וּבְי֥וֹם יְשׁוּעָ֖ה עֲזַרְתִּ֑יךָ וְאֶצׇּרְךָ֗ וְאֶתֶּנְךָ֙ לִבְרִ֣ית עָ֔ם לְהָקִ֣ים אֶ֔רֶץ לְהַנְחִ֖יל נְחָל֥וֹת שֹׁמֵמֽוֹת׃


Thus says the Lord, In an acceptable time have I answered thee, and in a day of salvation have I helped thee: and I will preserve thee, and give thee for a covenant of the people, to restore the land, and to assign desolate inheritances to their owners.


###### Isaiah 49:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:9)


לֵאמֹ֤ר לַֽאֲסוּרִים֙ צֵ֔אוּ לַאֲשֶׁ֥ר בַּחֹ֖שֶׁךְ הִגָּל֑וּ עַל־דְּרָכִ֣ים יִרְע֔וּ וּבְכׇל־שְׁפָיִ֖ים מַרְעִיתָֽם׃


That thou mayst say to the prisoners, Go forth; to them that are in darkness, Show yourselves. They shall feed in the ways, and their pastures shall be on all high places.


###### Isaiah 49:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:10)


לֹ֤א יִרְעָ֙בוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א יִצְמָ֔אוּ וְלֹא־יַכֵּ֥ם שָׁרָ֖ב וָשָׁ֑מֶשׁ כִּֽי־מְרַחֲמָ֣ם יְנַהֲגֵ֔ם וְעַל־מַבּ֥וּעֵי מַ֖יִם יְנַהֲלֵֽם׃


They shall not hunger nor thirst; neither shall the heat nor sun smite them: for he that has mercy on them shall lead them, even by the springs of water shall he guide them.


###### Isaiah 49:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:11)


וְשַׂמְתִּ֥י כׇל־הָרַ֖י לַדָּ֑רֶךְ וּמְסִלֹּתַ֖י יְרֻמֽוּן׃


And I will make all my mountains a way, and my highways shall be raised up.


###### Isaiah 49:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:12)


הִנֵּה־אֵ֕לֶּה מֵרָח֖וֹק יָבֹ֑אוּ וְהִֽנֵּה־אֵ֙לֶּה֙ מִצָּפ֣וֹן וּמִיָּ֔ם וְאֵ֖לֶּה מֵאֶ֥רֶץ סִינִֽים׃


Behold, these shall come from far: and, lo, these from the north and from the west; and these from the land of Sinim.


###### Isaiah 49:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:13)


רׇנּ֤וּ שָׁמַ֙יִם֙ וְגִ֣ילִי אָ֔רֶץ (יפצחו) [וּפִצְח֥וּ] הָרִ֖ים רִנָּ֑ה כִּֽי־נִחַ֤ם יְהֹוָה֙ עַמּ֔וֹ וַעֲנִיָּ֖יו יְרַחֵֽם׃{ס}


Sing, O heavens; and be joyful, O earth; and break forth into singing, O mountains: for the Lord has comforted his people, and has mercy upon his afflicted.


###### Isaiah 49:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:14)


וַתֹּ֥אמֶר צִיּ֖וֹן עֲזָבַ֣נִי יְהֹוָ֑ה וַאדֹנָ֖י שְׁכֵחָֽנִי׃


But Żiyyon said, The Lord has forsaken me, and my Lord has forgotten me.


###### Isaiah 49:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:15)


הֲתִשְׁכַּ֤ח אִשָּׁה֙ עוּלָ֔הּ מֵרַחֵ֖ם בֶּן־בִּטְנָ֑הּ גַּם־אֵ֣לֶּה תִשְׁכַּ֔חְנָה וְאָנֹכִ֖י לֹ֥א אֶשְׁכָּחֵֽךְ׃


Can a woman forget her sucking child, that she should not have compassion on the son of her womb? even these may forget, yet I will not forget thee.


###### Isaiah 49:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:16)


הֵ֥ן עַל־כַּפַּ֖יִם חַקֹּתִ֑יךְ חוֹמֹתַ֥יִךְ נֶגְדִּ֖י תָּמִֽיד׃


Behold, I have graven thee upon the palms of my hands; thy walls are continually before me.


###### Isaiah 49:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:17)


מִהֲר֖וּ בָּנָ֑יִךְ מְהָֽרְסַ֥יִךְ וּמַחֲרִיבַ֖יִךְ מִמֵּ֥ךְ יֵצֵֽאוּ׃


Thy children make haste; thy destroyers and they that made thee waste go away from thee.


###### Isaiah 49:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:18)


שְׂאִֽי־סָבִ֤יב עֵינַ֙יִךְ֙ וּרְאִ֔י כֻּלָּ֖ם נִקְבְּצ֣וּ בָֽאוּ־לָ֑ךְ חַי־אָ֣נִי נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֗ה כִּ֤י כֻלָּם֙ כָּעֲדִ֣י תִלְבָּ֔שִׁי וּֽתְקַשְּׁרִ֖ים כַּכַּלָּֽה׃


Lift up thy eyes round about, and behold: all these gather themselves together, and come to thee. As I live, says the Lord, thou shalt surely clothe thee with them all, as with an ornament, and bind them on, like a bride.


###### Isaiah 49:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:19)


כִּ֤י חׇרְבֹתַ֙יִךְ֙ וְשֹׁ֣מְמֹתַ֔יִךְ וְאֶ֖רֶץ הֲרִסֻתֵ֑ךְ כִּ֤י עַתָּה֙ תֵּצְרִ֣י מִיּוֹשֵׁ֔ב וְרָחֲק֖וּ מְבַלְּעָֽיִךְ׃


For thy waste and thy desolate places, and thy devastated land, shall now be too narrow for the inhabitants, and they that swallowed thee up shall be far away.


###### Isaiah 49:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:20)


ע֚וֹד יֹאמְר֣וּ בְאׇזְנַ֔יִךְ בְּנֵ֖י שִׁכֻּלָ֑יִךְ צַר־לִ֥י הַמָּק֖וֹם גְּשָׁה־לִּ֥י וְאֵשֵֽׁבָה׃


The children that were taken away from thee shall say again in thy ears, The place is too narrow for me: make room for me that I may dwell.


###### Isaiah 49:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:21)


וְאָמַ֣רְתְּ בִּלְבָבֵ֗ךְ מִ֤י יָֽלַד־לִי֙ אֶת־אֵ֔לֶּה וַאֲנִ֥י שְׁכוּלָ֖ה וְגַלְמוּדָ֑ה גֹּלָ֣ה**׀** וְסוּרָ֗ה וְאֵ֙לֶּה֙ מִ֣י גִדֵּ֔ל הֵ֤ן אֲנִי֙ נִשְׁאַ֣רְתִּי לְבַדִּ֔י אֵ֖לֶּה אֵיפֹ֥ה הֵֽם׃{פ}


Then shalt thou say in thy heart, Who has begotten me these, seeing I have lost my children, a solitary, an exile, and a wanderer to and fro? and who has brought up these? Behold, I was left alone; these, where have they been?


###### Isaiah 49:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:22)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הִנֵּ֨ה אֶשָּׂ֤א אֶל־גּוֹיִם֙ יָדִ֔י וְאֶל־עַמִּ֖ים אָרִ֣ים נִסִּ֑י וְהֵבִ֤יאוּ בָנַ֙יִךְ֙ בְּחֹ֔צֶן וּבְנֹתַ֖יִךְ עַל־כָּתֵ֥ף תִּנָּשֶֽׂאנָה׃


Thus says the Lord God, Behold, I will lift up my hand to the nations, and set up my standard to the peoples: and they shall bring thy sons in their arms, and thy daughters shall be carried on their shoulders.


###### Isaiah 49:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:23)


וְהָי֨וּ מְלָכִ֜ים אֹמְנַ֗יִךְ וְשָׂרֽוֹתֵיהֶם֙ מֵינִ֣יקֹתַ֔יִךְ אַפַּ֗יִם אֶ֚רֶץ יִשְׁתַּ֣חֲווּ לָ֔ךְ וַעֲפַ֥ר רַגְלַ֖יִךְ יְלַחֵ֑כוּ וְיָדַ֙עַתְּ֙ כִּי־אֲנִ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יֵבֹ֖שׁוּ קֹוָֽי׃{ס}


And kings shall be thy foster fathers, and their queens thy nursing mothers: they shall bow down to thee with their face towards the earth, and lick up the dust of thy feet; and thou shalt know that I am the Lord: for they shall not be ashamed that wait for me.


###### Isaiah 49:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:24)


הֲיֻקַּ֥ח מִגִּבּ֖וֹר מַלְק֑וֹחַ וְאִם־שְׁבִ֥י צַדִּ֖יק יִמָּלֵֽט׃{ס}


Shall the prey be taken from the mighty, or the captive of the victorious delivered?


###### Isaiah 49:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:25)


כִּי־כֹ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה גַּם־שְׁבִ֤י גִבּוֹר֙ יֻקָּ֔ח וּמַלְק֥וֹחַ עָרִ֖יץ יִמָּלֵ֑ט וְאֶת־יְרִיבֵךְ֙ אָנֹכִ֣י אָרִ֔יב וְאֶת־בָּנַ֖יִךְ אָנֹכִ֥י אוֹשִֽׁיעַ׃


But thus says the Lord, Even the captives of the mighty shall be taken away, and the prey of the terrible shall be delivered: for I will contend with him that contends with thee, and I will save thy children.


###### Isaiah 49:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/49:26)


וְהַאֲכַלְתִּ֤י אֶת־מוֹנַ֙יִךְ֙ אֶת־בְּשָׂרָ֔ם וְכֶעָסִ֖יס דָּמָ֣ם יִשְׁכָּר֑וּן וְיָדְע֣וּ כׇל־בָּשָׂ֗ר כִּ֣י אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ מֽוֹשִׁיעֵ֔ךְ וְגֹאֲלֵ֖ךְ אֲבִ֥יר יַעֲקֹֽב׃{ס}


And I will feed them that oppress thee with their own flesh; and they shall be drunk with their own blood, as with sweet wine: and all flesh shall know that I, the Lord am thy deliverer and the mighty One of Ya῾aqov is thy redeemer.



## Chapter 50

###### Isaiah 50:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:1)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה אֵ֣י זֶ֠ה סֵ֣פֶר כְּרִית֤וּת אִמְּכֶם֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר שִׁלַּחְתִּ֔יהָ א֚וֹ מִ֣י מִנּוֹשַׁ֔י אֲשֶׁר־מָכַ֥רְתִּי אֶתְכֶ֖ם ל֑וֹ הֵ֤ן בַּעֲוֺנֹֽתֵיכֶם֙ נִמְכַּרְתֶּ֔ם וּבְפִשְׁעֵיכֶ֖ם שֻׁלְּחָ֥ה אִמְּכֶֽם׃


Thus says the Lord, Where is the bill of your mother’s divorcement, with which I have put her away? or which of my creditors is it to whom I have sold you? Behold, for your iniquities have you sold yourselves, and for your transgressions was your mother put away.


###### Isaiah 50:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:2)


מַדּ֨וּעַ בָּ֜אתִי וְאֵ֣ין אִ֗ישׁ קָרָ֘אתִי֮ וְאֵ֣ין עוֹנֶה֒ הֲקָצ֨וֹר קָצְרָ֤ה יָדִי֙ מִפְּד֔וּת וְאִם־אֵֽין־בִּ֥י כֹ֖חַ לְהַצִּ֑יל הֵ֣ן בְּגַעֲרָתִ֞י אַחֲרִ֣יב יָ֗ם אָשִׂ֤ים נְהָרוֹת֙ מִדְבָּ֔ר תִּבְאַ֤שׁ דְּגָתָם֙ מֵאֵ֣ין מַ֔יִם וְתָמֹ֖ת בַּצָּמָֽא׃


Why, when I came, was there no man? when I called, was there none to answer? Is my hand shortened at all, that it cannot redeem? or have I no power to deliver? behold, at my rebuke I dry up the sea, I make the rivers a wilderness: their fish rots, because there is no water, and dies for thirst.


###### Isaiah 50:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:3)


אַלְבִּ֥ישׁ שָׁמַ֖יִם קַדְר֑וּת וְשַׂ֖ק אָשִׂ֥ים כְּסוּתָֽם׃{פ}


I clothe the heavens with blackness, and I make sackcloth their covering.


###### Isaiah 50:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:4)


אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה נָ֤תַן לִי֙ לְשׁ֣וֹן לִמּוּדִ֔ים לָדַ֛עַת לָע֥וּת אֶת־יָעֵ֖ף דָּבָ֑ר יָעִ֣יר**׀** בַּבֹּ֣קֶר בַּבֹּ֗קֶר יָעִ֥יר לִי֙ אֹ֔זֶן לִשְׁמֹ֖עַ כַּלִּמּוּדִֽים׃


The Lord God has given me the tongue of the learned, that I should know how to sustain him that is weary: he wakens morning by morning, he wakens my ear to hear as the learned.


###### Isaiah 50:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:5)


אֲדֹנָ֤י יֱהֹוִה֙ פָּתַֽח־לִ֣י אֹ֔זֶן וְאָנֹכִ֖י לֹ֣א מָרִ֑יתִי אָח֖וֹר לֹ֥א נְסוּגֹֽתִי׃


The Lord God has opened my ear, and I was not rebellious, nor did I turn away.


###### Isaiah 50:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:6)


גֵּוִי֙ נָתַ֣תִּי לְמַכִּ֔ים וּלְחָיַ֖י לְמֹֽרְטִ֑ים פָּנַי֙ לֹ֣א הִסְתַּ֔רְתִּי מִכְּלִמּ֖וֹת וָרֹֽק׃


I gave my back to the smiters, and my cheeks to them that plucked off the hair: I hid not my face from shame and spitting.


###### Isaiah 50:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:7)


וַאדֹנָ֤י יֱהֹוִה֙ יַֽעֲזׇר־לִ֔י עַל־כֵּ֖ן לֹ֣א נִכְלָ֑מְתִּי עַל־כֵּ֞ן שַׂ֤מְתִּי פָנַי֙ כַּחַלָּמִ֔ישׁ וָאֵדַ֖ע כִּי־לֹ֥א אֵבֽוֹשׁ׃


For the Lord God has helped me; therefore have I not been confounded: therefore have I set my face like a flint, and I knew that I should not be ashamed.


###### Isaiah 50:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:8)


קָרוֹב֙ מַצְדִּיקִ֔י מִֽי־יָרִ֥יב אִתִּ֖י נַ֣עַמְדָה יָּ֑חַד מִי־בַ֥עַל מִשְׁפָּטִ֖י יִגַּ֥שׁ אֵלָֽי׃


He is near that justifies me; who will contend with me? let us stand together: who is my adversary? let him come near to me.


###### Isaiah 50:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:9)


הֵ֣ן אֲדֹנָ֤י יֱהֹוִה֙ יַעֲזׇר־לִ֔י מִי־ה֖וּא יַרְשִׁיעֵ֑נִי הֵ֤ן כֻּלָּם֙ כַּבֶּ֣גֶד יִבְל֔וּ עָ֖שׁ יֹאכְלֵֽם׃{ס}


Behold, the Lord God will help me; who is he that shall condemn me? lo, they all shall grow old as a garment; the moth shall eat them up.


###### Isaiah 50:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:10)


מִ֤י בָכֶם֙ יְרֵ֣א יְהֹוָ֔ה שֹׁמֵ֖עַ בְּק֣וֹל עַבְדּ֑וֹ אֲשֶׁ֣ר**׀** הָלַ֣ךְ חֲשֵׁכִ֗ים וְאֵ֥ין נֹ֙גַהּ֙ ל֔וֹ יִבְטַח֙ בְּשֵׁ֣ם יְהֹוָ֔ה וְיִשָּׁעֵ֖ן בֵּאלֹהָֽיו׃{ס}


Who is there among you that fears the Lord, that obeys the voice of his servant, that walks in darkness, and has no light? let him trust in the name of the Lord, and rely upon his God.


###### Isaiah 50:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/50:11)


הֵ֧ן כֻּלְּכֶ֛ם קֹ֥דְחֵי אֵ֖שׁ מְאַזְּרֵ֣י זִיק֑וֹת לְכ֣וּ**׀** בְּא֣וּר אֶשְׁכֶ֗ם וּבְזִיקוֹת֙ בִּֽעַרְתֶּ֔ם מִיָּדִי֙ הָיְתָה־זֹּ֣את לָכֶ֔ם לְמַעֲצֵבָ֖ה תִּשְׁכָּבֽוּן׃{ס}


Behold all you that kindle a fire, and that girdle yourselves around with sparks; walk in the light of your fire, and in the sparks that you have kindled. This came to you from my hand; you shall lie down in grief.



## Chapter 51

###### Isaiah 51:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:1)


שִׁמְע֥וּ אֵלַ֛י רֹ֥דְפֵי צֶ֖דֶק מְבַקְשֵׁ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה הַבִּ֙יטוּ֙ אֶל־צ֣וּר חֻצַּבְתֶּ֔ם וְאֶל־מַקֶּ֥בֶת בּ֖וֹר נֻקַּרְתֶּֽם׃


Hearken to me, you that follow after righteousness, you that seek the Lord: look to the rock whence you are hewn, and to the hole of the pit from which you were dug out.


###### Isaiah 51:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:2)


הַבִּ֙יטוּ֙ אֶל־אַבְרָהָ֣ם אֲבִיכֶ֔ם וְאֶל־שָׂרָ֖ה תְּחוֹלֶלְכֶ֑ם כִּֽי־אֶחָ֣ד קְרָאתִ֔יו וַאֲבָרְכֵ֖הוּ וְאַרְבֵּֽהוּ׃


Look to Avraham your father, and to Sara that bore you: for he was but one when I called him, and I blessed him, and increased him.


###### Isaiah 51:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:3)


כִּי־נִחַ֨ם יְהֹוָ֜ה צִיּ֗וֹן נִחַם֙ כׇּל־חׇרְבֹתֶ֔יהָ וַיָּ֤שֶׂם מִדְבָּרָהּ֙ כְּעֵ֔דֶן וְעַרְבָתָ֖הּ כְּגַן־יְהֹוָ֑ה שָׂשׂ֤וֹן וְשִׂמְחָה֙ יִמָּ֣צֵא בָ֔הּ תּוֹדָ֖ה וְק֥וֹל זִמְרָֽה׃{ס}


For the Lord shall comfort Żiyyon: he will comfort all her waste places; and he will make her wilderness like ῾Eden, and her desert like the garden of the Lord; joy and gladness shall be found in it, thanksgiving, and the voice of melody.


###### Isaiah 51:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:4)


הַקְשִׁ֤יבוּ אֵלַי֙ עַמִּ֔י וּלְאוּמִּ֖י אֵלַ֣י הַאֲזִ֑ינוּ כִּ֤י תוֹרָה֙ מֵאִתִּ֣י תֵצֵ֔א וּמִ֨שְׁפָּטִ֔י לְא֥וֹר עַמִּ֖ים אַרְגִּֽיעַ׃


Hearken to me, my people; and give ear to me, O my nation: for Tora shall proceed from me, and I will make my judgment suddenly for a light of the peoples.


###### Isaiah 51:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:5)


קָר֤וֹב צִדְקִי֙ יָצָ֣א יִשְׁעִ֔י וּזְרֹעַ֖י עַמִּ֣ים יִשְׁפֹּ֑טוּ אֵלַי֙ אִיִּ֣ים יְקַוּ֔וּ וְאֶל־זְרֹעִ֖י יְיַחֵלֽוּן׃


My righteousness is near; my salvation has gone forth, and my arms shall judge the peoples; isles shall wait upon me, and on my arm shall they trust.


###### Isaiah 51:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:6)


שְׂאוּ֩ לַשָּׁמַ֨יִם עֵינֵיכֶ֜ם וְֽהַבִּ֧יטוּ אֶל־הָאָ֣רֶץ מִתַּ֗חַת כִּֽי־שָׁמַ֜יִם כֶּעָשָׁ֤ן נִמְלָ֙חוּ֙ וְהָאָ֙רֶץ֙ כַּבֶּ֣גֶד תִּבְלֶ֔ה וְיֹשְׁבֶ֖יהָ כְּמוֹ־כֵ֣ן יְמוּת֑וּן וִישֽׁוּעָתִי֙ לְעוֹלָ֣ם תִּֽהְיֶ֔ה וְצִדְקָתִ֖י לֹ֥א תֵחָֽת׃{פ}


Lift up your eyes to the heavens, and look upon the earth beneath; for the heavens shall vanish away like smoke, and the earth shall grow old like a garment, and they that dwell in it shall die in like manner: but my salvation shall be forever, and my righteousness shall not be overthrown.


###### Isaiah 51:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:7)


שִׁמְע֤וּ אֵלַי֙ יֹ֣דְעֵי צֶ֔דֶק עַ֖ם תּוֹרָתִ֣י בְלִבָּ֑ם אַל־תִּֽירְאוּ֙ חֶרְפַּ֣ת אֱנ֔וֹשׁ וּמִגִּדֻּפֹתָ֖ם אַל־תֵּחָֽתּוּ׃


Hearken to me, you that know righteousness, the people in whose heart is my Tora; fear not the taunts of men, neither be dismayed by their revilings.


###### Isaiah 51:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:8)


כִּ֤י כַבֶּ֙גֶד֙ יֹאכְלֵ֣ם עָ֔שׁ וְכַצֶּ֖מֶר יֹאכְלֵ֣ם סָ֑ס וְצִדְקָתִי֙ לְעוֹלָ֣ם תִּֽהְיֶ֔ה וִישׁוּעָתִ֖י לְד֥וֹר דּוֹרִֽים׃{ס}


For the moth shall eat them up like a garment, and the worm shall eat them like wool: but my righteousness shall be for ever, and my salvation for all generations.


###### Isaiah 51:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:9)


עוּרִ֨י עוּרִ֤י לִבְשִׁי־עֹז֙ זְר֣וֹעַ יְהֹוָ֔ה ע֚וּרִי כִּ֣ימֵי קֶ֔דֶם דֹּר֖וֹת עוֹלָמִ֑ים הֲל֥וֹא אַתְּ־הִ֛יא הַמַּחְצֶ֥בֶת רַ֖הַב מְחוֹלֶ֥לֶת תַּנִּֽין׃


Awake, awake, put on strength, O arm of the Lord; awake, as in the ancient days, in the generations of old. Art thou not it that has cut Raĥav in pieces, and wounded the crocodile?


###### Isaiah 51:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:10)


הֲל֤וֹא אַתְּ־הִיא֙ הַמַּחֲרֶ֣בֶת יָ֔ם מֵ֖י תְּה֣וֹם רַבָּ֑ה הַשָּׂ֙מָה֙ מַֽעֲמַקֵּי־יָ֔ם דֶּ֖רֶךְ לַעֲבֹ֥ר גְּאוּלִֽים׃


Art thou not it which dried the sea, the waters of the great deep; that made the depths of the sea a way for the ransomed to pass over?


###### Isaiah 51:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:11)


וּפְדוּיֵ֨י יְהֹוָ֜ה יְשׁוּב֗וּן וּבָ֤אוּ צִיּוֹן֙ בְּרִנָּ֔ה וְשִׂמְחַ֥ת עוֹלָ֖ם עַל־רֹאשָׁ֑ם שָׂשׂ֤וֹן וְשִׂמְחָה֙ יַשִּׂיג֔וּן נָ֖סוּ יָג֥וֹן וַאֲנָחָֽה׃{ס}


Therefore the redeemed of the Lord shall return, and come with singing to Żiyyon; and everlasting joy shall be upon their head: they shall obtain gladness and joy; and sorrow and sighing shall flee away.


###### Isaiah 51:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:12)


אָנֹכִ֧י אָנֹכִ֛י ה֖וּא מְנַחֶמְכֶ֑ם מִי־אַ֤תְּ וַתִּֽירְאִי֙ מֵאֱנ֣וֹשׁ יָמ֔וּת וּמִבֶּן־אָדָ֖ם חָצִ֥יר יִנָּתֵֽן׃


I, even I, am he that comforts you: who art thou, that thou shouldst be afraid of a mortal man, and of the son of man who shall be made as grass;


###### Isaiah 51:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:13)


וַתִּשְׁכַּ֞ח יְהֹוָ֣ה עֹשֶׂ֗ךָ נוֹטֶ֣ה שָׁמַ֘יִם֮ וְיֹסֵ֣ד אָ֒רֶץ֒ וַתְּפַחֵ֨ד תָּמִ֜יד כׇּל־הַיּ֗וֹם מִפְּנֵי֙ חֲמַ֣ת הַמֵּצִ֔יק כַּאֲשֶׁ֥ר כּוֹנֵ֖ן לְהַשְׁחִ֑ית וְאַיֵּ֖ה חֲמַ֥ת הַמֵּצִֽיק׃


and hast forgotten the Lord thy maker, that stretched forth the heavens, and laid the foundations of the earth; and hast feared continually every day because of the fury of the oppressor, as he makes ready to destroy? and where is the fury of the oppressor?


###### Isaiah 51:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:14)


מִהַ֥ר צֹעֶ֖ה לְהִפָּתֵ֑חַ וְלֹא־יָמ֣וּת לַשַּׁ֔חַת וְלֹ֥א יֶחְסַ֖ר לַחְמֽוֹ׃


The crouching one hastens that he may be loosed, and he shall not die in the pit, nor shall his bread fail.


###### Isaiah 51:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:15)


וְאָֽנֹכִי֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ רֹגַ֣ע הַיָּ֔ם וַיֶּהֱמ֖וּ גַּלָּ֑יו יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת שְׁמֽוֹ׃


And I am the Lord thy God, who stirs up the sea, that its waves roared: The Lord of hosts is his name.


###### Isaiah 51:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:16)
