וָאָשִׂ֤ם דְּבָרַי֙ בְּפִ֔יךָ וּבְצֵ֥ל יָדִ֖י כִּסִּיתִ֑יךָ לִנְטֹ֤עַ שָׁמַ֙יִם֙ וְלִיסֹ֣ד אָ֔רֶץ וְלֵאמֹ֥ר לְצִיּ֖וֹן עַמִּי־אָֽתָּה׃{ס}


And I have put my words in thy mouth, and I have covered thee in the shadow of my hand, that I may plant the heavens, and lay the foundations of the earth, and say to Żiyyon, Thou art my people.


###### Isaiah 51:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:17)


הִתְעוֹרְרִ֣י הִֽתְעוֹרְרִ֗י ק֚וּמִי יְר֣וּשָׁלַ֔͏ִם אֲשֶׁ֥ר שָׁתִ֛ית מִיַּ֥ד יְהֹוָ֖ה אֶת־כּ֣וֹס חֲמָת֑וֹ אֶת־קֻבַּ֜עַת כּ֧וֹס הַתַּרְעֵלָ֛ה שָׁתִ֖ית מָצִֽית׃


Awake, awake, stand up, O Yerushalayim, who hast drunk at the hand of the Lord the cup of his fury; thou hast drunk to the dregs, the deep bowl of staggering.


###### Isaiah 51:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:18)


אֵין־מְנַהֵ֣ל לָ֔הּ מִכׇּל־בָּנִ֖ים יָלָ֑דָה וְאֵ֤ין מַחֲזִיק֙ בְּיָדָ֔הּ מִכׇּל־בָּנִ֖ים גִּדֵּֽלָה׃


There is none to guide her among all the sons whom she has brought forth; nor is there any that takes her by the hand among all the sons that she has brought up.


###### Isaiah 51:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:19)


שְׁתַּ֤יִם הֵ֙נָּה֙ קֹרְאֹתַ֔יִךְ מִ֖י יָנ֣וּד לָ֑ךְ הַשֹּׁ֧ד וְהַשֶּׁ֛בֶר וְהָרָעָ֥ב וְהַחֶ֖רֶב מִ֥י אֲנַחֲמֵֽךְ׃


These two things have befallen thee; who shall console thee? desolation, and destruction, and the famine, and the sword: by whom shall I comfort thee?


###### Isaiah 51:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:20)


בָּנַ֜יִךְ עֻלְּפ֥וּ שָׁכְב֛וּ בְּרֹ֥אשׁ כׇּל־חוּצ֖וֹת כְּת֣וֹא מִכְמָ֑ר הַֽמְלֵאִ֥ים חֲמַת־יְהֹוָ֖ה גַּעֲרַ֥ת אֱלֹהָֽיִךְ׃


Thy sons have fainted, they lie at the head of all the streets, like a bison in a net: they are full of the fury of the Lord, the rebuke of thy God.


###### Isaiah 51:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:21)


לָכֵ֛ן שִׁמְעִי־נָ֥א זֹ֖את עֲנִיָּ֑ה וּשְׁכֻרַ֖ת וְלֹ֥א מִיָּֽיִן׃{פ}


Therefore hear now this, thou afflicted, and drunken, but not with wine:


###### Isaiah 51:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:22)


כֹּה־אָמַ֞ר אֲדֹנַ֣יִךְ יְהֹוָ֗ה וֵאלֹהַ֙יִךְ֙ יָרִ֣יב עַמּ֔וֹ הִנֵּ֥ה לָקַ֛חְתִּי מִיָּדֵ֖ךְ אֶת־כּ֣וֹס הַתַּרְעֵלָ֑ה אֶת־קֻבַּ֙עַת֙ כּ֣וֹס חֲמָתִ֔י לֹא־תוֹסִ֥יפִי לִשְׁתּוֹתָ֖הּ עֽוֹד׃


Thus says thy Lord the Lord, and thy God that pleads the cause of his people, Behold, I have taken out of thy hand the cup of staggering, the deep bowl of my fury; thou shalt no more drink it again:


###### Isaiah 51:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/51:23)


וְשַׂמְתִּ֙יהָ֙ בְּיַד־מוֹגַ֔יִךְ אֲשֶׁר־אָמְר֥וּ לְנַפְשֵׁ֖ךְ שְׁחִ֣י וְנַעֲבֹ֑רָה וַתָּשִׂ֤ימִי כָאָ֙רֶץ֙ גֵּוֵ֔ךְ וְכַח֖וּץ לַעֹבְרִֽים׃{פ}


but I will put it into the hand of them that afflict thee; who have said to thy soul, Bow down, that we may go over: and thou hast laid thy body like the ground, and like the street, to them that go over.



## Chapter 52

###### Isaiah 52:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:1)


עוּרִ֥י עוּרִ֛י לִבְשִׁ֥י עֻזֵּ֖ךְ צִיּ֑וֹן לִבְשִׁ֣י**׀** בִּגְדֵ֣י תִפְאַרְתֵּ֗ךְ יְרוּשָׁלַ֙͏ִם֙ עִ֣יר הַקֹּ֔דֶשׁ כִּ֣י לֹ֥א יוֹסִ֛יף יָבֹא־בָ֥ךְ ע֖וֹד עָרֵ֥ל וְטָמֵֽא׃


Awake, awake; put on thy strength, O Żiyyon; put on thy beautiful garments, O Yerushalayim, the holy city: for henceforth there shall no more come into thee the uncircumcised and the unclean.


###### Isaiah 52:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:2)


הִתְנַעֲרִ֧י מֵעָפָ֛ר ק֥וּמִי שְּׁבִ֖י יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם (התפתחו) [הִֽתְפַּתְּחִי֙] מוֹסְרֵ֣י צַוָּארֵ֔ךְ שְׁבִיָּ֖ה בַּת־צִיּֽוֹן׃{ס}


Shake thyself from the dust; arise, and sit down, O Yerushalayim: loose thyself from the bands of thy neck, O captive daughter of Żiyyon.


###### Isaiah 52:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:3)


כִּי־כֹה֙ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה חִנָּ֖ם נִמְכַּרְתֶּ֑ם וְלֹ֥א בְכֶ֖סֶף תִּגָּאֵֽלוּ׃{ס}


For thus says the Lord, You were sold for nought; and you shall be redeemed without money.


###### Isaiah 52:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:4)


כִּ֣י כֹ֤ה אָמַר֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה מִצְרַ֛יִם יָֽרַד־עַמִּ֥י בָרִֽאשֹׁנָ֖ה לָג֣וּר שָׁ֑ם וְאַשּׁ֖וּר בְּאֶ֥פֶס עֲשָׁקֽוֹ׃


For thus says the Lord God, My people went down aforetime to Miżrayim to sojourn there; and Ashshur oppressed them without cause.


###### Isaiah 52:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:5)


וְעַתָּ֤ה מַה־לִּי־פֹה֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה כִּֽי־לֻקַּ֥ח עַמִּ֖י חִנָּ֑ם מֹשְׁלָ֤ו יְהֵילִ֙ילוּ֙ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְתָמִ֥יד כׇּל־הַיּ֖וֹם שְׁמִ֥י מִנֹּאָֽץ׃


Now therefore, what have I here, says the Lord, that my people is taken away for nought? they that rule over them yell, says the Lord; and my name continually every day is blasphemed.


###### Isaiah 52:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:6)


לָכֵ֛ן יֵדַ֥ע עַמִּ֖י שְׁמִ֑י לָכֵן֙ בַּיּ֣וֹם הַה֔וּא כִּֽי־אֲנִי־ה֥וּא הַֽמְדַבֵּ֖ר הִנֵּֽנִי׃{ס}


Therefore my people shall know my name: therefore they shall know in that day that I am he that speaks: Behold, here I am.


###### Isaiah 52:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:7)


מַה־נָּאו֨וּ עַל־הֶהָרִ֜ים רַגְלֵ֣י מְבַשֵּׂ֗ר מַשְׁמִ֧יעַ שָׁל֛וֹם מְבַשֵּׂ֥ר ט֖וֹב מַשְׁמִ֣יעַ יְשׁוּעָ֑ה אֹמֵ֥ר לְצִיּ֖וֹן מָלַ֥ךְ אֱלֹהָֽיִךְ׃


How beautiful upon the mountains are the feet of him that brings good tidings, that announces peace; that brings good tidings of good, that announces salvation; that says to Żiyyon, Thy God reigns!


###### Isaiah 52:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:8)


ק֥וֹל צֹפַ֛יִךְ נָ֥שְׂאוּ ק֖וֹל יַחְדָּ֣ו יְרַנֵּ֑נוּ כִּ֣י עַ֤יִן בְּעַ֙יִן֙ יִרְא֔וּ בְּשׁ֥וּב יְהֹוָ֖ה צִיּֽוֹן׃


The voice of thy watchmen is heard: they lift up the voice; together shall they sing: for they shall see eye to eye, the Lord returning to Żiyyon.


###### Isaiah 52:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:9)


פִּצְח֤וּ רַנְּנוּ֙ יַחְדָּ֔ו חׇרְב֖וֹת יְרוּשָׁלָ֑͏ִם כִּֽי־נִחַ֤ם יְהֹוָה֙ עַמּ֔וֹ גָּאַ֖ל יְרוּשָׁלָֽ͏ִם׃


Break forth into joy, sing together, O waste places of Yerushalayim: for the Lord has comforted his people, he has redeemed Yerushalayim:


###### Isaiah 52:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:10)


חָשַׂ֤ף יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־זְר֣וֹעַ קׇדְשׁ֔וֹ לְעֵינֵ֖י כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֑ם וְרָאוּ֙ כׇּל־אַפְסֵי־אָ֔רֶץ אֵ֖ת יְשׁוּעַ֥ת אֱלֹהֵֽינוּ׃{ס}


the Lord has made bare his holy arm in the eyes of all the nations; and all the ends of the earth shall see the salvation of our God.


###### Isaiah 52:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:11)


ס֤וּרוּ ס֙וּרוּ֙ צְא֣וּ מִשָּׁ֔ם טָמֵ֖א אַל־תִּגָּ֑עוּ צְא֣וּ מִתּוֹכָ֔הּ הִבָּ֕רוּ נֹשְׂאֵ֖י כְּלֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


Depart, depart, go out from there, touch no unclean thing: go out of the midst of her; be clean, you that bear the vessels of the Lord.


###### Isaiah 52:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:12)


כִּ֣י לֹ֤א בְחִפָּזוֹן֙ תֵּצֵ֔אוּ וּבִמְנוּסָ֖ה לֹ֣א תֵלֵכ֑וּן כִּֽי־הֹלֵ֤ךְ לִפְנֵיכֶם֙ יְהֹוָ֔ה וּמְאַסִּפְכֶ֖ם אֱלֹהֵ֥י יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃{ס}


For you shall not go out with haste, nor go by flight: for the Lord will go before you; and the God of Yisra᾽el will be your rearguard.


###### Isaiah 52:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:13)


הִנֵּ֥ה יַשְׂכִּ֖יל עַבְדִּ֑י יָר֧וּם וְנִשָּׂ֛א וְגָבַ֖הּ מְאֹֽד׃


Behold, my servant shall prosper, he shall be exalted and extolled, and be very high.


###### Isaiah 52:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:14)


כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר שָׁמְמ֤וּ עָלֶ֙יךָ֙ רַבִּ֔ים כֵּן־מִשְׁחַ֥ת מֵאִ֖ישׁ מַרְאֵ֑הוּ וְתֹאֲר֖וֹ מִבְּנֵ֥י אָדָֽם׃


Just as many were astonished at thee; saying, Surely his visage is too marred to be that of a man, and his form, to be that of the sons of men:


###### Isaiah 52:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/52:15)


כֵּ֤ן יַזֶּה֙ גּוֹיִ֣ם רַבִּ֔ים עָלָ֛יו יִקְפְּצ֥וּ מְלָכִ֖ים פִּיהֶ֑ם כִּ֠י אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹֽא־סֻפַּ֤ר לָהֶם֙ רָא֔וּ וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־שָׁמְע֖וּ הִתְבּוֹנָֽנוּ׃{ס}


so shall he startle many nations; kings shall shut their mouths: for that which had not been told them shall they see; and that which they had not heard shall they perceive.



## Chapter 53

###### Isaiah 53:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:1)


מִ֥י הֶאֱמִ֖ין לִשְׁמֻעָתֵ֑נוּ וּזְר֥וֹעַ יְהֹוָ֖ה עַל־מִ֥י נִגְלָֽתָה׃


Who would have believed our report? and to whom is the arm of the Lord revealed?


###### Isaiah 53:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:2)


וַיַּ֨עַל כַּיּוֹנֵ֜ק לְפָנָ֗יו וְכַשֹּׁ֙רֶשׁ֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ צִיָּ֔ה לֹא־תֹ֥אַר ל֖וֹ וְלֹ֣א הָדָ֑ר וְנִרְאֵ֥הוּ וְלֹֽא־מַרְאֶ֖ה וְנֶחְמְדֵֽהוּ׃


For he grew up before him as a tender plant, and as a root out of a dry ground: he had no form nor comeliness, that we should look at him, and no countenance, that we should desire him.


###### Isaiah 53:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:3)


נִבְזֶה֙ וַחֲדַ֣ל אִישִׁ֔ים אִ֥ישׁ מַכְאֹב֖וֹת וִיד֣וּעַ חֹ֑לִי וּכְמַסְתֵּ֤ר פָּנִים֙ מִמֶּ֔נּוּ נִבְזֶ֖ה וְלֹ֥א חֲשַׁבְנֻֽהוּ׃


He was despised and rejected of men; a man of pains, and acquainted with sickness: and we hid as it were our faces from him; he was despised, and we esteemed him not.


###### Isaiah 53:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:4)


אָכֵ֤ן חֳלָיֵ֙נוּ֙ ה֣וּא נָשָׂ֔א וּמַכְאֹבֵ֖ינוּ סְבָלָ֑ם וַאֲנַ֣חְנוּ חֲשַׁבְנֻ֔הוּ נָג֛וּעַ מֻכֵּ֥ה אֱלֹהִ֖ים וּמְעֻנֶּֽה׃


But in truth he has borne our sicknesses and endured our pains; yet we did esteem him stricken, smitten of God, and afflicted.


###### Isaiah 53:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:5)


וְהוּא֙ מְחֹלָ֣ל מִפְּשָׁעֵ֔נוּ מְדֻכָּ֖א מֵעֲוֺנֹתֵ֑ינוּ מוּסַ֤ר שְׁלוֹמֵ֙נוּ֙ עָלָ֔יו וּבַחֲבֻרָת֖וֹ נִרְפָּא־לָֽנוּ׃


But he was wounded because of our transgressions, bruised because of our iniquities: his sufferings were that we might have peace, and by his injury we are healed.


###### Isaiah 53:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:6)


כֻּלָּ֙נוּ֙ כַּצֹּ֣אן תָּעִ֔ינוּ אִ֥ישׁ לְדַרְכּ֖וֹ פָּנִ֑ינוּ וַיהֹוָה֙ הִפְגִּ֣יעַ בּ֔וֹ אֵ֖ת עֲוֺ֥ן כֻּלָּֽנוּ׃


All we like sheep have gone astray; we have turned every one to his own way; and the Lord has caused the iniquity of us all to fall upon him.


###### Isaiah 53:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:7)


נִגַּ֨שׂ וְה֣וּא נַעֲנֶה֮ וְלֹ֣א יִפְתַּח־פִּיו֒ כַּשֶּׂה֙ לַטֶּ֣בַח יוּבָ֔ל וּכְרָחֵ֕ל לִפְנֵ֥י גֹזְזֶ֖יהָ נֶאֱלָ֑מָה וְלֹ֥א יִפְתַּ֖ח פִּֽיו׃


He was oppressed, but he humbled himself and opened not his mouth: as a lamb which is brought to the slaughter, and as a sheep before her shearers is dumb, so he did not open his mouth.


###### Isaiah 53:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:8)


מֵעֹ֤צֶר וּמִמִּשְׁפָּט֙ לֻקָּ֔ח וְאֶת־דּוֹר֖וֹ מִ֣י יְשׂוֹחֵ֑חַ כִּ֤י נִגְזַר֙ מֵאֶ֣רֶץ חַיִּ֔ים מִפֶּ֥שַׁע עַמִּ֖י נֶ֥גַע לָֽמוֹ׃


By oppression and false judgement was he taken away; and of his generation who considered? For he was cut off out of the land of the living, for the transgression of the people to whom the stroke was due.


###### Isaiah 53:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:9)


וַיִּתֵּ֤ן אֶת־רְשָׁעִים֙ קִבְר֔וֹ וְאֶת־עָשִׁ֖יר בְּמֹתָ֑יו עַ֚ל לֹא־חָמָ֣ס עָשָׂ֔ה וְלֹ֥א מִרְמָ֖ה בְּפִֽיו׃


For they made his grave among the wicked, and his tomb among the rich; because he had done no violence, neither was any deceit in his mouth.


###### Isaiah 53:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:10)


וַיהֹוָ֞ה חָפֵ֤ץ דַּכְּאוֹ֙ הֶחֱלִ֔י אִם־תָּשִׂ֤ים אָשָׁם֙ נַפְשׁ֔וֹ יִרְאֶ֥ה זֶ֖רַע יַאֲרִ֣יךְ יָמִ֑ים וְחֵ֥פֶץ יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּיָד֥וֹ יִצְלָֽח׃


But it pleased the Lord to crush him by disease: if his soul shall consider it a recompense for guilt, he shall see his seed, he shall prolong his days, and the purpose of the Lord shall prosper in his hand.


###### Isaiah 53:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:11)


מֵעֲמַ֤ל נַפְשׁוֹ֙ יִרְאֶ֣ה יִשְׂבָּ֔ע בְּדַעְתּ֗וֹ יַצְדִּ֥יק צַדִּ֛יק עַבְדִּ֖י לָרַבִּ֑ים וַעֲוֺנֹתָ֖ם ה֥וּא יִסְבֹּֽל׃


He shall see the travail of his soul, he shall be sated with seeing: by his knowledge did my servant justify the righteous One to the many, and did bear their iniquities.


###### Isaiah 53:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/53:12)


לָכֵ֞ן אֲחַלֶּק־ל֣וֹ בָרַבִּ֗ים וְאֶת־עֲצוּמִים֮ יְחַלֵּ֣ק שָׁלָל֒ תַּ֗חַת אֲשֶׁ֨ר הֶעֱרָ֤ה לַמָּ֙וֶת֙ נַפְשׁ֔וֹ וְאֶת־פֹּשְׁעִ֖ים נִמְנָ֑ה וְהוּא֙ חֵטְא־רַבִּ֣ים נָשָׂ֔א וְלַפֹּשְׁעִ֖ים יַפְגִּֽיעַ׃{פ}


Surely I will give him a portion with the great, and he shall divide the spoil with the strong; because he has poured out his soul to death, and was numbered with transgressors; but he bore the sin of many, and made intercession for the transgressors.



## Chapter 54

###### Isaiah 54:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:1)


רׇנִּ֥י עֲקָרָ֖ה לֹ֣א יָלָ֑דָה פִּצְחִ֨י רִנָּ֤ה וְצַֽהֲלִי֙ לֹא־חָ֔לָה כִּֽי־רַבִּ֧ים בְּֽנֵי־שׁוֹמֵמָ֛ה מִבְּנֵ֥י בְעוּלָ֖ה אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


Sing, O barren one, thou that didst not bear; break forth into singing, and cry aloud, thou that didst not travail with child: for more are the children of the desolate than the children of the married wife, says the Lord.


###### Isaiah 54:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:2)


הַרְחִ֣יבִי**׀** מְק֣וֹם אׇהֳלֵ֗ךְ וִירִיע֧וֹת מִשְׁכְּנוֹתַ֛יִךְ יַטּ֖וּ אַל־תַּחְשֹׂ֑כִי הַאֲרִ֙יכִי֙ מֵיתָרַ֔יִךְ וִיתֵדֹתַ֖יִךְ חַזֵּֽקִי׃


Enlarge the place of thy tent, and let them stretch forth the curtains of thy habitations: spare not: lengthen thy cords, and strengthen thy stakes;


###### Isaiah 54:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:3)


כִּֽי־יָמִ֥ין וּשְׂמֹ֖אול תִּפְרֹ֑צִי וְזַרְעֵךְ֙ גּוֹיִ֣ם יִירָ֔שׁ וְעָרִ֥ים נְשַׁמּ֖וֹת יוֹשִֽׁיבוּ׃


for thou shalt break forth on the right hand and on the left; and thy seed shall possess nations, and make desolate cities to be inhabited.


###### Isaiah 54:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:4)


אַל־תִּֽירְאִי֙ כִּֽי־לֹ֣א תֵב֔וֹשִׁי וְאַל־תִּכָּלְמִ֖י כִּ֣י לֹ֣א תַחְפִּ֑ירִי כִּ֣י בֹ֤שֶׁת עֲלוּמַ֙יִךְ֙ תִּשְׁכָּ֔חִי וְחֶרְפַּ֥ת אַלְמְנוּתַ֖יִךְ לֹ֥א תִזְכְּרִי־עֽוֹד׃


Fear not; for thou shall not be ashamed: neither be confounded; for thou shalt not be put to shame: but thou shalt forget the shame of thy youth, and shalt not remember the reproach of thy widowhood any more.


###### Isaiah 54:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:5)


כִּ֤י בֹעֲלַ֙יִךְ֙ עֹשַׂ֔יִךְ יְהֹוָ֥ה צְבָא֖וֹת שְׁמ֑וֹ וְגֹֽאֲלֵךְ֙ קְד֣וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֱלֹהֵ֥י כׇל־הָאָ֖רֶץ יִקָּרֵֽא׃


For thy Maker is thy husband: the Lord of hosts is his name; and thy redeemer is the Holy One of Yisra᾽el; The God of the whole earth is he called.


###### Isaiah 54:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:6)


כִּֽי־כְאִשָּׁ֧ה עֲזוּבָ֛ה וַעֲצ֥וּבַת ר֖וּחַ קְרָאָ֣ךְ יְהֹוָ֑ה וְאֵ֧שֶׁת נְעוּרִ֛ים כִּ֥י תִמָּאֵ֖ס אָמַ֥ר אֱלֹהָֽיִךְ׃


For the Lord has called thee as a woman forsaken and grieved in spirit; but a wife of youth, can she be cast off? says thy God.


###### Isaiah 54:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:7)


בְּרֶ֥גַע קָטֹ֖ן עֲזַבְתִּ֑יךְ וּבְרַחֲמִ֥ים גְּדֹלִ֖ים אֲקַבְּצֵֽךְ׃


For a small moment have I forsaken thee; but with great mercies will I gather thee.


###### Isaiah 54:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:8)


בְּשֶׁ֣צֶף קֶ֗צֶף הִסְתַּ֨רְתִּי פָנַ֥י רֶ֙גַע֙ מִמֵּ֔ךְ וּבְחֶ֥סֶד עוֹלָ֖ם רִחַמְתִּ֑יךְ אָמַ֥ר גֹּאֲלֵ֖ךְ יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


In the overflowing of wrath I hid my face from thee for a moment; but with everlasting faithful love will I have mercy on thee, says thy redeemer, the Lord.


###### Isaiah 54:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:9)


כִּֽי־מֵ֥י נֹ֙חַ֙ זֹ֣את לִ֔י אֲשֶׁ֣ר נִשְׁבַּ֗עְתִּי מֵעֲבֹ֥ר מֵי־נֹ֛חַ ע֖וֹד עַל־הָאָ֑רֶץ כֵּ֥ן נִשְׁבַּ֛עְתִּי מִקְּצֹ֥ף עָלַ֖יִךְ וּמִגְּעׇר־בָּֽךְ׃


For this is as the waters of Noaĥ to me: as I have sworn that the waters of Noaĥ should no more go over the earth; so have I sworn that I would not be furious with thee, nor rebuke thee.


###### Isaiah 54:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:10)


כִּ֤י הֶהָרִים֙ יָמ֔וּשׁוּ וְהַגְּבָע֖וֹת תְּמוּטֶ֑ינָה וְחַסְדִּ֞י מֵאִתֵּ֣ךְ לֹא־יָמ֗וּשׁ וּבְרִ֤ית שְׁלוֹמִי֙ לֹ֣א תָמ֔וּט אָמַ֥ר מְרַחֲמֵ֖ךְ יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


For the mountains shall depart, and the hills be removed; but my faithful love shall not depart from thee, neither shall the covenant of my peace be removed, says the Lord that has mercy on thee.


###### Isaiah 54:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:11)


עֲנִיָּ֥ה סֹעֲרָ֖ה לֹ֣א נֻחָ֑מָה הִנֵּ֨ה אָנֹכִ֜י מַרְבִּ֤יץ בַּפּוּךְ֙ אֲבָנַ֔יִךְ וִֽיסַדְתִּ֖יךְ בַּסַּפִּירִֽים׃


O thou afflicted, tossed with tempest, and not comforted, behold, I will lay thy stones with fair colours, and lay thy foundations with sapphires.


###### Isaiah 54:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:12)


וְשַׂמְתִּ֤י כַּֽדְכֹד֙ שִׁמְשֹׁתַ֔יִךְ וּשְׁעָרַ֖יִךְ לְאַבְנֵ֣י אֶקְדָּ֑ח וְכׇל־גְּבוּלֵ֖ךְ לְאַבְנֵי־חֵֽפֶץ׃


And I will make thy windows of rubies, and thy gates of beryl, and all thy borders of choicest stones.


###### Isaiah 54:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:13)


וְכׇל־בָּנַ֖יִךְ לִמּוּדֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְרַ֖ב שְׁל֥וֹם בָּנָֽיִךְ׃


And all thy children shall be taught of the Lord; and great shall be the peace of thy children.


###### Isaiah 54:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:14)


בִּצְדָקָ֖ה תִּכּוֹנָ֑נִי רַחֲקִ֤י מֵעֹ֙שֶׁק֙ כִּי־לֹ֣א תִירָ֔אִי וּמִ֨מְּחִתָּ֔ה כִּ֥י לֹֽא־תִקְרַ֖ב אֵלָֽיִךְ׃


In righteousness shalt thou be established: keep away from oppression; then thou shalt not fear: and from terror; then it shall not come near thee.


###### Isaiah 54:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:15)


הֵ֣ן גּ֥וֹר יָג֛וּר אֶ֖פֶס מֵאוֹתִ֑י מִי־גָ֥ר אִתָּ֖ךְ עָלַ֥יִךְ יִפּֽוֹל׃


Behold, they may well gather together, but not by me: whoever shall gather together against thee shall fall for thy sake.


###### Isaiah 54:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:16)


(הן) [הִנֵּ֤ה] אָנֹכִי֙ בָּרָ֣אתִי חָרָ֔שׁ נֹפֵ֙חַ֙ בְּאֵ֣שׁ פֶּחָ֔ם וּמוֹצִ֥יא כְלִ֖י לְמַעֲשֵׂ֑הוּ וְאָנֹכִ֛י בָּרָ֥אתִי מַשְׁחִ֖ית לְחַבֵּֽל׃


Behold, I have created the smith that blows upon the fire of the coals, and that brings forth an instrument for his work; and I have created the waster to destroy.


###### Isaiah 54:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/54:17)


כׇּל־כְּלִ֞י יוּצַ֤ר עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ לֹ֣א יִצְלָ֔ח וְכׇל־לָשׁ֛וֹן תָּקוּם־אִתָּ֥ךְ לַמִּשְׁפָּ֖ט תַּרְשִׁ֑יעִי זֹ֡את נַחֲלַת֩ עַבְדֵ֨י יְהֹוָ֧ה וְצִדְקָתָ֛ם מֵאִתִּ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


No weapon that is formed against thee shall prosper; and every tongue that shall rise against thee in judgment thou shalt condemn. This is the heritage of the servants of the Lord and the recompense of their righteousness appointed by me, says the Lord.



## Chapter 55

###### Isaiah 55:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:1)


ה֤וֹי כׇּל־צָמֵא֙ לְכ֣וּ לַמַּ֔יִם וַאֲשֶׁ֥ר אֵֽין־ל֖וֹ כָּ֑סֶף לְכ֤וּ שִׁבְרוּ֙ וֶאֱכֹ֔לוּ וּלְכ֣וּ שִׁבְר֗וּ בְּלוֹא־כֶ֛סֶף וּבְל֥וֹא מְחִ֖יר יַ֥יִן וְחָלָֽב׃


Ho, every one that thirsts, come to the water, and he that has no money; come, buy, and eat; come, buy wine and milk without money and without price.


###### Isaiah 55:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:2)


לָ֤מָּה תִשְׁקְלוּ־כֶ֙סֶף֙ בְּלוֹא־לֶ֔חֶם וִיגִיעֲכֶ֖ם בְּל֣וֹא לְשׇׂבְעָ֑ה שִׁמְע֨וּ שָׁמ֤וֹעַ אֵלַי֙ וְאִכְלוּ־ט֔וֹב וְתִתְעַנַּ֥ג בַּדֶּ֖שֶׁן נַפְשְׁכֶֽם׃


Why do you spend money for that which is not bread? and your labour for that which satisfies not? hearken diligently to me, and eat that which is good, and let your soul delight itself in fatness.


###### Isaiah 55:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:3)


הַטּ֤וּ אׇזְנְכֶם֙ וּלְכ֣וּ אֵלַ֔י שִׁמְע֖וּ וּתְחִ֣י נַפְשְׁכֶ֑ם וְאֶכְרְתָ֤ה לָכֶם֙ בְּרִ֣ית עוֹלָ֔ם חַֽסְדֵ֥י דָוִ֖ד הַנֶּאֱמָנִֽים׃


Incline your ear, and come to me: hear, and your soul shall live; and I will make an everlasting covenant with you, even the sure loving promises of David.


###### Isaiah 55:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:4)


הֵ֛ן עֵ֥ד לְאוּמִּ֖ים נְתַתִּ֑יו נָגִ֥יד וּמְצַוֵּ֖ה לְאֻמִּֽים׃


Behold, I have made him a witness to the peoples, a leader and commander of nations.


###### Isaiah 55:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:5)


הֵ֣ן גּ֤וֹי לֹֽא־תֵדַע֙ תִּקְרָ֔א וְג֥וֹי לֹֽא־יְדָע֖וּךָ אֵלֶ֣יךָ יָר֑וּצוּ לְמַ֙עַן֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהֶ֔יךָ וְלִקְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל כִּ֥י פֵאֲרָֽךְ׃{ס}


Behold, thou shalt call a nation that thou knowst not, and nations that knew not thee shall run to thee, because of the Lord thy God, and for the Holy One of Yisra᾽el; for he has glorified thee.


###### Isaiah 55:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:6)


דִּרְשׁ֥וּ יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּהִמָּֽצְא֑וֹ קְרָאֻ֖הוּ בִּֽהְיוֹת֥וֹ קָרֽוֹב׃


Seek the Lord while he may be found, call upon him while he is near:


###### Isaiah 55:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:7)


יַעֲזֹ֤ב רָשָׁע֙ דַּרְכּ֔וֹ וְאִ֥ישׁ אָ֖וֶן מַחְשְׁבֹתָ֑יו וְיָשֹׁ֤ב אֶל־יְהֹוָה֙ וִֽירַחֲמֵ֔הוּ וְאֶל־אֱלֹהֵ֖ינוּ כִּֽי־יַרְבֶּ֥ה לִסְלֽוֹחַ׃


let the wicked forsake his way, and the unrighteous man his thoughts: and let him return to the Lord, and he will have mercy upon him; and to our God, for he will abundantly pardon.


###### Isaiah 55:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:8)


כִּ֣י לֹ֤א מַחְשְׁבוֹתַי֙ מַחְשְׁב֣וֹתֵיכֶ֔ם וְלֹ֥א דַרְכֵיכֶ֖ם דְּרָכָ֑י נְאֻ֖ם יְהֹוָֽה׃


For my thoughts are not your thoughts, neither are your ways my ways, says the Lord.


###### Isaiah 55:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:9)


כִּֽי־גָבְה֥וּ שָׁמַ֖יִם מֵאָ֑רֶץ כֵּ֣ן גָּבְה֤וּ דְרָכַי֙ מִדַּרְכֵיכֶ֔ם וּמַחְשְׁבֹתַ֖י מִמַּחְשְׁבֹֽתֵיכֶֽם׃


For as the heavens are higher than the earth, so are my ways higher than your ways, and my thoughts than your thoughts.


###### Isaiah 55:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:10)


כִּ֡י כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר יֵרֵד֩ הַגֶּ֨שֶׁם וְהַשֶּׁ֜לֶג מִן־הַשָּׁמַ֗יִם וְשָׁ֙מָּה֙ לֹ֣א יָשׁ֔וּב כִּ֚י אִם־הִרְוָ֣ה אֶת־הָאָ֔רֶץ וְהוֹלִידָ֖הּ וְהִצְמִיחָ֑הּ וְנָ֤תַן זֶ֙רַע֙ לַזֹּרֵ֔עַ וְלֶ֖חֶם לָאֹכֵֽל׃


For as the rain comes down, and the snow from heaven, and returns not there, but waters the earth, and makes it bring forth and bud, that it may give seed to the sower, and bread to the eater:


###### Isaiah 55:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:11)


כֵּ֣ן יִהְיֶ֤ה דְבָרִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר יֵצֵ֣א מִפִּ֔י לֹא־יָשׁ֥וּב אֵלַ֖י רֵיקָ֑ם כִּ֤י אִם־עָשָׂה֙ אֶת־אֲשֶׁ֣ר חָפַ֔צְתִּי וְהִצְלִ֖יחַ אֲשֶׁ֥ר שְׁלַחְתִּֽיו׃


so shall my word be that goes out of my mouth: it shall not return to me void, but it shall accomplish that which I please, and it shall prosper in that for which I sent it.


###### Isaiah 55:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:12)


כִּֽי־בְשִׂמְחָ֣ה תֵצֵ֔אוּ וּבְשָׁל֖וֹם תּוּבָל֑וּן הֶהָרִ֣ים וְהַגְּבָע֗וֹת יִפְצְח֤וּ לִפְנֵיכֶם֙ רִנָּ֔ה וְכׇל־עֲצֵ֥י הַשָּׂדֶ֖ה יִמְחֲאוּ־כָֽף׃


For you shall go out with joy, and be led forth with peace: the mountains and the hills shall break forth before you into singing, and all the trees of the field shall clap their hands.


###### Isaiah 55:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/55:13)


תַּ֤חַת הַֽנַּעֲצוּץ֙ יַעֲלֶ֣ה בְר֔וֹשׁ (תחת) [וְתַ֥חַת] הַסִּרְפַּ֖ד יַעֲלֶ֣ה הֲדַ֑ס וְהָיָ֤ה לַֽיהֹוָה֙ לְשֵׁ֔ם לְא֥וֹת עוֹלָ֖ם לֹ֥א יִכָּרֵֽת׃{פ}


Instead of the thorn shall the cypress come up, and instead of the nettle shall the myrtle tree come up: and it shall be to the Lord for a name, for an everlasting sign that shall not be cut off.



## Chapter 56

###### Isaiah 56:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:1)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה שִׁמְר֥וּ מִשְׁפָּ֖ט וַעֲשׂ֣וּ צְדָקָ֑ה כִּֽי־קְרוֹבָ֤ה יְשׁוּעָתִי֙ לָב֔וֹא וְצִדְקָתִ֖י לְהִגָּלֽוֹת׃


Thus says the Lord, Keep judgment, and do justice: for my salvation is near to come, and my righteousness to be revealed.


###### Isaiah 56:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:2)


אַשְׁרֵ֤י אֱנוֹשׁ֙ יַֽעֲשֶׂה־זֹּ֔את וּבֶן־אָדָ֖ם יַחֲזִ֣יק בָּ֑הּ שֹׁמֵ֤ר שַׁבָּת֙ מֵֽחַלְּל֔וֹ וְשֹׁמֵ֥ר יָד֖וֹ מֵעֲשׂ֥וֹת כׇּל־רָֽע׃{ס}


Happy is the man that does this, and the son of man that lays hold on it; that keeps the Sabbath and does not profane it, and keeps his hand from doing any evil.


###### Isaiah 56:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:3)


וְאַל־יֹאמַ֣ר בֶּן־הַנֵּכָ֗ר הַנִּלְוָ֤ה אֶל־יְהֹוָה֙ לֵאמֹ֔ר הַבְדֵּ֧ל יַבְדִּילַ֛נִי יְהֹוָ֖ה מֵעַ֣ל עַמּ֑וֹ וְאַל־יֹאמַר֙ הַסָּרִ֔יס הֵ֥ן אֲנִ֖י עֵ֥ץ יָבֵֽשׁ׃{פ}


Neither let the son of the stranger, that has joined himself to the Lord, speak, saying, The Lord shall surely separate me from his people: neither let the eunuch say, Behold, I am a dry tree.


###### Isaiah 56:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:4)


כִּי־כֹ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה לַסָּֽרִיסִים֙ אֲשֶׁ֤ר יִשְׁמְרוּ֙ אֶת־שַׁבְּתוֹתַ֔י וּבָחֲר֖וּ בַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר חָפָ֑צְתִּי וּמַחֲזִיקִ֖ים בִּבְרִיתִֽי׃


For thus says the Lord to the eunuchs that keep my sabbaths, and choose the things that please me, and take hold of my covenant;


###### Isaiah 56:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:5)


וְנָתַתִּ֨י לָהֶ֜ם בְּבֵיתִ֤י וּבְחֽוֹמֹתַי֙ יָ֣ד וָשֵׁ֔ם ט֖וֹב מִבָּנִ֣ים וּמִבָּנ֑וֹת שֵׁ֤ם עוֹלָם֙ אֶתֶּן־ל֔וֹ אֲשֶׁ֖ר לֹ֥א יִכָּרֵֽת׃{ס}


And to them will I give in my house and within my walls a memorial better than sons and daughters: I will give them an everlasting name, that shall not be cut off.


###### Isaiah 56:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:6)


וּבְנֵ֣י הַנֵּכָ֗ר הַנִּלְוִ֤ים עַל־יְהֹוָה֙ לְשָׁ֣רְת֔וֹ וּֽלְאַהֲבָה֙ אֶת־שֵׁ֣ם יְהֹוָ֔ה לִֽהְי֥וֹת ל֖וֹ לַעֲבָדִ֑ים כׇּל־שֹׁמֵ֤ר שַׁבָּת֙ מֵֽחַלְּל֔וֹ וּמַחֲזִיקִ֖ים בִּבְרִיתִֽי׃


Also the sons of the stranger, that join themselves to the Lord, to serve him, and to love the name of the Lord, to be his servants, every one that keeps the sabbath and does not profane it, and all that hold fast to my covenant.


###### Isaiah 56:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:7)


וַהֲבִיאוֹתִ֞ים אֶל־הַ֣ר קׇדְשִׁ֗י וְשִׂמַּחְתִּים֙ בְּבֵ֣ית תְּפִלָּתִ֔י עוֹלֹתֵיהֶ֧ם וְזִבְחֵיהֶ֛ם לְרָצ֖וֹן עַֽל־מִזְבְּחִ֑י כִּ֣י בֵיתִ֔י בֵּית־תְּפִלָּ֥ה יִקָּרֵ֖א לְכׇל־הָעַמִּֽים׃


Even them will I bring to my holy mountain, and make them joyful in my house of prayer: their burnt offerings and their sacrifices shall be accepted on my altar; for my house shall be called a house of prayer for all peoples.


###### Isaiah 56:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:8)


נְאֻם֙ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֔ה מְקַבֵּ֖ץ נִדְחֵ֣י יִשְׂרָאֵ֑ל ע֛וֹד אֲקַבֵּ֥ץ עָלָ֖יו לְנִקְבָּצָֽיו׃


The Lord God who gathers the outcasts of Yisra᾽el says, Yet will I gather others to him, beside those of him that are already gathered.


###### Isaiah 56:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:9)


כֹּ֖ל חַיְת֣וֹ שָׂדָ֑י אֵתָ֕יוּ לֶאֱכֹ֥ל כׇּל־חַיְת֖וֹ בַּיָּֽעַר׃{פ}


All you beasts of the field, come into the forest, to devour all the beasts thereof.


###### Isaiah 56:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:10)


צֹפָ֞ו עִוְרִ֤ים כֻּלָּם֙ לֹ֣א יָדָ֔עוּ כֻּלָּם֙ כְּלָבִ֣ים אִלְּמִ֔ים לֹ֥א יוּכְל֖וּ לִנְבֹּ֑חַ הֹזִים֙ שֹֽׁכְבִ֔ים אֹהֲבֵ֖י לָנֽוּם׃


His watchmen are all blind: they are ignorant, they are all dumb dogs, they cannot bark; dreaming, lying down, loving to slumber.


###### Isaiah 56:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:11)


וְהַכְּלָבִ֣ים עַזֵּי־נֶ֗פֶשׁ לֹ֤א יָֽדְעוּ֙ שׇׂבְעָ֔ה וְהֵ֣מָּה רֹעִ֔ים לֹ֥א יָדְע֖וּ הָבִ֑ין כֻּלָּם֙ לְדַרְכָּ֣ם פָּנ֔וּ אִ֥ישׁ לְבִצְע֖וֹ מִקָּצֵֽהוּ׃


Yea, the dogs are greedy, they never have enough, and they are shepherds that cannot understand: they all look to their own way, every one for his gain, from his quarter.


###### Isaiah 56:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/56:12)


אֵתָ֥יוּ אֶקְחָה־יַ֖יִן וְנִסְבְּאָ֣ה שֵׁכָ֑ר וְהָיָ֤ה כָזֶה֙ י֣וֹם מָחָ֔ר גָּד֖וֹל יֶ֥תֶר מְאֹֽד׃


Come, say they, I will fetch wine, and we will fill ourselves with strong drink; and to morrow shall be as this day, and much more abundant.



## Chapter 57

###### Isaiah 57:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:1)


הַצַּדִּ֣יק אָבָ֔ד וְאֵ֥ין אִ֖ישׁ שָׂ֣ם עַל־לֵ֑ב וְאַנְשֵׁי־חֶ֤סֶד נֶאֱסָפִים֙ בְּאֵ֣ין מֵבִ֔ין כִּֽי־מִפְּנֵ֥י הָרָעָ֖ה נֶאֱסַ֥ף הַצַּדִּֽיק׃


The righteous perishes, and no man lays it to heart: and merciful men are taken away, none considering that the righteous is taken away from the evil to come.


###### Isaiah 57:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:2)


יָב֣וֹא שָׁל֔וֹם יָנ֖וּחוּ עַל־מִשְׁכְּבוֹתָ֑ם הֹלֵ֖ךְ נְכֹחֽוֹ׃{ס}


He that walks in his uprightness, shall enter in peace to them that rest in their graves.


###### Isaiah 57:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:3)


וְאַתֶּ֥ם קִרְבוּ־הֵ֖נָּה בְּנֵ֣י עֹנְנָ֑ה זֶ֥רַע מְנָאֵ֖ף וַתִּזְנֶֽה׃


But draw near, O sons of the sorceress, the seed of the adulterer and the harlot.


###### Isaiah 57:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:4)


עַל־מִי֙ תִּתְעַנָּ֔גוּ עַל־מִ֛י תַּרְחִ֥יבוּ פֶ֖ה תַּאֲרִ֣יכוּ לָשׁ֑וֹן הֲלוֹא־אַתֶּ֥ם יִלְדֵי־פֶ֖שַׁע זֶ֥רַע שָֽׁקֶר׃


Against whom do you make sport? against whom do you open wide your mouth, and draw out the tongue? are you not children of transgression, a seed of falsehood,


###### Isaiah 57:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:5)


הַנֵּֽחָמִים֙ בָּאֵלִ֔ים תַּ֖חַת כׇּל־עֵ֣ץ רַעֲנָ֑ן שֹׁחֲטֵ֤י הַיְלָדִים֙ בַּנְּחָלִ֔ים תַּ֖חַת סְעִפֵ֥י הַסְּלָעִֽים׃


inflaming yourselves among the terebinths, under every green tree, slaying the children in the valleys under the clefts of the rocks?


###### Isaiah 57:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:6)


בְּחַלְּקֵי־נַ֣חַל חֶלְקֵ֔ךְ הֵ֥ם הֵ֖ם גּוֹרָלֵ֑ךְ גַּם־לָהֶ֞ם שָׁפַ֥כְתְּ נֶ֙סֶךְ֙ הֶעֱלִ֣ית מִנְחָ֔ה הַ֥עַל אֵ֖לֶּה אֶנָּחֵֽם׃


Among the smooth stones of the wadi is thy portion; they, they are thy lot: even to them hast thou poured a drink offering, thou hast offered a meal offering. Shall I be appeased for these things?


###### Isaiah 57:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:7)


עַ֤ל הַר־גָּבֹ֙הַּ֙ וְנִשָּׂ֔א שַׂ֖מְתְּ מִשְׁכָּבֵ֑ךְ גַּם־שָׁ֥ם עָלִ֖ית לִזְבֹּ֥חַ זָֽבַח׃


Upon a lofty and high mountain hast thou set thy bed: also thou wentest up to offer sacrifice.


###### Isaiah 57:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:8)


וְאַחַ֤ר הַדֶּ֙לֶת֙ וְהַמְּזוּזָ֔ה שַׂ֖מְתְּ זִכְרוֹנֵ֑ךְ כִּ֣י מֵאִתִּ֞י גִּלִּ֣ית וַֽתַּעֲלִ֗י הִרְחַ֤בְתְּ מִשְׁכָּבֵךְ֙ וַתִּכְרׇת־לָ֣ךְ מֵהֶ֔ם אָהַ֥בְתְּ מִשְׁכָּבָ֖ם יָ֥ד חָזִֽית׃


Behind the doors also and the posts hast thou set up thy (unclean) symbol: for thou hast uncovered thyself to others than me, and art gone up; thou hast enlarged thy bed, and made a covenant with some of them; thou didst love their lying with thee, thou didst select a place.


###### Isaiah 57:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:9)


וַתָּשֻׁ֤רִי לַמֶּ֙לֶךְ֙ בַּשֶּׁ֔מֶן וַתַּרְבִּ֖י רִקֻּחָ֑יִךְ וַתְּשַׁלְּחִ֤י צִרַ֙יִךְ֙ עַד־מֵ֣רָחֹ֔ק וַתַּשְׁפִּ֖ילִי עַד־שְׁאֽוֹל׃


And thou wentest to the king with ointment, and didst increase thy perfumes, and didst send thy messengers far off, and sent down even to She᾽ol.


###### Isaiah 57:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:10)


בְּרֹ֤ב דַּרְכֵּךְ֙ יָגַ֔עַתְּ לֹ֥א אָמַ֖רְתְּ נוֹאָ֑שׁ חַיַּ֤ת יָדֵךְ֙ מָצָ֔את עַל־כֵּ֖ן לֹ֥א חָלִֽית׃


Thou art wearied with the length of the way; yet thou didst not say, It is hopeless: thou hast found a renewal of thy strength; therefore thou wast not sick.


###### Isaiah 57:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:11)


וְאֶת־מִ֞י דָּאַ֤גְתְּ וַתִּֽירְאִי֙ כִּ֣י תְכַזֵּ֔בִי וְאוֹתִי֙ לֹ֣א זָכַ֔רְתְּ לֹא־שַׂ֖מְתְּ עַל־לִבֵּ֑ךְ הֲלֹ֨א אֲנִ֤י מַחְשֶׁה֙ וּמֵ֣עֹלָ֔ם וְאוֹתִ֖י לֹ֥א תִירָֽאִי׃


And of whom hast thou been afraid or feared, that thou wast false, and hast not remembered me, nor laid it to thy heart? is it not that I held my peace even of old? therefore thou fearest me not?


###### Isaiah 57:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:12)


אֲנִ֥י אַגִּ֖יד צִדְקָתֵ֑ךְ וְאֶֽת־מַעֲשַׂ֖יִךְ וְלֹ֥א יוֹעִילֽוּךְ׃


I will declare thy righteousness; and as for thy works, they shall not profit thee.


###### Isaiah 57:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:13)


בְּזַֽעֲקֵךְ֙ יַצִּילֻ֣ךְ קִבּוּצַ֔יִךְ וְאֶת־כֻּלָּ֥ם יִשָּׂא־ר֖וּחַ יִקַּח־הָ֑בֶל וְהַחוֹסֶ֥ה בִי֙ יִנְחַל־אֶ֔רֶץ וְיִירַ֖שׁ הַר־קׇדְשִֽׁי׃


When thou criest, let thy collection of idols deliver thee; but the wind shall carry them all away; a breath shall bear them off: but he that puts his trust in me shall possess the land, and shall inherit my holy mountain.


###### Isaiah 57:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:14)


וְאָמַ֥ר סֹֽלּוּ־סֹ֖לּוּ פַּנּוּ־דָ֑רֶךְ הָרִ֥ימוּ מִכְשׁ֖וֹל מִדֶּ֥רֶךְ עַמִּֽי׃{ס}


And one shall say, Bank up, build up, prepare the way, take up the stumblingblock out of the way of my people.


###### Isaiah 57:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:15)


כִּי֩ כֹ֨ה אָמַ֜ר רָ֣ם וְנִשָּׂ֗א שֹׁכֵ֥ן עַד֙ וְקָד֣וֹשׁ שְׁמ֔וֹ מָר֥וֹם וְקָד֖וֹשׁ אֶשְׁכּ֑וֹן וְאֶת־דַּכָּא֙ וּשְׁפַל־ר֔וּחַ לְהַחֲיוֹת֙ ר֣וּחַ שְׁפָלִ֔ים וּֽלְהַחֲי֖וֹת לֵ֥ב נִדְכָּאִֽים׃


For thus says the high and lofty One that inhabits eternity, whose name is Holy; I dwell on high and in a holy place, yet with him also that is of a contrite and humble spirit, to revive the spirit of the humble, and to revive the heart of the contrite ones.


###### Isaiah 57:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:16)


כִּ֣י לֹ֤א לְעוֹלָם֙ אָרִ֔יב וְלֹ֥א לָנֶ֖צַח אֶקְצ֑וֹף כִּי־ר֙וּחַ֙ מִלְּפָנַ֣י יַעֲט֔וֹף וּנְשָׁמ֖וֹת אֲנִ֥י עָשִֽׂיתִי׃


For I will not contend for ever, neither will I be always angry: but the spirit and the soul which I have made, should faint before me.


###### Isaiah 57:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:17)


בַּעֲוֺ֥ן בִּצְע֛וֹ קָצַ֥פְתִּי וְאַכֵּ֖הוּ הַסְתֵּ֣ר וְאֶקְצֹ֑ף וַיֵּ֥לֶךְ שׁוֹבָ֖ב בְּדֶ֥רֶךְ לִבּֽוֹ׃


For the iniquity of his covetousness was I wrathful, and smote him; I hid me, and was angry: but he went on perversely in the way of his heart.


###### Isaiah 57:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:18)


דְּרָכָ֥יו רָאִ֖יתִי וְאֶרְפָּאֵ֑הוּ וְאַנְחֵ֕הוּ וַאֲשַׁלֵּ֧ם נִחֻמִ֛ים ל֖וֹ וְלַאֲבֵלָֽיו׃


I have seen his ways, and will heal him: I will lead him also, and bestow comforts on him and on his mourners.


###### Isaiah 57:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:19)


בּוֹרֵ֖א (נוב) [נִ֣יב] שְׂפָתָ֑יִם שָׁל֨וֹם׀שָׁל֜וֹם לָרָח֧וֹק וְלַקָּר֛וֹב אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָ֖ה וּרְפָאתִֽיו׃


I will create a new expression of the lips: Peace, peace, both for far and near, says the Lord. And I will heal him.


###### Isaiah 57:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:20)


וְהָרְשָׁעִ֖ים כַּיָּ֣ם נִגְרָ֑שׁ כִּ֤י הַשְׁקֵט֙ לֹ֣א יוּכָ֔ל וַיִּגְרְשׁ֥וּ מֵימָ֖יו רֶ֥פֶשׁ וָטִֽיט׃


But the wicked are like the troubled sea, which cannot be still, and whose waters cast up mire and dirt.


###### Isaiah 57:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/57:21)


אֵ֣ין שָׁל֔וֹם אָמַ֥ר אֱלֹהַ֖י לָרְשָׁעִֽים׃{פ}


There will be no peace, says my God, for the wicked.



## Chapter 58

###### Isaiah 58:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:1)


קְרָ֤א בְגָרוֹן֙ אַל־תַּחְשֹׂ֔ךְ כַּשּׁוֹפָ֖ר הָרֵ֣ם קוֹלֶ֑ךָ וְהַגֵּ֤ד לְעַמִּי֙ פִּשְׁעָ֔ם וּלְבֵ֥ית יַעֲקֹ֖ב חַטֹּאתָֽם׃


Cry aloud, spare not, lift up thy voice like the shofar, and show my people their transgression, and the house of Ya῾aqov their sins.


###### Isaiah 58:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:2)


וְאוֹתִ֗י י֥וֹם יוֹם֙ יִדְרֹשׁ֔וּן וְדַ֥עַת דְּרָכַ֖י יֶחְפָּצ֑וּן כְּג֞וֹי אֲשֶׁר־צְדָקָ֣ה עָשָׂ֗ה וּמִשְׁפַּ֤ט אֱלֹהָיו֙ לֹ֣א עָזָ֔ב יִשְׁאָל֙וּנִי֙ מִשְׁפְּטֵי־צֶ֔דֶק קִרְבַ֥ת אֱלֹהִ֖ים יֶחְפָּצֽוּן׃


Yet they seek me daily, and desire to know my ways, as a nation that did righteousness, and forsook not the ordinance of their God, they ask of me judgments of justice; they desire that God should be near.


###### Isaiah 58:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:3)


לָ֤מָּה צַּ֙מְנוּ֙ וְלֹ֣א רָאִ֔יתָ עִנִּ֥ינוּ נַפְשֵׁ֖נוּ וְלֹ֣א תֵדָ֑ע הֵ֣ן בְּי֤וֹם צֹֽמְכֶם֙ תִּמְצְאוּ־חֵ֔פֶץ וְכׇל־עַצְּבֵיכֶ֖ם תִּנְגֹּֽשׂוּ׃


Why have we fasted, say they, and Thou seest not? why have we afflicted our soul, and Thou takest no knowledge? Behold, in the day of your fast you pursue your business, and exact all your payments.


###### Isaiah 58:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:4)


הֵ֣ן לְרִ֤יב וּמַצָּה֙ תָּצ֔וּמוּ וּלְהַכּ֖וֹת בְּאֶגְרֹ֣ף רֶ֑שַׁע לֹא־תָצ֣וּמוּ כַיּ֔וֹם לְהַשְׁמִ֥יעַ בַּמָּר֖וֹם קוֹלְכֶֽם׃


Behold, you fast for strife and debate, and to smite with the fist of wickedness: you fast not this day to make your voice to be heard on high.


###### Isaiah 58:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:5)


הֲכָזֶ֗ה יִֽהְיֶה֙ צ֣וֹם אֶבְחָרֵ֔הוּ י֛וֹם עַנּ֥וֹת אָדָ֖ם נַפְשׁ֑וֹ הֲלָכֹ֨ף כְּאַגְמֹ֜ן רֹאשׁ֗וֹ וְשַׂ֤ק וָאֵ֙פֶר֙ יַצִּ֔יעַ הֲלָזֶה֙ תִּקְרָא־צ֔וֹם וְי֥וֹם רָצ֖וֹן לַיהֹוָֽה׃


Is such the fast that I have chosen? a day for a man to afflict his soul? is it to bow down his head like a bulrush, and to spread sackcloth and ashes under him? wilt thou call this a fast, and an acceptable day to the Lord?


###### Isaiah 58:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:6)


הֲל֣וֹא זֶה֮ צ֣וֹם אֶבְחָרֵ֒הוּ֒ פַּתֵּ֙חַ֙ חַרְצֻבּ֣וֹת רֶ֔שַׁע הַתֵּ֖ר אֲגֻדּ֣וֹת מוֹטָ֑ה וְשַׁלַּ֤ח רְצוּצִים֙ חׇפְשִׁ֔ים וְכׇל־מוֹטָ֖ה תְּנַתֵּֽקוּ׃


Is not this rather the fast that I have chosen? to loose the chains of wickedness, to undo the bands of the yoke, and to let the oppressed go free, and to break every yoke?


###### Isaiah 58:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:7)


הֲל֨וֹא פָרֹ֤ס לָרָעֵב֙ לַחְמֶ֔ךָ וַעֲנִיִּ֥ים מְרוּדִ֖ים תָּ֣בִיא בָ֑יִת כִּֽי־תִרְאֶ֤ה עָרֹם֙ וְכִסִּית֔וֹ וּמִבְּשָׂרְךָ֖ לֹ֥א תִתְעַלָּֽם׃


Is it not to share thy bread with the hungry, and that thou bring the poor that are cast out to thy house? when thou seest the naked, that thou cover him; and that thou hide not thyself from thy own flesh?


###### Isaiah 58:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:8)


אָ֣ז יִבָּקַ֤ע כַּשַּׁ֙חַר֙ אוֹרֶ֔ךָ וַאֲרֻֽכָתְךָ֖ מְהֵרָ֣ה תִצְמָ֑ח וְהָלַ֤ךְ לְפָנֶ֙יךָ֙ צִדְקֶ֔ךָ כְּב֥וֹד יְהֹוָ֖ה יַאַסְפֶֽךָ׃


Then shall thy light break forth like the morning, and thy health shall spring forth speedily: and thy righteousness shall go before thee; the glory of the Lord shall be thy rearguard.


###### Isaiah 58:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:9)


אָ֤ז תִּקְרָא֙ וַיהֹוָ֣ה יַעֲנֶ֔ה תְּשַׁוַּ֖ע וְיֹאמַ֣ר הִנֵּ֑נִי אִם־תָּסִ֤יר מִתּֽוֹכְךָ֙ מוֹטָ֔ה שְׁלַ֥ח אֶצְבַּ֖ע וְדַבֶּר־אָֽוֶן׃


Then shalt thou call, and the Lord shall answer; thou shalt cry, and he shall say, Here I am. If thou take away from the midst of thee the yoke, the pointing of the finger, and speaking iniquity;


###### Isaiah 58:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:10)


וְתָפֵ֤ק לָֽרָעֵב֙ נַפְשֶׁ֔ךָ וְנֶ֥פֶשׁ נַעֲנָ֖ה תַּשְׂבִּ֑יעַ וְזָרַ֤ח בַּחֹ֙שֶׁךְ֙ אוֹרֶ֔ךָ וַאֲפֵלָתְךָ֖ כַּֽצׇּהֳרָֽיִם׃


and if thou draw out thy soul to the hungry, and satisfy the afflicted soul; then shall thy light rise in darkness, and thy gloom be as the noonday:


###### Isaiah 58:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:11)


וְנָחֲךָ֣ יְהֹוָה֮ תָּמִיד֒ וְהִשְׂבִּ֤יעַ בְּצַחְצָחוֹת֙ נַפְשֶׁ֔ךָ וְעַצְמֹתֶ֖יךָ יַחֲלִ֑יץ וְהָיִ֙יתָ֙ כְּגַ֣ן רָוֶ֔ה וּכְמוֹצָ֣א מַ֔יִם אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־יְכַזְּב֖וּ מֵימָֽיו׃


and the Lord shall guide thee continually, and satisfy thy soul in drought, and make strong thy bones: and thou shalt be like a watered garden, and like a spring of water, whose waters fail not.


###### Isaiah 58:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:12)


וּבָנ֤וּ מִמְּךָ֙ חׇרְב֣וֹת עוֹלָ֔ם מוֹסְדֵ֥י דוֹר־וָד֖וֹר תְּקוֹמֵ֑ם וְקֹרָ֤א לְךָ֙ גֹּדֵ֣ר פֶּ֔רֶץ מְשֹׁבֵ֥ב נְתִיב֖וֹת לָשָֽׁבֶת׃


And they that shall be of thee shall build the old waste places: thou shalt raise up the foundations of many generations, and thou shalt be called, The repairer of the breach, The restorer of paths to dwell in.


###### Isaiah 58:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:13)


אִם־תָּשִׁ֤יב מִשַּׁבָּת֙ רַגְלֶ֔ךָ עֲשׂ֥וֹת חֲפָצֶ֖ךָ בְּי֣וֹם קׇדְשִׁ֑י וְקָרָ֨אתָ לַשַּׁבָּ֜ת עֹ֗נֶג לִקְד֤וֹשׁ יְהֹוָה֙ מְכֻבָּ֔ד וְכִבַּדְתּוֹ֙ מֵעֲשׂ֣וֹת דְּרָכֶ֔יךָ מִמְּצ֥וֹא חֶפְצְךָ֖ וְדַבֵּ֥ר דָּבָֽר׃


If thou restrain thy foot because of the sabbath, from pursuing thy business on my holy day; and call the sabbath a delight, the holy day of the Lord honourable; and shalt honour it, not doing thy own ways, nor pursuing thy own business, nor speaking of vain matters,


###### Isaiah 58:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/58:14)


אָ֗ז תִּתְעַנַּג֙ עַל־יְהֹוָ֔ה וְהִרְכַּבְתִּ֖יךָ עַל־[בָּ֣מֳתֵי] (במותי) אָ֑רֶץ וְהַאֲכַלְתִּ֗יךָ נַחֲלַת֙ יַעֲקֹ֣ב אָבִ֔יךָ כִּ֛י פִּ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה דִּבֵּֽר׃{פ}


then shalt thou delight thyself in the Lord; and I will cause thee to ride upon the high places of the earth, and feed thee with the heritage of Ya῾aqov thy father: for the mouth of the Lord has spoken it.



## Chapter 59

###### Isaiah 59:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:1)


הֵ֛ן לֹא־קָצְרָ֥ה יַד־יְהֹוָ֖ה מֵהוֹשִׁ֑יעַ וְלֹא־כָבְדָ֥ה אׇזְנ֖וֹ מִשְּׁמֽוֹעַ׃


Behold, the Lord’s hand is not shortened, that it cannot save; neither is his ear heavy, that it cannot hear:


###### Isaiah 59:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:2)


כִּ֤י אִם־עֲוֺנֹֽתֵיכֶם֙ הָי֣וּ מַבְדִּלִ֔ים בֵּינֵכֶ֕ם לְבֵ֖ין אֱלֹהֵיכֶ֑ם וְחַטֹּאותֵיכֶ֗ם הִסְתִּ֧ירוּ פָנִ֛ים מִכֶּ֖ם מִשְּׁמֽוֹעַ׃


but your iniquities have made a separation between you and your God, and your sins have hid his face from you, that he will not hear.


###### Isaiah 59:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:3)


כִּ֤י כַפֵּיכֶם֙ נְגֹאֲל֣וּ בַדָּ֔ם וְאֶצְבְּעוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם בֶּעָוֺ֑ן שִׂפְתֽוֹתֵיכֶם֙ דִּבְּרוּ־שֶׁ֔קֶר לְשׁוֹנְכֶ֖ם עַוְלָ֥ה תֶהְגֶּֽה׃


For your hands are defiled with blood, and your fingers with iniquity; your lips have spoken lies, your tongue mutters wickedness.


###### Isaiah 59:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:4)


אֵין־קֹרֵ֣א בְצֶ֔דֶק וְאֵ֥ין נִשְׁפָּ֖ט בֶּאֱמוּנָ֑ה בָּט֤וֹחַ עַל־תֹּ֙הוּ֙ וְדַבֶּר־שָׁ֔וְא הָר֥וֹ עָמָ֖ל וְהוֹלֵ֥יד אָֽוֶן׃


None calls in uprightness, nor any pleads in truth: they trust in vanity, and speak lies; they conceive mischief, and bring forth iniquity.


###### Isaiah 59:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:5)


בֵּיצֵ֤י צִפְעוֹנִי֙ בִּקֵּ֔עוּ וְקוּרֵ֥י עַכָּבִ֖ישׁ יֶאֱרֹ֑גוּ הָאֹכֵ֤ל מִבֵּֽיצֵיהֶם֙ יָמ֔וּת וְהַזּוּרֶ֖ה תִּבָּקַ֥ע אֶפְעֶֽה׃


They hatch vipers’ eggs, and weave the spider’s web: he that eats of their eggs dies, and from that which is crushed, a viper breaks out.


###### Isaiah 59:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:6)


קֽוּרֵיהֶם֙ לֹֽא־יִהְי֣וּ לְבֶ֔גֶד וְלֹ֥א יִתְכַּסּ֖וּ בְּמַעֲשֵׂיהֶ֑ם מַֽעֲשֵׂיהֶם֙ מַֽעֲשֵׂי־אָ֔וֶן וּפֹ֥עַל חָמָ֖ס בְּכַפֵּיהֶֽם׃


Their webs shall not become garments, neither shall they cover themselves with their works: their works are works of iniquity, and the act of violence is in their hands.


###### Isaiah 59:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:7)


רַגְלֵיהֶם֙ לָרַ֣ע יָרֻ֔צוּ וִֽימַהֲר֔וּ לִשְׁפֹּ֖ךְ דָּ֣ם נָקִ֑י מַחְשְׁבֹֽתֵיהֶם֙ מַחְשְׁב֣וֹת אָ֔וֶן שֹׁ֥ד וָשֶׁ֖בֶר בִּמְסִלּוֹתָֽם׃


Their feet run to evil, and they make haste to shed innocent blood: their thoughts are thoughts of iniquity; wasting and destruction are in their paths.


###### Isaiah 59:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:8)


דֶּ֤רֶךְ שָׁלוֹם֙ לֹ֣א יָדָ֔עוּ וְאֵ֥ין מִשְׁפָּ֖ט בְּמַעְגְּלוֹתָ֑ם נְתִיבֽוֹתֵיהֶם֙ עִקְּשׁ֣וּ לָהֶ֔ם כֹּ֚ל דֹּרֵ֣ךְ בָּ֔הּ לֹ֥א יָדַ֖ע שָׁלֽוֹם׃


The way of peace they know not; and there is no judgment in their goings: they have made them crooked paths: whoever walks in them knows no peace.


###### Isaiah 59:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:9)


עַל־כֵּ֗ן רָחַ֤ק מִשְׁפָּט֙ מִמֶּ֔נּוּ וְלֹ֥א תַשִּׂיגֵ֖נוּ צְדָקָ֑ה נְקַוֶּ֤ה לָאוֹר֙ וְהִנֵּה־חֹ֔שֶׁךְ לִנְגֹה֖וֹת בָּאֲפֵל֥וֹת נְהַלֵּֽךְ׃


Therefore is justice far from us, neither does righteousness overtake us: we wait for light, but behold darkness: for brightness, but we walk in gloom.


###### Isaiah 59:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:10)


נְגַשְׁשָׁ֤ה כַֽעִוְרִים֙ קִ֔יר וּכְאֵ֥ין עֵינַ֖יִם נְגַשֵּׁ֑שָׁה כָּשַׁ֤לְנוּ בַֽצׇּהֳרַ֙יִם֙ כַּנֶּ֔שֶׁף בָּאַשְׁמַנִּ֖ים כַּמֵּתִֽים׃


We grope for the wall like the blind, and we grope as if we had no eyes: we stumble at noonday as in the night; among the strong we are as dead men.


###### Isaiah 59:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:11)


נֶהֱמֶ֤ה כַדֻּבִּים֙ כֻּלָּ֔נוּ וְכַיּוֹנִ֖ים הָגֹ֣ה נֶהְגֶּ֑ה נְקַוֶּ֤ה לַמִּשְׁפָּט֙ וָאַ֔יִן לִישׁוּעָ֖ה רָחֲקָ֥ה מִמֶּֽנּוּ׃


We roar all like bears, we moan and moan like doves: we look for judgment, but there is none; for salvation, but it is far off from us.


###### Isaiah 59:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:12)


כִּֽי־רַבּ֤וּ פְשָׁעֵ֙ינוּ֙ נֶגְדֶּ֔ךָ וְחַטֹּאותֵ֖ינוּ עָ֣נְתָה בָּ֑נוּ כִּֽי־פְשָׁעֵ֣ינוּ אִתָּ֔נוּ וַעֲוֺנֹתֵ֖ינוּ יְדַעֲנֽוּם׃


For our transgressions are multiplied before Thee, and our sins testify against us: for our transgressions are with us; and as for our iniquities, we know them;


###### Isaiah 59:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:13)


פָּשֹׁ֤עַ וְכַחֵשׁ֙ בַּֽיהֹוָ֔ה וְנָס֖וֹג מֵאַחַ֣ר אֱלֹהֵ֑ינוּ דַּבֶּר־עֹ֣שֶׁק וְסָרָ֔ה הֹר֧וֹ וְהֹג֛וֹ מִלֵּ֖ב דִּבְרֵי־שָֽׁקֶר׃


in transgressing and denying the Lord, and departing away from our God, speaking oppression and revolt, conceiving and uttering from the heart words of falsehood.


###### Isaiah 59:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:14)


וְהֻסַּ֤ג אָחוֹר֙ מִשְׁפָּ֔ט וּצְדָקָ֖ה מֵרָח֣וֹק תַּעֲמֹ֑ד כִּֽי־כָשְׁלָ֤ה בָֽרְחוֹב֙ אֱמֶ֔ת וּנְכֹחָ֖ה לֹא־תוּכַ֥ל לָבֽוֹא׃{ס}


And judgment is turned away backwards, and justice stands afar off: for truth is fallen in the street and upright dealing cannot enter.


###### Isaiah 59:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:15)


וַתְּהִ֤י הָאֱמֶת֙ נֶעְדֶּ֔רֶת וְסָ֥ר מֵרָ֖ע מִשְׁתּוֹלֵ֑ל וַיַּ֧רְא יְהֹוָ֛ה וַיֵּ֥רַע בְּעֵינָ֖יו כִּי־אֵ֥ין מִשְׁפָּֽט׃


And truth is absent; and he that departs from evil makes himself ridiculous: and the Lord saw it, and it displeased him for there was no judgment.


###### Isaiah 59:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:16)


וַיַּרְא֙ כִּֽי־אֵ֣ין אִ֔ישׁ וַיִּשְׁתּוֹמֵ֖ם כִּ֣י אֵ֣ין מַפְגִּ֑יעַ וַתּ֤וֹשַֽׁע־לוֹ֙ זְרֹע֔וֹ וְצִדְקָת֖וֹ הִ֥יא סְמָכָֽתְהוּ׃


And he saw that there was no man, and was astonished that there was no intercessor: therefore his arm brought salvation to him; and his righ­teousness, it sustained him.


###### Isaiah 59:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:17)


וַיִּלְבַּ֤שׁ צְדָקָה֙ כַּשִּׁרְיָ֔ן וְכ֥וֹבַע יְשׁוּעָ֖ה בְּרֹאשׁ֑וֹ וַיִּלְבַּ֞שׁ בִּגְדֵ֤י נָקָם֙ תִּלְבֹּ֔שֶׁת וַיַּ֥עַט כַּמְעִ֖יל קִנְאָֽה׃


For he put on righteousness as a breastplate, and a helmet of salvation upon his head; and he put on the garments of vengeance for clothing, and was clad with zeal as a cloak.


###### Isaiah 59:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:18)


כְּעַ֤ל גְּמֻלוֹת֙ כְּעַ֣ל יְשַׁלֵּ֔ם חֵמָ֣ה לְצָרָ֔יו גְּמ֖וּל לְאֹיְבָ֑יו לָאִיִּ֖ים גְּמ֥וּל יְשַׁלֵּֽם׃


According to their deeds, so will he repay, fury to his adversaries, recompense to his enemies; to the islands he will repay recompense.


###### Isaiah 59:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:19)


וְיִֽרְא֤וּ מִֽמַּעֲרָב֙ אֶת־שֵׁ֣ם יְהֹוָ֔ה וּמִמִּזְרַח־שֶׁ֖מֶשׁ אֶת־כְּבוֹד֑וֹ כִּֽי־יָב֤וֹא כַנָּהָר֙ צָ֔ר ר֥וּחַ יְהֹוָ֖ה נֹ֥סְסָה בֽוֹ׃


So shall they fear the name of the Lord from the west, and his glory from the rising of the sun: when affliction comes like a flood which the wind of the Lord drives forth.


###### Isaiah 59:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:20)


וּבָ֤א לְצִיּוֹן֙ גּוֹאֵ֔ל וּלְשָׁבֵ֥י פֶ֖שַׁע בְּיַֽעֲקֹ֑ב נְאֻ֖ם יְהֹוָֽה׃


But to Żiyyon a redeemer shall come, and to them that turn from transgression in Ya῾aqov, says the Lord.


###### Isaiah 59:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/59:21)


וַאֲנִ֗י זֹ֣את בְּרִיתִ֤י אוֹתָם֙ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה רוּחִי֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר עָלֶ֔יךָ וּדְבָרַ֖י אֲשֶׁר־שַׂ֣מְתִּֽי בְּפִ֑יךָ לֹֽא־יָמ֡וּשׁוּ מִפִּ֩יךָ֩ וּמִפִּ֨י זַרְעֲךָ֜ וּמִפִּ֨י זֶ֤רַע זַרְעֲךָ֙ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה מֵעַתָּ֖ה וְעַד־עוֹלָֽם׃{ס}


As for me, this is my covenant with them, says the Lord; My spirit that is upon thee, and my words which I have put in thy mouth, shall not depart out of thy mouth, nor out of the mouth of thy seed, nor out of the mouth of thy seed’s seed, says the Lord, from henceforth and for ever.



## Chapter 60

###### Isaiah 60:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:1)


ק֥וּמִי א֖וֹרִי כִּ֣י בָ֣א אוֹרֵ֑ךְ וּכְב֥וֹד יְהֹוָ֖ה עָלַ֥יִךְ זָרָֽח׃


Arise, shine; for thy light is come, and the glory of the Lord is risen upon thee.


###### Isaiah 60:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:2)


כִּֽי־הִנֵּ֤ה הַחֹ֙שֶׁךְ֙ יְכַסֶּה־אֶ֔רֶץ וַעֲרָפֶ֖ל לְאֻמִּ֑ים וְעָלַ֙יִךְ֙ יִזְרַ֣ח יְהֹוָ֔ה וּכְבוֹד֖וֹ עָלַ֥יִךְ יֵרָאֶֽה׃


For, behold, the darkness shall cover the earth, and gross darkness the peoples: but the Lord shall arise upon thee, and his glory shall be seen upon thee.


###### Isaiah 60:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:3)


וְהָלְכ֥וּ גוֹיִ֖ם לְאוֹרֵ֑ךְ וּמְלָכִ֖ים לְנֹ֥גַהּ זַרְחֵֽךְ׃


And nations shall walk at thy light, and kings at the brightness of thy rising.


###### Isaiah 60:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:4)


שְׂאִֽי־סָבִ֤יב עֵינַ֙יִךְ֙ וּרְאִ֔י כֻּלָּ֖ם נִקְבְּצ֣וּ בָֽאוּ־לָ֑ךְ בָּנַ֙יִךְ֙ מֵרָח֣וֹק יָבֹ֔אוּ וּבְנוֹתַ֖יִךְ עַל־צַ֥ד תֵּאָמַֽנָה׃


Lift up thy eyes round about, and see: all they gather themselves together, they come to thee: thy sons shall come from far, and thy daughters shall be nursed at thy side.


###### Isaiah 60:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:5)


אָ֤ז תִּרְאִי֙ וְנָהַ֔רְתְּ וּפָחַ֥ד וְרָחַ֖ב לְבָבֵ֑ךְ כִּֽי־יֵהָפֵ֤ךְ עָלַ֙יִךְ֙ הֲמ֣וֹן יָ֔ם חֵ֥יל גּוֹיִ֖ם יָבֹ֥אוּ לָֽךְ׃


Then thou wilt see, and be filled with light, and thy heart shall fear and be enlarged; because the abundance of the sea shall be turned to thee, the riches of the nations shall come to thee.


###### Isaiah 60:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:6)


שִֽׁפְעַ֨ת גְּמַלִּ֜ים תְּכַסֵּ֗ךְ בִּכְרֵ֤י מִדְיָן֙ וְעֵיפָ֔ה כֻּלָּ֖ם מִשְּׁבָ֣א יָבֹ֑אוּ זָהָ֤ב וּלְבוֹנָה֙ יִשָּׂ֔אוּ וּתְהִלֹּ֥ת יְהֹוָ֖ה יְבַשֵּֽׂרוּ׃


The multitude of camels shall cover thee, the dromedaries of Midyan and ῾Efa; all they from Sheva shall come: they shall bring gold and incense; and they shall proclaim the praises of the Lord.


###### Isaiah 60:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:7)


כׇּל־צֹ֤אן קֵדָר֙ יִקָּ֣בְצוּ לָ֔ךְ אֵילֵ֥י נְבָי֖וֹת יְשָֽׁרְת֑וּנֶךְ יַעֲל֤וּ עַל־רָצוֹן֙ מִזְבְּחִ֔י וּבֵ֥ית תִּפְאַרְתִּ֖י אֲפָאֵֽר׃


All the flocks of Qedar shall be gathered together to thee, the rams of Nevayot shall minister to thee: they shall come up with acceptance on my altar, and I will glorify the house of my glory.


###### Isaiah 60:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:8)


מִי־אֵ֖לֶּה כָּעָ֣ב תְּעוּפֶ֑ינָה וְכַיּוֹנִ֖ים אֶל־אֲרֻבֹּתֵיהֶֽם׃


Who are these that fly as a cloud, and as the doves to their windows?


###### Isaiah 60:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:9)


כִּי־לִ֣י**׀** אִיִּ֣ים יְקַוּ֗וּ וׇאֳנִיּ֤וֹת תַּרְשִׁישׁ֙ בָּרִ֣אשֹׁנָ֔ה לְהָבִ֤יא בָנַ֙יִךְ֙ מֵרָח֔וֹק כַּסְפָּ֥ם וּזְהָבָ֖ם אִתָּ֑ם לְשֵׁם֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה אֱלֹהַ֔יִךְ וְלִקְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל כִּ֥י פֵאֲרָֽךְ׃


Surely the isles shall wait for me, and the ships of Tarshish first, to bring thy sons from far, their silver and their gold with them, to the name of the Lord thy God, and to the Holy One of Yisra᾽el, because he has glorified thee.


###### Isaiah 60:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:10)


וּבָנ֤וּ בְנֵֽי־נֵכָר֙ חֹמֹתַ֔יִךְ וּמַלְכֵיהֶ֖ם יְשָֽׁרְת֑וּנֶךְ כִּ֤י בְקִצְפִּי֙ הִכִּיתִ֔יךְ וּבִרְצוֹנִ֖י רִחַמְתִּֽיךְ׃


And the sons of strangers shall build up thy walls, and their kings shall minister to thee: for in my wrath I smote thee, but in my favour have I had mercy on thee.


###### Isaiah 60:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:11)


וּפִתְּח֨וּ שְׁעָרַ֧יִךְ תָּמִ֛יד יוֹמָ֥ם וָלַ֖יְלָה לֹ֣א יִסָּגֵ֑רוּ לְהָבִ֤יא אֵלַ֙יִךְ֙ חֵ֣יל גּוֹיִ֔ם וּמַלְכֵיהֶ֖ם נְהוּגִֽים׃


Therefore thy gates shall be open continually; they shall not be shut day or night; that men may bring to thee the riches of the nations, and their kings with their train.


###### Isaiah 60:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:12)


כִּֽי־הַגּ֧וֹי וְהַמַּמְלָכָ֛ה אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־יַעַבְד֖וּךְ יֹאבֵ֑דוּ וְהַגּוֹיִ֖ם חָרֹ֥ב יֶחֱרָֽבוּ׃


For the nation and kingdom that will not serve thee shall perish; yea, those nations shall be utterly wasted.


###### Isaiah 60:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:13)


כְּב֤וֹד הַלְּבָנוֹן֙ אֵלַ֣יִךְ יָב֔וֹא בְּר֛וֹשׁ תִּדְהָ֥ר וּתְאַשּׁ֖וּר יַחְדָּ֑ו לְפָאֵר֙ מְק֣וֹם מִקְדָּשִׁ֔י וּמְק֥וֹם רַגְלַ֖י אֲכַבֵּֽד׃


The glory of Levanon shall come to thee, the cypress, the maple, and the box tree, together, to beautify the place of my sanctuary; and I will make the place of my feet glorious.


###### Isaiah 60:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:14)


וְהָלְכ֨וּ אֵלַ֤יִךְ שְׁח֙וֹחַ֙ בְּנֵ֣י מְעַנַּ֔יִךְ וְהִֽשְׁתַּחֲו֛וּ עַל־כַּפּ֥וֹת רַגְלַ֖יִךְ כׇּל־מְנַאֲצָ֑יִךְ וְקָ֤רְאוּ לָךְ֙ עִ֣יר יְהֹוָ֔ה צִיּ֖וֹן קְד֥וֹשׁ יִשְׂרָאֵֽל׃


The sons also of them that afflicted thee shall come bending to thee; and all they that despised thee shall bow themselves down at the soles of thy feet; and they shall call thee, The city of the Lord, The Żiyyon of the Holy One of Yisra᾽el.


###### Isaiah 60:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:15)


תַּ֧חַת הֱיוֹתֵ֛ךְ עֲזוּבָ֥ה וּשְׂנוּאָ֖ה וְאֵ֣ין עוֹבֵ֑ר וְשַׂמְתִּיךְ֙ לִגְא֣וֹן עוֹלָ֔ם מְשׂ֖וֹשׂ דּ֥וֹר וָדֽוֹר׃


Whereas thou hast been forsaken and hated, so that no man went through thee, I will make thee an eternal excellency, a joy of many generations.


###### Isaiah 60:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:16)


וְיָנַקְתְּ֙ חֲלֵ֣ב גּוֹיִ֔ם וְשֹׁ֥ד מְלָכִ֖ים תִּינָ֑קִי וְיָדַ֗עַתְּ כִּ֣י אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ מֽוֹשִׁיעֵ֔ךְ וְגֹאֲלֵ֖ךְ אֲבִ֥יר יַעֲקֹֽב׃


Thou shalt also suck the milk of the nations, and shalt suck the breast of kings: and thou shalt know that I the Lord shall save thee, and the mighty One of Ya῾aqov shall redeem thee.


###### Isaiah 60:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:17)


תַּ֣חַת הַנְּחֹ֜שֶׁת אָבִ֣יא זָהָ֗ב וְתַ֤חַת הַבַּרְזֶל֙ אָ֣בִיא כֶ֔סֶף וְתַ֤חַת הָעֵצִים֙ נְחֹ֔שֶׁת וְתַ֥חַת הָאֲבָנִ֖ים בַּרְזֶ֑ל וְשַׂמְתִּ֤י פְקֻדָּתֵךְ֙ שָׁל֔וֹם וְנֹגְשַׂ֖יִךְ צְדָקָֽה׃


For brass I will bring gold, and for iron I will bring silver, and for wood brass, and for stones iron: I will also make thy officers peace, and thy taskmasters, righteousness.


###### Isaiah 60:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:18)


לֹא־יִשָּׁמַ֨ע ע֤וֹד חָמָס֙ בְּאַרְצֵ֔ךְ שֹׁ֥ד וָשֶׁ֖בֶר בִּגְבוּלָ֑יִךְ וְקָרָ֤את יְשׁוּעָה֙ חוֹמֹתַ֔יִךְ וּשְׁעָרַ֖יִךְ תְּהִלָּֽה׃


Violence shall no more be heard in thy land, wasting nor destruction within thy borders; but thou shalt call thy walls Salvation, and thy gates Praise.


###### Isaiah 60:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:19)


לֹא־יִֽהְיֶה־לָּ֨ךְ ע֤וֹד הַשֶּׁ֙מֶשׁ֙ לְא֣וֹר יוֹמָ֔ם וּלְנֹ֕גַהּ הַיָּרֵ֖חַ לֹא־יָאִ֣יר לָ֑ךְ וְהָֽיָה־לָ֤ךְ יְהֹוָה֙ לְא֣וֹר עוֹלָ֔ם וֵאלֹהַ֖יִךְ לְתִפְאַרְתֵּֽךְ׃


The sun shall be no more thy light by day; neither for brightness shall the moon give light to thee: but the Lord shall be to thee an everlasting light, and thy God thy glory.


###### Isaiah 60:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:20)


לֹא־יָב֥וֹא עוֹד֙ שִׁמְשֵׁ֔ךְ וִירֵחֵ֖ךְ לֹ֣א יֵאָסֵ֑ף כִּ֣י יְהֹוָ֗ה יִֽהְיֶה־לָּךְ֙ לְא֣וֹר עוֹלָ֔ם וְשָׁלְמ֖וּ יְמֵ֥י אֶבְלֵֽךְ׃


Thy sun shall no more go down; nor shall thy moon withdraw itself: for the Lord shall be thy everlasting light, and the days of thy mourning shall be ended.


###### Isaiah 60:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:21)


וְעַמֵּךְ֙ כֻּלָּ֣ם צַדִּיקִ֔ים לְעוֹלָ֖ם יִ֣ירְשׁוּ אָ֑רֶץ נֵ֧צֶר (מטעו) [מַטָּעַ֛י] מַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יָדַ֖י לְהִתְפָּאֵֽר׃


Thy people also shall be all righteous: they shall inherit the land for ever; they shall be the branch of my planting, the work of my hands, that I may be glorified.


###### Isaiah 60:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/60:22)


הַקָּטֹן֙ יִהְיֶ֣ה לָאֶ֔לֶף וְהַצָּעִ֖יר לְג֣וֹי עָצ֑וּם אֲנִ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה בְּעִתָּ֥הּ אֲחִישֶֽׁנָּה׃{ס}


The least one shall become a thousand, and the smallest one a strong nation: I the Lord will hasten it in its time.



## Chapter 61

###### Isaiah 61:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:1)


ר֛וּחַ אֲדֹנָ֥י יֱהֹוִ֖ה עָלָ֑י יַ֡עַן מָשַׁח֩ יְהֹוָ֨ה אֹתִ֜י לְבַשֵּׂ֣ר עֲנָוִ֗ים שְׁלָחַ֙נִי֙ לַחֲבֹ֣שׁ לְנִשְׁבְּרֵי־לֵ֔ב לִקְרֹ֤א לִשְׁבוּיִם֙ דְּר֔וֹר וְלַאֲסוּרִ֖ים פְּקַח־קֽוֹחַ׃


The spirit of the Lord God is upon me; because the Lord has anointed me to announce good tidings to the meek; he has sent me to bind up the broken hearted, to proclaim liberty to the captives, and the opening of the prison to them that are bound;


###### Isaiah 61:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:2)


לִקְרֹ֤א שְׁנַת־רָצוֹן֙ לַֽיהֹוָ֔ה וְי֥וֹם נָקָ֖ם לֵאלֹהֵ֑ינוּ לְנַחֵ֖ם כׇּל־אֲבֵלִֽים׃


to proclaim an acceptable year of the Lord, and a day of vengeance of our God; to comfort all that mourn;


###### Isaiah 61:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:3)


לָשׂ֣וּם**׀** לַאֲבֵלֵ֣י צִיּ֗וֹן לָתֵת֩ לָהֶ֨ם פְּאֵ֜ר תַּ֣חַת אֵ֗פֶר שֶׁ֤מֶן שָׂשׂוֹן֙ תַּ֣חַת אֵ֔בֶל מַעֲטֵ֣ה תְהִלָּ֔ה תַּ֖חַת ר֣וּחַ כֵּהָ֑ה וְקֹרָ֤א לָהֶם֙ אֵילֵ֣י הַצֶּ֔דֶק מַטַּ֥ע יְהֹוָ֖ה לְהִתְפָּאֵֽר׃


to appoint to those who mourn in Żiyyon, to give to them a garland instead of ashes, the oil of joy instead of mourning, the garment of praise instead of the spirit of heav­iness, that they might be called trees of righteousness, the planting of the Lord, that he might be glorified.


###### Isaiah 61:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:4)


וּבָנוּ֙ חׇרְב֣וֹת עוֹלָ֔ם שֹׁמְמ֥וֹת רִאשֹׁנִ֖ים יְקוֹמֵ֑מוּ וְחִדְּשׁוּ֙ עָ֣רֵי חֹ֔רֶב שֹׁמְמ֖וֹת דּ֥וֹר וָדֽוֹר׃


And they shall build the old wastes, they shall raise up the former desolations, and they shall renew the waste cities, the desolations of many generations.


###### Isaiah 61:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:5)


וְעָמְד֣וּ זָרִ֔ים וְרָע֖וּ צֹאנְכֶ֑ם וּבְנֵ֣י נֵכָ֔ר אִכָּרֵיכֶ֖ם וְכֹרְמֵיכֶֽם׃


And strangers shall stand and feed your flocks, and the sons of the alien shall be your ploughmen and your vine­dressers.


###### Isaiah 61:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:6)


וְאַתֶּ֗ם כֹּהֲנֵ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ תִּקָּרֵ֔אוּ מְשָׁרְתֵ֣י אֱלֹהֵ֔ינוּ יֵאָמֵ֖ר לָכֶ֑ם חֵ֤יל גּוֹיִם֙ תֹּאכֵ֔לוּ וּבִכְבוֹדָ֖ם תִּתְיַמָּֽרוּ׃


But you shall be named, Priests of the Lord: men shall say of you, Ministers of our God. You shall eat the riches of the nations, and you shall gain grandeur by their wealth.


###### Isaiah 61:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:7)


תַּ֤חַת בׇּשְׁתְּכֶם֙ מִשְׁנֶ֔ה וּכְלִמָּ֖ה יָרֹ֣נּוּ חֶלְקָ֑ם לָכֵ֤ן בְּאַרְצָם֙ מִשְׁנֶ֣ה יִירָ֔שׁוּ שִׂמְחַ֥ת עוֹלָ֖ם תִּהְיֶ֥ה לָהֶֽם׃


Instead of your shame, you shall have a double portion; and instead of dishonour they shall rejoice in their lot: therefore in their land they shall possess a double portion; everlasting joy shall be theirs.


###### Isaiah 61:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:8)


כִּ֣י אֲנִ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ אֹהֵ֣ב מִשְׁפָּ֔ט שֹׂנֵ֥א גָזֵ֖ל בְּעוֹלָ֑ה וְנָתַתִּ֤י פְעֻלָּתָם֙ בֶּאֱמֶ֔ת וּבְרִ֥ית עוֹלָ֖ם אֶכְר֥וֹת לָהֶֽם׃


For I the Lord love judgment, I hate robbery with burnt offerings; and I will give them their reward in truth, and I will make an everlasting covenant with them.


###### Isaiah 61:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:9)


וְנוֹדַ֤ע בַּגּוֹיִם֙ זַרְעָ֔ם וְצֶאֱצָאֵיהֶ֖ם בְּת֣וֹךְ הָעַמִּ֑ים כׇּל־רֹֽאֵיהֶם֙ יַכִּיר֔וּם כִּ֛י הֵ֥ם זֶ֖רַע בֵּרַ֥ךְ יְהֹוָֽה׃{פ}


And their seed shall be known among the nations, and their off­spring among the peoples: all that see them shall acknowledge them, that they are the seed which the Lord has blessed.


###### Isaiah 61:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:10)


שׂ֧וֹשׂ אָשִׂ֣ישׂ בַּֽיהֹוָ֗ה תָּגֵ֤ל נַפְשִׁי֙ בֵּֽאלֹהַ֔י כִּ֤י הִלְבִּישַׁ֙נִי֙ בִּגְדֵי־יֶ֔שַׁע מְעִ֥יל צְדָקָ֖ה יְעָטָ֑נִי כֶּֽחָתָן֙ יְכַהֵ֣ן פְּאֵ֔ר וְכַכַּלָּ֖ה תַּעְדֶּ֥ה כֵלֶֽיהָ׃


I will greatly rejoice in the Lord, my soul shall be joyful in my God; for he has clothed me with the garments of salvation, he has covered me with the robe of righteous­ness, as a bridegroom decks himself with a garland, and as a bride adorns her­self with her jewels.


###### Isaiah 61:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/61:11)


כִּ֤י כָאָ֙רֶץ֙ תּוֹצִ֣יא צִמְחָ֔הּ וּכְגַנָּ֖ה זֵרוּעֶ֣יהָ תַצְמִ֑יחַ כֵּ֣ן**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה יַצְמִ֤יחַ צְדָקָה֙ וּתְהִלָּ֔ה נֶ֖גֶד כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִֽם׃


For as the earth brings forth her growth, and as the garden causes the things that are sown in it to spring forth; so the Lord God will cause righteousness and praise to spring forth before all the nations.



## Chapter 62

###### Isaiah 62:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:1)


לְמַ֤עַן צִיּוֹן֙ לֹ֣א אֶחֱשֶׁ֔ה וּלְמַ֥עַן יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם לֹ֣א אֶשְׁק֑וֹט עַד־יֵצֵ֤א כַנֹּ֙גַהּ֙ צִדְקָ֔הּ וִישׁוּעָתָ֖הּ כְּלַפִּ֥יד יִבְעָֽר׃


For the sake of Żiyyon I will not hold my peace, and for the sake of Yeru­shalayim, I will not be still, until her righteousness goes forth like radiance, and her salvation like a burning torch.


###### Isaiah 62:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:2)


וְרָא֤וּ גוֹיִם֙ צִדְקֵ֔ךְ וְכׇל־מְלָכִ֖ים כְּבוֹדֵ֑ךְ וְקֹ֤רָא לָךְ֙ שֵׁ֣ם חָדָ֔שׁ אֲשֶׁ֛ר פִּ֥י יְהֹוָ֖ה יִקֳּבֶֽנּוּ׃


And the nations shall see thy righteousness, and all kings thy glory: and thou shalt be called by a new name, which the mouth of the Lord shall express.


###### Isaiah 62:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:3)


וְהָיִ֛ית עֲטֶ֥רֶת תִּפְאֶ֖רֶת בְּיַד־יְהֹוָ֑ה (וצנוף) [וּצְנִ֥יף] מְלוּכָ֖ה בְּכַף־אֱלֹהָֽיִךְ׃


Thou shalt also be a crown of glory in the hand of the Lord, and a royal diadem in the hand of thy God.


###### Isaiah 62:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:4)


לֹא־יֵאָמֵר֩ לָ֨ךְ ע֜וֹד עֲזוּבָ֗ה וּלְאַרְצֵךְ֙ לֹא־יֵאָמֵ֥ר עוֹד֙ שְׁמָמָ֔ה כִּ֣י לָ֗ךְ יִקָּרֵא֙ חֶפְצִי־בָ֔הּ וּלְאַרְצֵ֖ךְ בְּעוּלָ֑ה כִּֽי־חָפֵ֤ץ יְהֹוָה֙ בָּ֔ךְ וְאַרְצֵ֖ךְ תִּבָּעֵֽל׃


Thou shalt no more be termed Forsaken; neither shall thy land any more be termed Desolate: but thou shalt be called Ḥefżi-va (My Delight is in Her,) and thy land Be῾ula (Espoused:) for the Lord delights in thee, and thy land shall be espoused.


###### Isaiah 62:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:5)


כִּֽי־יִבְעַ֤ל בָּחוּר֙ בְּתוּלָ֔ה יִבְעָל֖וּךְ בָּנָ֑יִךְ וּמְשׂ֤וֹשׂ חָתָן֙ עַל־כַּלָּ֔ה יָשִׂ֥ישׂ עָלַ֖יִךְ אֱלֹהָֽיִךְ׃


For as a young man takes to himself a virgin, so shall thy sons take thee to themselves, and as the bridegroom rejoices over the bride, so shall thy God rejoice over thee.


###### Isaiah 62:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:6)


עַל־חוֹמֹתַ֣יִךְ יְרוּשָׁלַ֗͏ִם הִפְקַ֙דְתִּי֙ שֹֽׁמְרִ֔ים כׇּל־הַיּ֧וֹם וְכׇל־הַלַּ֛יְלָה תָּמִ֖יד לֹ֣א יֶחֱשׁ֑וּ הַמַּזְכִּרִים֙ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֔ה אַל־דֳּמִ֖י לָכֶֽם׃


I have set watchmen upon thy walls, O Yerushalayim, who shall never hold their peace day or night: you that make mention of the Lord, take no rest,


###### Isaiah 62:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:7)


וְאַֽל־תִּתְּנ֥וּ דֳמִ֖י ל֑וֹ עַד־יְכוֹנֵ֞ן וְעַד־יָשִׂ֧ים אֶת־יְרוּשָׁלַ֛͏ִם תְּהִלָּ֖ה בָּאָֽרֶץ׃


and give him no rest till he establishes, and till he makes Yerushalayim a praise in the earth.


###### Isaiah 62:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:8)


נִשְׁבַּ֧ע יְהֹוָ֛ה בִּימִינ֖וֹ וּבִזְר֣וֹעַ עֻזּ֑וֹ אִם־אֶתֵּן֩ אֶת־דְּגָנֵ֨ךְ ע֤וֹד מַֽאֲכָל֙ לְאֹ֣יְבַ֔יִךְ וְאִם־יִשְׁתּ֤וּ בְנֵֽי־נֵכָר֙ תִּֽירוֹשֵׁ֔ךְ אֲשֶׁ֥ר יָגַ֖עַתְּ בּֽוֹ׃


The Lord has sworn by his right hand, and by the arm of his strength, Surely I will no more give thy corn to be food for thy enemies; and the sons of the stranger shall not drink thy wine for which thou hast laboured:


###### Isaiah 62:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:9)


כִּ֤י מְאַסְפָיו֙ יֹאכְלֻ֔הוּ וְהִֽלְל֖וּ אֶת־יְהֹוָ֑ה וּמְקַבְּצָ֥יו יִשְׁתֻּ֖הוּ בְּחַצְר֥וֹת קׇדְשִֽׁי׃{ס}


but they that have gathered it shall eat it, and praise the Lord; and they that have brought it together shall drink it in the courts of my sanctuary.


###### Isaiah 62:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:10)


עִבְר֤וּ עִבְרוּ֙ בַּשְּׁעָרִ֔ים פַּנּ֖וּ דֶּ֣רֶךְ הָעָ֑ם סֹ֣לּוּ סֹ֤לּוּ הַֽמְסִלָּה֙ סַקְּל֣וּ מֵאֶ֔בֶן הָרִ֥ימוּ נֵ֖ס עַל־הָעַמִּֽים׃


Go through, go through the gates; prepare the way of the people; bank up, build up the highway; clear the stones; lift up a standard for the people.


###### Isaiah 62:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:11)


הִנֵּ֣ה יְהֹוָ֗ה הִשְׁמִ֙יעַ֙ אֶל־קְצֵ֣ה הָאָ֔רֶץ אִמְרוּ֙ לְבַת־צִיּ֔וֹן הִנֵּ֥ה יִשְׁעֵ֖ךְ בָּ֑א הִנֵּ֤ה שְׂכָרוֹ֙ אִתּ֔וֹ וּפְעֻלָּת֖וֹ לְפָנָֽיו׃


Behold, the Lord has proclaimed to the end of the world, Say to the daughter of Żiyyon, Behold, thy salvation comes; behold, his reward is with him, and his wages before him.


###### Isaiah 62:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/62:12)


וְקָרְא֥וּ לָהֶ֛ם עַם־הַקֹּ֖דֶשׁ גְּאוּלֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֑ה וְלָךְ֙ יִקָּרֵ֣א דְרוּשָׁ֔ה עִ֖יר לֹ֥א נֶעֱזָֽבָה׃{ס}


And they shall call them, The holy people, The redeemed of the Lord: and thou shalt be called, Derusha (Sought out,) a city not forsaken.



## Chapter 63

###### Isaiah 63:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:1)


מִי־זֶ֣ה**׀** בָּ֣א מֵאֱד֗וֹם חֲמ֤וּץ בְּגָדִים֙ מִבׇּצְרָ֔ה זֶ֚ה הָד֣וּר בִּלְבוּשׁ֔וֹ צֹעֶ֖ה בְּרֹ֣ב כֹּח֑וֹ אֲנִ֛י מְדַבֵּ֥ר בִּצְדָקָ֖ה רַ֥ב לְהוֹשִֽׁיעַ׃


Who is this that comes from Edom, with crimsoned garments from Bożra? this one that is glorious in his apparel, striding in the greatness of his strength? I that speak in righteousness, mighty to save.


###### Isaiah 63:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:2)


מַדּ֥וּעַ אָדֹ֖ם לִלְבוּשֶׁ֑ךָ וּבְגָדֶ֖יךָ כְּדֹרֵ֥ךְ בְּגַֽת׃


Why is thy apparel red, and thy garments like his that treads in the winepress?


###### Isaiah 63:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:3)


פּוּרָ֣ה**׀** דָּרַ֣כְתִּי לְבַדִּ֗י וּמֵֽעַמִּים֙ אֵֽין־אִ֣ישׁ אִתִּ֔י וְאֶדְרְכֵ֣ם בְּאַפִּ֔י וְאֶרְמְסֵ֖ם בַּחֲמָתִ֑י וְיֵ֤ז נִצְחָם֙ עַל־בְּגָדַ֔י וְכׇל־מַלְבּוּשַׁ֖י אֶגְאָֽלְתִּי׃


I have trodden the winepress alone; and of the peoples there was none with me: for I have trodden them in my anger, and trampled them in my fury; and their blood was sprinkled upon my garments, and I have stained all my raiment.


###### Isaiah 63:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:4)


כִּ֛י י֥וֹם נָקָ֖ם בְּלִבִּ֑י וּשְׁנַ֥ת גְּאוּלַ֖י בָּֽאָה׃


For the day of vengeance is in my heart, and the year of my redeemed is come.


###### Isaiah 63:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:5)


וְאַבִּיט֙ וְאֵ֣ין עֹזֵ֔ר וְאֶשְׁתּוֹמֵ֖ם וְאֵ֣ין סוֹמֵ֑ךְ וַתּ֤וֹשַֽׁע־לִי֙ זְרֹעִ֔י וַחֲמָתִ֖י הִ֥יא סְמָכָֽתְנִי׃


And I looked and there was none to help; and I gazed astonished but there was none to uphold: therefore has my own arm brought salvation to me; and my fury, it has upheld me.


###### Isaiah 63:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:6)


וְאָב֤וּס עַמִּים֙ בְּאַפִּ֔י וַאֲשַׁכְּרֵ֖ם בַּחֲמָתִ֑י וְאוֹרִ֥יד לָאָ֖רֶץ נִצְחָֽם׃{ס}


And I trod down peoples in my anger, and made them drunk with my fury, and I poured out their lifeblood on the earth:


###### Isaiah 63:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:7)


חַֽסְדֵ֨י יְהֹוָ֤ה׀אַזְכִּיר֙ תְּהִלֹּ֣ת יְהֹוָ֔ה כְּעַ֕ל כֹּ֥ל אֲשֶׁר־גְּמָלָ֖נוּ יְהֹוָ֑ה וְרַב־טוּב֙ לְבֵ֣ית יִשְׂרָאֵ֔ל אֲשֶׁר־גְּמָלָ֥ם כְּֽרַחֲמָ֖יו וּכְרֹ֥ב חֲסָדָֽיו׃


I will mention the acts of the Lord’s faithful love, and the praises of the Lord, according to all that the Lord has bestowed on us, and the great goodness toward the house of Yisra᾽el which he has bestowed on them according to his mercies, and according to the abundance of his faithful love.


###### Isaiah 63:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:8)


וַיֹּ֙אמֶר֙ אַךְ־עַמִּ֣י הֵ֔מָּה בָּנִ֖ים לֹ֣א יְשַׁקֵּ֑רוּ וַיְהִ֥י לָהֶ֖ם לְמוֹשִֽׁיעַ׃


For he said, Surely they are my people, children that will not lie: so he was their deliverer.


###### Isaiah 63:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:9)


בְּֽכׇל־צָרָתָ֣ם**׀** (לא) [ל֣וֹ] צָ֗ר וּמַלְאַ֤ךְ פָּנָיו֙ הוֹשִׁיעָ֔ם בְּאַהֲבָת֥וֹ וּבְחֶמְלָת֖וֹ ה֣וּא גְאָלָ֑ם וַֽיְנַטְּלֵ֥ם וַֽיְנַשְּׂאֵ֖ם כׇּל־יְמֵ֥י עוֹלָֽם׃


In all their affliction he was afflicted, and the angel of his presence saved them: in his love and in his pity he redeemed them; and he bore them, and carried them all the days of old.


###### Isaiah 63:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:10)


וְהֵ֛מָּה מָר֥וּ וְעִצְּב֖וּ אֶת־ר֣וּחַ קׇדְשׁ֑וֹ וַיֵּהָפֵ֥ךְ לָהֶ֛ם לְאוֹיֵ֖ב ה֥וּא נִלְחַם־בָּֽם׃


But they rebelled, and vexed his holy spirit: therefore he was turned to be their enemy, and he fought against them.


###### Isaiah 63:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:11)


וַיִּזְכֹּ֥ר יְמֵֽי־עוֹלָ֖ם מֹשֶׁ֣ה עַמּ֑וֹ אַיֵּ֣ה**׀** הַֽמַּעֲלֵ֣ם מִיָּ֗ם אֵ֚ת רֹעֵ֣י צֹאנ֔וֹ אַיֵּ֛ה הַשָּׂ֥ם בְּקִרְבּ֖וֹ אֶת־ר֥וּחַ קׇדְשֽׁוֹ׃


Then he remembered the days of old, Moshe, and his people saying, Where is he that brought them up out of the sea with the shepherds of his flock? where is he that put his holy spirit within him?


###### Isaiah 63:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:12)


מוֹלִיךְ֙ לִימִ֣ין מֹשֶׁ֔ה זְר֖וֹעַ תִּפְאַרְתּ֑וֹ בּ֤וֹקֵֽעַ מַ֙יִם֙ מִפְּנֵיהֶ֔ם לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת ל֖וֹ שֵׁ֥ם עוֹלָֽם׃


That caused his glorious arm to go at the right hand of Moshe, dividing the water before them, to make himself an everlasting name?


###### Isaiah 63:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:13)


מוֹלִיכָ֖ם בַּתְּהֹמ֑וֹת כַּסּ֥וּס בַּמִּדְבָּ֖ר לֹ֥א יִכָּשֵֽׁלוּ׃


That led them through the deep, like a horse in the wilderness, that they should not stumble?


###### Isaiah 63:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:14)


כַּבְּהֵמָה֙ בַּבִּקְעָ֣ה תֵרֵ֔ד ר֥וּחַ יְהֹוָ֖ה תְּנִיחֶ֑נּוּ כֵּ֚ן נִהַ֣גְתָּ עַמְּךָ֔ לַעֲשׂ֥וֹת לְךָ֖ שֵׁ֥ם תִּפְאָֽרֶת׃


Like cattle going down into the valley, the spirit of the Lord gave them rest. So Thou didst lead Thy people, to make Thyself a glorious name.


###### Isaiah 63:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:15)


הַבֵּ֤ט מִשָּׁמַ֙יִם֙ וּרְאֵ֔ה מִזְּבֻ֥ל קׇדְשְׁךָ֖ וְתִפְאַרְתֶּ֑ךָ אַיֵּ֤ה קִנְאָֽתְךָ֙ וּגְב֣וּרֹתֶ֔ךָ הֲמ֥וֹן מֵעֶ֛יךָ וְֽרַחֲמֶ֖יךָ אֵלַ֥י הִתְאַפָּֽקוּ׃


Look down from heaven and see; from the habitation of Thy holiness and of Thy glory: where is Thy zeal and Thy mighty acts? Thy compassion and Thy mercies are withheld from me.


###### Isaiah 63:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:16)


כִּֽי־אַתָּ֣ה אָבִ֔ינוּ כִּ֤י אַבְרָהָם֙ לֹ֣א יְדָעָ֔נוּ וְיִשְׂרָאֵ֖ל לֹ֣א יַכִּירָ֑נוּ אַתָּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ אָבִ֔ינוּ גֹּאֲלֵ֥נוּ מֵעוֹלָ֖ם שְׁמֶֽךָ׃
