Thou art our father, though Avraham be ignorant of us, and Yisra᾽el acknowledge us not: Thou, O Lord, art our father, our redeemer; Thy name is from everlasting.


###### Isaiah 63:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:17)


לָ֣מָּה תַתְעֵ֤נוּ יְהֹוָה֙ מִדְּרָכֶ֔יךָ תַּקְשִׁ֥יחַ לִבֵּ֖נוּ מִיִּרְאָתֶ֑ךָ שׁ֚וּב לְמַ֣עַן עֲבָדֶ֔יךָ שִׁבְטֵ֖י נַחֲלָתֶֽךָ׃


O Lord, why hast Thou made us to stray from Thy ways, and hardened our heart from Thy fear? Return for Thy servants’ sake, the tribes of Thy inheritance.


###### Isaiah 63:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:18)


לַמִּצְעָ֕ר יָרְשׁ֖וּ עַם־קׇדְשֶׁ֑ךָ צָרֵ֕ינוּ בּוֹסְס֖וּ מִקְדָּשֶֽׁךָ׃


The people of Thy holiness possessed it but a little while: our adversaries have trodden down Thy sanctuary.


###### Isaiah 63:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/63:19)


הָיִ֗ינוּ מֵֽעוֹלָם֙ לֹא־מָשַׁ֣לְתָּ בָּ֔ם לֹֽא־נִקְרָ֥א שִׁמְךָ֖ עֲלֵיהֶ֑ם לֽוּא־קָרַ֤עְתָּ שָׁמַ֙יִם֙ יָרַ֔דְתָּ מִפָּנֶ֖יךָ הָרִ֥ים נָזֹֽלּוּ׃


We have become like those over whom Thou never didst rule, who were not called by Thy name. Oh, that Thou wouldst rend the heavens, that Thou wouldst come down, that the mountains would melt away at Thy presence.



## Chapter 64

###### Isaiah 64:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:1)


כִּקְדֹ֧חַ אֵ֣שׁ הֲמָסִ֗ים מַ֚יִם תִּבְעֶה־אֵ֔שׁ לְהוֹדִ֥יעַ שִׁמְךָ֖ לְצָרֶ֑יךָ מִפָּנֶ֖יךָ גּוֹיִ֥ם יִרְגָּֽזוּ׃


As when fire kindles brushwood, as when fire causes the water to bubble up; to make Thy name known to Thy enemies, that the nations might tremble at Thy presence.


###### Isaiah 64:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:2)


בַּעֲשׂוֹתְךָ֥ נוֹרָא֖וֹת לֹ֣א נְקַוֶּ֑ה יָרַ֕דְתָּ מִפָּנֶ֖יךָ הָרִ֥ים נָזֹֽלּוּ׃{ס}


When Thou didst terrible things which we looked not for; when Thou didst come down, the mountains flowed down at Thy presence.


###### Isaiah 64:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:3)


וּמֵעוֹלָ֥ם לֹא־שָׁמְע֖וּ לֹ֣א הֶאֱזִ֑ינוּ עַ֣יִן לֹֽא־רָאָ֗תָה אֱלֹהִים֙ זוּלָ֣תְךָ֔ יַעֲשֶׂ֖ה לִמְחַכֵּה־לֽוֹ׃


For since the beginning of the world men have not heard, nor perceived by the ear, neither has the eye seen, that a god, beside Thee, should do such a thing for him that waits for him.


###### Isaiah 64:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:4)


פָּגַ֤עְתָּ אֶת־שָׂשׂ֙ וְעֹ֣שֵׂה צֶ֔דֶק בִּדְרָכֶ֖יךָ יִזְכְּר֑וּךָ הֵן־אַתָּ֤ה קָצַ֙פְתָּ֙ וַֽנֶּחֱטָ֔א בָּהֶ֥ם עוֹלָ֖ם וְנִוָּשֵֽׁעַ׃


Thou didst strike down him that rejoiced and worked righteousness: who remembered Thee in Thy ways: behold, Thou hast been angry, and we have sinned. In those ways we remain always: how then shall we be saved?


###### Isaiah 64:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:5)


וַנְּהִ֤י כַטָּמֵא֙ כֻּלָּ֔נוּ וּכְבֶ֥גֶד עִדִּ֖ים כׇּל־צִדְקֹתֵ֑ינוּ וַנָּ֤בֶל כֶּֽעָלֶה֙ כֻּלָּ֔נוּ וַעֲוֺנֵ֖נוּ כָּר֥וּחַ יִשָּׂאֻֽנוּ׃


And we are all as one that is unclean, and all our righteousnesses as filthy rags; and we all do fade as a leaf; and our iniquities, like the wind, take us away.


###### Isaiah 64:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:6)


וְאֵין־קוֹרֵ֣א בְשִׁמְךָ֔ מִתְעוֹרֵ֖ר לְהַחֲזִ֣יק בָּ֑ךְ כִּֽי־הִסְתַּ֤רְתָּ פָנֶ֙יךָ֙ מִמֶּ֔נּוּ וַתְּמוּגֵ֖נוּ בְּיַד־עֲוֺנֵֽנוּ׃


And there is none that calls upon Thy name, that stirs himself up to take hold of Thee: for Thou hast hid Thy face from us, and hast consumed us, through the force of our iniquities.


###### Isaiah 64:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:7)


וְעַתָּ֥ה יְהֹוָ֖ה אָבִ֣ינוּ אָ֑תָּה אֲנַ֤חְנוּ הַחֹ֙מֶר֙ וְאַתָּ֣ה יֹצְרֵ֔נוּ וּמַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יָדְךָ֖ כֻּלָּֽנוּ׃


But now, O Lord, Thou art our father; we are the clay, and Thou our potter; and we all are the work of Thy hand.


###### Isaiah 64:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:8)


אַל־תִּקְצֹ֤ף יְהֹוָה֙ עַד־מְאֹ֔ד וְאַל־לָעַ֖ד תִּזְכֹּ֣ר עָוֺ֑ן הֵ֥ן הַבֶּט־נָ֖א עַמְּךָ֥ כֻלָּֽנוּ׃


Be not so very indignant, O Lord, neither remember iniquity for ever: behold, see, we beseech Thee, we are all Thy people.


###### Isaiah 64:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:9)


עָרֵ֥י קׇדְשְׁךָ֖ הָי֣וּ מִדְבָּ֑ר צִיּוֹן֙ מִדְבָּ֣ר הָיָ֔תָה יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם שְׁמָמָֽה׃


Thy holy cities are become a wilderness, Żiyyon is a wilderness, Yerushalayim a desolation.


###### Isaiah 64:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:10)


בֵּ֧ית קׇדְשֵׁ֣נוּ וְתִפְאַרְתֵּ֗נוּ אֲשֶׁ֤ר הִֽלְל֙וּךָ֙ אֲבֹתֵ֔ינוּ הָיָ֖ה לִשְׂרֵ֣פַת אֵ֑שׁ וְכׇל־מַחֲמַדֵּ֖ינוּ הָיָ֥ה לְחׇרְבָּֽה׃


Our holy and our beautiful house, where our fathers praised Thee, is burnt with fire: and all our pleasant things are laid waste.


###### Isaiah 64:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/64:11)


הַעַל־אֵ֥לֶּה תִתְאַפַּ֖ק יְהֹוָ֑ה תֶּחֱשֶׁ֥ה וּתְעַנֵּ֖נוּ עַד־מְאֹֽד׃{פ}


Wilt Thou restrain Thyself at these things, O Lord? wilt Thou hold Thy peace, and afflict us so very greatly?



## Chapter 65

###### Isaiah 65:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:1)


נִדְרַ֙שְׁתִּי֙ לְל֣וֹא שָׁאָ֔לוּ נִמְצֵ֖אתִי לְלֹ֣א בִקְשֻׁ֑נִי אָמַ֙רְתִּי֙ הִנֵּ֣נִי הִנֵּ֔נִי אֶל־גּ֖וֹי לֹא־קֹרָ֥א בִשְׁמִֽי׃


I was ready to be sought by those who did not ask for me. I was ready to be found by those who did not seek me. I said, Here I am, here I am, to a nation that was not called by my name.


###### Isaiah 65:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:2)


פֵּרַ֧שְׂתִּי יָדַ֛י כׇּל־הַיּ֖וֹם אֶל־עַ֣ם סוֹרֵ֑ר הַהֹֽלְכִים֙ הַדֶּ֣רֶךְ לֹא־ט֔וֹב אַחַ֖ר מַחְשְׁבֹתֵיהֶֽם׃


I have spread out my hands all the day to a rebellious people, which walks in a way that was not good, after their own thoughts;


###### Isaiah 65:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:3)


הָעָ֗ם הַמַּכְעִסִ֥ים אֹתִ֛י עַל־פָּנַ֖י תָּמִ֑יד זֹֽבְחִים֙ בַּגַּנּ֔וֹת וּֽמְקַטְּרִ֖ים עַל־הַלְּבֵנִֽים׃


a people that provoke me to anger continually to my face; that sacrifice in gardens, and burn incense upon altars of brick;


###### Isaiah 65:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:4)


הַיֹּֽשְׁבִים֙ בַּקְּבָרִ֔ים וּבַנְּצוּרִ֖ים יָלִ֑ינוּ הָאֹֽכְלִים֙ בְּשַׂ֣ר הַחֲזִ֔יר (ופרק) [וּמְרַ֥ק] פִּגֻּלִ֖ים כְּלֵיהֶֽם׃


that sit on the graves, and spend the night in vaults, that eat swine’s flesh, and broth of abominable things is in their vessels;


###### Isaiah 65:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:5)


הָאֹֽמְרִים֙ קְרַ֣ב אֵלֶ֔יךָ אַל־תִּגַּשׁ־בִּ֖י כִּ֣י קְדַשְׁתִּ֑יךָ אֵ֚לֶּה עָשָׁ֣ן בְּאַפִּ֔י אֵ֥שׁ יֹקֶ֖דֶת כׇּל־הַיּֽוֹם׃


that say, Stand by thyself, come not near to me; for I am holier than thou. These are a smoke in my nose, a fire that burns all the day.


###### Isaiah 65:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:6)


הִנֵּ֥ה כְתוּבָ֖ה לְפָנָ֑י לֹ֤א אֶֽחֱשֶׁה֙ כִּ֣י אִם־שִׁלַּ֔מְתִּי וְשִׁלַּמְתִּ֖י עַל־חֵיקָֽם׃


Behold, it is written before me: I will not keep silence, but will repay,


###### Isaiah 65:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:7)


עֲ֠וֺנֹֽתֵיכֶ֠ם וַעֲוֺנֹ֨ת אֲבוֹתֵיכֶ֤ם יַחְדָּו֙ אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה אֲשֶׁ֤ר קִטְּרוּ֙ עַל־הֶ֣הָרִ֔ים וְעַל־הַגְּבָע֖וֹת חֵרְפ֑וּנִי וּמַדֹּתִ֧י פְעֻלָּתָ֛ם רִאשֹׁנָ֖ה (על) [אֶל־]חֵיקָֽם׃{ס}


indeed I will pay home into their bosoms your iniquities, and the iniquities of your fathers together, says the Lord, who burned incense on the mountains, and blasphemed me on the hills: therefore will I repay their former work in full measure into their bosom.


###### Isaiah 65:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:8)


כֹּ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֨ר יִמָּצֵ֤א הַתִּירוֹשׁ֙ בָּֽאֶשְׁכּ֔וֹל וְאָמַר֙ אַל־תַּשְׁחִיתֵ֔הוּ כִּ֥י בְרָכָ֖ה בּ֑וֹ כֵּ֤ן אֶֽעֱשֶׂה֙ לְמַ֣עַן עֲבָדַ֔י לְבִלְתִּ֖י הַֽשְׁחִ֥ית הַכֹּֽל׃


Thus says the Lord, As the wine is found in the cluster, and one says, Destroy it not; for a blessing is in it: so will I do for my servants’ sakes that I may not destroy them all.


###### Isaiah 65:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:9)


וְהוֹצֵאתִ֤י מִֽיַּעֲקֹב֙ זֶ֔רַע וּמִֽיהוּדָ֖ה יוֹרֵ֣שׁ הָרָ֑י וִירֵשׁ֣וּהָ בְחִירַ֔י וַעֲבָדַ֖י יִשְׁכְּנוּ־שָֽׁמָּה׃


And I will bring forth a seed out of Ya῾aqov, and out of Yehuda an inheritor of my mountains: and my chosen ones shall inherit it, and my servants shall dwell there.


###### Isaiah 65:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:10)


וְהָיָ֤ה הַשָּׁרוֹן֙ לִנְוֵה־צֹ֔אן וְעֵ֥מֶק עָכ֖וֹר לְרֵ֣בֶץ בָּקָ֑ר לְעַמִּ֖י אֲשֶׁ֥ר דְּרָשֽׁוּנִי׃


And Sharon shall be a fold of flocks, and the valley of ῾Akhor a place for the herds to lie down in, for my people that have sought me.


###### Isaiah 65:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:11)


וְאַתֶּם֙ עֹזְבֵ֣י יְהֹוָ֔ה הַשְּׁכֵחִ֖ים אֶת־הַ֣ר קׇדְשִׁ֑י הַעֹרְכִ֤ים לַגַּד֙ שֻׁלְחָ֔ן וְהַֽמְמַלְאִ֖ים לַמְנִ֥י מִמְסָֽךְ׃


But you that forsake the Lord that forget my holy mountain, that set out a table for Gad (Fortune,) and fill the cup of liquor for Meni (Destiny,)


###### Isaiah 65:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:12)


וּמָנִ֨יתִי אֶתְכֶ֜ם לַחֶ֗רֶב וְכֻלְּכֶם֙ לַטֶּ֣בַח תִּכְרָ֔עוּ יַ֤עַן קָרָ֙אתִי֙ וְלֹ֣א עֲנִיתֶ֔ם דִּבַּ֖רְתִּי וְלֹ֣א שְׁמַעְתֶּ֑ם וַתַּעֲשׂ֤וּ הָרַע֙ בְּעֵינַ֔י וּבַאֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־חָפַ֖צְתִּי בְּחַרְתֶּֽם׃{פ}


you will I destine for the sword, and you shall all bow down to the slaughter: because when I called, you did not answer; when I spoke, you did not hear; but did evil before my eyes, and did choose that in which I delighted not.


###### Isaiah 65:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:13)


לָכֵ֞ן כֹּה־אָמַ֣ר**׀** אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֗ה הִנֵּ֨ה עֲבָדַ֤י׀יֹאכֵ֙לוּ֙ וְאַתֶּ֣ם תִּרְעָ֔בוּ הִנֵּ֧ה עֲבָדַ֛י יִשְׁתּ֖וּ וְאַתֶּ֣ם תִּצְמָ֑אוּ הִנֵּ֧ה עֲבָדַ֛י יִשְׂמָ֖חוּ וְאַתֶּ֥ם תֵּבֹֽשׁוּ׃


Therefore thus says the Lord God, Behold, my servants shall eat, but you shall be hungry: behold, my servants shall drink, but you shall be thirsty: behold, my servants shall rejoice, but you shall be ashamed:


###### Isaiah 65:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:14)


הִנֵּ֧ה עֲבָדַ֛י יָרֹ֖נּוּ מִטּ֣וּב לֵ֑ב וְאַתֶּ֤ם תִּצְעֲקוּ֙ מִכְּאֵ֣ב לֵ֔ב וּמִשֵּׁ֥בֶר ר֖וּחַ תְּיֵלִֽילוּ׃


behold, my servants shall sing for joy of heart, but you shall cry for sorrow of heart, and shall howl for vexation of spirit.


###### Isaiah 65:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:15)


וְהִנַּחְתֶּ֨ם שִׁמְכֶ֤ם לִשְׁבוּעָה֙ לִבְחִירַ֔י וֶהֱמִֽיתְךָ֖ אֲדֹנָ֣י יֱהֹוִ֑ה וְלַעֲבָדָ֥יו יִקְרָ֖א שֵׁ֥ם אַחֵֽר׃


And you shall leave your name for a curse to my chosen ones: for the Lord God shall slay thee and call his servants by another name:


###### Isaiah 65:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:16)


אֲשֶׁ֨ר הַמִּתְבָּרֵ֜ךְ בָּאָ֗רֶץ יִתְבָּרֵךְ֙ בֵּאלֹהֵ֣י אָמֵ֔ן וְהַנִּשְׁבָּ֣ע בָּאָ֔רֶץ יִשָּׁבַ֖ע בֵּאלֹהֵ֣י אָמֵ֑ן כִּ֣י נִשְׁכְּח֗וּ הַצָּרוֹת֙ הָרִ֣אשֹׁנ֔וֹת וְכִ֥י נִסְתְּר֖וּ מֵעֵינָֽי׃


so that he who blesses himself on the earth, shall bless himself by the God of truth; and he that swears on the earth, shall swear by the God of truth; because the former troubles are forgotten, and because they are hid from my eyes.


###### Isaiah 65:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:17)


כִּֽי־הִנְנִ֥י בוֹרֵ֛א שָׁמַ֥יִם חֲדָשִׁ֖ים וָאָ֣רֶץ חֲדָשָׁ֑ה וְלֹ֤א תִזָּכַ֙רְנָה֙ הָרִ֣אשֹׁנ֔וֹת וְלֹ֥א תַעֲלֶ֖ינָה עַל־לֵֽב׃


For, behold, I create new heavens and a new earth: and the former things shall not be remembered, nor come to mind.


###### Isaiah 65:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:18)


כִּֽי־אִם־שִׂ֤ישֽׂוּ וְגִ֙ילוּ֙ עֲדֵי־עַ֔ד אֲשֶׁ֖ר אֲנִ֣י בוֹרֵ֑א כִּי֩ הִנְנִ֨י בוֹרֵ֧א אֶת־יְרוּשָׁלַ֛͏ִם גִּילָ֖הֿ וְעַמָּ֥הּ מָשֽׂוֹשׂ׃


But be glad and rejoice for ever in that which I create: for, behold, I create Yerushalayim a rejoicing, and her people a joy.


###### Isaiah 65:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:19)


וְגַלְתִּ֥י בִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם וְשַׂשְׂתִּ֣י בְעַמִּ֑י וְלֹא־יִשָּׁמַ֥ע בָּהּ֙ ע֔וֹד ק֥וֹל בְּכִ֖י וְק֥וֹל זְעָקָֽה׃


And I will rejoice in Yerushalayim, and joy in my people: and the voice of weeping shall be no more heard in her, nor the voice of crying.


###### Isaiah 65:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:20)


לֹא־יִהְיֶ֨ה מִשָּׁ֜ם ע֗וֹד ע֤וּל יָמִים֙ וְזָקֵ֔ן אֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹֽא־יְמַלֵּ֖א אֶת־יָמָ֑יו כִּ֣י הַנַּ֗עַר בֶּן־מֵאָ֤ה שָׁנָה֙ יָמ֔וּת וְהַ֣חוֹטֶ֔א בֶּן־מֵאָ֥ה שָׁנָ֖ה יְקֻלָּֽל׃


There shall be no more there an infant who lives a few days, nor an old man that has not filled his days: for the youngest shall die a hundred years old; and the sinner being a hundred years old shall be deemed accursed.


###### Isaiah 65:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:21)


וּבָנ֥וּ בָתִּ֖ים וְיָשָׁ֑בוּ וְנָטְע֣וּ כְרָמִ֔ים וְאָכְל֖וּ פִּרְיָֽם׃


And they shall build houses, and inhabit them; and they shall plant vineyards, and eat the fruit of them.


###### Isaiah 65:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:22)


לֹ֤א יִבְנוּ֙ וְאַחֵ֣ר יֵשֵׁ֔ב לֹ֥א יִטְּע֖וּ וְאַחֵ֣ר יֹאכֵ֑ל כִּֽי־כִימֵ֤י הָעֵץ֙ יְמֵ֣י עַמִּ֔י וּמַעֲשֵׂ֥ה יְדֵיהֶ֖ם יְבַלּ֥וּ בְחִירָֽי׃


They shall not build, and another inhabit; they shall not plant, and another eat: for as the days of a tree shall the days of my people be, and my chosen ones shall long enjoy the work of their hands.


###### Isaiah 65:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:23)


לֹ֤א יִֽיגְעוּ֙ לָרִ֔יק וְלֹ֥א יֵלְד֖וּ לַבֶּהָלָ֑ה כִּ֣י זֶ֜רַע בְּרוּכֵ֤י יְהֹוָה֙ הֵ֔מָּה וְצֶאֱצָאֵיהֶ֖ם אִתָּֽם׃


They shall not labour in vain, nor bring forth for confusion, for they are the seed of the blessed of the Lord, and their offspring with them.


###### Isaiah 65:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:24)


וְהָיָ֥ה טֶרֶם־יִקְרָ֖אוּ וַאֲנִ֣י אֶעֱנֶ֑ה ע֛וֹד הֵ֥ם מְדַבְּרִ֖ים וַאֲנִ֥י אֶשְׁמָֽע׃


And it shall come to pass, that before they call, I will answer; and while they are yet speaking, I will hear.


###### Isaiah 65:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/65:25)


זְאֵ֨ב וְטָלֶ֜ה יִרְע֣וּ כְאֶחָ֗ד וְאַרְיֵה֙ כַּבָּקָ֣ר יֹאכַל־תֶּ֔בֶן וְנָחָ֖שׁ עָפָ֣ר לַחְמ֑וֹ לֹֽא־יָרֵ֧עוּ וְלֹֽא־יַשְׁחִ֛יתוּ בְּכׇל־הַ֥ר קׇדְשִׁ֖י אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃{ס}


The wolf and the lamb shall feed together, and the lion shall eat straw like the ox: and dust shall be the serpent’s food. They shall not hurt nor destroy in all my holy mountain, says the Lord.



## Chapter 66

###### Isaiah 66:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:1)


כֹּ֚ה אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֔ה הַשָּׁמַ֣יִם כִּסְאִ֔י וְהָאָ֖רֶץ הֲדֹ֣ם רַגְלָ֑י אֵי־זֶ֥ה בַ֙יִת֙ אֲשֶׁ֣ר תִּבְנוּ־לִ֔י וְאֵי־זֶ֥ה מָק֖וֹם מְנוּחָתִֽי׃


Thus says the Lord, The heaven is my throne, and the earth is my footstool: where is the house that you would build for me? and where is the place of my rest?


###### Isaiah 66:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:2)


וְאֶת־כׇּל־אֵ֙לֶּה֙ יָדִ֣י עָשָׂ֔תָה וַיִּהְי֥וּ כׇל־אֵ֖לֶּה נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה וְאֶל־זֶ֣ה אַבִּ֔יט אֶל־עָנִי֙ וּנְכֵה־ר֔וּחַ וְחָרֵ֖ד עַל־דְּבָרִֽי׃


For all those things has my hand made, and so all those things came to be, says the Lord: but to this man will I look, to him that is poor and of a contrite spirit, and trembles at my word.


###### Isaiah 66:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:3)


שׁוֹחֵ֨ט הַשּׁ֜וֹר מַכֵּה־אִ֗ישׁ זוֹבֵ֤חַ הַשֶּׂה֙ עֹ֣רֵֽף כֶּ֔לֶב מַעֲלֵ֤ה מִנְחָה֙ דַּם־חֲזִ֔יר מַזְכִּ֥יר לְבֹנָ֖ה מְבָ֣רֵֽךְ אָ֑וֶן גַּם־הֵ֗מָּה בָּֽחֲרוּ֙ בְּדַרְכֵיהֶ֔ם וּבְשִׁקּוּצֵיהֶ֖ם נַפְשָׁ֥ם חָפֵֽצָה׃


He that kills an ox is like one who slays a man; that sacrifices a lamb, is like one who breaks a dog’s neck; that offers a meal offering, like one who offers pig’s blood; that burns incense, like one who blesses an idol: for they have chosen their own ways, and their soul delights in their abominations.


###### Isaiah 66:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:4)


גַּם־אֲנִ֞י אֶבְחַ֣ר בְּתַעֲלֻלֵיהֶ֗ם וּמְגֽוּרֹתָם֙ אָבִ֣יא לָהֶ֔ם יַ֤עַן קָרָ֙אתִי֙ וְאֵ֣ין עוֹנֶ֔ה דִּבַּ֖רְתִּי וְלֹ֣א שָׁמֵ֑עוּ וַיַּעֲשׂ֤וּ הָרַע֙ בְּעֵינַ֔י וּבַאֲשֶׁ֥ר לֹא־חָפַ֖צְתִּי בָּחָֽרוּ׃{ס}


I also will choose torments for them, and will bring their fears upon them; because when I called, none did answer; when I spoke, they did not hear: but they did evil before my eyes, and chose that in which I delighted not.


###### Isaiah 66:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:5)


שִׁמְעוּ֙ דְּבַר־יְהֹוָ֔ה הַחֲרֵדִ֖ים אֶל־דְּבָר֑וֹ אָמְרוּ֩ אֲחֵיכֶ֨ם שֹׂנְאֵיכֶ֜ם מְנַדֵּיכֶ֗ם לְמַ֤עַן שְׁמִי֙ יִכְבַּ֣ד יְהֹוָ֔ה וְנִרְאֶ֥ה בְשִׂמְחַתְכֶ֖ם וְהֵ֥ם יֵבֹֽשׁוּ׃


Hear the word of the Lord, you that tremble at his word; Your brethren that hated you, that cast you out for my name’s sake, have said, Let the Lord be glorified: that we may see your joy; but they shall be ashamed.


###### Isaiah 66:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:6)


ק֤וֹל שָׁאוֹן֙ מֵעִ֔יר ק֖וֹל מֵהֵיכָ֑ל ק֣וֹל יְהֹוָ֔ה מְשַׁלֵּ֥ם גְּמ֖וּל לְאֹיְבָֽיו׃


A voice of tumult from the city, a voice from the temple, the voice of the Lord rendering recompense to his enemies.


###### Isaiah 66:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:7)


בְּטֶ֥רֶם תָּחִ֖יל יָלָ֑דָה בְּטֶ֨רֶם יָב֥וֹא חֵ֛בֶל לָ֖הּ וְהִמְלִ֥יטָה זָכָֽר׃


Before she travailed, she brought forth; before her pain came, she was delivered of a man child.


###### Isaiah 66:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:8)


מִֽי־שָׁמַ֣ע כָּזֹ֗את מִ֤י רָאָה֙ כָּאֵ֔לֶּה הֲי֤וּחַל אֶ֙רֶץ֙ בְּי֣וֹם אֶחָ֔ד אִם־יִוָּ֥לֵֽד־גּ֖וֹי פַּ֣עַם אֶחָ֑ת כִּי־חָ֛לָה גַּם־יָלְדָ֥ה צִיּ֖וֹן אֶת־בָּנֶֽיהָ׃


Who has heard of such a thing? who has seen such things? Shall the earth be made to bring forth in one day? or shall a nation be born in one moment? for as soon as Żiyyon travailed, she brought forth her children.


###### Isaiah 66:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:9)


הַאֲנִ֥י אַשְׁבִּ֛יר וְלֹ֥א אוֹלִ֖יד יֹאמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה אִם־אֲנִ֧י הַמּוֹלִ֛יד וְעָצַ֖רְתִּי אָמַ֥ר אֱלֹהָֽיִךְ׃{ס}


Shall I bring to the birth, and not cause to bring forth? says the Lord: or shall I who cause to bring forth, thereupon shut the womb? says thy God.


###### Isaiah 66:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:10)


שִׂמְח֧וּ אֶת־יְרוּשָׁלַ֛͏ִם וְגִ֥ילוּ בָ֖הּ כׇּל־אֹהֲבֶ֑יהָ שִׂ֤ישׂוּ אִתָּהּ֙ מָשׂ֔וֹשׂ כׇּל־הַמִּֽתְאַבְּלִ֖ים עָלֶֽיהָ׃


Rejoice with Yerushalayim, and be glad with her, all you that love her: rejoice for joy with her, all you that did mourn for her:


###### Isaiah 66:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:11)


לְמַ֤עַן תִּֽינְקוּ֙ וּשְׂבַעְתֶּ֔ם מִשֹּׁ֖ד תַּנְחֻמֶ֑יהָ לְמַ֧עַן תָּמֹ֛צּוּ וְהִתְעַנַּגְתֶּ֖ם מִזִּ֥יז כְּבוֹדָֽהּ׃{ס}


that you may suck, and be satisfied with the breast of her consolations; that you may drink deeply, and be delighted with the abundance of her glory.


###### Isaiah 66:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:12)


כִּי־כֹ֣ה**׀** אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֗ה הִנְנִ֣י נֹטֶֽה־אֵ֠לֶ֠יהָ כְּנָהָ֨ר שָׁל֜וֹם וּכְנַ֧חַל שׁוֹטֵ֛ף כְּב֥וֹד גּוֹיִ֖ם וִֽינַקְתֶּ֑ם עַל־צַד֙ תִּנָּשֵׂ֔אוּ וְעַל־בִּרְכַּ֖יִם תְּשׇׁעֳשָֽׁעוּ׃


For thus says the Lord, Behold, I will extend peace to her like a river, and the glory of the nations like a flowing stream: then shall you suck, you shall be carried upon her sides, and be dandled upon her knees.


###### Isaiah 66:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:13)


כְּאִ֕ישׁ אֲשֶׁ֥ר אִמּ֖וֹ תְּנַחֲמֶ֑נּוּ כֵּ֤ן אָֽנֹכִי֙ אֲנַ֣חֶמְכֶ֔ם וּבִירוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם תְּנֻחָֽמוּ׃


As one whom his mother comforts, so will I comfort you; and you shall be comforted in Yerushalayim.


###### Isaiah 66:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:14)


וּרְאִיתֶם֙ וְשָׂ֣שׂ לִבְּכֶ֔ם וְעַצְמוֹתֵיכֶ֖ם כַּדֶּ֣שֶׁא תִפְרַ֑חְנָה וְנוֹדְעָ֤ה יַד־יְהֹוָה֙ אֶת־עֲבָדָ֔יו וְזָעַ֖ם אֶת־אֹיְבָֽיו׃{ס}


And when you see this, your heart shall rejoice, and your bones shall flourish like grass: and the hand of the Lord shall be known toward his servants, and his indignation toward his enemies.


###### Isaiah 66:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:15)


כִּֽי־הִנֵּ֤ה יְהֹוָה֙ בָּאֵ֣שׁ יָב֔וֹא וְכַסּוּפָ֖ה מַרְכְּבֹתָ֑יו לְהָשִׁ֤יב בְּחֵמָה֙ אַפּ֔וֹ וְגַעֲרָת֖וֹ בְּלַהֲבֵי־אֵֽשׁ׃


For, behold, the Lord will come with fire, and with his chariots like a storm, to render his anger with fury, and his rebuke with flames of fire.


###### Isaiah 66:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:16)


כִּ֤י בָאֵשׁ֙ יְהֹוָ֣ה נִשְׁפָּ֔ט וּבְחַרְבּ֖וֹ אֶת־כׇּל־בָּשָׂ֑ר וְרַבּ֖וּ חַֽלְלֵ֥י יְהֹוָֽה׃


For by fire will the Lord execute judgment, and with his sword, upon all flesh: and the slain of the Lord shall be many.


###### Isaiah 66:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:17)


הַמִּתְקַדְּשִׁ֨ים וְהַמִּֽטַּהֲרִ֜ים אֶל־הַגַּנּ֗וֹת אַחַ֤ר (אחד) [אַחַת֙] בַּתָּ֔וֶךְ אֹֽכְלֵי֙ בְּשַׂ֣ר הַחֲזִ֔יר וְהַשֶּׁ֖קֶץ וְהָעַכְבָּ֑ר יַחְדָּ֥ו יָסֻ֖פוּ נְאֻם־יְהֹוָֽה׃


They that sanctify themselves, and purify themselves in the gardens behind one in the midst, eating swine’s flesh, and the detestable thing, and the mouse, shall perish together, says the Lord.


###### Isaiah 66:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:18)


וְאָנֹכִ֗י מַֽעֲשֵׂיהֶם֙ וּמַחְשְׁבֹ֣תֵיהֶ֔ם בָּאָ֕ה לְקַבֵּ֥ץ אֶת־כׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֖ם וְהַלְּשֹׁנ֑וֹת וּבָ֖אוּ וְרָא֥וּ אֶת־כְּבוֹדִֽי׃


For I know their works and their thoughts: the time shall come, that I will gather all nations and tongues; and they shall come, and see my glory.


###### Isaiah 66:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:19)


וְשַׂמְתִּ֨י בָהֶ֜ם א֗וֹת וְשִׁלַּחְתִּ֣י מֵהֶ֣ם׀פְּ֠לֵיטִ֠ים אֶֽל־הַגּוֹיִ֞ם תַּרְשִׁ֨ישׁ פּ֥וּל וְל֛וּד מֹ֥שְׁכֵי קֶ֖שֶׁת תֻּבַ֣ל וְיָוָ֑ן הָאִיִּ֣ים הָרְחֹקִ֗ים אֲשֶׁ֨ר לֹֽא־שָׁמְע֤וּ אֶת־שִׁמְעִי֙ וְלֹא־רָא֣וּ אֶת־כְּבוֹדִ֔י וְהִגִּ֥ידוּ אֶת־כְּבוֹדִ֖י בַּגּוֹיִֽם׃


And I will set a sign among them, and I will send those that escape of them to the nations, to Tarshish, Pul, and Lud, that draw the bow, to Tuval, and Yavan, to the distant islands, that have not heard my fame, and have not seen my glory; and they shall declare my glory among the nations.


###### Isaiah 66:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:20)


וְהֵבִ֣יאוּ אֶת־כׇּל־אֲחֵיכֶ֣ם מִכׇּל־הַגּוֹיִ֣ם׀מִנְחָ֣ה׀לַיהֹוָ֡ה בַּסּוּסִ֡ים וּ֠בָרֶ֠כֶב וּבַצַּבִּ֨ים וּבַפְּרָדִ֜ים וּבַכִּרְכָּר֗וֹת עַ֣ל הַ֥ר קׇדְשִׁ֛י יְרוּשָׁלַ֖͏ִם אָמַ֣ר יְהֹוָ֑ה כַּאֲשֶׁ֣ר יָבִ֩יאוּ֩ בְנֵ֨י יִשְׂרָאֵ֧ל אֶת־הַמִּנְחָ֛ה בִּכְלִ֥י טָה֖וֹר בֵּ֥ית יְהֹוָֽה׃


And they shall bring all your brethren out of all the nations for an offering to the Lord upon horses, and in chariots, and in litters, and upon mules, and upon fleet camels, to my holy mountain Yerushalayim, says the Lord, as the children of Yisra᾽el bring an offering in a clean vessel to the house of the Lord.


###### Isaiah 66:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:21)


וְגַם־מֵהֶ֥ם אֶקַּ֛ח לַכֹּהֲנִ֥ים לַלְוִיִּ֖ם אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


And I will also take of them for priests and for Levites, says the Lord.


###### Isaiah 66:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:22)


כִּ֣י כַאֲשֶׁ֣ר הַשָּׁמַ֣יִם הַ֠חֲדָשִׁ֠ים וְהָאָ֨רֶץ הַחֲדָשָׁ֜ה אֲשֶׁ֨ר אֲנִ֥י עֹשֶׂ֛ה עֹמְדִ֥ים לְפָנַ֖י נְאֻם־יְהֹוָ֑ה כֵּ֛ן יַעֲמֹ֥ד זַרְעֲכֶ֖ם וְשִׁמְכֶֽם׃


For as the new heavens and the new earth, which I will make, shall remain before me, says the Lord, so shall your seed and your name remain.


###### Isaiah 66:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:23)


וְהָיָ֗ה מִֽדֵּי־חֹ֙דֶשׁ֙ בְּחׇדְשׁ֔וֹ וּמִדֵּ֥י שַׁבָּ֖ת בְּשַׁבַּתּ֑וֹ יָב֧וֹא כׇל־בָּשָׂ֛ר לְהִשְׁתַּחֲוֺ֥ת לְפָנַ֖י אָמַ֥ר יְהֹוָֽה׃


And it shall come to pass, that every new moon, and every sabbath, shall all flesh come to bow down to the ground before me, says the Lord.


###### Isaiah 66:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Isaiah/r/66:24)


וְיָצְא֣וּ וְרָא֔וּ בְּפִגְרֵי֙ הָאֲנָשִׁ֔ים הַפֹּשְׁעִ֖ים בִּ֑י כִּ֣י תוֹלַעְתָּ֞ם לֹ֣א תָמ֗וּת וְאִשָּׁם֙ לֹ֣א תִכְבֶּ֔ה וְהָי֥וּ דֵרָא֖וֹן לְכׇל־בָּשָֽׂר׃


And they shall go forth, and look upon the carcasses of the men that have rebelled against me: for their worm shall not die, neither shall their fire be quenched; and they shall be an abhorrence to all flesh.
